prathamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||

mahāmuneḥ |

ko vā kartā pramā kāsya kiṃ vā cāsya prayojanam ||

garbhī vai kolikaṃ cāpi śiṣyavyākhyānamiṣyate ||

48. na rakṣayanti guhyaṃ te prakāśayanti sarvataḥ
49. sākṣādbuddhavadācāryamindraṃ te paryupāsya ca
50. kṣaṇād dveṣaṃ prakurvanti tathaivānunayaṃ kṣaṇāt
51. agamyaṃ caiva gacchanti mātaraṃ bhaginīṃ suta [[m ]
52. mātāpitṛvadhaṃ caiva tathānyaprāṇighātakam
53. mṛṣā vādaṃ vadantyeva cauryaṃ caiva viśeṣataḥ
54. paradārāṃśca gacchanti kurvantyanyacca garhitam
55. samādhi te na jānanti tathatājñānasaṃbhavam
56. svalpamātreṇa doṣeṇa kurvantyapyabhicārakam
57. yogaprayogahīnāśca na ca sidhyanti mantriṇaḥ
58. bāhyaśāstraratā nityaṃ tatprayogaikanaipunāḥ
59. svasamayaṃ parityajya bhavantyeva niraṅakuśāḥ
60. mantramudrāprayogeṇa jīvikāṃ kalpayanti te
61. vyākhyānaṃ ca kariṣyanti yatra labdhirbhaviṣyati

mahāṃścāsau vairocanaśceti mahāvairocano vītarāgarūpamapanīya mahāvajradhararūpamātmānamabhinirmāya rāgeṇa rāgaprahāṇārthaṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ mahā rāgo mahārāgaḥ sattvapāvanaśaucaviṣayeṣvāsaktiḥ sa eva nayo yasya samādheḥ, taṃ samāpadya tamanantaroktaṃ sarvatathāgatavyūhamiti māṇḍaleyān svakāye praveśayāmāseti svabījākṣararaśmyaṅa kuśenākṛṣya svakāye skandhadhātvādi- svabhāvena praveśitavānityarthaḥ |
atha te sarvatathāgatā mahāvajradharasya kāye pratiṣṭhā bhagavataḥ paripoṣaṇārthamiti bhagavataḥ paramānandasukhenāhlādanārthaṃ svavimvāni strīvimvānyabhinirmāyeti pūrvāvasthāmapanīya divyastrīrūpamabhi- nirmāya bhagavataḥ kāyānniṣkrāntā nirgatavantaḥ |

virajaska- mahāsamayo mahāvajradharaḥ tasya maṇḍalaṃ mahāsamaya maṇḍalamadhiṣṭhāpayāmāseti nirmita- vānityarthaḥ |
svacchaṃ tatsvabhāvamiti |
kūṭāgāravahiḥ sthitānāmapi sphaṭi- kāntargatarūpadarśanavanmāṇḍaleya devatārūpadarśanāt |
nānārūpaṃ samantāditi |
indranīlādivicitravarṇaviśeṣamāha
buddhameghasamākīrṇamiti
utsargamaṇḍaleya- devatābhirvyāptam
sphuliṅgagahanākulamiti
daśadikkrodharaśmiprajvalita- gahanam
svacchādimaṇḍalairyuktamiti
candramaṇḍalādyāsanāni svacchādimaṇḍalāni tairyuktaṃ sarvatāthāgataṃ puramiti
sarvatāthāgatānāmidaṃ puraṃ maṇḍalamityarthaḥ
athetyādinā kāyavākcittādhipatirmahāvajradharo hyanantaroktamahāsamaya- maṇḍalamadhye ātmānaṃ pratiṣṭhāpitavān
atha svatvityādi
bodhicittavajrasya hṛdaye vijahāreti pañcakulaprativaddhadevatācakram
punarapi bodhicittavajrasya kāye sthitiṃ cakāra iti neyārthaḥ
akṣobhya : tathāgato vijñānaskandhātmakaḥ
sarvatathāgatā rūpaskandhādayaḥ
tairyā uṣitā nilayīkṛtā yoṣit
kāsau ? prajñāpāramitā
saiva bhagaṃ yoniḥ buddhotpattikāraṇatvāt
yathoktaṃ bhagavatā `sandhyāvyākaraṇatantre sarvabuddhoṣitā yā vā bhūmiḥ syāt sā trayodaśī
sā ca yoṣitsamākhyātā taddharmaṃ bhaga ucyate || iti
tasmin virajaskamahāsamayamaṇḍalamātmānaṃ pratiṣṭhāpayāmāsa sthāpitavān
kīdṛgvidhamityāha
svacchaṃ sphaṭikavat tatsvabhāvam ābhāsasvabhāvaṃ nānārūpaṃ pañcaraśmisamākīrṇatvāt sarvatathāgatātmakatvād buddhameghasamākīrṇa kiraṇāmala- krodhatvāt
sphuliṅgagahanākulam
ālokādyābhāsatrayanirmitatvāt
svacchādi- maṇḍalairyuktam sarvatāthāgataṃ puram mahāvajradharadeham
kīdṛgvidham ?
mahā- samayamaṇḍalaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ praveśitavāniti yāvat
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane atha prabhāsvarapraveśādanantaraṃ bhagavān mahāvajradharaḥ sarvatathāgatakāya- vākcittādhipatiḥ sarvakulātmakamātmānaṃ paramārthasatyād vyutkāyabhavanirvāṇaika- rasasvabhāvena sarvatathāgatamahāsamayamaṇḍalamadhye pratiṣṭhāpayāmāseti trailokyādhi- patitvena sthāpitavānityarthaḥ
“sarvākāśāvakāśe śrīvanasattva : pratiṣṭhitaḥ iti sambare vacanāt
atha khalvakṣobhyastathāgata ityādinā sattvārthakriyāṃ prati skandhādyanupraveśena nirmāṇakāyagrahaṇaṃ darśayatīti nītārthaḥ
tamarthaṃ dyotayannāha
athetyādi
atītānāgatavartamānatathāgatānāṃ candravajrādikrameṇa ekīkaraṇaṃ sarvatathāgatābhi- bhavanam
tadarthaṃ vajraṃ tadeva nāma yasya samādheḥ taṃ samādhi samāpannaḥ
samanantaretyādi sarvatathāgatavajjrasamayasaṃsthito'bhūditi
mahāvajra eva māṇḍaleyāḥ saṃvṛttā ityarthaḥ
tena mahāvajjradharamāṇḍaleyena kiṃ kṛtamityāha
sarvasattvāḥ śrīvajrasattvāvegenāviṣṭāḥ vajrasattvatvaṃ prāptāḥ
ata āha sukha- saumanasyalābhino'bhūvanniti
samastāṅgam
sarvatathāgatāḥ skandhādisvabhāvāḥ saṃvṛtibodhicittākāreṇābhibhūyante ekīkriyante yena samādhinā sa eva vajramivābhedyattrāt
tadeva nāma yasya samādheḥ taṃ samāpannaḥ
samanantaretyādi
sarvākāśadhāturityatra dvāsaptati nāḍīsahasrāṇāṃ suśiraṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ vajramatra upāyajñānaṃ tanmayaṃ tatsva- bhāvamabhūt bhūtavānityarthaḥ
sarvasattvā iti sthāṇvādayaḥ
vajrasattvādhiṣṭhā- neneti upāyajñānodayena sarvatathāgatasukhasaumanasyalābhino'bhūvanniti
dvayendriya- samāyogānmahārāgānalena dravībhūtāḥ paramānandasukhamanubhavanta ityarthaḥ
garbhī
sarvatathāgatāḥ pañcaskandhāḥ teṣāmabhibhavanaṃ paramārthasatye caikīkaraṇam, tadeva vajjramabhedyatvāt
tannāmakaṃ samādhimāpannaḥ samādhisamāpannānantaramevamākāśa- dhātuḥ
śūnyattrayaṃ sarvatathāgatavatraṃ sarvaśūnyaṃ tanmayastatsvabhāvo bhūtaḥ
atha yāvanta ityādināpi imamevārthaṃ dyotayati kolikavyākhyā
imamadhimokṣeṇa mahāvajradharaṃ niṣpādyedānīṃ pariśuddhāpariśuddhadevatāmūrti darśayannāha
athetyādi sarvatathāgata kāyavākcittavatraṃ tyakṣaram alaṅghyatvāt
tadeva samayastasyānubhavaḥ
udbhūtābhedyatvāt
vajraṃ samādhi samāpannaḥ
mahāvidyāpuruṣamūrtimiti mahāvidyāmantrākṣarāṇi tairniṣpāditā puruṣamūrtiryasya prathamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā sa mahāvidyāpuruṣamūrtiḥ sarvatathāgatamantrādhiṣṭhānamadhiṣṭhāpayāmāseti sarvatathā- gatānāṃ mantrāḥ oṃkārādayaḥ tairadhiṣṭhānaṃ nyāso yasya sa tathoktaḥ
anenaitat sūcitaṃ syāt
sādhanoktanyāyena caturyogakrameṇa trisattvaparyantaṃ yāvat sarvamantramūrti mahāvajradharamātmānamadhiṣṭhitavān
satyādhiṣṭhāne kimākaraḥ
saṃvṛtta ityāha
trimukhākāreṇa vineyānāṃ dṛśyatāṃ gataḥ
ata āha
sarvalokadhātusthitaiḥ saṃdṛśyate smeti neyārthaḥ
sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittavajriṇaḥ samayaḥ prabhāsvaraḥ tasmādudbhavaḥ utpādo vajravadabhedyo yasya sa tathoktaḥ, mahā śreṣṭho'prameyo vidvāṃśca vīryotsāhakriyātmakaḥ
atyadbhutasya kartā ca mahānityabhidhīyate
mahāvidyā paramārthasatyam, puruṣaḥ saṃvṛtisatyam
tābhyāṃ niṣpāditā sarvā- kāravaropetādiyuktā puruṣākāradevatāmūrtiḥ mahāvidyāpuruṣamūrtiḥ
mahāvajradharaḥ sarvatathāgatamantrādhiṣṭhānamadhiṣṭhāpayāmāseti
sarvatathāgataparamāṇuparighaṭita- mātmānaṃ niṣpāditavānityabhiprāyaḥ
samanantara ityādi pūrvavat nītārthaḥ
athetyādi
akṣobhyādayastathāgatākṛṣṭacittāḥ pūrvoktād hṛdayasamūhānniṣkra myodānamudīritavantaḥ
aho āścarye
hi śabdo yasmādarthe
bodhicitta- pravartanamiti
dvividhadevatārūpam
vineyānāṃ santāne pravartanaṃ bodhicitta- pravartanam
sarvatathāgatānāmidaṃ sārvatāthāgatam
gopanīyaṃ guhyam
ūhāpaga- tatvādapratarkyam
traidhātukānniḥsṛtatvādanālayamiti nāsandhyam
idānīmanāgatavineyānāṃ śiṣyācāryasaṃbandhapratipādanāyāha
athetyānantarye
samājamāgamyeti
mīlanaṃ kṛtvā sarvatathāgatapūjāspharaṇasamayatattvaratnānāṃ samūhā meghā staiḥ saṃpūjya praṇamya coktavantaḥ
neyārthaḥ
sarvatathāgata- rūpādayaḥ taiḥ kṛtā' pūjā sarvatathāgatapūjā tasyāḥ spharaṇaṃ vahulīkaraṇaṃ tadeva samayo'laṅghya tvāt
tattvaratnāni strī (? tri) ratnāni teṣāṃ meghāḥ samūhāstaiḥ saṃpūjya praṇipatyaivamāha
nītārthaḥ
bhāṣasva samājamiti saṃvandhaḥ
tattvaṃ yathābhūtam
vajrasārāstathāgatāḥ teṣāmekībhāvalakṣaṇaṃ samuccayaḥ
sarvatathāgata- guhyamiti sarvatathāgataparamparāyātaṃ guhyaṃ gopanīyam
guhyasaṃbhavamiti rahasyātma- bhūtaṃ samājamiti
dvayorekībhāvaḥ samājam
akṣarārthaḥ
candrapadmasamāyogaṃ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane samājaṃ samastāṅgam
prajñopāyasamāyogaṃ samājam
garbho
saṃvṛtiparamārtha- satyayormīlanaṃ samājam
guhyasaṃbhavamityasyaiva viśeṣaṇam
guhyaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tasmāt saṃbhūtamadvayajñānātmakaṃ mahāvajradharamiti yāvat
kolikam
athetyanantaraṃ yo'dhicitta vajradharaḥ sarvatathāgatānevaṃ vakti
sādhu sādhvi- tyabhyupagame
abhimatārthadyotakatvāt praśnasya
kiṃ tu śabdo vitarke
sarva- tathāgatānāmapi sandehakaro'yamiti
ghaṭamānayogino'na tathāgatāḥ bodhi- sattvāśca draṣṭavyāḥ
nahi kleśavāsanāprahīṇānāṃ buddhabodhisattvānāṃ saṃśayo'sti
tathāgatā ana ratnapudgalāḥ paripakvakuśalamūlā: śrutipāraṅgatā buddhatvābhi- lāṣiṇo niyatagotrāḥ
guruvaktreṇa vinā teṣāmapyaviṣaya: samājārthaḥ ūhāpagatatvāt
kuto'nyeṣāṃ bodhisattvānāmiti ?
candanādīnāṃ pudgalānām paripakvakuśalamūlānāṃ hīnādhimuktīnāṃ pratipādite'pi durgrāhyatvādanutsāha- māvedayati
evaṃ bhagavadvacanamupaśrutya niyatagotrakāṇāṃ ratnapudgalānāmapya viṣayo'- yamityāścaryaprāptāḥ
kiṃ punarniyatagotrakāṇāṃ puṇḍarīkādīnāmityadbhuta- prāptāstataḥ punarapi sarvatathāgatā bhagavantamevamāhuḥ
yadiha yasmādevaṃguṇa- viśiṣṭe'pīti ratnapudgalāneva sūcayati
sandhyābhāṣā
sarvatathāgatānāṃ kāyavākcittaguhyaṃ samayasaṃvarādi kulatrayapariṇāmaḥ
akṣarārthaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ kāyavākcittaguhyaṃ tyakṣaraṃ samastāṅgam
sarvatathāgatānāṃ kāyavākcittaguhyaṃ jñānatrayaprakṛtyābhāsavāyuvāhanam
sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittaguhyaṃ garbho
jñānamayadehaḥ
kolikam
sarvatathāgatavajjrasamayasaṃbhavapadairadhiṣṭhānaṃ kṛtvā
prakāśayatviti saṃvandhaḥ
sarvatathāgatādhiṣṭhānaṃ kāyādyāveśa: te kṛtvā
kai: ? sarvatathāgatavajjrasamayasaṃbhavapadaiḥ
sarvatathāgatavajrasamayāḥ kāyavajrādayaḥ teṣāṃ saṃbhavapadāni tadutpattibījākṣarāṇi
samastāṅgam
vajrasamayo dvayendriyasamāpattiḥ
tatsaṃbhavapadāni
bodhicittavindavaḥ tairadhiṣṭhānamabhiṣekaḥ taṃ kṛtvā
garbho
vajrasamayaḥ prabhāsvaraṃ tatsaṃbhavapadāni mahāśūnyātiśūnyaśūnyāni tairadhiṣṭhānaṃ vineyajanasantāne dvividhadevatāmūrti- pradyotanaṃ kṛtvā saṃprakāśayatu bhagavāniti
kolikam
evamadhyeṣito bhagavānadhyeṣaṇāṃ viditvā teṣāmabhiṣekārtha jñānamaṇḍalaṃ nirmiṇoti
jñānameva pradīpo jñānapradīpaḥ
akṣarārthaḥ
jñānapradīpa akṣobhyaḥ sattvasantāneṣu jñānālokakaratvāt
samastāṅgam
jñānameva vijñānatrayābhāsaṃ daśadigavabhāsanāt
pradīpa iva pradīpastaṃ samādhiṃ samāpannaḥ
garbho
jñā avabodhane
naḥ pratiṣedhe vartate
avabodhābhāvo yasmin tajjñānam
kiṃ tat ? paramārthasatyamanabhilāpyam
aśeṣatejāṃsyabhibhūyānantalokadhātvavabhāsanāt tadeva pradīpaḥ
abhedyatvād vajram
tannāmakaṃ samādhi samāpadya kolikam
prabhāsvarasamādhervyutthāya pañcakleśākīrṇasattvadhātuṃ vilokya kleśasvabhāva- parijñānameva bodhiḥ tatsvabhāvo'hamiti kṛtvā vineyānāṃ dveṣakleśa viśodhanāya idaṃ dveṣakulaparamasārahṛdayaṃ svakāyavākcittavaprebhyo niścārayediti
idamiti buddhistham
mahākaruṇopāya krodho dveṣaḥ tasya kulaṃ dveṣakulam
ākāṃkṣita- phalāvāhakatvāt paramasāraṃ hṛdayamantraṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayet samāpattitaḥ samutsṛṣṭavān
yathārutam
vajradhṛgiti pañcākārābhisaṃbodhisaṃsūcakam
vajraṃ dhārayatīti vajjradhṛk
neyārthaḥ
satyadvayātmakaṃ vajrajñānaṃ dhārayatīti vajradhṛk
nītārthaḥ
athāsminnityādi
niṣīdayāmāseti saṃvandhaḥ
sa eveti pūrvokto mahāvajradharaḥ
kāyavākcitteti vyātmako vidyāpuruṣa iti
satyadvayātmakaḥ
akṣobhyamahāmudrākṛṣṭasukhādyātmikā
tayā saṃyogaḥ ekībhāvaḥ
sa eva paramapadaṃ kṛṣṇasitaraktākāreṇeti pūrvavat
kāyavākcittavajre niṣīdayāmāseti
mahāvajradharasya kāyavākcittavatre niṣaṇṇavānityarthaḥ
vajrasattvāt sargaḥ prathamaḥ
yathārutam
ādiyogānantaramutsargamaṇḍalaṃ nirdiśati
atha bhagavān hṛdayaṃ niścāra- yāmāseti saṃvandhaḥ
samayodbhavavastraṃ nāma samādhiḥ
vibhaktārthaḥ
mūḍhasattva- viśodhanāya śāntaraso mohaḥ sa eva paramapadaṃ tasya kulam
paramasāretyādi gatārtham
jinajigiti
jayanaśīlā jinā mārāḥ tān dvādaśākāradharma- cakreṇa jayatīti jinajik
neyārthaḥ
vajropamasamādhinā caturmārābhibhavanāt jinajik
nītārthaḥ
sa eva bhagavān purato niṣīdayāmāseti saṃvandhaḥ
vairocanamahāmudrayā sahaikībhūya purata ityagrato bhagavantamevālokayan niṣaṇṇavān
yathārutam
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane athetyādi pūrvavat saṃvandhaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ ratnaṃ saṃvṛtibodhicittaṃ tataḥ saṃbhavatīti ratnasaṃbhavaḥ
vajraśrīḥ sā eva mahāmudrā sampadyasmin samādhau tamāmukhīkṛtya
neyārthaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ ratnaṃ paramārthabodhicittaṃ tasmāt saṃbhavatīti ratnasaṃbhavaḥ vajraśrīravinaśvaraśrīḥ
tadeva nāma yasya samādheḥ taṃ samāpannaḥ
nītārthaḥ
sarvāśāparipūrakatvāccintāmaṇiḥ tasya kulaṃ paramasāretyādi gatārtham
ratnadhṛgiti
sarvāśāparipūrakaṃ ratnaṃ dhārayatīti ratnadhṛk
neyārthaḥ
bhūtakoṭiratnaṃ dhārayatīti ratnadhṛk
nītārthaḥ
ratnasaṃbhavamahāmudrāsamayasa hai- kībhāvaḥ saṃyogaḥ
sa eva gaganapadam
gaganavannirlepasamatājñānaṃ gaganapadamucyate
dhanamadagarvitasattvānāṃ dāne buddhāsanaṃ kṛtvā mānaviśuddhipade saṃsthāpya bhagavato dakṣiṇapārśve niṣaṇṇāḥ
yathārutam
evaṃ rāgarati sattvānāṃ rāgeṇa rāgaprahāṇārthamamitābhayogaṃ darśayannāha athetyādi
tathaiva saṃvandhaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ mahārāgaḥ paramānandaḥ
tanniṣpanna- bodhicittaṃ tasmāt saṃbhavo'bhitābhaḥ
neyārthaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ mahārāgaḥ prabhāsvaraṃ tasmādudbhavo'mitābhaḥ
tadeva vajrāntamālambya
nītārthaḥ
pratyavekṣaṇā- jjñāneṣvāsaktiḥ vajjrarāgaḥ tasya kulaṃ paramasāretyādivibhaktārthaḥ
āroligiti
ā samantā[t] roḥ saṃsāraḥ tasmāllik gacchatīti ārolik
neyārthaḥ
ro iti jñānatrayaṃ tasmāt paramārthasatyaṃ gatavāniti
nītārthaḥ
athetyādi pūrvavat saṃvandhaḥ
sarvatathāgatāmogho amoghasiddhiḥ
sa eva samaya: kṛtyānuṣṭhānalakṣaṇasakalajagadarthasampādanenālaṅaghyanīyatvāt
tadeva vajjraṃ yasmin samādhau sa tathoktaḥ
neyārthaḥ
amoghasamayaḥ prabhāsvaraḥ animittaphaladānāt tadeva vajraṃ tamālamvanaḥ samādhistathoktaḥ
nītārthaḥ
īrṣyākleśaviśodhane- naivākṛṣya samaya bhraṃśādidoṣāpanayanārthaṃ kulaṃ samayākarṣaṇakulam
neyārthaḥ
samayaḥ sūkṣmadhātu[:] tenopāyajñānamākṛṣya paramānandamutpādya pariśodhanakulaṃ samayākarṣaṇakulam
nītārthaḥ
paramasāretyādi vibhaktārtham
prajñādhṛgiti
pravicayalakṣaṇā prajñā, tāṃ dhārayatīti prajñādhṛk
neyārthaḥ
prajñā candrābhāsaḥ tāṃ sūkṣmadhātunā dhārayatīti prajñādhṛk
nītārthaḥ
uttare niṣaṇṇaḥ
yathārutam
prathamapaṭala ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā dveṣa ityādi
vineyānāṃ mokṣābhilāṣotpādanaṃ kāmaḥ
anāsravamahāsukhaṃ mokṣaḥ
neyārthaḥ
kulā hyete kāmamokṣaprasādhakā iti ete kulāḥ pañca- skandhasvabhāvāḥ kāmopabhogena mokṣaprasādhakāḥ athavā kāmaprasādhakā mokṣa prasādhakāśceti
yathārutam
ata āha vyākhyātantre skandhā ete hi vṛddhāsyaṃ bodhyarthapratyayodbhavāḥ
te'pi tathāgatāḥ khyātāstathatādvayayogataḥ // iti
nītārthaḥ
punarvairocanādīnāmupabhogāya devīcatuṣṭayamutsṛjati
athetyādi niścārayāmāseti saṃvandhaḥ
vajradharānurāgaṇasamayā māmakī tadālamvanasamādhiṃ samāpannaḥ
yathārutam
dveṣaratīti
dveṣo'kṣobhyastasmin ratiḥ
dveṣaratiḥ
neyārthaḥ
dveṣakleśaviśuddhe ratiḥ dveṣaratiḥ
nītārthaḥ
sa eva vajradharo māmakīrūpo bhūtvā nairṛtakoṇe upaviśet
yathārutam
sarvatathā[ga]to vairocanaḥ tadanurāgaṇavajrā locanā
yathārutam
moharatīti

śūnyāḥ sarvadharmā ityālamvanaṃ kāyavākcitta- visaṃvādanam
samastāṅgam
ābhāsatrayasvabhāvāni kāyavākcittāni ślakṣṇadhātostadābhāse praviśya kṣaṇakṣaṇamanubhavaṃ kārayitvā punarniḥsvabhāvā- pādanaṃ kāyavākcittavisaṃvādanam
garbho
skandhadhātvāyatanādīnāṃ piṇḍa- grāhānubhedakrameṇa nirābhāsīkaraṇaṃ kāyavākcittavisaṃvādanam
abhedyatvāt tadvajjram
tadālamvanaṃ samādhiḥ sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittavisaṃvādanaṃ vajro nāma
kolikam
sarvatathāgataprajñādharo'moghasiddhiḥ
tadanurāgaṇavajrā samayatārā tadākārā cittaikāgratāṃ kṛtavāniti yāvat
yathārutam
vajjraratīti
vajro'- moghasiddhiḥ tasmin ratiḥ
vajraratiḥ
neyārthaḥ
vajrajñāne ratiḥ
nītārthaḥ
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane athetyādi
uttare aiśānakoṇe upaviśet
yathārutam
etāścaturdevyaḥ sahacaryaḥ
tābhiḥ saha saṃyogaṃ kurvatāṃ sarvathāgatānāmagra sampanniṣpadyeta iti
agramahiṣī kāmaiśvaryam
agramahiṣī sampacchabdenoktam
yathārutam
athetyādi
krodhaṃ niścārayāmāseti saṃvandhaḥ
vairocana evābhiṣeka- svabhāvo vajraṃ yasya sa virocanavajraḥ tadālamvanasamādhiḥ
vairocanavajro nāma sarvatathāgatamaṇḍalamadhiṣṭhīyate niṣpādyate yena sa tathoktaḥ
yathārutam
yamāntakṛditi
yamo narakastasya bhagavān mahākrodhasamādhināntaṃ karoti nāśayatīti yamāntakṛt
neyārthaḥ
yamo mohāndhakāraḥ avidyā tasya paramārtha- satyenāntaṃ karoti nāśayatīti yamāntakṛt
nītārthaḥ
athetyādi pūrvadvāre niṣaṇṇavān
yathārutam
athetyādi
sarvatathāgatānāmabhisaṃbodhiḥ ratnasaṃbhavaḥ
samukuṭe vajro yasyāparājitasya krodhasya tadālamvanaḥ
samādherabhisaṃbodhivajjo nāma
neyārthaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāmabhisaṃbodhiḥ prabhāsvaraṃ tadeva vajraṃ tamālambya
nītārthaḥ
prajñāntakṛditi
prajñayā kleśaṃ nāśayatīti prajñāntakṛt
neyārthaḥ
vikalpātmikā prajñā
tasyāḥ prabhāsvarapraveśenāntaṃ karoti prajñāntakṛt
nītārthaḥ
athetyādi
vajrasamayairduṣṭasaṃtrāsanaṃ vajrasamayasaṃtrāsanam
athavā vajjrasamayālau- kikaḍākinyaḥ teṣāṃ saṃtrāsanaṃ vajrasamayasaṃtrāsanam
dakṣiṇadvāre niṣaṇṇavān
yathārutam
athetyādi
dharmaratno'mitābhaḥ
sa evākaro gotraṃ yasya hayagrīvasya tannāmakaṃ samādhiṃ samāpadya
neyārthaḥ
dharmaratnaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ sa evākara utpatti- sthānaṃ yasya hayagrīvasya tadālamvanasamādhistaṃ samāpannaḥ
nītārthaḥ
padmānta- kṛditi
udakāliptapadmavat kleśairavalipto kṛtvā tāneva nāśayatīti padmānta- kṛt
neyārthaḥ
padmaṃ yoniḥ
tasmin dvayendriyasamāpatyupāyena paramānandasukhaṃ karoti utpādayatīti padmāntakṛt
nītārthaḥ
l athetyādi
paścime niṣaṇṇavān
yathārutam
sarvatathāgatakāyavākcitto bhagavānamoghasiddhiḥ sa eva vajro yasyāryāmṛtakuṇḍalikrodhasya tadālamvanasamādhi- stathocyate
neyārthaḥ
sarvatathāgata kāyavākcittavajrāṇāṃ viśuddhisthānaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tadālambya
nītārthaḥ
vighnāntakṛditi
vighnānāmantaṃ vināśaṃ karotīti prathamapaṭala ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā vighnāntakṛt
neyārthaḥ
vighnāścatvāro dhātavaḥ
te kṣayavṛddhibhyāṃ śarīra- pīḍanād vighnāḥ
teṣāmupāyajñānodayena vināśaṃ karotīti vighnāntakṛt nītārthaḥ
athetyādi
uttare niṣaṇṇavān
yathārutam
māṇḍaleya santoṣaṇāya samayasattvāmaṇḍalam
samayasattvāḥ sarvavighnavidhvaṃsakatvāt
yathārutam
evaṃ dvāracatuṣṭaye nyaset
pūrvakoṇādārabhya kalādīn nyaset
pūrvapaṭṭikā- mārabhya maitreyādīn nyaset
garbhamaṇḍalād vāhye pūrvakoṇādārabhya rūpavajrādīn nyaset
sarveṣāmāsanādiniyamaḥ sādhanopayikāyāṃ kṛta eva
anācāryakaṃ pravartanaṃ mā bhūditi
yathaiva devatāyogo maṇḍalaṃ cānyatra paripūrṇa bhagavatā pradarśitam
tathaivātrāpi yathākāya maṇḍalaṃ dvātriṃśaddevatā vinyāsaṃ bhavati, tatho- tsargamaṇḍalamapīti
kāyamaṇḍala eva tāvat dvātriṃśaddevatāvinyāsena bhavita- vyamiti
kathametad gamyate ?
bhagavataiva vivṛtaṃ vyākhyātantre sāmājikānāṃ niḥsandigdhārthamavatāryate
śṛṇutvaṃ mahāsattva nānyacittena saṃsmaret
vajrācāryasya kāye'smin kramaḥ kāryājinasthitiḥ
prathamaṃ kāyavajreṇa rūpaskandhena saṃsthitaḥ
anurāgaṇavajreṇa vedanāskandhena saṃsthitaḥ
vāgvajreṇa bhagavān saṃjñāskandhena saṃsthitaḥ
sarvaprajanavajreṇa saṃskāraskandhena saṃsthitaḥ
cittavatreṇa bhagavān vijñānayantrasaṃsthitau
māṃsādi pṛthivīdhāturbhagavatī locanā sthitā
śoṇitādirjaladhāturbhagavatī māmakī sthitā
uṣṇāditejodhāturbhagavatī pāṇḍaravāsinī sthitā
preraṇādimaruddhā tustārā bhagavatī sthitā
netradvayendriyaṃ tasya kṣitigarbhastathāgataḥ
śrotradvayendriyaṃ tasya vajrapāṇistathāgataḥ
ghrāṇasthitendriyaṃ tasya gaganagañjastathāgataḥ
jihvāsthitamindriyaṃ tasya lokeśvarastathāgataḥ
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane manasi sthitamindriyaṃ tasya mañjughoṣastathāgataḥ
caturdhātumayaṃ piṇḍaṃ śarīraṃ tu saṃjñitam
|
sarvanivaraṇaviṣkambhitastathāgatastatra tiṣṭhati
sarvasandhiśarīrasya samantabhadrastathāgataḥ
sarvasnāyuśarīrasya maitreyastathāgataḥ
dakṣiṇe bhujamūle tu yamāntakastathāgataḥ
vāme ca bhujamūle tu aparājitastathāgataḥ
mukhapadme saṃtiṣṭhata hayagrīvastathāgataḥ
guhyapradeśe tiṣṭhati amṛtakuṇḍalistathāgataḥ
dakṣiṇabāhau tiṣṭhati acalo'sau tathāgataḥ
vāmavāhau tiṣṭhati ṭakkirājastathāgataḥ
dakṣiṇajānau ca tiṣṭhati nīladaṇḍastathāgataḥ
vāme jānau ca tiṣṭhati mahāvalatathāgataḥ
svaśīrṣamūni tiṣṭhati cakravartī tathāgataḥ
adhaḥ pāde ca tiṣṭhati sumbharājastathāgataḥ
samastāścaiva kāye'smin sarvatathāgatāḥ sthitāḥ
kāyamaṇḍalamityuktaṃ vajrācāryasya kāyikam
ete sakalasaṃbuddhā samastā buddhakāyikāḥ
praveśayanti nirvāṇaṃ vajrī saṃskṛtadeśanam
śīghraṃ nirvāsi bhagavaṃ paripakvakuśalo bhava
tasmād vajraguroḥ kāye jināḥ sarve pratiṣṭhitāḥ
paścātkālaṃ mahāsattva śṛṇu he amoghadarśaka
sadā ca sarvasattvaiśca guruḥ sevyaḥ prayatnataḥ
tathāmoghadarśanā mahābodhisattvā mahāyaśāḥ
tuṣṭāḥ pramuditā hṛṣṭāḥ praṇamyāgrapatitāḥ
ityāha 'vajramāla[yām
anena nyāyena lekhyamaṇḍale'pi dvātriṃśaddevatā- vinyāsaḥ kartavya iti
nidānamadhikṛtyāpi yo'rthaḥ saṃdarśito bhavet
uktastatraiva tantrārthaḥ śeṣaḥ prāsaṅgiko bhavet
iti
prathamapaṭala ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā gṛhyasamāje sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittarahasye guhyasamāje sarvatathāgatamaṇḍalādhiṣṭhāna- paṭala iti
kāyavākcittarahasya mālokyādhigamya kṛte'smin guhyasamāje samādhimaṇḍalādhiṣṭhānaṃ yasmin sa tathoktaḥ
sarvatathāgatapaṭalaḥ
paṭo yathā daṃśamaśakādyupadravaṃ nivārayati, tathāyaṃ tantrārthaḥ kleśakarmajanmādyupadravanivāraṇāt paṭa iva paṭaḥ
taṃ lāti gṛhṇātīti taddhārako granthasamūhaḥ paṭalaḥ
||
pradīpodyotanaṭīkāyāṃ prathamapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
|| dvitīyaḥ paṭalaḥ || evaṃ prathamapaṭalena sarvaṃ sampādya niṣpannakramadyotanāya dvitīyaṃ bodhi- cittapaṭalamāha
athetyādi
bhagavantaṃ praṇipatyaivamāhuriti saṃvandhaḥ
kāya- vākcittādhipatirmahāvajradharaḥ
tasya pūjāṃ kṛtvā praṇamya ca bhagavantamadhye- ṣayanti
bhāṣasva bodhicittamanuttaramiti saṃvandhaḥ
sarvāvaraṇavimuktatvāt
sāraṃ kāyavākcittatrayāṇāmabhedarūpatvādutkṛṣṭam, kāraṇe kāryopacārāt
kriyādinayāśriteṣvaprakāśyatvāt guhyam
ataevānuttaram
atha bhagavān bodhisattvānevamāheti saṃvandhaḥ
cetasaivetyādinā
yadi bhagavānasmākaṃ pariśuddhadevatāmūrtiniṣpattaye mahābodhicittopadeśaṃ dadyāt, tadā mahāsattvārthaḥ syāditi
evaṃ cetasaiva cetaḥ parivitarkamājñāyetyāha
yathārutam
utpādayantu bhavanta ityādi trivajrābhedya svarūpeṇa bodhicittamutpādayantu
tatkathamityatrāha cittaṃ kāyākāreṇeti kāyavajrākāreṇa
kāyaṃ cittākāreṇeti cittavajrākāreṇa
cittaṃ vākpravyāhāreṇeti
vācaḥ pravyāhāro'bhilapan
tena vāgvatreṇetyarthaḥ
prathetyādi kāyavākcittāni ākāśākāreṇa śūnyatāsvabhāvena
saṃyojya ekīkṛtyeti
neyārthaḥ
utpādayantu manomayadehaṃ niṣpādayantu
kathamityāha cittaṃ kāyākāreṇeti
cittatrayaṃ sūkṣmadhātunā saha saṃbhogikadehākāreṇa yatkāya- niṣpādanaṃ bhavitumeva taditi kāyaṃ cittākāreṇa
na hi cittādanyat kiścidasti
cittaṃ vākyavyāhāreṇeti
yovākpravyāhārastena svarūpeṇa cittamevotpādayantviti
kāyetyādi
evaṃ niṣpannakāyavākcittāni ākāśā- kāreṇeti
ākāśasvabhāvena prabhāsvareṇa saṃyojyaikīkṛtya mahāvajradharamātmānaṃ niṣpādayediti
uktaṃ ca śrīvajramālāmahāyogatantre vaidhātukasamaṃ nāsti sārabhūtaṃ svajīvitam
paśyati svapuraṃ bhāsvatprasaṃkhyāyutadhātukam // dvitīyapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā yogāntaram
vaidhātuke paraṃ nāsti sārabhūtaṃ svajīvitam
jīvitaṃ paśyati svapnaṃ athavā yastridhātukam // iti
traidhātukākāra tattvaṃ ekatvaṃ saṃprayacchati
adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ tatkāryasiddhimavāpnuyāt
aho hītyādi
samantato bhadraḥ mahāvajjradharastasya bodhicittapravartanam
aho āścarye, hiryasmāt
yenānutpādaprayogeṇeti
anutpādaḥ paramārthasatyam
tasmin manomayakāyasya praveśaḥ anutpādaprayogaḥ
tena utpāda iti prabhāsvaraḥ pariśuddhadevatāmūrtigrahaṇalakṣaṇam
vyutthānamayamutpādo yena pragīyate kathyata iti nāsandhyam
ṣaṭtathāgatānāṃ yathāsvaṃ bodhicittagāthābhirimameva samādhiṃ dyotayati
sarvatathāgatānāmabhisaṃbodhiḥ satyadvayādvaidhīkāralakṣaṇaḥ
tasya nayaḥ
tatsākṣātkaraṇopāyaḥ sa eva vajravadabhedyatvād vajraṃ yasya samādheḥ
sa tathoktaḥ
nāsandhyam
sthiravalasarvapadārthānāmabhāve sati bhāvanāyā prabhāva: bhāvyābhāvāt
bhāvanā naiva bhāvaneti
yāpi sato bhāvanā sā na bhāvanā, vināpi bhāvanāyā vidyamānatvāt iti
itthaṃ bhāvābhāvayukto yo bhāvaḥ sa na syāt
tasmād bhāvyo bhāvako bhāvanā nopalabhyate
akṣarārthaḥ
urvyādibhedena sarvadharmān nirākṛtya śūnyāḥ saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛtā dharmā iti yā bhāvanā tasyā abhāvaḥ śūnyatābhiniveśadṛṣṭitvāt
nimittanirākaraṇāyāha bhāvanā naiva bhāvaneti
yāpi hetuphalākārābhiniveśabhāvanā sāpi naiva yuktā hetuphalādvayatvāt
praṇidhānaṃ nirākartumāha
iti bhāva ityādi
ityevaṃ vāhyākāraṃ yatpraṇi- dhānaphalaṃ tadapi na vidyate praṇidhānādyupalambhābhāvāt
tena vāhyavastvākāra- bhāvanā nopalabhyate
na vidyate cittamayatvāt
samastāṅgam
abhāveti
ye svapnādayaḥ teṣāmabhāve yadbhāvyabhūtaṃ cittamātraṃ tadabhāvāviśuddhaṃ sarvāsattvāt
saṃvṛtisatyaṃ nirākartumāha
bhāvanā naiva bhāvaneti
yā saṃvṛtisatyātmikā māyopamadevatārūpabhāvanā sāpi na yuktā
prabhāsvareṇāpi viśuddhatvāt
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane ityevaṃ saṃvṛtisatyabhāvo na bhāvaḥ syāt
tasya satyadvayābhinnatvāt
tena bhāvanā satyadvayādvayākārabhāvanā nopalabhyate
kṛcchrādupalabhyata iti yāvat
garbhī
abhāve paramārthasatye bhāvanāyā abhāvaḥ ādiśuddhatvāt
yā saṃvṛti- satyātmikā bhāvanā sāpi na bhāvyate
satyatvādityanena prakāreṇa satyadvayātmako bhāvaḥ
bhāvyo na syāt
satyadvayādvaidhīkāradarśanamātreṇa mokṣatvāt
tena bhāvako bhāvanā bhāvyamitye [va] yo'bhiniveśa: yuganaddha- kramādhigatānāṃ nopalabhyate na vidyate ityuktavān mahāvajradharaḥ kolikam
atha bhagavān vairocanavajra udājahāreti saṃvandhaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ abhisamantākāreṇa samayaḥ prabhāsvaraṃ tadeva vajraṃ tadālamvanaḥ samādhiḥ
sarvabhāvetyādi
bhājanabhūtaiḥ sarvabhāvairvigataṃ punaḥ skandhadhātvāyanātmakaiḥ grāhyagrāhakaiśca varjitam dharmanairātmyaṃ prabhāsvaram
tena samatādvayībhāvaḥ
tayā dharmanairātmyasamatayā ādyanutpannaṃ svacittaṃ trivajrābhedyavigrahaṃ śūnyatā- svabhāvaṃ cakṣurmātragocaratvādityuktavān vairocanastathāgataḥ
nāsandhyam
sarvatathāgatānāmakṣayaṃ bhūtakoṭi: tadeva vajraṃ tadālamvanaḥ samādhiḥ
sarvatathāgatānāmakṣayavajjo nāma anutpannā ime bhāvā iti
amī bāhyātmikāḥ padārthāḥ anutpannāḥ prakṛtiprabhāsvaratvāt
tasmād dharmāstāvanna santi
na ca dharmateti
teṣāṃ dharmatāpi na vidyate dharmābhāvāt
ataeva idaṃ bodhinayaṃ bodhyupāyaḥ dṛḍhaḥ sāra ākāśavat
nairātmyalakṣaṇaḥ prabhāsvara ityuktavān akṣobhyastathāgataḥ
nāsandhyam
athetyādi tathaiva saṃvandhaḥ
sarvatathāgatanairātmyaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ tadeva vajraṃ tadālamvanaḥ samādhiḥ
sarvatathāgatanairātmyavajjo nāma samādhiḥ
abhāvā iti nirnimittatvāt, sarvadharmā iti sāsravānāsravāḥ
dharmalakṣaṇavarjitā iti dharmā eva tāvat na siddhāḥ kutasteṣāṃ lakṣaṇam ?
vandhyāsutasya ca śyāmatvagauratvādivat
kathaṃ tarhi devatāniṣpattiriti cet ?
dharmanairātmya- saṃbhūtā iti
dharmanairātmyaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ tasmāt svādhiṣṭhānakrameṇa saṃbhūtāḥ dharma- nairātmyasaṃbhūtāḥ
pūrvāyedha sāmarthyādityuktavān
ratnasaṃbhavaḥ
nāsandhyam
I athetyādi jñānācireva pradīpo jñānāciḥpradīpaḥ
kimbhūta[:] prabhāsvaralokaḥ
tadeva vajraṃ tadālamvanaḥ samādhiḥ jñānārciḥ pradīpavajjo nāma
anutpanneṣva- dvitīyapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā I jāteṣu dharmeṣviti saṃskṛtāsaṃskṛteṣu
na bhāva iti bhāvyatvaṃ nāsti
na ca bhāvaneti
bhāvanāpi na vidyate
bhāvyābhāvāt
kathaṃ devatāyogaḥ syādityāha
ākāśapadayogeneti
ākāśaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ padaṃ saṃvṛtisatyaṃ tayoryoge ekībhāve
etenākāśapadayogena
iti bhāvaḥ pragīyate ityanena nyāyena bhāvo mahāvajradhara- mūttiḥ
pragīyate kathyate ityuktavānamitābhastathāgataḥ
nāsandhyam
athetyādi pūrvavat samvandhaḥ
sarvatathāgatābhibhavanavajraḥ samādhiḥ
vibhaktārthaḥ
prakṛtiprabhāsvaradharmā iti
prakṛtiśca prabhāsvaraṃ ca prakṛti- prabhāsvaram
prakṛtiḥ saṃvṛtisatyam
prabhāsvaraṃ paramārthasatyam
satyadvaya- svabhāvā vāhyādhyātmikapadārthāḥ
ataevādiśuddhā nabhaḥ samāḥ
pariśuddhatvānna bodhiḥ
phalarūpā nābhisamayaḥ
tatprāpako hetuḥ
hetuphalayorabhedābhāvādida- miti
hetuphalayorabhedaparijñānameva dṛḍho bodhimārga ityuktavānamoghasiddhiḥ
nāsandhyam
atha khalvityādi
sarvatathāgatānāṃ kāyavākcittaguhyaṃ manomayadehaḥ
tasya dharmatā paramārthasatyena viśuddhiḥ
tasyā akṣaraṃ tadvaradyotako vākyavandhaḥ
taṃ śrutvā āścaryaprāptāḥ
saṃvṛtisatyenādbhutaprāptāḥ paramārthasatyena
aho
buddheti saṃvṛtisatyadvāreṇa stavaḥ
aho dharmeti paramārthasatyadvāreṇa
aho dharmasya deśaneti satyadvayābhinnākāro dyotikāyā dharmadeśanāyāḥ kāraṇatvena stauti
śuddhatattvārthaḥ prabhāsvaraḥ
tena śuddhārthaḥ pariśuddhadehaḥ
evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭa bodhicitta mahāvajradhara tubhyaṃ namaḥ
dharmanairātmyasaṃbhūteti paramārthasatyāt sañjāta ityarthaḥ
buddhabodhiniṣpanna- kramaḥ
tena vineyān niṣpādayatīta buddhabodhiprapūrakaḥ
sarvakalpanātītatvāt nirvikalpaḥ
ataevānālamvanatvāt nirālamva tubhyaṃ namaḥ
I samantetyādi
saṃsāranirvāṇayorbhadratvāt samantabhadraḥ
sarvasampatsvabhāva- tvāt sarvārthaḥ
devatācakraspharaṇāt bodhicittapravartakaḥ
vodhiḥ paramārthasatyam
taccaryate prāpyate yena bodhicaryā saṃvṛtisatyam
mahāvajra satyadvayātmaka tubhyaṃ namaḥ
cittamityādi
cittasvarūpaṃ kīdṛgvidham ?
tāthāgataṃ tathatāṃ gatasta- thāgataḥ tathāgata eva tāthāgataḥ
ataeva śuddhaṃ paramārthasatyam
pariśuddha- guhyasamājapradīpodyotane manomayadeha ityarthaḥ
kāyavākcittavajreṇa iti vajrādyākāreṇa vineyānāṃ dṛśyamānatvāt kāyavākcittavatrī buddhabodhiprāpakatvādapratiṣṭhita nirvāṇadhātau sthāpanāt buddhabodhipraṇetāraḥ tubhyaṃ namaḥ
yathārutavyākhyā
bodhicittapradīpaka: paṭalaḥ bodhicittapaṭalaḥ ||
|| pradīpodyotanaṭīkāyāṃ dvitīyapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
|| tṛtīyaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
dvividhayogināṃ vividhasūkṣmayogabhāvanā kartavyeti tṛtīyapaṭalamupakṣipannāha athetyādi udājahāreti saṃvandhaḥ
kāyavākcittavajrastathāgato mahāvajradharaḥ
sarvatathāgatākṣobhyādayaḥ teṣāṃ sattvārthanimittaṃ daśadigvyāptaye spharaṇāni tānyeva meghāḥ dharmāmbupravarṣaṇāt
sa evaṃ vyūho vaicityasattvāśayabhedena vicitra-- nānārūpadarśanāt
evaṃ vicitrābhedyaṃ samādhimālambya idamiti buddhisthaṃ vajravyūhamiti vajrāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ vyūhaḥ samūho yasmin sa vajravyūhaḥ tannāmakaḥ samādhyādhārapaṭalaḥ samādhipaṭalaḥ tamudājahāra ||
evaṃ paṭalamavatārya devatāniṣpattyaṅgabhūto mantraḥ ādau nirdiśyate
oṃ śūnyatājñānavajjrasvabhāvātmako'hamiti
ākāśadhātumadhyasthamiti dharmodayasthaḥ
bhāvayedityādi vairocanādi- devatācakram
raśmimeghamahāvyūhamiti raśmayaḥ tattatkulaprativaddhadevatāḥ tāsāṃ meghaḥ samūhaḥ sa eva vyūhaḥ yathāsthānasanniviṣṭatvāt
buddhajvālāsamaprabhamiti svādhipatisamānavarṇam
| pañcaraśmisamākīrṇamiti pañcavarṇabhūbhāgayuktam
samantātparimaṇḍala- miti sarvatobhadram
pañcakāmaguṇākīrṇam rūpavajrādisahitam
pañcopahāra- maṇḍitam devādisannidhāpitapañcopahārasahitam
bhāvayitvetyādi
saṃkṣepeṇaiva devatācakraṃ dhyātvā vimvamātmānaṃ madhye cakramadhye vibhāvayedadhipatitvena sthāpayedityarthaḥ
atha vairocanādīnāṃ mahāmudrāṃ mahāvajradharabimbamiva madhye bhāvayediti darśayitumāha
vairocana ityādi kāyavākcittalakṣitāṃ vajradharasya nirmāṇabhūtaṃ vairocanamahāmudrāṃ kāyavajjratve'pi vairocanasya kāyavākcittayorguṇabhāvena vidyamānatvāt kāyavākcittalakṣitām
saṃhārādvayayoga iti saṃhriyate'smin devatācakramiti saṃhāro mahāvajradharaḥ tenādvayayogato'kṣobhyamahāmudrāṃ bhāvayet
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane evaṃ ratnaketvādimahāmudrāṃ maṇḍalamadhya vibhāvayediti
yasya vajradharāgrasya madhye vimbaṃ samālikhet
sa bhavenmaṇḍalapadaṃ tasya iti vacanāt
eṣāṃ varṇādiprabhedaṃ darśayannāha
indranīletyādi
kāyavākcittavajro mahāvajradharaḥ akṣobhyānupraveśena dveṣavatro bhūtvendranīlavarṇaḥ saṃjātaḥ
ataeva vikaṭotkaṭabhīṣaṇaḥ
īṣaddaṃṣṭrākarālaḥ krodhamahārāgamiśrībhāvāt
sphaṭikenduprabhākāro vairocanaḥ taṃ jaṭākalāpamaṇḍitaṃ prabhāmaṇḍalasahitaṃ sarvalakṣaṇālaṅkṛtadehaṃ pradhānahastena cakradharaṃ dhyāyāt
jāmbūnadaprabhākāraṃ kanakavarṇaratnasaṃbhavam sphurattathāgatavyūhaṃ dhyāyāt
navaśūlamityādi
tasya dveṣavajrasya pāṇī vibhāvayet
makaratetyādinā amoghasiddhisvarūpamāha
haste rattamityādinā ratna- saṃbhavasya cihnaṃ kathayati
padmarāgetyādinā amitābhacihnamāha
pañcaraśmītyādi sitādikiraṇopetaṃ viśvaṃ sakalaṃ jagadarthasādhanarūpatvāt
amoghavajriṇaṃ khaḍgahastaṃ dhyāyāt ||
eṣāmutpādanāyāṅgabhūtamādiyogaṃ darśayitumāha athetyādi
dharmadhātureva svabhāvo yasya taṃ samādhi samāpadya kāyavākcittānyadhiṣṭhīyante niṣpādyante yena sa kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaḥ tanmantramuktavān mahāvajradharaḥ
oṃ dharmadhātu- svabhāvātmako'hamiti
anenātmānaṃ candramaṇḍalākāramadhiṣṭhāya tadupari tyakṣarādinā devatāvigrahaṃ niṣpādya vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa sūkṣmayogaṃ vibhāvayediti
neyārthaḥ
utpattikramasamādhiḥ
athetyādi
sarvatathāgatāḥ paśvaraśmayaḥ teṣāṃ spharaṇamūrdhvadvāreṇa pravṛttiḥ meghāccandraṃ ekaikasmin maṇḍale caturmaṇḍalapravṛtte vyūhaḥ sitādivarṇenābhinnatvāt
taṃ samādhimālambya vajraṃ tyakṣaraṃ tasya vyūho na vṛndaṃ ālikālīnāṃnapuṃsakādisvarūpāṇāṃ tyakṣare niviṣṭatvāt
samādhāya taṃ aharniśaṃ praveśasthitivyutthānena jāpyata iti samādhiḥ
īdṛgvidhaḥ samādhiḥ yasmin sa tathoktaḥ
taṃ paṭalamāha
oṃ śūnyatetyādimantreṇa punarucyate
tṛtīyapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā omityādyekasaṃyuktaṃ śūnyateti dvitīyakam
tṛtīyajñānamityevaṃ caturthaṃ vajrasaṃjñakam ||
pañcamaṃ tu svabhāvākhyaṃ ṣaṣṭho'hamiti cocyate
evaṃ tāthāgataṃ ṣaṭkaṃ kathitamuttame jane
oṃ śūnyateti yugmādi jñānavatraṃ dvitīyakam
tatsvabhāvātmako'haṃ syāt tritattvaṃ cātra kalpitam ||
tritattvamekaṃ bhavet samyak
vyaktaṃ vyaktasaṃjñakamityārṣam
ākāśadhātumadhyasthaṃ buddhamaṇḍalaṃ trisaṃyogadehaṃ bhāvayet dhyāyāt
kiṃbhūtam ?
raśmimeghamahāvyūhamiti
ābhāsattrayasamuhamālokapuñjaṃ buddhajvālā- samaprabhamiti indrāyudhavat sitādipañcaprabhālaṅkṛtam
pañcaraśmisamākīrṇamiti
sūkṣmadhātuparighaṭitam
samantātparimaṇḍalamiti
sarvabhāvasvabhāvam
pañcakāmaguṇākīrṇamiti udvāhādipaśñcavaraśmayaḥ tairupetam
pañcopahāramaṇḍita- miti upaharanti viṣayān ityupahārāḥ pañcendriyāṇi tairbhūṣitaṃ sakalākṣakatvāt
īdṛgvidhaṃ buddhamaṇḍalaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ praveśayet
bhāvayitveti
evaṃvidhamātmānaṃ prabhāsvaragataṃ dhyātvā samāseneti jhaṭityākāreṇa vimbaṃ mahāvajradharasvarupaṃ madhya iti saṃsāranirvāṇayormadhye sadasatpakṣarahitamātmānaṃ vibhāvayet vijānī- yādityarthaḥ
anenaiva krameṇa vairocanādikaṃ yathāśayabhedena svātmānaṃ niṣpādya svārtha kuryāditi ata āha
vairocanamahāmudrāṃ kāyavākcittalakṣitām ityādi
imameva mahāvajradharayogaṃ dṛḍhīkartumāha
atha bhagavānityādi
dharma- dhātuprabhāsvaraḥ taṃ samādhimālamvya kāyavākcittānyadhiṣṭhīyante niṣpādyante yena sa kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaṃ svaṃ mantramuktavān mahāvajragharaḥ ||
oṃ dharmadhātu- svabhāvātmako'ham ||
asyārtho vyākhyātantreṇocyate omiti prathamaṃ jñeyaṃ dvitīyo dharmasaṃjñitaḥ
tṛtīyo dhātviti proktaḥ svabhāvākhyaścaturthakaḥ ||
ātmakaḥ pañcamo jñeyaḥ syādahaṃ ṣaṣṭhayogataḥ | uccāryedaṃ tato mantraṃ bhāvayed bhāvanātmakaḥ ||
iti |
nītārthaḥ |
|
|
idānīṃ svacittadṛḍhīkaraṇārtha trividhasūkṣmayogabhāvanāmāha |
pañcavarṇa- |ityādi |
pañcakulaprabhavaṃ mahāratnaṃ vajrādicihnaṃ ratnamiva mahārghatvāt tat sarṣapaphalapramāṇaṃ nāsikāgre bhāvayet svadevatāyogena |
sadā aharniśam ||
sthiramityādi |
yadā darśananimittaṃ prakāśitaṃ copajāyate tadā vajrādikaṃ bhāvanāyā niścalaṃ viditvā bhāvanāvaśitvotpādanāya sphārayet |
asthiraṃ naiva sphārayediti |
bhāvyamānaṃ na sphārayet |
sphārayedityādinā vajrādau spharaṇena sthirīkṛte mahāmudrārūpamapi sphārayedityāha |
sphārayetpravarairityādi |
pravarairmedhaiḥ buddhasaṃdohairbuddhajvālāsamaprabhaiḥ sitādivarṇopetaiḥ ||
cakravajramahāmeghairiti |
cakravajjraratnameghopalakṣitaiḥ| padmakośavarāyudhairiti|
padmakhaṅgadharairityarthaḥ|
bodhisatvamahāmeghairityupalakṣaṇatvāt |
pañcakulasaṃvandhi- nībhirdevatābhirapi spharaṇātmakaḥ sādhakaḥ gaganakuharaṃ sphārayet chādayediti samastāṅgam |
mahāratnaṃ saṃvṛtirbodhicittaṃ sarṣapaphalamātraṃ nāsikāgre svakuliśa- mukhe pañcavarṇaṃ pañcatathāgatātmakaṃ kṛtvā sadā bhāvayet |
sthiraṃ tu sphārayedratna- miti |
yathoktavidhinā vajrapadmasaṃskārapūrvakaṃ svacittaratnaṃ sthirīkṛtya yogataḥ samāpattitaḥ sphārayet niścārayet |
sphārayet |
pravarairmedhairityādinā bodhicittaniryātaputrapautrādyutsargamaṇḍale kathayati |
garbhī |
parapadaṃ sarvatathāgatagatātmakam |
mahāratnamanāhatākhyam, buddharatnotpādanāt sarṣapasthalamātnakam |
sarṣapaphalapramāṇaṃ vindurūpatvāt |
nāsikāgra iti |
hṛtpadmakaṇikāyāṃ prayatnena tātparyeṇa praveśasthitivyutthānasaṃhārayogena sadāharniśaṃ bhāvayet |
sthiraṃ śāśvatam |
kiṃ tat ?
anāhatam |
tasmāt ratnapañcavarṇa- |raśmivṛndaṃ sphārayet praveśasthitivyutthānaiḥ pravartayet |
asthiramityādinānāhata- tattvādhigama evemaṃ yogaṃ kuryāditi darśayati |
idānīṃ sphāraṇavidhāna pradarśayitumāha |
buddhajvāletyādi |
buddhajvālāṃ pañcaraśmayaḥ |
tatsamaprabhaiḥ maṇḍalacatuṣṭayairdakṣiṇādibhedena sphārayet tathāgatameghairgaganakuharaṃ pūrayediti |
kolikam |
vairocanādīnāmutpattikramaṃ darśayannāha ākāśadhātumadhyasthaṃ buddhamaṇḍalamālikhet |
ityādi |
tathatālamvanapūrvakaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā tadupari cakraṃ vibhāvya tṛtīyapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā tatparāvṛttyā yathāvad vairocanaṃ bhāvayet |
evaṃ padmaparāvṛttyā padmamaṇḍalasaṃkāśaṃ padmarāgaprabhamamitābhaṃ padmakule yogī bhāvayet |
ratnatatparaḥ ratnakulayogī ratnasaṃbhavaṃ ratnamaṇḍalasaṃkāśaṃ pītavarṇa dhyāyāt |
yogamaṇḍalaṃ viśvavajraṃ tatsaṃbhūtamamoghasiddhi dhyāyāt |
ākāśasannidhā- viti |
ākāśena sannidhiḥ sādṛśyaṃ tasminnākāśasādṛśye sthitam |
gagana- śyāmamityarthaḥ |
eta ityādi |
kāyavākcittasādhanairupalakṣitaṃ yatkāyamaṇḍalaṃ kiṃ tat ?
akṣobhyaḥ sarvavajjrasaṃbhūtaḥ |
ete vai pravarā buddhāḥ |
sarvajñākāra- lābhina iti yogaḥ |
sarvajñatvaṃ sattvairlambhayituṃ grāhayituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te |
|
vajravyūha iti |
vajrāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ vyūhaḥ saṃghāto yatra vajravyūhapaṭala iti |
neyārthaḥ |
ākāśadhātuprabhāsvaraṃ tadvayavasthitaṃ candramaṇḍalamanāhataṃ ālikhet vijānīyāt |
svacchamaṇḍalamadhyasthamanāhatasaṃbhūtaṃ cakramaṇḍalaṃ vāruṇa- maṇḍalamakṣobhyasvabhāvaṃ śuklavarṇaṃ mandapracāratvāt stabdhaṃ bhāvayet pravartamānaṃ vijānīyāt |
padmamaṇḍalasaṃkāśaṃ raktavarṇamagnimaṇḍalaṃ padmabhāvanairamitābha- svabhāvairdakṣiṇena pravartamānaṃ vijānīyāt |
ratnamaṇḍalasaṃkāśaṃ pītavarṇaṃ māhendra- maṇḍalaṃ ratnatatparaḥ ratnasaṃbhavajāpī tvadbhutaḥ pravartamānaṃ vijānīyāt |
yoga- maṇḍalaṃ vāyavyamaṇḍalaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇamamoghasiddhisvabhāvaṃ vāmapracāraṃ vijānīyāt |
vyākhyātantre vispaṣṭaṃ darśitaḥ |
tadihāvatāryate |
anādinidhanaṃ yogaṃ susūkṣmamiti kalpayet |
avyaktavyaktasaṃyogaṃ niḥsvabhāvasvabhāvajam ||
prakṛtisiddhimādyaikamekānekavivarjitam |
pratītyotpādadharmākhye sarvabhāvaikasaṃbhavam ||
ātmānamīdṛśaṃ kṛtvā saṃlikheddharmamaṇḍalam |
raktakṛṣṇaṃ tathā pītaṃ sitaṃ caiva samāsataḥ ||
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane turyatukasaṃyogāt saṃlikhet pāṇḍarādikam |
tāsāṃ madhye svadaivatyaṃ turyayogaparamaṃ param ||
ūrdhva ṃ tiryaksamādhastāt pāṇḍarādi yathāyatham |
agnivāyavyamāhendravāruṇe pratimaṇḍale ||

caturthaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
devatālamvanapūrvakaṃ sūkṣmayogabhāvanāsādhitaviśeṣāṇāṃ śiṣyāṇāṃ rajo- maṇḍalābhiṣekāya guroradhyeṣaṇavidhimāha athetyādi adhyepitavanta iti yāvat |
yathārutam |
sarvata [[]thāgatamiti akṣobhyadvāreṇa stutvā ārādhayati |
śāntamiti |
vairocanasvabhāvena |
sarvatāthāgatālayamiti |
ratnaketusvabhāvena |
sarvadharma ityamitābhasvabhāvena |
agranairātmyamityamoghasiddhisvabhāvena ||
punaḥ kulatrayasvabhāvena saṃstauti sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpūrṇamityādi |
sarvalakṣaṇa- saṃpūrṇa sarvālakṣaṇarvājataṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhāṣeti saṃvandhaḥ |
yathoktaṃ maṇḍalaṃ lakṣaṇopetaṃ nyūnātiriktadoṣarahitaṃ samantabhadrakāyāgraṃ pravaraṃ maṇḍalaṃ prakāśayetyarthaḥ |
śāntadharmāgrasaṃbhūtaṃ paramārthasatyanirjātajñānamābhāsatrayaṃ taccaryā rāgādayaḥ tāḥ prakṛtī: abhisaṃbodhibhāvanā yā viśodhayatīti jñānacaryāviśodhakaḥ, prāptasarvaiśvaryatvāt |
samantabhadraḥ vāgīśvaryaprāptatvāt vācāgraḥ |
sarvasattvamahācittastvaṃ sarvasattvacittavajratvāt|
śuddhaḥ rāgādimalāpagamāt |
vicāryamāṇe rāgādīnāmanupalambhāt |
prakṛtinirmalaḥ |
ataeva samantabhadraḥ svacittapratiṣedhāt |
cittāgraḥ sarvasattvanirvāṇapuraprāpaṇāt |
bhāṣa vajraṃ maṇḍalavidhi prakāśaya |
nāsandhyam |
vajradharavacanāvatārāyāha saṃgītikāraḥ athetyādi |
vajramadvayajñānaṃ du|
tāraṇād vajradharaḥ|
vineyānuguṇaśāsanāt śāstā |
nilokaḥ bhūrbhuvaḥsvaḥ- svabhāvatvāt tridhātukaḥ, kāmadhātvādisvabhāvena sthitatvāt |
trilokavara- vajrāḥ pratyekabuddhādayaḥ teṣāmagraḥ prabhuḥ, trilokāgrā brahmādayastānanuśāsti |
evaṃbhūto mahāvajradharaḥ ||
ramyaṃ manojñaṃ sarvatāthāgatāgatālayaṃ tanniketabhūtam |
sarvatathāgatāgatatvāt sarvatāthāgataṃ cittamaṇḍalamiti kāyavāṅmaṇḍalābhyāṃ vyāvṛttiḥ |
maṇḍa: māraḥ taṃ lāti gṛhṇātīti maṇḍalaṃ tasyākāro yasmin tanmaṇḍalākṛtimiti |
evaṃbhūtaṃ cittamaṇḍalaṃ bhāṣate |
yathārutam |
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane mahāvajradharavacanena maṇḍalaṃ darśayannāha |
athāta ityādi |
atheti vākyo- pādāne ataḥśabdo hetvarthe |
śipyavacanamaṅgīkṛtya, yasmādayaṃ śraddhādiguṇasampannaḥ ato'sya maṇḍalaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmītyabhiprāyaḥ |
cittapradhānaṃ maṇḍalaṃ cittamaṇḍalaṃ vajjrakulaprādhānyāduttamam |
ata evedaṃ prathamamuktam |
cittavajño'kṣobhyaḥ |
tadvat mārādibhirakampyatvā [t] cittavajrapratīkāśaṃ kāyavākcittānāṃ maṇḍa: sāraṃ guhyaṃ talatīti kāyavākcittamaṇḍalaṃ tatsamyakpravakṣyāmīti yogaḥ |
navenetyādinā tatsūtreṇa vidhi darśayati
navena nānyopayuktena suniyuktena samyak valitena supramāṇena maṇḍaladviguṇena cāruṇā paśñcaraṅgikena
evaṃvidhena sūtreṇa sūtrayet vibhajet
prājñaḥ praduṣṭakarmācāryaḥ
maṇḍalasūcanāt sūtram

tasyetyādi
caturasrādiguṇopetasya tasya vāhyamaṇḍalasyābhyantarataścakraṃ garbhamaṇḍalaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ vṛttākāramālikhet
mudrānyāsamityādividhiḥ bhūsaṃśo dhanādiko vi[dhānaṃ] vidhiḥ
tatra dṛṣṭaṃ vidhidṛṣṭaṃ tena karmaṇā
ādhāramaṇḍalaṃ niṣpādya tatastadanantaramācāryo vajrādimudrānyāsaṃ kuryāt

akṣobhyādidevatācakraṃ mahāmudrā
sarvasattvasantānādyapekṣayā āsāṃ triprakārāṇāmapi mudrāṇāṃ nyāsaṃ kuryāt
tatropalakṣaṇārthaṃ cihnamudrāṃ darśayitumāha tasya madhya ityādi
bhayasyāpi bhayapradamiti
bhayasyāpi bhayajanakaṃ vajraṃ dveṣatattvatvāt
vajrāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ jvālāḥ pañcaraśmayaḥ tairvibhūṣitaṃ cakraṃ visphuliṅga- gahanākulaṃ raśmicchaṭākīrṇam
ratnamayatvaṃ padmasya pratyavekṣaṇajñānātmakatvena tadamitābhasya padmarāga- sadṛśadīptiṃ paścimadigbhāge likhet
uttareṇetyādi
raśma caturthapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā yedityata āha
samādhiṣya ca ityādi
tāṃ pūrvoktāṃ yoṣitaṃ kuśalo mantrī
guṇamekhalāmiti
mekhaleva mekhalā
guṇaiḥ kṛtā mekhalā mālā yasyāḥ sā guṇamekhalā tāṃ sākṣāt māmakīmiva samādhiṣya prākṛtabhāvamapanīya pañcakulakalāpinīṃ kṛtvā ramayet
tataḥ saumyaṃ śukraṃ sṛjet
vajramārgā vahiṣkuryāt
kathaṃ buddhapadaṃ buddhaprabhavam
ākāśadhātvalaṅkṛtaṃ gaganābhogālaṅkāra- bhūtaṃ sṛjet
bodhicittaparamāṇughaṭitagaganakuharavyāpitathāgatavyūhaṃ bhāvayedityuktaṃ bhavati
na kevalaṃ bodhicittamātreṇaiva pūjāvidhānaṃ api tu kāyaniṣyandairapīti darśayannāha
viṇmūtretyādi
pañcāmṛtaṃ devatānāṃ nivedayet
carubhāvena maṇḍala- cakrasya pratipādayet
tenaivātmānaṃ prīṇayediti
evaṃ kṛte ko guṇa ityāha
evamityādi
buddhā rūpaskandhādayaḥ
bodhisattvāścakṣurādyāyatanāni
ete buddha- vodhisattvāstuṣyanti tuṣṭiṃ gacchanti
mahāyaśāḥ paṭupracārāḥ lokanayanānanda- karā bhavanti
neyārthaḥ
ārṣavyākhyānamucyate
athātaḥ kathayiṣyāmi maṇḍalaṃ cittamuttamam
vajrajñānapratīkāśaṃ kāyavākcittamaṇḍalam
navātmakamidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ suniyuktaṃ tathoditam
dvividhāgamasaṅketaṃ supramāṇaṃ taducyate
saṃvṛtiḥ paramārthaṃ yadadvayaṃ śobhanaṃ matam
prajñāsūtramidaṃ tasmāt tadupāyānuvarṇitam
prajñopāyaikasūtreṇa dharmātsaṃbhogamuttamam
sūtritaṃ maṇḍalaṃ caitaṃ pratītyā dvādaśāṅgataḥ
satyāryaṃ caturastraṃ syāt brahmavihārakoṇakam
dvārasaṃgrahavastvākhyaṃ pratyekaṃ turyasaṃjñakam
dharmatattvārthasaṃketaṃ cakrābhyantaramaṇḍalam
abhedyajñānacihnaṃ tad vajñendranīlamudrakam
pañcākāraprabhedākhyaṃ śūlaṃ saṃsāranāśakam
prāksthitādarśamātraṃ tu cakraṃ jvālāvibhūṣitam
samantasamatājvālaṃ ratnaṃ yāmyekasaṃjñitam
pratyavekṣaṇapadmākhyaṃ padmarāgā vipaścimam
|
uttarottaracittaṃ yajjagadvikalpadālanam
kṛtyānuṣṭhānakhaṅgaṃ syād raśmijvālāsamaprabham
āgneyyāṃ nendraṃ maitrīṃ tu nīlameghasamaprabham
nairṛtyo karuṇākhyā syānmāmakīkulavajrakam
vāyavyāṃ padmasaṃjñā tu muditā susthitānanā
aiśānyutpalamābhāti sopekṣā nīlasaṃjñitā
dānapūrvaṃ tathā dvāre mudgaraṃ kāntisuprabham
tathaiva priyavādyākhyaṃ daṇḍaṃ vajrācisuprabham
arthacaryā tu padmākhyaṃ paścime hayakandhare
vajraṃ samānacaryārthaṃ vajrakuṇḍali vajrinaḥ
taditthaṃ kathitaṃ samyak mudrāvinyāsalakṣaṇam
yathā saṃsthānataḥ pūjā bhavet trividhabhāvanaiḥ
mahattvapūrvakaṃ hyetanmudrāmaṇḍalamucyate
kṣaṇādikālabhedena saṃjñā syāt ṣoḍaśābdikā
anutpādoṣitāśāntayoṣitā iti niścitā
śāntadharmānapetākhyā kānti suprabhoditām
pratītyavāsanā gandhaṃ puṣpaṃ jñānavikāsanam
niḥsvabhāvakule jñeyaṃ sarvajñajñānamavyayam
kāmayedīdṛśīṃ prājño yoṣitaṃ dharmadhātukam
na cādhyātmaṃ na vāhyo'ntaṃ nobhaye'nyatra saṃsthitāḥ
asthānasthitiyogā syādato māmakī matā
tatsvabhāvaikayogastu adhiṣṭhānaṃ taducyate
ākāśaikatvasaṃvāsasaumyaṃ buddhapadaṃ bhavet
|
parikalpitāḥ
ataḥ saṃharaṇād vidyād viṣayāḥ sūtraṃ jñānendriyaṃ śastaṃ śukraṃ viśuddhidharmatā
raktaṃ sarvajñatājñānaṃ ye dharmāḥ parikalpitāḥ
ta eva devatā: khyātā niḥsvabhāvo nivedanam
evaṃ tuṣyanti te buddhā jinaurasā viśeṣataḥ
laukikī kalpanā yeṣāṃ teṣāmeva yathoditam
iti
caturthapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā evaṃ lekhyamaṇḍalādivyapadeśena pariśuddhadevatārūpameva pratīpādayatīti
nītārthaḥ
sarvatathāgatetyādi
cittavajrādhipatimaṇḍalaścittamaṇḍalamiti
maṇḍaṃ sāraṃ saṃvṛtisatyam
taṃ lāti gṛhṇātīti maṇḍalam
neyārthaḥ
maṇḍaṃ sāraṃ paramārthasatyam
taṃ lāti gṛhṇātīti maṇḍalam
yathoktaṃ bhagavatā vyākhyātantre
sarvāṅgaṃ bhāvanātītaṃ kalpanākalpavarjitam
mātrāvindusamātītaṃ etanmaṇḍalamuttamam
iti
||
pradīpodyotanaṭīkāyāṃ caturthapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
nītārthaḥ
||
pañcamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
adhigatakramadvayaḥ prāptābhiṣekaścāpi caryā vinā na sidhyatīti darśayannāha athetyādi
sarvatathāgatānāṃ kāyavākcittātmako rājā trailokyādhipatiḥ hariharādilaukikadevatānāṃ śrāvakādilokottarāṇāṃ ca ṛddhyādibhirabhibhava- nādagraḥ
bhurbhuḥsvararthaśāsakatvād bhuvaneśvaraḥ
svaparayūthyānāṃ caryā sarvacaryā
arthyata ityarthaḥ
agradharmaścāsāvarthaścāgradharmārthaḥ
mahāvajradharaḥ sarvacaryābhiḥ
prāpto hyagradharmārthaḥ|
sa tathoktaḥ sarvairapi mumukṣubhiḥ|
sarvajñatvasya prādhānyāt|
prapañcatā niṣprapañcatā atyantaniṣprapañcateti trividhā caryā|
caratyanayeti caryā|
tasyā lakṣaṇaṃ svarūpaṃ bhāṣate|
yathārutam|
nirvikalpetyādi|
nirvikalpaḥ paramārthasatyaṃ gāmbhīryalakṣaṇam|
artha saṃvṛtisatyamaudāryalakṣaṇam|
etad dvayamadvayākāreṇa samyak bhāvayanti ye nirvikalpārthasaṃbhūtāste yoginaḥ|
rāgādyākulā api pravarāmutkṛṣṭāṃ siddhi mahāmudrāsiddhi sādhayanti niṣpādayanti|
agrayānaṃ vajrayānaṃ sarvayānādhaḥkaraṇāt|
anuttaram atyutkṛṣṭaṃ tamāśrityeti vākyaśeṣaḥ|
nāsandhyam|
svabhāvaparijñānameva buddhatvakāraṇam, na jātyādikamiti darśayannāha|
caṇḍāletyādi|
jātyā jugupsitatvaṃ darśayati|
veṇukāra iti karmaṇā nāma ādiśabdādanyeṣāmapi karmaṇām, māraṇārthāḥ kūṭayantraśastravāgurādayaḥ|
tairarthaṃ prayojanaṃ cintayantīti māraṇārthānucintakāḥ|
sidhyantyagrayāne'smin mahāyāne hyanuttare|
agrā ratnapudgalāḥ|
te yāntyanenetyagrayānam|
anuttaramahāyānaṃ vajrayānam|
tasmin sidhyanti|
ānantaryetyādi|
mātṛpitṛbhikṣuvadhabuddhapratimābhedasaddharmapratikṣepakākhyāni pañca karmāṇi maraṇānantaraṃ narakayātanādānantaryāṇi atropacārādānantaryaśabdaḥ tatkāriṣu vartate|
prabhṛtiśabdenopānantaryaparigrahaḥ|
dūṣaṇaṃ mātu arhantyā niyatisthasya māraṇam|
bodhisattvasya śaikṣasya sadya āpadvārihārikā|
paścamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ānantaryasabhāgāni pañcamaṃ stūpabhedanam|
upānantaryānyetāni mahāpāpakṛtā iti||
catasṛbhirmūlāpattibhiḥ cchinnatālavad buddhaśāsanād bhraṣṭā narakaparāyaṇāḥ, te'pyatra guruprasādena niṣpannakramaṃ prāpya sidhyanti|
buddhatvaṃ prāpnuvanti|
ki punaścaṇḍālādaya iti taddoṣān laghayati|
evaṃ pātrāpāttranirapekṣārthakāraṇāt yānamāhātmyaṃ prakaṭitam|
evaṃ sakala- pāpiṣṭhajanaviśodhakaṃ vajrayānaṃ gurudrohī kadācit sādhayatīti kasyacidāśaṅkā syāt|
atastasmin nirāsāyāha|
ācāryanindakaparetyādi|
pūrvamācāryaṃ paryupāsya śrutacintānukrameṇa tattvamadhigamyedānīṃ kimaneneti yo gurumavamanyate sa ācāryanindakaḥ|
tena sahāmiṣādisaṃbhogaiḥ saṃmodanaṃ prīṇanamudarādipuraṇaṃ vā kurvanti, ye'nye ācāryanindakāḥ parāstāvanna sidhyanti|
kiṃ punarācārya- nindakaḥ ?
naiveti kadācidapi naiva sidhyatītyavadhārayati|
sarvapāpakāriṇaṃ sādhayatīti asādhyasādhanāt|
samāja eva sādhanaṃ tasmin sādhane na sidhyanti|
kiṃ punaranyatreti|
yathoktaṃ śrīmāyājālatantre atvācāryanindāṃ vai svapne'pi darśanaṃ bhavet|
ye vācāryanindakāste grastā vighnavināyakaiḥ||
krūrakarmā durātmāno varjanīyāḥ sadā bhuvi||
iti|
yathārutam||
tantrarājaṃ viśeṣyānācāryakamidaṃ mā bhūditi lokaviruddhāpalāpena caryā- viśeṣaṃ darśayannāha|
prāṇātipātina ityādi|
asyārtho vyākhyātantrādavatāryate skandhādinisvabhāvatvaṃ ye kurvanti śamathoditam|
prāṇātipātinaste syuragrayānavyavasthitāḥ||
adhyātmyaṃ ca tathā vāhyaṃ pratyayodbhavadeśakāḥ|
mṛṣāvādaratāste syurasthānasthitiyoginaḥ||
samyaksaṃbuddhajñānaṃ yad dravyaṃ tadabhidhīyate|
tadāharaṇaśīlā ye paradravyaratā matāḥ||
tathataikasvabhāvā ye tathatānityayoginaḥ|
te te kāmaratāḥ sattvāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ syuragrasādhane||
trasyate (?) śūnyatāmohānmūtramindriyamucyate|
guhya samājapradīpodyotane viṣayāviti vikhyātā bhoktāraḥ śamanāstayoḥ|
mahāyānāgradharme'smin bhavyāste khalu sādhane||
sandhyābhāṣeyam||
mātṛbhaginīmityādi|
māteti mātṛvadanukūlā|
evaṃ bhaginīduhityaḥ|
āsāmanyatamāṃ svakulāṅganāmādāya sādhanoktakrameṇa pañcakulakalāpinīṃ kṛtvā yāṃ sādhakaḥ kāmayet sa vipulāmutkṛṣṭāṃ siddhiṃ gacchet|
mahāyānākhyaḥ agradharmaḥ yeṣāṃ te mahāyānāgradharmāṇaḥ|
teṣāṃ madhye vipulāṃ siddhi gacchet|
ananukūla- mudrāsamāyogāt avaśyaṃ cittavikṣepaḥ saṃjāyate|
ānukūlyaṃ punarjanmāntare'pi sauhārdād bhavati|
mantratantrādiṣu śikṣitā sādhanānukūlā mudrā yadi nāsādyeta tadā kathamityāha|
buddhasyetyādi|
buddhasya mātaraṃ prajñāpāramitāṃ svahṛdi sthitāṃ niścārya tayā saha samāpattiṃ kuryāt|
hṛdayasthā mahādevī yogino yogavāhinī|
jananī sarvabuddhānāṃ vajradhāteśvarī smṛtā||
iti vacanāt|
kāmayanniti|
mātṛbhaginīduhitṛvaddhitaiṣiṇībhīḥ samayajñābhiḥ|
jñānamudrayā ca paramānandasukhamanubhavan na ca lipyata iti rāgādikleśairnaiva spṛśyate|
na kevalaṃ rāgādidoṣairna lipyate, api tu sarvasampattimāsādyata ityāha|
sidhyata ityādi|
tasya sādhakasya dhīḥ suśikṣitā mudrā yasya tasya dhīmataḥ nirvikalpasya svabhāvajñasya|
buddhatvaṃ mahāvajradharatvam sidhyate svayameva niṣpadyate|
neyārthaḥ||
punarārṣavyākhyānamucyate|
prajñāpāramitāṃ prajñāṃ dharmakāyaikamātṛkām|
kāmayennisvabhāvākhyāṃ tathatādvayayogataḥ||
saṃbhogatulyatāṃ jātāṃ tāmeva bhaginīṃ matām|
kāmayenmantramūrtyā tu svādhidaivatayogavān|
sādhako bhāvayet tāṃ tu putrīṃ nirmāṇarūpiṇīm|
kāmayannīdṛśīṃ yogī bhaginīmātṛputrikām|
sa siddhiṃ vipulāṃ gacchenmahāyānāgradharmasu||
nītārthaḥ||
athetyādinā āścaryaprāptāḥ caṇḍālādīnāṃ buddhatvasiddhiśravaṇena, adbhutaprāptāḥ ānantaryādipāpakāriṇāṃ mahāsukhapadaprāptiśravaṇena, evamāścaryā- paścamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā dbhṛtopetāḥ sarvanivaraṇaviṣkambhiprabhṛtayaḥ puṇḍarīkādimṛdvindriyāḥ prāṇāti- pātina ityādi vajrapadānāṃ sandhyābhāṣitānāṃ yathārutamarthamabhiniviśamānāḥ kimiyaṃ bhagavānityādi codayanti|
kiṃ bhāṣata iti yogaḥ|
bhagavān saṃbhāradvayopetaḥ sarvatathāgata āryabhūtaḥ|
anena padadvaye durvacanasyāsthānatā darśitā|
sarvatathāgatā niṣpannā aniṣpannāḥ|
teṣāṃ pariṣat samūhaḥ tanmadhye|
tattrāpi durbhāṣitaṃ vaktuṃ naiva yujyate|
durbhāṣitasya vacanaṃ durbhāṣitavacanam|
prāṇātipātina ityādi anāryakarmapravartanaṃ lakṣaṇam|
udāhṛyata ityudāhāraḥ durbhāṣitavacanaṃ ca tadudāhāraśca durbhāṣitodāhāraḥ|
taṃ kimarthaṃ kuruta iti|
athetyādi|
vodhisattvānāṃ vacanasamanantaraṃ sarvatathāgatāḥ akṣobhyādayaḥ tānevamāhuḥ alamityādi|
alaṃ paryāptau|
yuṣmadabhiprāyo jñātaḥ|
kiṃ bahunetyabhiprāyaḥ|
sandhyāyoktaṃ bhagavadvacanam yathārutaṃ gṛhītvā durbhāṣitamiti manyamānā evaṃ vadiṣyatha|
kasmāt prāṇā- tipātina ityādi vajrapadoktāyā dharmatāyā buddhānām atyantaboddhṛṇāṃ ratna- pudgalānāṃ sārajñānināṃ sāro'pratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaṃ tasmin jñānamavabodho yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ|
sāradharmaṃ paramārthasatyam|
arthaḥ saṃvṛtisatyam|
tābhyāmabhinnā- kāreṇa saṃbhūtāḥ saṃprāptāḥ sāradharmārthasaṃbhūtāḥ|
evaṃ viśiṣṭarūpā dharmatā|
ratnapudgalānāmeva deyā nānyeṣām|
ataeva sandhyābhāṣayā deśiteti tathā- gatānāmabhiprāyaḥ eṣa bodhicarīpadamiti eṣa iyaṃ bodhicarī bodhicaryāpadaṃ vajradharatvam|
anayā dhruvaṃ vajradharatvamavaśyamavāpyata iti caryāpadamityuktam|
yathārutam||
atha khalvanabhilāpyetyādi vibhaktārtham
pūrvaṃ prāṇātipātādīnāmiti - kartavyatayā vidhānena bhītā bhayamāpannāḥ kaṭukavipākatvāt
akaraṇena ca bhagavadājñālaṅghanena ca bhayāt saṃtrastāḥ kiṃkartavyavimūḍhatayā saṃbhrāntaḥ santaḥ
paścāt prāṇātipātina ityādi vajrapadaṃ tathāgatasakāśāt niścayārthaṃ śrutvā harṣaparavaśatayā mūrchitā abhūvan
anadhigatapurvadharmatādhigamena, tadekāgra- manaskatvāt niruddhā śeṣendriyavṛttayaḥ saṃjātā ityabhiprāyaḥ
neyārthaḥ
punarārṣavyākhyānamucyate
asthānakamidaṃ guhyaṃ śāntaikaṃ sukhamuttamam
sandhyāya kathitaṃ cedaṃ samyaksaṃbodhiprāpakam
ākāśānantatāyogād rūpādīnāmanantatā
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane te vai tathāgatāḥ proktā bodhisattvāstathaiva ca
tattvasaṃgrahamevedaṃ maṇḍalaṃ yat svakāyataḥ
kalpayantīha satkāyā bodhisattvā hi mūcchitām
na jānanti taditthaṃ te sukhenonmādacoditāḥ
bodhisattvānmahāsattvān vakṣyanti mūcchitā iti
nītārthaḥ
athetyādi etānmahābodhisattvāniti yāvat sugamam
yathārutam
ākāśasamatā tattulyatā
na dvayamadvayam
ākāśasamatādvayam
tadeva vajraṃ tannāmakaṃ samādhiṃ samāpannaḥ
samanantarasamāpannasya samādhirasamāpanna- mātrasya bhagavata ityādi vibhaktārthaḥ
evaṃbhūtasya mahāvajradharasya prabhayā raśmisaṃhatyā spṛṣṭamātrayā sparśamātrayā sveṣu sveṣvāsaneṣu sthitā abhūvanniti
punaḥ smṛti pratilabdhavanta iti
neyārthaḥ
ākāśaṃ śūnyattrayaṃ samatā prabhāsvaraṃ tayoradvayaṃ yuganaddhatā
abhedyatvād vajram
tannāmakaṃ samādhi samāpanna iti
asthānakasamādhisthiteṣu bodhi- sattvarūpāvasthitasvātmasu prāpitavān samanantarasamāpannasyeti evaṃ kṛtādhiṣṭhāna- mātrasya mahāvajradharasaṃvandhinyā prabhayā ālokapuñjena spṛṣṭamātrayeti prabhāviśeṣaṇaṃ spṛśyata iti spṛṣṭo vāyuḥ tasya mātrā yasyā: tayā prabhayā sūkṣmadhātvanupraviṣṭajñānaraśmiprasaraiḥ prabhāsvarāt vyutthitavanta ityarthaḥ
sveṣu sveṣvāsaneṣviti
yathāsvaṃ lokadhātuṣu sthitā iti
sattvārthakriyā niṣpādana- samarthā abhūvan
nītārthaḥ
athetyādi
bhagavato gambhīradharmadeśanākauśalyenāścaryaprāptāḥ
abhūta- pūrvaṃ bhūtamityadbhutaḥ
atyadbhutenaudvilyaṃ atyadbhutaudvilyaṃ saṃbhramaḥ
deśanādhi- gatasamādhiviśeṣeṇa niṣpannasattvarāśidarśanāt
atyadbhutaudvilyaprāptāḥ
evaṃ dharmāṇāṃ evaṃprakāraṃ dharmaghoṣamiti dharmasaṃprayuktaṃ ghoṣamakārṣuḥ kṛtavantaḥ
dharma- deśanāprasannāstathāgatāḥ dharmastavanadvāreṇa taddeśakameva aho ityādibhiḥ gāthābhiḥ stutavantaḥ
I aho vismaye
dharma iti deśanālakṣaṇo dharmaḥ
dvitīyena dharmaśabdena tatpratipādyo'rtho vivakṣitaḥ
dharmārthasaṃbhava iti
dharmo'bhidhānalakṣaṇaḥ artho'bhi- pañcamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā dheyalakṣaṇaḥ tatsaṃbhavo dharmārthasaṃbhavaḥ
ko'sau ? dharmaśuddhayārthanairātmyamiti
dharma [:] śrutamayaḥ śuddhārthaḥ cintāmayaḥ
tābhyāmāmukhīkṛtaṃ nairātmyam
kiṃ tat ? bhāvanāmayavajjrarāja iti
vajrāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ rājā adhipatirmahāvajradharaḥ
evaṃvidhāya tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ
bhagavataḥ satyadvayātmakatvāt āmreḍitavacanam
kāyavākcittaiḥ saṃśuddhaḥ kāyādvināmanyo'nyamapṛthaktvāt ākāśasamatā dī prabhāsvaraṃ tadālayaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ utpādanirodhāpagamāt
nirvikāraviśuddhā- bhāsatvāt nirābhāsaḥ
vajrakāya iti indrāyudhavat
pañcaraśmiparighaṭitā- vinaśvarasarvākāravaropetadeho vajrakāya tubhyaṃ namo nama iti pūrvavadāmreḍitam
sarvabuddhaguṇaiścitatvāt cittaṃ tathāgatānāmādibhūtatvāt śreṣṭhaḥ pradhānaḥ
vyadhvapathe vartituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te vyadhvapathavartinaḥ
atītādikālavartinaḥ padārthāḥ tvam sarvabhāvasvabhāvatvāt
mahākāśaprabhāsvarastvameva, tvameva dhātubhūtaḥ
traidhātukatvaṃ ākāśapadaṃ ṛddhayaiśvaryaprāptatvāt
tubhyaṃ namo nama iti satyadvayātmakatvādāgreḍitam
ākāśakāyasaṃbhūta iti
ākāśavat kāyo yasya tadākāśakāyajñāna- mādarśajñānaṃ tasmāt saṃbhūto vairocanaḥ, tanmayatvāt
ākāśapathaṃ samatājñānaṃ tasmād vartate niṣpādyate tatsaṃbhavatayā yaḥ sa ākāśapathavartako ratnasaṃbhavaḥ
ākāśavaccittaṃ yasya sa ākāśacitto'kṣobhyaḥ suviśuddhadharmadhātusvabhāvatvāt
dharmāḥ padmalīnāsteṣāmagraḥ pratyavekṣaṇājñānātmako'mitābhaḥ
sakalajagadarthāścaryāḥ teṣāṃ padaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ kṛtvānuṣṭhānajñānasvabhāvo'moghasiddhiḥ
anayā gāthayā pañcakulātmakatvena bhagavantaṃ mahāvajradharaṃ stauti
svaparārthasādhikā caryā samantacaryā saivāgraṃ viśiṣṭaṃ samantacaryāgraṃ tadartha- pratipādakaḥ paṭalaḥ samantacaryāgro nāma
nāsandhyam|| || pradīpodyotanaṭīkāyāṃ pañcamapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā || || ṣaṣṭhaḥ paṭalaḥ || svādhiṣṭhānakramāhaṅkārapūrvikā caryā kartavyeti kāyādyadhiṣṭhānakramaḥ pradarśyate
athetyādi
sarvatathāgatakāyavākcitto'kṣobhyaḥ
tasya guhyaṃ tadadhiṣṭhā- yako mantraḥ sa yasmin tannāmakaṃ samādhi cittaikāgratāṃ samāpadya āmukhīkṛtya idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ cittamadhiṣṭhīyate
yena sacittādhiṣṭhānaḥ mana- strāyate iti mantraḥ tamudājahāra kathitavān
oṃ sarvatathāgatacittavatrasvabhāvātmako'ham
iti
athetyādi
virajavajra ādarśajñānātmako vairocanaḥ
tasya padaṃ tadadhi- ṣṭhāyako mantraḥ
sa yasmin
tannāmakaṃ samādhimālambya kāyādhiṣṭhānamantra- mudājahāra
oṃ sarvatathāgatakāyavajrasvabhāvātmako'ham
iti
athetyādi
amitamapramāṇamāyuḥ yasmāt labhyate so'mitāyuḥ samatā- dvayapratyavekṣaṇājñānātmako'mitābhaḥ
tasya vajrastadadhiṣṭhāyako'bhedyasvabhāvo mantraḥ
sa yasmin tannāmakaṃ samādhimāmukhīkṛtya vāgadhiṣṭhānamantramudājahāra
oṃ sarvatathāgatavāgvajrasvabhāvātmako'ham
iti
evaṃ kāyādyadhiṣṭhānamantramuddiśya punarnirdiśannāha
trivajramityādi
trayaḥ kāyavākcittavajrāḥ yasmin tat trivajram
kiṃ tat ? padaṃ śarīram, devatācakrasyādhāratvāt
tāthāgataṃ akṣobhyādisvabhāvam
guhyaṃ śrāvakādi- bhiraparijñātatvāt
padavibhāvanamiti
padaṃ locanādivyūhatritayam, svaparārtha- sampadādhāratvāt
tasya vibhāvanam
kāyavajrādyadhiṣṭhānārtham spharaṇaṃ yasmiṃsta- devam
mantrā akṣobhyādayaḥ
te lakṣyante yaistāni mantralakṣaṇāni vyakṣarāṇi
taiḥ śiraḥ kaṇṭhahṛddeśeṣu vinyasya tairlakṣitam
tadeva ebhiranantaroktaiḥ kāyā- dyadhiṣṭhānamantraiḥ pravarairutkṛṣṭai niṣpādayedadhitiṣṭhediti
neyārthaḥ
niṣpannakramayogināṃ kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaṃ vyākhyātantrādavatāryate
athākṣobhyo mahāvīraḥ kāyavākcittaguhyakam
samādhi tu samāpadya cittādhiṣṭhānasaṃjñakam
ṣaṣṭhapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā udājahāra mantraṃ sa omityādi prakīrtitam
sarvatathāgateti dviguṇākhyāparikalpitam
cittavatreti dvidvisaṃyuktaṃ svabhāvātmakapañcamam
ahaṃ ṣaṣṭhābhidhāne syāt ṣaṭkāyattrikasaṃjñikam
atha vairocanaḥ śrīmān virajavajrasaṃjñikam
samādhiṃ tu samāpadya kāyādhiṣṭhānakaṃ vadet
omiti brahmaṇākhyātaṃ sarvatāthāgatādvikam
kāyarāgagato hyeṣa vajravedaḥ prakīrttitaḥ
svabhāvātmakakāmaḥ syāt tadahaṃ kārttika ucyate
sandhyāya kalitaṃ samyak prajñābhedena bhedayet
athāmitābhasaṃjñī tu samatādvayavajrakam | samādhiṃ ca samāpadya vāgadhiṣṭhānakaṃ vadet ||
omityādi samākhyātaṃ sarvatāthāgatādvikam | vāgiti triguṇaṃ jñeyaṃ yugaṃ vajrasaṃjñakaṃ bhavet ||
svabhāvātmakakāmākhya mahamiti sa tu smṛtaḥ | trayaścaite samākhyātā: pratyekaṃ paṭvikoditāḥ ||
kramavyatikramaṃ coktaṃ syāccittakāyavāktathā | trivajrasaṃjñino jātā adhipatyaikadharmataḥ ||
iti trivajraṃ tāthāgataṃ guhyamiti
trivajraṃ jñānatrayam
kīdṛk ?
tāthāgataṃ tathāgatādāyātam guhyaṃ utpattikramayogināmagocaratvāt
padaṃ padavibhāvanamiti
padaṃ paramārthasatyam
vijñānatrayapratiṣṭhābhūtatvāt padaṃ saṃvṛtisatyam
tad vibhāvyate utpadyate yasmāt tatpadavibhāvanam
paramārthasatyasyaiva tadviśeṣaṇam
ebhiranantaroktaiḥ pravaraiścatuḥśūnyaiḥ
mantrāstathāgatāḥ
teṣāṃ lakṣaṇāni pañcaraśmayaḥ
taiḥ pañcalakṣaṇairlakṣitam viṣayībhūtaṃ vajradehaṃ niṣpādayet ghaṭa- yediti
nītārthaḥ
evamātmānaḥ kāyādyadhiṣṭhānaṃ kṛtvā svakulāṅganā pañcakulakalāpinīṃ kṛtvā ubhayacakrānurāgaṇamantrāvatāraṇāyāha atha bhagavānityādiḥ vibhaktārthaḥ
oṃ sarvatathāgatānurāgaṇavantrasvabhāvātmako'ham | iti ||
anena liṅgayet
samāpattyanantaraṃ sarvatathāgatapūjā mantrāvatāraṇāyāha atha bhagavānityādi
etadapi gatārtham ||
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane oṃ sarvatathāgatapūjāvajrasvabhāvātmako'ham | iti ||
anantarokto yo vidhistenai[va] sadāmantriṇā vartitavyamiti
tasminneva dāḍharyamutpādayitumāha
pañca kāmaguṇairbuddhān pūjayed vidhinā sadā iti
niṣpannakramayogināṃ punaranyaṃ pūjāvidhi nirdiśannāha
pañcopahāramityādi
upahriyante līyante ityupahārāḥ
pañcopahārā yasmin sa pañcopahāraḥ
ṣaṣṭha- stathāgataḥ
tasya pūjā
pañcatathāgatasvabhāvenādarśādipañcajñānairhetubhūtaiḥ prabhā- svarādhigamaḥ
tābhiḥ pūjābhiḥ
laghu kṣipram
ihaiva janmani buddhatvaṃ trikāyā- tmakaṃ saugatapadamāpnuyāt
iti itthaṃ dviprakārāṃ pūjāmāha
bhagavān sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittā- dhipatirmahāvajradharaḥ
amumeva pūjāvidhiṃ darśayannāha vyākhyātantre
rūpaṃ śabdaṃ gandhaṃ rasaṃ spraṣṭavyaṃ dharmadhātukam | kṛtvā tannisvabhāvaṃ syād buddhapūjā taduttamā
cakṣurādijñānasambhūtaiḥ pañcābhijñopaśobhitaiḥ
ebhiḥ pañcopahārākhyairlaghu buddhatvamāpnuyāt ||
iti
sandhyābhāṣā ||
evaṃ vāhyādhyātmikapūjāṃ nirdiśya niṣpannakrameṇa vinā śīghraṃ bodhirna labhyata iti darśayitumāha
athetyādi
mahāvajradhara idaṃ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ sarva- tathāgatamantraṃ sarvatathāgatavākkāyamanasī ca (?)
upalakṣaṇānmantrasya
teṣāṃ sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittānāṃ rahasyaṃ viśuddhipadamudājahāra
mantranidhyapti ityādi
mantrā ālikālijamitāḥ (?)
sarpādi- svabhāvāḥ teṣāṃ strīpuṃnapuṃsakatvena tyakṣarāvarodhānmantrāḥ tyakṣarāṇi
teṣāṃ praveśasthitivyutthānasvabhāvena parijñānaṃ mantranidhyaptiḥ
tatra pūrvakaṃ kāyena vajrajāpasya sādhanabhūtena nirmāṇaśarīreṇa vācā vajrajāpenopalakṣitaḥ
manasi hṛdisthito vajrasattvaḥ coditaḥ
yathābhūtaparijñānena viṣayīkṛtaḥ
bhāvayediti
yogisantānasya vakṣyamāṇakāścatasro'vasthā niṣpādayet
kāstā ityāha
pravarāmityāha
pravarāmutkṛṣṭāṃ cittavivekalakṣaṇāṃ vajra- jāpādadhikatvāt siddhimiti
svādhiṣṭhānalakṣaṇā mahāmudrā manaḥ santoṣayatīti manaḥ santoṣaṇaṃ mahāmudrāviśuddhikaratvāt
priyāmiṣṭāṃ bhavasama ekalakṣaṇāṃ mahāvajradharamūttiṃ niṣpādayet
anena vajrajāpasyānuśaṃsā varṇitā bhavati
ṣaṣṭhapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā cittanidhyaptītyādi
cittasya catvāryuddeśapadāni nirdiśannāha
nidhyaptidvitīyakrameṇa yathābhūtaparijñānaṃ tatkṛtam
cittasya nairātmyaṃ yat tat cittanairātmyam
vācā kāya ityādi
vākkāyayorapi nairātmyam
vibhāva- namiti
cittaprativedhaṃ niṣpādayet kuryādityarthaḥ
anena kāyavākcittalakṣaṇaṃ prajñopāyopalabdhattrayaṃ darśitaṃ bhavati
tataḥ kimityāha
trisaṃyogamityādi
trayāṇāṃ jñānānāṃ saṃyogaḥ ekībhāvo yasmin taṃ trisaṃyogaṃ manomayadevatārūpam
ākāśasamatā paramārthasatyam
tadālaya utpattisthānaṃ yasya vajradharasya taṃ niṣpādayet
kāyavākcittanidhyapteḥ svabhāvo nopalabhyata iti
kāyavākcittānāṃ nidhyaptyā nirūpaṇena yaḥ prāptaḥ svabhāvaḥ saṃvṛtisatyaṃ sa nopalabhyate, sarveṣāṃ paramārthasatyagamanāt
anenābhisaṃbodhikramaḥ prakaṭitaḥ
itthaṃ jñāna- maye'pi kāye pratiṣṭhāmalabhamānasya kadācit kalpitayogena devatāmūrtī sthitirlabhyata ityāśaṅkā syāt
tannirākartumāha
mantramūrtiprayogeṇetyādi
mantrāḥ praṇavādayaḥ
taiḥ kṛtā mūrtiḥ
mantramūrtiḥ
tasyāḥ prayogo vajra- catuṣkaṃ tena na bodhirna buddhatvam
yasmānmantrādyakṣarāṇi tāvatsāmā̭grījani- tānyutpannapradhvaṃsitvāt
khasamāni kutastairjanitā mūrtiḥ
na ca bhāvaneti
bhāvyamānadevatārūpābhāvāt tadbhāvanāyāpyabhāvaḥ
evaṃ dharmasaṃbhogakāyā- dīnāmapi heyatvāt
samājātmapratiṣṭhānalakṣaṇaṃ mahāvajradharapadaṃ darśayannāha
vicāryetyādi
idamityanantaroktaṃ kāyavākcittalakṣaṇaṃ mantranidhyaptyādikrameṇa vicārya parijñāya kāyavākcittāni yairlakṣyante tatkāyavākcittalakṣaṇaṃ śūnyattrayaṃ samāsena saṃvṛtasatyasya paramārthasatyenaikīkaraṇena bodhisaṃyogaṃ samādhi mantrakalpitaṃ bhāvayediti
bodhiḥ paramārthasatyaṃ tena saṃyogo yasya taṃ bodhisaṃyogaṃ samādhi- vajropamasamādhim
mantrāḥ pañcaraśmayaḥ
taiḥ kalpitaṃ ghaṭitaṃ bhāvayet abhyaset
anena yuganaddhavāhikramaḥ pradarśito bhavati
sandhyābhāṣā ||
idānīṃ tṛtīyapaṭaloktameva sūkṣmayogaṃ punarapi vāyuvijñānasahitaṃ nirdiṣṭa- māha
athetyādi
athaśabdo niṣpannakramapradarśanānantaram
vajradhara iti
vajraṃ dhārayatīti vajradharaḥ
akṣarārthaḥ
ādarśādijñānapañcakaṃ tanmayatvena dhāraṇād vajradharaḥ
samastāṅgam
manomayadehadhāraṇād vajradharaḥ ||
garbhī ||
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane vajramadvayajñānaṃ tasya tanmayatvena dhāraṇād vajradharaḥ ||
kolikam
ubhayasaṃbhāropetatvāt śrīmān
sarvatathāgatalocanādayastābhiracitaḥ sarvatathāgatā- citaḥ
sarvabuddhā vairocanādayasteṣāṃ madhye agraḥ sarvajñaścittavajraḥ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ bhāvanottamaṃ bhāṣate
ākāśadhātumadhyasthaṃ prabhāsvaraskaṃ candramaṇḍalamālokapuñjaṃ bhāvayediti
pūrva saṃbhogakāyaṃ niṣpādayet
buddhavimbaṃ prabhāvitveti paścāt
pañcaskandhādyanu- praveśena sarvatathāgataparamāṇughaṭitaṃ nirmāṇakāyamātmānaṃ niṣpādya māṇakrameṇa sūkṣmayogaṃ samārabhet ||
nāsāgre sarṣapaṃ cintet sarṣape sacarācaram | bhāvayejjñānapradaṃ ramyaṃ rahasyaṃ jñānakalpitam ||
vakṣya- ityanena vajrapadena sūtreṇa prākṛtasattvānāmutpattiṃ kalpitadevatālambanaṃ vāyutattvaṃ mantratattvaṃ jñānatattvaṃ pariśuddhadevatātattvaṃ ca nirdiśati
ādau sattvotpatti- kāraṇaṃ nirdiśyate
ūrdhvasthitāḥ prāṇāyāmādvayendriyasamāgame ādhyātmika- prajñopāyasamāpattyā harṣaprerite sakalāṅgaṃ mahārāgānalena dravībhūya bodhicita- vindurūpeṇa padmanāsāgre patati
/
etadbhavotpattikramabhāvakānāṃ tathāgataiḥ sarṣapaśabdena saṃketitam
imamāha samājottare pañcajñānamayaṃ śvāsaṃ pañcabhūtasvarūpakam | niścārya pañcanāśāgre piṇḍarūpeṇa kalpayet ||
pañcavarṇaṃ mahāratnaṃ prāṇāyāma iti smṛtaḥ || iti
sarṣapè sacarācaramiti

amṛtaṃ śukramityuktaṃ tatprasūtaṃ jagattrayam
iti vacanāt
asminneva sarṣape kalpitayogābhyāsaṃ sacarācaraṃ vajrasatvaṃ cintayet
asyaiva viśeṣaṇaṃ bhāvayet jñānapadaṃ ramyaṃ rahasyaṃ jñānakalpita- miti
neyārthaḥ
niṣpannakramasya cāha
nāsāgre sarṣapaṃ cintedityādi
hṛdaye guhye kaṇṭhe nābhau sarvaśarīreṣu sthitāḥ pañcajñānasvabhāvāḥ pañcaraśmayaḥ prāṇāyāmo bhūtvā nāsāgre ūrdhvaṃnāsikāyāṃ ghrāṇavivarāccaturmaṇḍalāvartena paripāṭyā niścaranti
yathoktaṃ vajramālāyām
ṣaṣṭhapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā nāsāgre sarṣapaṃ nāma prāṇāyāmasya kalpanāt
prāṇāyāme sthitāḥ pañcaraśmayo buddhabhāvataḥ ||
ūrdhvaṃ prāṇād viniṣkrāntā vāmadakṣiṇadvandvataḥ
stabdhaśceti caturdhā syād velā svādhyātmikī smṛtā ||
svamantrādhāra yogena divā rātrī ca sarvataḥ
viharedarddhayāmikā velā paripāṭyā yathākramam ||
nāsāgre sarṣapaṃ cintedityasya vajrapadasyārthaḥ
anena vāyutattvaṃ mantratattvaṃ kathitaṃ bhavati
sarṣape sacarācaramiti
sarṣape sūkṣmadhātau
ādhārādheyabhūtaṃ sacarācaraṃ vijñānatrayaṃ vodhātmakaṃ cintet vijānīyādidaṃ jñānatattvaṃ prabhāvayet
jñānapadaṃ ramyaṃ rahasyaṃ jñānakalpitamiti
imamarthaṃ mahāyānasūtre'pi sandhyābhāṣayā nirdiṣṭam
eka hi vāla yatheva hu buddhā yantika vālika gaṅga nadīva iti

devatātattvamucyate
