ākāśadhātumadhyastha ityādi
ākāśadhāturatna [T]nāhataṃ tanmadhyasthaṃ tatprabhavaṃ sūryamaṇḍalaṃ agnimaṇḍalaṃ raktaraśmisvabhāvaṃ dakṣiṇād viniṣkrāntaṃ raśmikalāpaṃ bhāvayed vijānīyāt
Buddhavimbaṃ padaṃ dhyātveti
buddho vairocanaḥ
vimvamamitābhaḥ
tayoḥ padaṃ praṇavākārau tatpraveśasthiti- dhyānopalakṣitayoḥ oṃkārākārayorupariṣṭāladaṃ hūṃkāraṃ vyutthānātmakaṃ nyaset
parikalpayet
ayamamitābhajāpaḥ
candramaṇḍalaṃ vāruṇaṃ maṇḍalaṃ stabdha- svabhāvaṃ locanāyā ākāraḥ tena saṃyogo yasya tadavadātamasphuṭapracāraṃ lakṣayet
vajrapadye'tha bhāvayediti
atha oṃkāraparāvṛttyanantaraṃ padmaṃ āḥkāre tasmin parāvṛtte vajrahū~kāraṃ vyutthānaṃ pravartayet
ayaṃ vajrajāpaḥ
ratnamaṇḍalaṃ ratnasaṃbhavasya māhendramaṇḍalaṃ dvandvapracāraṃ pītavarṇa ādiyogaṃ prayatnena tasyopari vibhāvayediti
tasya maṇḍalasyopariṣṭāt ādiyogaṃ kṣyakṣaraṃ spharaṇasaṃharaṇānilayātmakaṃ prayatnena ekāgratathā bhāvayet
lakṣayet ayaṃ ratnajāpaḥ
ākāśadhātumadhyasthaṃ bhāvayedraśmimaṇḍalam
a iti
nāhatādraśmimaṇḍalaṃ vāyavyamaṇḍalaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ vāmādviniṣkrāntaṃ tasyopari buddhapadaṃ huṃkāraṃ saumyaṃ hrasvaṃ praveśasthityanantaraṃ sṛjet sphared
ayamamoghasiddhi- jāpaḥ
parivāraṃ viśeṣata iti
eṣāṃ caturṇāṃ maṇḍalānāmekaikasya maṇḍalasya pravṛttyā vitaraṇaṃ maṇḍalanitayaṃ parivārabhūtaṃ tadantarbhāvena sṛjediti
asya vajrajāpasyāhorātraṃ jāpasaṃkhyā vyākhyātantrādavatāryate
pāṇḍarādijāpaḥ proktaḥ pañcaviṃśatśatadvayam
caturguṇitaṃ samyakcaturyogaṃ śataṃ nava ||
navaśataṃ tu yad dṛṣṭaṃ caturviṃśatparikramaiḥ ||
pratyutpādād bhavettasya dvayayutaṃ śataṣoḍaśam || iti
ubhayayogināmaparaṃ sūkṣmayogāntaramāha
nīlotpaletyādi
nīlotpalavarṇaṃ vajraṃ yavaphalapramāṇaṃ svakuliśacchidre bhāvayet
caṇakāsthipramāṇapadmaṃ keśara- paṃktyālaṃkṛtaṃ dharmodayāntargataṃ bodhiparāyaṇaṃ bodhiprāpakaṃ bhāvayet
tatra yoṣinnā- sikāgre cakraratnavajrāṇāṃ bhāvanaṃ caṇakāsthipramāṇenaiva kulabhedena bhāvayet
tataḥ kimityāha
sidhyata ityādi
bodhipadaṃ bodhiradvayajñānaṃ tasya padaṃ kāraṇam
kiṃ tat ?
paramārthasatyam
kleśaviśodhanād ramyaṃ manoharam
sarvasiddhayaḥ śāntikādayaḥ
guṇāḥ kāmaiśvaryādayo'ṣṭau
eṣāṃ sarvasiddhi- guṇānāmālayaṃ sthānabhūtaṃ vodhipadaṃ sidhyate
tasya yoginaḥ pratyakṣo bhavati
kathaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ pratyakṣīkarotītyāha
sṛjedityādi
buddhān skandhādīn bodhipratiṣṭhitān
nirmāṇakāyāvasthitān
samāseneti jñānatrayaṃ saṃyogena sthitaṃ kṛtvā tatra paramārthasatye sṛjet nikṣipet
evaṃ paramārthasatyapraveśānantaraṃ vyutthānaṃ pradarśayitumāha
niścārayedityādi
kāyavākcittalakṣitaṃ trikāyātmakaṃ dharmaprabhāsvaraṃ tat padaṃ kāraṇaṃ yasya tad dharmapadaṃ mahāvajjradharamātmānaṃ niṣpādayediti
sandhyābhāṣā
idānīṃ pañcakāmaguṇadvāreṇa niṣpannakramaṃ dyotayannāha
atha vajradhara ṣaṣṭhapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ityādi
atha sūkṣmayogānantaraṃ sarvasattvārthaḥ advayajñānaṃ tasya deśakaḥ sarvasattvārtha- deśakaḥ
sarvacaryāprapañcaniṣprapañcātyantaniṣprapañcacaryāstāsāmagracaryā bhusuku caryā tayā saṃśuddhaṃ vajradhara padaprāpaṇāduttamaṃ guhyaṃ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ bhāṣate
ṣaṇmāsamityādi
rūpaśabdarasānvita iti tvavajrābhedyasvabhāvātmānaṃ jñātvā ca ṣaṇmāsaṃ satataṃ bhāvayet
viṇmūtra iti
viṇmūtrāhārakṛtyarthaṃ śarīrasthityarthaṃ siddhiphalārthī kuryāt
miti ||
māṃsamityādi
āhārakṛtyarthaṃ upahārārthaṃ krodhadevatānāṃ nivedyārthaṃ yadi māsaṃ kalpayet tadā mahāmāsaṃ prakalpayet
rahasyaṃ māraṇādi karma tasmin bhavaṃ tadviṣayaṃ rahasyam
kiṃ tat ?
kāyavākcittaṃ sidhyate a[T]nukūlye samāpadyate
na kevalaṃ māraṇādiṣu iti darśayati
sarvasiddhiṣviti
guli- kādisiddhiṣvātenasyā ( ? ) yoginaḥ kāyavākcittasausthityaṃ jāyate
evaṃ vajrakulinaḥ krodhānāmupahāramuktvā tathāgatādikulakrodhānāmupadarśayannāha
hastimāṃsetyādi
uttamamiti
gomāṃsaṃ bhakṣayediti
etanmāṃsacatuṣṭaya- māhārakṛtyarthaṃ nivedyārthaṃ tathāgatakulādikrodhagaṇairbhakṣayet
eteṣāmabhāve kiṃ kuryādityāha
anyad yatkiñcidapi māṃsaṃ anyad yatkiñcidapi māṃsaṃ dadyāt
annaṃ tu odanaṃ punaḥ na ca naiva bhakṣayet khādayet
krodhānāṃ raudrakarmāṇi
annasyānivedyatvāt phalaṃ darśayati
buddhānāma- kṣobhyādīnāṃ svakulīnapūjākaraṇāt priyo bhavati
dhīmatāṃ svamudrāsahitānāṃ tatkulaprativaddhabuddhabodhisattvānāmapi punarapyanuśaṃsāmeva darśayati
anenetyādi
anena valipradānayogena laghu kṣipraṃ buddhatvaṃ bodhyatvaṃ prāpnuyāt na kevalaṃ jñānī bhavati kiṃtu kāmadhātvīśvaro loke sa bhaveta padakarmakṛt iti
kāma- dhātuḥ sarvāḥ striyaḥ tāsu viśvaramaṇeśvaro bhavati
ṣaṭtriṃśanmerūṇāṃ yāvantaḥ paramāṇavaḥ yoṣitastasya tāvatyo bhaviṣyanti
guṇālayetyatraivoktatvāt
loke karmabhūmau padamavaivartakaṃ padaṃ tasya karma
sa tu pāvanamocanalakṣaṇaṃ tat karotīti padakarmakṛditi
sarvaduḥkhadaumarnasyābhibhavanaṃ tejaḥ tad yasyāsti guhyasamājapradīpodyotane sa tejasvī aṣṭābhirlokadharmairalaṅghayatvād valavān parikalpitayogināmadhi- kataratvāt
śreṣṭhaḥ vajrasattvo yauvanasamanvitatvāt
kāntimān sadā-
pramuditatvāt
priyadarśanaḥ dharmatāyogaṃ mahāyogī imaṃ lokaṃ mitrādiprakārabhinnaṃ janaṃ darśane
darśanamavabodhaḥ
tat trividhaṃ cakṣuṣā śravaṇena manasā vā
tasmin darśanamātre saṃjāte vaśamānayediti
ānayatīti prāpte viparyayaḥ
naiva coditaḥ vāhyayantramudrādibhiścoditaḥ preritaḥ
naiva nimittaṃ samādhi- sthitasya nirnimitta phalamiti vairocanābhisaṃbodhitantre vacanāt
sarvasaukhya- manābhogenaiva saṃpadyate
idamanantaroktaṃ tatsarvabuddhānāmiti
sarve kalpitayoginaḥ tebhyo viśiṣṭā buddhā boddhāraḥ niṣpannakramayogasamādhisthāḥ
teṣāṃ rahasyaṃ guhyaṃ bodhicitta - uttamamiti
uttamā bodhiḥ tatprāpaṇādupacārāt
idamanantaroktameva mantra- guhyaṃ tathāgatarahasyaṃ prākṛtakāyavākcittaṃ laṅghayate yena tat kāyavākcittalaṅghanaṃ tattvam
etaduktaṃ bhavati
prākṛtadehamapanīya yena vajramayadehamāpyate tādṛgvidhaṃ tattvamidameveti tathā coktaṃ, evaṃ samādhiyuktena nirvikalpena mantriṇaḥ
kālāvadhiṃ parityajya prāpyate'nuttaraṃ padam
iti
neyārthaḥ
eṣāmeva vajrapadānāṃ vyākhyātantrānusāreṇa vibhaṅgo'vatāryate
atha tathāgate so'pi bhāṣate guhyamuttamam
sarvacaryāgrasaṃśoddhā sarvasattvārthabhāṣakaḥ
kṣaṇaṃ lavaṃ muhūrtaṃ ca pakṣamāsābdakālakam
trisaṃkhyaikakālākhyaṃ kṣaṇamekaṃ •vidhīyate
ṣaṇmāsākhyaṃ tadeva syāt saṃbhogaṃ nisvabhāvataḥ
kṣaṇaikamudbhavecchastaṃ tyadhvātītaṃ ca bhāvataḥ
rūpaśabdarasairyuktaṃ ṣaṇmāsaṃ bhāvayet kṛtī
kṛtvā vai sa mahāpūjāṃ guhyaṃ tattvaṃ vibhāvayet
viṣayendriyaniḥsattvaṃ viṇmūtrāhārakṛtyakam
kurvanti tanmayāṃ siddhi mahāraṃ nirvikalpakam
ṣaṣṭhapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā tattvānuttaramevedaṃ bodhicittaikasaṃbhavam
sidhyate nirvikalpārthamanāvilamasaṃskṛtam
māmiti māmakī proktā samabhisaṃbodhisaṃjñakam
tattvadhyānaprītikāhāraṃ māṃsaṃ ceti taducyate
ākāśānantayogāśvamahāmāṃsaṃ tu tadbhavet
trivajraṃ sidhyate kṣipraṃ rahasyaṃ paramaṃ padam
mahāsamayahastyaśvaṃ tathā śvānādimāṃsakam
skandhādikulabhedena yaduktaṃ samasaṃkhyataḥ
udyogādvayamāhāraṃ dhyānaprītyaikasaṃjñinām
kavelikaḥ svarūpaṃ syāt lokottaraṃ ca laukikam
lokottaratamā ye ca nirvikalpaviśāradāḥ
tathataikasvabhāvāste saṃsāretaravarjitāḥ
priyā bhavanti buddhānāṃ yogenānena suvratāḥ
dhīmatāṃ bodhisattvānāṃ bodhi śīghramavāpnuyāt
kāmadhātvīśvarā loke bhavanti padayoginaḥ
tejasvino valāḍhyāśca kāntimān priyadarśanāḥ
vaśaṃ kurvanti te lokaṃ darśanenaiva codakāḥ
rahasyaṃ tadidaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ buddhānāṃ bodhimuttamam
guhyamantramidaṃ tattvaṃ trivajrālaṅghanaṃ param
sarvatāthāgataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ samayaṃ samayottamam
iti nītārthaḥ
kāyavākcittanityena ( ? ) tattvenāvinaśvarasvabhāvenādhiṣṭhīyanti niṣpā- dyante tadvācako granthasamūhaḥ paṭalaḥ kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhāno nāma
|| pradīpodyotanaṭīkāyāṃ ṣaṣṭhapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
|| saptamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
evaṃ sāmānyenottamayogināṃ caryāṃ saṃpradarśya tāmeva punarapi prapañcayitumāha athetyādi
mahāsamayo mahāvajradharaḥ tasya tattvaṃ ṣaḍaṅgaṃ mantracaryā sāmānya- sādhanaṃ tābhyāṃ prāptā saṃbodhiḥ vajradharatvam
mahāsamayatattvamantracaryāsaṃbodhiḥ
tadarthaḥ paṭalaḥ tatpratipādako granthasamūhaḥ
tamudājahāra bhagavāniti yogaḥ
sarvakāmetyādi
sarvakāmā rūpādayo viṣayāḥ
teṣāmupabhogo'nubhāvaḥ
taiḥ sevyamānaiḥ|kathamityata āha
yathecchata iti
icchā cchandaḥ
yā yā icchāyathecchantaḥ
icchāgrahaṇaprakāraḥ
cakṣurādīni cakṣūrūpādīni hīnamadhyapraṇītānyekākāreṇa viṣayī kurvanti
yogināmapi indriyecchānurūpeṇa ca bhoktavyam
sevya- mānairyathecchta ityatra prapañcacaryāṃ carediti vākyaśeṣaḥ
tataḥ kimityāha
anenetyādi
anenānantaroktena yogeneti viṣayendriyasamāyogena
khalu niścayena
buddhatvaṃ vajradharatvaṃ laghu kṣipramihaiva prāpnoti
evaṃ prapañcacaryāṃ nirdiśya niṣprapañcacaryāmadhikṛtyāha
sarvetyādi
yathecchata iti ātmecchānurūpataḥ
yathāsukhamanubhūyamānaiḥ pañcakāmaguṇaiḥ svātmānaṃ parāṃśca pūjayet tarpayet
kathaṃ svādhidaivatayogena māyopamasamādhinā
ataevāha
duṣkarairityādi
kāyaklamayogena vihārādinivartanāni duṣkarāṇi
vṛkṣamūlādidvādaśavrataguṇaiḥ
niyamāḥ giripātanāgnipraveśādīni tīvrāṇi kāṣṭhāni
etaiḥ sevyamānaḥ pīḍyamāno na sidhyati
bodhiṃ na labhate
tarhi kathamityāha
sarvakāmopabhogaistu sevayannāśu sidhyatīti
sarvaiḥ kāmyante prārthyanta iti sarvakāmāḥ teṣāmupabhogaiḥ sevayan mahāvajradharamārādhayannāśu ihaiva janmani mahāmudrāsiddhilābhī bhavati
yathārutaṃ niṣprapañcacaryā
evaṃ kāyapīḍādvāreṇa mahāmudrāsiddheḥ prāpti parihṛtya manaḥpīḍāmapi sādhakasya niṣeddhumāha
bhikṣāśinetyādi
bhikṣāśanaśīlena na japtavyaṃ na pralapitavyam
bhikṣāśanamupalakṣaṇārtham
tasmād bhikṣāṭanavat anyābhirapi paryeṣaṇādidainyavṛttibhiḥ samanvitena na japtavyamiti
na bhaikṣarato bhavediti
bhaikṣamityāmiṣopalakṣaṇam
bhaikṣarata āhāraprasakto na bhavet
yāvanmātreṇa saptamapaṭalapaṭkoṭivyākhyā śarīrasthitirbhavati tāvatā santuṣṭenātyantaniṣprapañcacaryā caritavyā
evaṃbhūtacaryāsthitaḥ kiṃ kuryādityāha
japedityādi
mantraṃ tyakṣaraṃ abhinnāṅga abhinnadevatāyogaḥ sarvakāmopabhogakṛt mantrī japediti
evaṃ kṛte ko guṇa ityāha
kāyavākcittetyādi
kāyavākcittānāṃ sausthityaṃ samāhitatā
tat prāpyate bodhiṃ mahāvajradharatvaṃ samaśnute samyak aśnute prāpnoti
anyathetyādinā duṣkaracaryāyāṃ doṣaṃ saukhyacaryāyāmanuśaṃsā muddyotayati
viṣamāṃ parihāreṇa maraṇaṃ kālakriyāṃ kṛtvā tataścyutaḥ san narake'vīcyādau pacyate
neyārthaḥ
ārṣavyākhyānamucyate
bhinnākārakṣetraṃ bhikṣā taduccāraṇamaśanam
ebhibhikṣāśinā neṣṭāṃ yojayanti hitavratāḥ
āśvāsaṃ praśvāsaṃ yogī caturyogaikatatparaḥ
japenmantramabhinnāṅgaṃ prajñopāyapade sthitaḥ
ayutadvayaṃ sahasraṃ ca ṣaṭśatāni tathaiva ca
ahorātreṇa yogīndro japasaṃkhyāṃ karoti ca
nītārthaḥ
atītā api jñānino'nayaiva caryayā mahāvajradharatvaṃ prāptā iti nirdeṣṭu- māha
buddhāścetyādi
buddhā ratnapudgalāḥ bodhisattvāḥ puṇḍarīkāḥ
mantra- caryāḥ kriyācaryobhayaṃ tattroktāḥ caryāstāḥ samagrā yogacaryā tāṃ carituṃ śīlaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathā
dharmāḥ saṃvṛtisatyaṃ akṣaraṃ paramārthasatyaṃ dharmayuktamakṣaraṃ tad yuga- naddhātmakaṃ mahāvajjradharatvaṃ sarvaiśvaryapadatvāt
śreṣṭhamutkṛṣṭaṃ prāptāḥ sākṣātkṛ- tavantaḥ
sarvakāmopasevanairiti punarduṣkarādikameva niṣedhayati
asamādhāna-
karatvāt
nāsandhyam
teṣāṃ sevyabhūtaviṣayāṇāṃ traividhyaṃ darśayituṃ pīṭhikāmāracayati
sarvetyādi
kāmyante sarvasattvaiḥ prārthyanta iti kāmāḥ
buddhatvaṃ pradānaguṇayogād guṇāḥ
kāmāścaite guṇāśca kāmaguṇāḥ
tān pañcapañcendriyaviṣayabhūtān rāgiṇaḥ rāgīti prāpte viṣayeṣvāsaktaḥ
śradvayakramo sarveryāpatheṣu sevet abhyaset
evaṃ kṛte kimityāha
toṣayedityādi
bodhisattvāṃścakṣurādisva- bhāvān kṣitigarbhādīṃstoṣayet
tuśabdo'vadhāraṇe
kṣitigarbhādayastuṣṭāḥ kiṃ kurvanti ityāha
rāgayedityādi
bodhiḥ pṛthivyādisvabhāvā locanādayaḥ
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane tābhiryuktāḥ prabhāsvareṇa nirdagdhakleśāḥ tejasvinaḥ bodhisauriṇaḥ vairocanādayaḥ tān locanādiyuktavairocanāditathāgatān rūpaskandhādisvabhāvān rāgayet
santoṣayeyuḥ prīṇayeyuḥ ārṣatvāt
karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhī
vaividhyaṃ darśayati
rūpamityādi
hīnamadhyapraṇītā trividhaṃ triprakāram
rūpaṃ cakṣurviṣayaṃ vijñāya jñātvā tatra praṇītena devatānāṃ svakulīnānāṃ nānā- rūpeṇa pūjanātmakaḥ prapañcacaryāsthito mantrī devatācakraṃ pūjayet saṃtarpayet
sa eva bhagavāniti
trividhaṃ rūpamiti yaduktaṃ tatrānyat viśvaḥ sarvātmakaḥ sa bhagavān vairocana eva
tathaiva trividhaṃ śabdaṃ śrotraviṣayaṃ jñātvā tatra praṇītaṃ devatānāṃ svakulīnānāṃ nivedayet pratipādayet
sa eveti
yaḥ triprakāraḥ śabdaḥ sa bhagavān ratna- keturnānyaḥ
tathā trividhaṃ gandhaṃ jñātvā viśiṣṭabuddhādīnāmamitābhakulīnānāṃ nivedayet upaḍhaukayet
sa eveti
gandho nāma kaścit sa eva dharmadharo'mitābhaḥ
rasamityādi
trividhaṃ rasaṃ jñātvā tatra praṇītaṃ devatānāṃ karmakulīnānāṃ nivedayet
yastrividho rasaḥ sa bhagavānamoghasiddhireva
sparśamityādi
tathaiva triprakāraṃ sparśa jñātvā praṇītena svakulaṃ vajrakulaṃ nivedayet tarpayet
sa eveti yastrividhaḥ sparśaḥ sa evākṣobhyaḥ
rūpetyādinānantaroktamevārthaṃ piṇḍīkṛtya kathayati
evaṃ rūpādīnāṃ vairocanādisvarūpatvāt
teṣu sadāharniśaṃ cittaṃ manaskāraṃ niyojayedutpādayet
idaṃ tadityādi
yo'nantaroktaścittotpādaḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ adhvavartināṃ guhyaṃ rahasyabhūtaṃ buddhatvaphalavāhakatvāt sārasamuccayam
evaṃ rūpādīnyupāyasvabhāvena vyavasthāpya punasteṣāmeva locanādiprajñā- svabhāvapariṇatiṃ darśayitumāha
rūpetyādi
athaveti pakṣāntaradyotako nipātaḥ
rūpādibhirviṣayairdevatāṃ prajñāṃ bhāvayet
sadā
punasteṣāmeva rūpavajrādīnāṃ kulabhedena pariṇati darśayitumāha
athaveti
kulānāṃ bhedaḥ kulabhedaḥ
sa vibhāvyate jñāyate yaistaiḥ
kulabhedabhāvanārūpa- vajrādayaḥ pañcakulīnāḥ taiḥ kulabhedavibhāvanaiḥ bhāvayet mahāvajradharamālamvayet
sarvayogo hi bhagavān vajrasattva ityādi sambare vacanāt samastāṅgam
saptamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā prapañcacaryārūpamityādi manaāyāma ... nāpāvyākṛtabhedena trividham ābhāsatrayo- dbhutarāgavirāgopekṣāprakṛtibhiḥ
viṣayabhāve cakṣuṣā vijñāyānubhūya pūjanātmakaḥ pūjanāgrahaṇasyopalakṣaṇatvāt
ayameva pūjyapūjakasvabhāvaḥ san pūjayedātmānameva
tasmādviśvaḥ sthiracalātmakaḥ sarvakulātmakaḥ buddho vairocanālaya iti
vairo- cano yasmin līyate sa vairocanālayaḥ vairocananiṣpādako bhagavān
saṃbhāradvayo buddho mahāvajradharo yaḥ sa evaṃ yogī
tathaiva triprakāraṃ śabdamanubhūya devatānāṃ prajñopāyopalabdhānāṃ nivedayet
ratnākaraḥ prabhavatyasmāditi ratnākaraḥ prabhuḥ viśvātmako mahāvajradharo yaḥ vijñānatrayātmako yogī sa eva
tathā triprakāraṃ gandhaṃ prāpya viṣayīkṛtya buddhādīnāmālokādīnāṃ nivedayet
rāgadharmadharo'mitābhaḥ mahāvajradharaḥ sa eva rasa ityādi
amoghavajro amoghasiddhiḥ tamutpādaka- svabhāvena dhārayatītyamoghavajradhṛk
mahāvajradharaḥ sa evāsparśamityādi
svakulamātmānaṃ nivedayet
akṣobhyākāramakṣobhyābhāsaṃ lambhayituṃ prasavituṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa akṣobhyākāralābhī vajrī mahāvajradharo yaḥ sa eveti
lābhīti prāpte lābhina ityārṣam
rūpetyādinānantaroktamarthamavadyotayati
rūpādiṣu sadeti
atītānāgatakṣaṇayorabhedavat vartamānakṣaṇe cittaṃ vijñānatrayaṃ niyoja- yediti
rūpādiścittamevedamiti jānīyādityabhisandhiḥ
idamevānantaroktaṃ yat parijñānaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ ratnapudgalānāṃ deyabhūtaṃ candanādīnāmaprakāśyatvāt
guhyaṃ sārasamuccayamiti samyak uddhṛtya cīyate rāśīkriyate iti samuccayam
sārāstyadhvavartinastathāgatāḥ
teṣāṃ samuccayamanantaroktanayaḥ
rūpetyādi
mantrī abhinnāṅgajāpī triprakārai rūpādibhiḥ devatāṃ vijñānatrayaṃ bhāvayet
jānīyāt
jñānatrayasyaiva prakṛtibhedaṃ darśayitumāha
athavetyādi
tatra teṣu rūpādiṣu kulāni rāgavirāgamadhyarāgaprabhṛtayaḥ
teṣāṃ bhedā viśeṣāḥ teṣāṃ vibhāvanairanubhāvanaiḥ bhāvayet jānīyāt
etaduktaṃ bhavati
rūpādi viṣayān vāhyābhiniveśaparihāreṇa mṛdvādimātrasthitarāgādiṣaṣṭyuttaraśataprakṛtisvabhāvānu- bhavanena jānīyāditi
garbho
l niṣprapañcacaryārūpamityādi praṇītāditrividharūpaṃ vijñāyeti
ābhāsatrayatvena parijñāya pūjanātmakaḥ svabhāvajñaḥ pūjayet prabhāsvarasvabhāvena pariṇāmayet
iyamevānuttarā pūjā
prabhāsvara ityukto yaḥ sa eva mahāvajradharaḥ
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane tasyotpattakāraṇatvāt
evaṃ śabdādīnāmapi prabhāsvarasvabhāvo vijñeyaḥ
yathoktaṃ vyākhyātantre
adhyātmaṃ ca tathā vāhyamubhayānupalabdhitaḥ rūpādi trividhaṃ tad syāt devatānāṃ nivedayet
iti
iyamatyantaniṣprapañcacaryā kolikam
idānīṃ ṣaḍanusmṛtirāha
buddhānusmṛtītyādi
bhāvayan kāyavākcittamiti
bhāvayata iti
prāpte kāyaṃ bhāvayataḥ śatakulaprabhedena kāyavivekaṃ kurvato buddhānusmṛtirbhavati
evaṃ vajrajāpaparyantena vācaṃ vivecayato dharmānusmṛtiḥ
prakṛtyābhāsakrameṇa cittaṃ vivecayato vajrānusmṛtiḥ kulānusmṛtiriti
kulaṃ kāyavākcittaṃ tasyānusmṛtiḥ
jñānatrayasvabhāvena pṛthakparijñānaṃ saṃyogaḥ paścādekīkaraṇam tasmāt trisaṃyogāt krodha upāyaṃ jñānaṃ tasyānusmṛtirbhāvanā nairantaryotpādaḥ
tayā krodhānusmṛtibhāvanayā devatāmahāmudrārūpaṃ tasyānusmṛtiḥ jalacandrādyākāreṇa parijñānaṃ tayā saṃyogaḥ paramārthasatyena ekīkaraṇaṃ tasmāt saṃyogāt bhāvayan satyadvayākāramālamvyan, bodhi mahāsukhaikalakṣaṇaṃ vajradharatvaṃ prāpnuyāt
ayamevārthaṃ dyotayannāha vyākhyātantre sarvabuddhoṣite ramye bhage śāntaikasaṃjñite svaṃ tattvādvayasaṃbhūtaṃ vajraṃ tatraiva yojayet
buddhānusmṛtisaṃyogaṃ bhage liṅgaṃ prakīrtitam
prakṣepādvayayogaḥ syād buddhavimvaṃ vibhāvanam
tato visphārayed dhīmān romakūpāgrasaṃsthitam
buddhameghaiḥ spharet tatra maṇḍalaistu viśeṣataḥ iti
sandhyābhāṣā
evaṃ ṣaḍanusmṛtidvāreṇa pariśuddhaṃ devatārūpaṃ niṣpādya sarvayogisādhāraṇaṃ samāpattikramaṃ darśayitumāha
ṣoḍaśābdikāmityādi
navayauvanādyalaṅkṛtāṃ yoṣitaṃ prāpya yathālabdharūpāmavikalāṅgatayā suprabhāṃ sulāvaṇyāṃ pracchanne manonukūle rūpādiviṣayalakṣaṇāṃ svātmapūjāṃ prārabhet
kaiḥ adhiṣṭhānapadai- stribhiriti adhitiṣṭhati viṣayān ityadhiṣṭhānaṃ rāgādiprakṛtitrayaṃ tasya padāni manojñādiviṣayāḥ
evamadhiṣṭhānapadaistribhiḥ viprakārairviṣayaiḥ svātmānaṃ pūjayet
saptamapaṭala paṭkoṭivyākhyā evaṃ mahāvajradharasya sparśavatrayā samāpattimuktvā vairocanādīnādarśayannāha |
tāthāgatīmityādi |
tathāgatasyeyaṃ tāthāgatī |
tatkulīnānyamudrāṇāṃ pradhānatvāt mahābhāryā |

vāśabdo'nuktasamuccuccayārthaḥ |

buddho vairocanaḥ |

romakūpāgravivarairiti romakūpāt romāgrād romavivaraṃ romadvayāntarālaṃ tasmād buddhameghād vairocanameghāt sphārayet niścārayet |

cittaṃ vajradharasya ceta
akṣobhyasya saṃvandhiścittaiśvaryaṃ pratilabhate
niṣpannakramastho manasā yadyat prārthayati tattadayatnenaiva saṃpadyata ityarthaḥ
dharmadharo'mitābhaḥ
tasya vācā tatsaṃvandhi rāgaiśvaryamavāpyate
kathaṃ pratiśrutkopamārutasvabhāvajño yat pravyāharati guhyasamājapradīpodyotane tatsarvaṃ saṃpadyate iti vāgaiśvaryaṃ tasya caramabhavikasya yuganaddhātmakasya
ihaiva janmanīti karmavaśena janmotpattiṃ niṣedhayati
dṛṣṭe dharme ca nirvāṇamityādi- vacanāt
asyaiva samādheḥ phalaviśeṣaṃ darśayitumāha
kāyavākcittetyādi
kāya- vākcittasaṃsiddhi mahāmudrāsaṃsiddhi tathā hīnāḥ mantraparāyaṇāḥ
tajjā tatsaṃbandhinyaścānyā laukikasiddhayaḥ tasya yuganaddhasamādhisthasya sidhyanti
japena abhinnāṅgajapena dṛṣṭasatyena trivajrābhedyabhāvanairiti
trivastraṃ vaidhātukam
tenābhinnam
bhāvanairabhinnākārabhāvanayā hetubhūtayā sidhyanti
yadi bhinnākāra- bhāvanā syāt tadā laukikasiddhayo'pi na sidhyanti kiṃ punarlokottarā iti
sāmarthyāduktaṃ bhavati
sarvayogo hi bhagavān vajrasattvastathāgatasyopabhogaṃ sarvaṃ vai traidhātukamaśeṣata iti vacanāt
mantracaryāpaṭala iti
mantrayanti japantīti mantrāḥ sādhakāsteṣāṃ caryā niṣprapañcābhyāsasvabhāvā
saivāgraḥ śreṣṭha udyotakaḥ śabdasamūhaḥ paṭalo mantra- caryāno nāma
yathārutam
||pradīpoddyotanaṭīkāyāṃ saptamapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
|| aṣṭamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
evaṃ caryāsthitena yoginā saṃbhāraparipuraṇārthamanuttarapūjā kartavyeti pradarśanāyāṣṭamaṃ paṭalamupakṣipannāha atha bhagavānityādi
atheti caryāśravaṇā- nantaram
ratnaketurbhagavantamadhyeṣayāmāseti saṃvandhaḥ
ratnaṃ bodhicittaṃ tadeva keturdhvajaṃ yasya sa ratnaketuḥ bodhicittābhiṣekaprārthakatvāt
bhage vyavasthitatvāt bhagavān
sarvetyādi
sarvakulātmakaṃ parameśvaramiti
paramālocanādayaḥ tāsāmīśvaraṃ prabhuṃ mahāvajradharaṃ stuvantyaneneti stotraṃ vakṣyamāṇakam
tena stottrarājenādhyeṣayāmāsārādhitavānityarthaḥ
taddarśayati vajjrarājetyādi
vajravadabhedyasvarūpeṇa rājata iti vajrarājaḥ mahāyāna iti mahāntaḥ vahavaḥ sattvā yena buddhatvaṃ yānti sa mahāyānaḥ
tvaṃ ākāśacaryāviśodhaka
ākāśaṃ paramārthasatyam
tadviśuddhā caryā ākāśacaryā
tayā yogisantānaṃ viśodhayatīti ākāśacaryāviśodhakaḥ
sarvavikalpa- prapañcāpagatā caryā samantabhadracaryā
tayāgraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ
jayantīti jinā: pratyeka- buddhādayaḥ teṣāmuttamaḥ tvaṃ pūjāṃ deśa prakāśaya
yathārutam
rāgadveṣamahāmohaśodhakaṃ vajrayānaṃ rāgadveṣamahāmohavajrayānam
tattra- karṣeṇa deśakaḥ vajrayānapradeśakaḥ
ākāśadhātutulyatvādagra:
pūjāṃ ghoṣa bhāṣasva
jinā vartinaḥ tathāgatāḥ
teṣāmālayaḥ janakatvāt
sakalavāsanāvirahitamasaṃsārapravartanaṃ jñānaṃ mokṣaḥ
tatprāpako heturmārgaḥ
tasya praṇetāraḥ deśakaḥ praṇeta iti prāpte praṇetāra ityārṣam
yāntyaneneti yānam
śrāvakādiyānattrayam
tadeva panthāḥ tatsvabhāvena pravartanāt
triyānapatha- vartako bhagavān
buddhasaubhāgyaśuddhātma iti
buddhā vairocanādayaḥ teṣu saubhāgyaṃ adhipatitvaṃ tena śuddhātma bhāṣa pūjāṃ narottama
bodhicitta viśālākṣeti
bodhisvabhāvaṃ cittaṃ yasya sa bodhicittaḥ
viśālānyakṣīni yasya sa viśālākṣaḥ dharmacakraṃ dvādaśākāraṃ tasya trailokyeṣu pravartanāt dharmacakrapravartakaḥ trivajrābhedyalakṣitatvāt
kāyavākcittasaṃśuddhaḥ vajrayāna tubhyaṃ namaḥ
neyārthaḥ
rāgadveṣamahāmoheti
jñānatrayadvāreṇa bhagavantamāmantrya stauti
rāga upāyaprakṛtiḥ
dveṣaḥ prajñāprakṛtiḥ
mahāmoho'vidyāprakṛtiḥ
vajrayāna pradeśaka iti
vajraṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tadevānantaroktaṃ prakṛtyābhāsaṃ viśodhayatīti yānam
tasya pradarśakatvād
vajrayānaṃ pradarśako bhagavān pradhānendriyamātragrāhyatvāt
ākāśadhātutulyaḥ
sarveṣāmādibhūtatvādagraḥ
ghoṣa pūjāṃ jinālaya iti
pūjāmadvayajñānātmikāṃ ghoṣa prakāśaya
jinā vairocanādayaḥ ālīyante yasmin
sa jinālayaḥ
mokṣamārgapraṇetāra iti
vajrakāyatvenāprameyadevīsamāpattyā āsaṃsāraṃ paramānandalakṣaṇena devamanuṣyādatikrāntāste guṇaiśvaryasukhasyānubhavanā sakala- sattvamocanā lesa kṣayakaraṇena mokṣaḥ, tatprāptyupāyadeśakatvāt mokṣamārga-
praṇetā bhagavān prajñopāyopalabdhistrayaṃ triyāṇaṃ sa eva panthāḥ mārgaḥ paramārtha- satyapattanasya |
tatpravarttakaḥ triyānapathavartakaḥ |
sarvasaukhyātmasādhanād buddha saubhāgya satyadvayātmakatvāt |
śuddhātmā |
evaṃ guṇaviśiṣṭa bhāṣa pūjāṃ narottama ||
bodhiḥ paramārthasatyam |
cittaṃ saṃvṛtisatyam |
bodhisaṃyuktaṃ cittaṃ bodhicittaṃ sa eva mahāvajradharaḥ |
viśālāni vistīrṇāni pañcākṣīṇi yasya sa viśālākṣaḥ tāni katamāni ?
māṃsadivyaprajñādharmabuddhākhyāni |
dharmacakrapravartaka iti |
dharmāḥ saṃskṛtā asaṃskṛtāḥ satyadvayātmakāḥ |
teṣāṃ cakraṃ samūhaḥ taṃ sattvaloke pravartayatīti dharmacakrapravartakaḥ, bhūtakoṭiviśuddhakāyavākcittātmakatvāt |
kāyavākcittasaṃśuddho bhagavān |
abhraṃśayānātmakatvād vajrayānaṃ tubhyaṃ namo'stu nītārthaḥ |
sarvākāśo mahākṣara iti |
sarvākāśaṃ sarvaśūnyaṃ tadviśuddhayā bhagavānapi sarvākāśaḥ|
mahāṃścāsāvakṣaraśceti mahākṣaraḥ |
sarveṣāmabhiṣekabhūtatvāt sarvā- bhiṣekaḥ, gaganagañjasamādhinā sarvasattvamanorathaparipūraṇāt sarvārthaḥ |
trailo- kyādhipatyāt sarveśaḥ|
vajraṃ paramārthasatyaṃ sarvāśāparipūraṇāt |
ratnamiva saṃvṛtisatyaṃ tatsvabhāvena taddhārayatīti vajjraratnadhṛk |
nāsandhyam |
pūjāṃ tāthāgatīṃ śreṣṭhāmiti |
tāthāgatāstyadhvavartino jināḥ |
tatsaṃ- vandhinīṃ pūjāṃ tāthāgatīṃ pūjāṃ śreṣṭhāmutpattimāruḍhānāmasādhāraṇatvād viśiṣṭa- tamām |
kasmādityāha |
trivajrābhedyāt svarūpeṇa saṃsthitā pūjā tām |
aṣṭamapaṭala ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā vaidhātukasya svakāyavākcittaiḥ samājīkaraṇāt kāyavākcittasaubhāgyām |
jināḥ saṃbhavantyasyā iti jinasaṃbhavā tāṃ pūjāṃ bhāṣate sma lokaḥ |
sandhyā- bhāṣā |
tāmeva darśayannāha |
prāpyetyādi |
kamanīyarūpatvāt kanyā |
viśāle satyadvayātmake akṣiṇī yasyā mahāmudrāyāḥ tāṃ viśālākṣīm |
tasyā eva viśeṣaṇaṃ rūpayauvanamaṇḍiteti |
pañcaviṃśatikāṃ gṛhyeti |
pañcaviṃśatidevatātmikāṃ mahāmudrāṃ svādhiṣṭhānalakṣaṇāṃ tāṃ prāpya gurumukhā [t] labdhvā tāmevānuttarapūjāṃ prakalpayet kuryāt |
tiryagbhyo'pi gṛhya prakalpayediti punaryojyam |
tiryak- śabdasya pārśvacāritvāt |
tenādhipateranyanmāṇḍaleyadevatārūpaṃ mahāvajradharavat gṛhyate mahāvajradharavikurvītatvāt |
sandhyābhāṣā |
lokottaratamayogināṃ pūjāṃ nirdiśyedānīmutpattikramayogināṃ pūjāṃ darśayitu- māha |
śucāvityādi |
vāśabdo bhinnakramaḥ pakṣāntarapradarśanārtho draṣṭavyaḥ |
sarvabhūmiśodhanakrameṇa pariśuddhe vivikte janasaṃpātarahite pṛthivīpradeśe bhūbhāge |
jinātmajo mantrī |
śāntāḥ sajjanāḥ teṣāṃ gṛhe śivālaye devatākule īdṛgvidhe pradeśe |
viṭ goviṣṭhā mūtraṃ gomūtraṃ toyaṃ kevalamudakaṃ ādiśabdena gandhakalkā- di gṛhyate |
ebhirvaḍādibhiḥ pūrvokte pradeśe vilepanaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kurvīta kuryāt |
vāśabdo'nuktasamuccayārthaḥ |
tena puṣpādiparigrahaḥ |
śaśvat pratyahaṃ kimartham ?
jinapūjahetoḥ svadevatārādhanārtham |
evaṃ pūjopakaraṇaṃ saṃpādya sādhanoktakrameṇa kṛtayogānuyogasyātmanaḥ svamudrā- yāśca pañcakulīkaraṇaṃ darśayannāha |
stanāntaramityādi |
stanāntaramiti prāpte hṛdayasthāne |
yāvaditi |
yāvadatra hūṃkārajamakṣobhyakulaṃ nyaset |
śikhāntamiti śikhāśabdena keśā upalakṣyante yāvat keśāntam |
keśabhūmau oṃkārajaṃ vairocanaṃ nyaset |
madhya iti hṛtśikhāntayormadhye kiṃ tat kaṇṭhapradeśe mukhamaṇḍalaṃ tatra āḥkāraṃ saṃbhavamamitābhaṃ nyaset |
caraṇāntaramiti yāvat |
padasyorumūlasya cāntaraṃ caraṇāntaram |
tasmiṃścaraṇāntare|
vāśabdaścaraṇadvaye iti samuccayārthaḥ |
tatna hākārajamamoghasiddhiṃ nyaset |
vidhijñaḥ utpattikramajñaḥ |
nābhikaṭīguhya ityatra saptamīlopaḥ |
eṣu pradeśeṣu svakārajaṃ ratnasaṃbhavakulaṃ nyaset |
jināḥ vairocanādayaḥ |
jinātmajāstatkulīnāḥ jināḥ svajinātmajāśceti jinātmajāḥ guhya samājapradīpodyotane teṣāṃ vairocanādīnāṃ tatkulīnānāṃ ca sādhanoktavidhinā pañcakulātmakānāṃ nyāsaṃ yathāyathaṃ kuryāt iti yāvat |
evaṃ kṛtaṃ samāpatteḥ bhavotpattikramaṃ darśayitumāha |
ākāśadhātu ityādi |
ākāśadhātuḥ svamudrādharmodayaḥ |
tanmadhyasyaṃ jñānasāgaraṃ bodhicittamātmānaṃ candramadhyasthamanantaroktasaṃvṛtibodhicittamadhyasthaṃ bhāvayet dhyāyāt |
tadanantaraṃ tathāgatāṃ spharitvā budhaḥ śāntavastradhṛk |
hṛdaye ātmani saṃhāraṃ ca prakurvīta |
yadīti |
spharaṇasaṃharaṇaṃ yadīcchā syāt, tadā kuryāditi bhāvaḥ |
śāntaṃ cittasamādhānaṃ tadeva vajraṃ taddhāraṇāt śāntavastradhṛk |
evamātmānaṃ niṣpādya punaḥ stūpaṃ ca pañcabhūratnamayādikaṃ kuryāt |
catūratnaṃ dhātusvabhāvaṃ locanādicatuṣṭayaṃ tanmayatvāt |
catūratnamayaṃ raśmimālā sitādiraśmimālā tābhirbhuṣitaṃ śobhitam ||
jñānodadhīti |
vyadhvebhya ityasya viśeṣyarūpaṃ jñānodadhayastathāgatāḥ vyadhvāstraikālyavartinaḥ vyadhvavati tathāgatānāmālayaṃ kṣetrabhūtaṃ cintayet |
svakāye caityaṃ bhāvayet |
pūrvavat svaromakūpavivarebhyaḥ pūjāmeghāṃ yathābhilaṣitārtha- saṃpādanena sattvadhātūn pūjayantyanugṛhṇantīti pūjā: tathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ meghāḥ sandohāstān spharet niścārayet |
buddho buddhānutpattikramanipuṇaḥ |
yathārutam ||
idānīmubhayayogitādhāraṇaṃ pūjāṅgaṃ darśayannāha |
padyamityādi |
śvetādi- pañcavarṇatvāt pañcavidhaṃ kulabhedena jñātvā vicakṣaṇaḥ vibhāgajñaḥ |
utpalaṃ ca tathaiva pañcavarṇam |
jātipuṣpaṃ trividhāṃ trivarṇāṃ kṛtvā devatānāṃ nivedayet |
tathaiva kaṇikārapuṣpaṃ mallikāṃ yūthikāṃ karavīrakusumaṃ ca dhvātvā dhyāna- valena niṣpādya mantrī sarvathā tathāgatān yojayediti |
neyārthaḥ |
ārṣavyākhyānamāha |
ye daśavāyavaḥ proktāste padmotpalasaṃjñakāḥ |
trivajratattvamākhyātaṃ jātyekatā vidhā matā ||
kaṇikāraṃ tathā mallikā yūthikā karavīrakam |
locanādi yathāsaṃkhyaṃ māhendrādīṃ tu yojayet ||
niḥsvabhāvapadanyāsād devatārūpayogataḥ |
sā bhavenmahatī pūjā sarvabudvaikatoṣaṇe ||
tadetatpuṣpasandhyeyaṃ na pratipatsyantītare janāḥ |
anavabodhatasteṣāṃ siddhiḥ syād dūrataḥ sthitā ||
abhavyā vayamityevamuktvātmānaṃ kṣipanti te |
tathā hyātmapratikṣepāt sarvabuddhān kṣipanti hi ||
kṣiptvā tu sarvabuddhān vai nirvāṇataragāminaḥ ||
iti nītārthaḥ ||
idānīmubhayayogisādhāraṇaṃ guhyābhiṣekamaṇḍalaṃ darśayitumāha |
yojanetyādi |
pratipārśva pañcaviṃśati yojanatvāt yojanaśatavistāraṃ cakraśabdenopacārācca- kram |
vairocana evābhimataḥ |
tasya maṇḍalaṃ cakramaṇḍalaṃ bhāvayet niṣpādayet |
manasā likhanena vā |
caturasramityādi vakṣyamāṇakamalāpi yojanīyam |
kulānāmakṣobhyādikulānāṃ pūrvādidikṣu nyāsaṃ kuryāt |
sadā vicakṣaṇaḥ sarvakālā- pramattaḥ|
yathā cakramaṇḍalaśabdena vairocanamaṇḍalamupāttaṃ tathā padmamamitābha- maṇḍalam |
candramaṇḍalamakṣobhyamaṇḍalam |
khaḍgamaṇḍalamamoghasiddhimaṇḍalam |
utpalamupalaratnaṃ takāracchandonurodhārthaḥ daśadiśīti |
yathā utpalaṃ ratna- saṃbhavamaṇḍalaṃ budhaḥ vijño bhāvayet śatayojanāt prabhṛti yāvat |
koṭiyojana- pramāṇaṃ mahecchānāṃ rucivaśānmaṇḍalaṃ caturvarṇopetatvāt caturatnamayaṃ svacchaṃ sphaṭikavat |
anena śvetavarṇaparigrahaḥ |
caityamiva caityaṃ maṇḍalaṃ prakṛtinirmalamiti |
maṇḍalatattvātmakatvāt |
kulānāṃ pañcakulānāmālayamādhārabhūtaṃ prājño guhyā- bhiṣekajño yogī pūjāhetunā guhyābhiṣekārthaṃ bhāvayediti yāvat |
evaṃ guruvyāpārānantaraṃ guhyābhiṣekakāmena śiṣyeṇa yadanuṣṭheyaṃ taddarśayannāha |
pañvakāmaguṇairityādi |
pañcakāmaguṇarūpādi pañcaviṣayā: teṣāmiti maṇḍalā - dheyabhūtadevatānāṃ pūjodadhiṃ vividhapūjāsamudraṃ samārabhet kartumupakramet |
tānyeva pañcakāmaguṇān prapañcayitumāha |
ratnetyādi |
ratnaṃ padmarāgādi
vastraṃ pattrorṇādikaṃ cakṣurindriyaviṣayamuktvā ādiśabdena śrotādīndriyānāṃ viṣayaṃ vidadhāti
nityaṃ yāvadabhiṣekaparisamāpti pratyahaṃ pūjayedārādhayet
kimartham ?
sarvakalimalaprakṣālanena jñānodayakāraṇād bodhirabhiṣekaḥ tasmin kāṃkṣayā cchndena pūjayet
// pañcopahārāḥ puṣpādayaḥ
taiḥ pūjā pañcopahārapūjā
tāsāmagraiviśiṣṭa- tarairdevatāṃ ca guruṃ [ca] toṣayet
sadeti
devatāgurusādhāraṇīṃ pūjāmuktvā guhyasamājapradīpodyotane gurorviśeṣapūjāṃ kanyāmityādinā darśayati
kanyāmabhraṣṭayauvanāṃ ratnamāsaṃ- sārasthāyi paramānāsravamahāsukhaṃ tasyotpattikṣetratvād ratnakarī śreṣṭhāṃ padminīṃ sarvastrīguṇopetatvāt
anekaprakāraratnavastragandhavilepanādyalaṅkṛtām īdṛgvidhāṃ kanyāṃ kamanīyarūpāṃ sarvabuddhānāmiti guroḥ sarvabuddhamayatvāt
gurureko'pi sarvabuddhaśabdena vivakṣitaḥ
tasmai dadyāt pratipādayet
kasmā- disyāha sidhyate'tīvasādhane iti
sādhyate niṣpādyate iti sādhanaṃ sāmānya- phalam
atīvasādhanaṃ viśiṣṭasādhanam
kiṃ tat ?
buddhatvaṃ tasmin siddhaye siddhinimittaṃ dadyāt
guruviṣaye deyadharmapratipattaye paricchedābhāvaṃ darśayitu- māha
saptaratnetyādi
padmarāgendranīlavaidūryamarakatavajjramuktāpravālamiti saptaratnāni
taiḥ paripūrṇamidaṃ kṣetraṃ pratidinaṃ dadyāt
tāḥ ( ? ) strī puruṣa - maṇicakrakhaṅgagajāśvaratnairvā vicakṣaṇaḥ puṇyasaṃbhārajñānakuśalaḥ
pratyahaṃ prājñaḥ prakarṣajñānavān jñānārthī gurusakāśāddvayajñānapratiṣedhaṃ kartukāmaḥ
tadupadeśena mahāmudrāsiddhisādhanakāṅakṣayā dadyāt
prājño dhyānenāpi sarvaṃ sampādayaditi
I evaṃ saṃpāditapūjopakaraṇe śiṣye guruḥ kiṃ kuryādityāha
adhipatītyādi
| yathā svamaṇḍalādhipateryā mahāmudrā tāṃ samādāya gṛhītvā maṇḍalācāryo buddhamaṇḍalamadhyataḥ tathāgatacakramadhye
rāgo mahāsukhaṃ tatra buddhiryeṣāṃ te rāgabuddhayaḥ
teṣāṃ rāgabuddhīnāṃ buddhānāṃ tathāgatānāṃ sparśasaṃyogaṃ sparśavatrājanitasukhaṃ dadyāt
tat kathamityāha
ākāśetyādi
ākāśadhātu gaganaṃ tanmadhyasthaṃ vyūhaṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhāvayet dhyāyāt
dhyātvā rūpaskandhādibhāvena vairocanādijñānamayadehe nirmāṇakāyagrahaṇārthaṃ praveśayet
tataḥ svavimbaṃ mahāvajradharasvarūpamātmadehaṃ tathāgatamayaṃ vairocanādisarva- tathāgataparamāṇuparighaṭitaṃ kṛtvā viḍiti viṣayā mindriyaṃ viṣayai rūpādibhiḥ guruṃ mānyaṃ pūjayet prīṇayet
tāmeva pūjāṃ yoṣitamityādinā darśayati
yoṣitaṃ śiṣyapratipāditāṃ cāruvaktrāṃ harṣeṇa prasannacetanāṃ manohlā- danakaratvāt manoramām
I adhiṣṭhānapadaṃ dhyātveti
adhitiṣṭhantītyadhiṣṭhānāni vairocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ padaṃ praṇavādivījān mudrādehe yathāsthānaṃ dhyātvā vinyasya pañcakulakalāpinīṃ aṣṭama paṭala ṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā kṛtvā tattvapūjāṃ samāpattilakṣaṇāṃ prakalyayet adhitiṣṭhet
vāhyapūjāpekṣayā iyaṃ [de]tratattvapūjā rāgavirāgamadhyarāgānukrameṇa kleśaviśuddhipadasākṣātkaraṇāt
śukre guhyaviśālākṣyāmiti
samāpattipūjānantaraṃ viśālākṣyāḥ śukraṃ gṛhya striyāṃ mudrāpadmasthitaṃ bodhicittaṃ bodhicalitādhyāśayatvāt
dṛḍhabuddhimān vajraśiṣyaḥ guruṇā dattamanena svakāyasthānasarvatathāgatāntaryāmīti bhakṣayet abhyavaharediti
guruśca teneva bodhicittena saṃkhyādisaṃsthitaṃ gandhodraka- mamoghamaṅgalagāthādipūrvakaṃ śiṣyamabhiṣiñcet
yathoktaṃ bhagavatā guhyendra- tilake
utsṛjya ratnojvalapadmamadhyataḥ saṃguhya mūrtiḥ sakalaṃ jinānām
abhiṣiñcaya mūrdhnāmalaratnavarṣai- viśuddhavajrodbhavajñānatoyaiḥ
|| iti
idaṃ taditi
yadanantaroktaṃ pūjāvyapadeśena guhyābhiṣecanam, tatsarva- mantrāṇāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ saṃvandhibhūtaṃ tajjñānavajriṇāṃ ghaṭamānayogināṃ mantra- siddhikaraṃ mantrālaukikasiddhipradāyakāḥ ālikālijanitāḥ sarpādayo mantrāḥ
teṣāṃ siddhiravikalasiddhisaṃpādanaṃ tatra karotīti mantrasiddhikaraṃ kriyātantrādi- nayapravṛttānāṃ gopyatvād rahasyam
avinaśvarakāyavākcittanivartanāt kāya- vākcittapūjanamiti
yathārutam
|| pradīpoddyotanaṭīkāyāmaṣṭamapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
|| navamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
nivartatābhiṣekasyādbhuta karaṇenāpi bodhisādhanamāha
| athetyādi
atha guhyābhiṣekānantaraṃ caturvyomātmakatayā sarvākāśo bhagavān
ataeva mahākṣaraḥ
sarveṣāṃ kriyātantrādiprapannānāmabhiṣekacaryā sarvābhiṣekacaryā tāsā- muparisthitatvāt sarvābhiṣekacaryāgraḥ
sarvaiḥ siddhikāmairarthyante yācyante iti sarvārthā vairocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ parameśvaraḥ ṣaṣṭhastathāgataḥ
evaṃvidho bhagavān trivajrābhedyamaṇḍalaṃ ghoṣata iti saṃvandhaḥ
kāyavākcittasaṃbhoga iti
ātmanaḥ kāyavākcittānāṃ saṃbhoga upabhogo yasmin maṇḍale tat kāyavākcittasaṃbhogaṃ trivajrābhedyā vairocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ maṇḍalamatyadbhutamaṇḍalaṃ paramaṃ viśiṣṭataraṃ ratnapudgalānāmeva viṣayatvād rahasyam aprakāśyam
keṣām ?
buddhajñānināṃ śrāvakādīnāṃ tattnāsakaratvāt
evaṃvidhalokaviruddhālāpadvāreṇa bodhiprāptyupāyaṃ yo bhāṣate smeti prāpte yathārutam
ākāśadhātumadhyasthaṃ buddhamaṇḍalaṃ mahāvajradha[ra] vimvamakṣobhyavajraṃ prabhāvitveti tatparāvṛttyā akṣobhyaṃ niṣpādya pāṇau vajraṃ vibhāvayediti pradhāna- cihnakathanam
nānyeṣāṃ cihnānāṃ niṣedhaḥ
sphuliṅgāni viprucaḥ vyatigahanaṃ ghanībhūtam ataeva dīptaṃ sitādi- raśmibhirāplutaṃ vajraṃ bhāvayet
anena traikālyasaṃbhūtatathāgatān dhyātvā cūrṇayet
teṣāṃ kāyavākcittānāṃ saṃbhogaḥ samūhaḥ taṃ vajreṇa cūrṇitaṃ naṣṭamastaṅgataṃ bhāvayet
etadanantaroktaṃ paramaṃ dhyānaṃ cittasiddhisamāvahaṃ cittaiśvaryāvāham
anenaiva guhyavajreṇa sarvasattvān vighātayed vināśayet
anena dhyāna- viśeṣeṇa vinaṣṭā akṣobhyavajrasya buddhakṣetre jinaurasā bodhisattvā jāyante
ayaṃ dveṣakulatattvasamayaḥ
atrāha, yadyekasyāṃ nivṛttāyāṃ vahavaḥ santi nirvṛtāḥ
sa mahāpuṇyarāśiḥ syādanyathā pāpakaṃ param
iti
navamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā athetyādinā mohakulasamayamāha
sa eva mahāvajradharaḥ ajñānaṃ moha avidyā
tena mokṣaprasādhako bhūtakoṭiprāpakaḥ jñānamokṣaprasādhakaḥ svabhāvaśuddhaḥ prakṛtiśuddhaḥ
puṇyāpuṇyābhyāmalepyatvāt nirlepaḥ
sattvasantānagatamātsarya- kleśāpanayanātmikā caryā bodhicaryā
tāṃ prakarṣeṇa deśayatīti bodhicaryā- pradeśakaḥ
buddhabodhiniṣpādakaṃ tattvamaviparītaṃ dravyākarṣaṇasamayaṃ bhāṣate
ākāśe- tyādi gatārthaḥ
vajramaṇḍalaṃ mahāvajradharamahāmudrām
tatparāvṛtyā vairocanamātmānaṃ bhāvayitvā sarvabuddhān māṇḍaleyān vibhāva- yet utsṛjet
kathamityāha
sarvaratnaprayogeṇeti
sarvaratnaṃ sitādivarṇopetaṃ ratnajātaṃ tadarthaḥ prayogaḥ sarvaratnaprayogaḥ
tena vajravimvānakṣobhyāditathāgatān vibhāvayet prerayet
yad ratnaṃ yena yena tathāgatena savarṇaṃ taduddharaṇārthaṃ taṃ taṃ prerayediti
evaṃ tathāgatān saṃprerya sādhakena yadanuṣṭheyaṃ tad darśayati
haraṇamityādi
haraṇaṃ tathāgatairdravyākarṣaṇaṃ trivajreṇeti kāyena vācā manasā ca vibhāvayet
tadyathā romakūpavivaranirgata sitādiraśmiyogairabhimataratnajātamāvadhya niśca- līkṛtya tataḥ svajihvābjadalavinirgata hrīḥ kārāṅakuśenākṛṣya svagṛhe sthāpitam
manasā dhyāyāditi bhagavannityādinā
mātsaryadoṣakaluṣitasantānānāṃ viśuddhimāha
cintāmaṇirabhilaṣitārthapradāyako maṇiviśeṣaḥ
tatsamā dravyodadhirdravyasamudro'- kṣayatvāt
tena prapūritāḥ prītā bhavanti ye dravyapatayaḥ
na kevalaṃ laukika- sampatsampannā bhavanti
aurasā ityādi
urasijātā aurasāḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ pradhānaputrā bhavanti
paścāt tathāgatāśca bhavanti
sarvakulārṇave akṣobhyādikulārṇavamadhye moha- kulasya vairocanasaṃvandhidevatācakrasya tattvaṃ rahasyaṃ ayamevālaṅghayatvāt samayaḥ
ata āha bhagavān śrīvairocanābhisaṃbodhicaryātantre iha bodhisattvaḥ sarveṇa sarvamadattvādānāt prati virato bhavati anyatra matsariṇa iti
athetyādi
rāgakulasamayāvatāraḥ sa eva bhagavān svakārārpaṇena vijñānaṃ rañjayatīti rūpādayo rāgāntairmumukṣūṇāṃ mokṣaṃ sarvaduḥkhopaśamanalakṣaṇaṃ prasā- dhayatīti rāgamokṣaprasādhakaḥ
guhyaśuddhaḥ satyadvayātmakaḥ
apratiṣṭhitanirvāṇa- sthitatvānnirālamvaḥ amitābhaḥ samayacakramudghoṣayati kathayati
ākāśetyādi
padmamaṇḍalaṃ mahāvajradharavimvam amitābhotpādakatvāt
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane upacāreṇa pūrvavat
mahāvajradharaparāvṛttyā amitābhaṃ dhyātvā buddhairmāṇḍaleya- devatābhiḥ sarvakūṭāgārodaraṃ pūrayet
yathāsthānaṃ spharet
tatra maṇḍale sarveṣāṃ māṇḍaleyānāṃ strīpuruṣākārāṇāṃ yoṣidākārasaṃyogaṃ vibhāvayet dhyāyāt
yoṣidākāraḥ styākāraḥ
tena saṃyogaḥ saṃyojanaṃ strī- rūpākāreṇa pariṇāmanam
kathamityāha
catuḥ samayayogeneti
catuḥsamayaścatu- ryogaḥ tena yogo devātākāraniṣpādanaṃ catuḥsamayayogaḥ
catuḥsamayayogena yoṣidākāraṃ bhāvayediti yojanīyam
idamanantaroktaṃ dhyānaṃ vajranayeti mahāyāne kāmaiśvaryapradhānatvāduttamam- evaṃ dhyātvā kimityāha
dvayendriyaprayogeṇānurāgaṇādipūrvakaṃ vajra- padmasamāyogena sarvāṃstān viśvaramaṇakriyayā svaramaṇakriyayā ekakṣaṇamupabhuñjayet kāmayet
idamanantaroktaṃ yatsarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvasādhakānāṃ trikāyaṃ bhedyasādhana- miti dharmasaṃbhoganirmāṇarūpa trikāyābhedyatvasādhanaṃ niṣpādakaṃ dhyānam
ayaṃ rāgakule samayaḥ karaṇīyaḥ
mantriṇotpattikarmārūḍhena sādhanīyo'nuṣṭhātavyaḥ
oṃśabdo'vadhāraṇe
sādhanīya eveti
athetyādi
amoghasiddhiḥ samayāvatāraḥ sa eva bhagavān vajramantraḥ praveśādilakṣaṇo vajrajāpaḥ
tadupadeśena vineyānāmabhipretārthaṃ sādhayatīti vajramantrārthasādhakaḥ
jñānaṃ dharmakāyaḥ tasmād vyutthitaḥ jñānasaṃbhūtaḥ
atha ca māyopamadehatvāt nairātmyaḥ nirgata ātmā yasya sa nirātmā
nirātmā eva nairātmyaḥ mahāvajradharaḥ idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ vacanamabravīditi saṅgītikāravacanam
ākāśetyādi
buddhamaṇḍalaṃ mahāvajradharaṃ dhyāyāt
tatparāvṛttyā vajrā- moghasiddhi bhāvayitvā tadanantaraṃ sarvabuddhān māṇḍaleyān bhāvayet
mṛṣā vāgvajrapadamiti
mṛṣā sarvadharmāḥ māyopamatvādityasya vajra- padasyārthe sthitvā sarvavimvān sarvasattvān tattraiva sthitān vibhāvayed vijā- nīyāt
tataḥ kiṃ kuryādityāha
visaṃvādayedityādi
jinān sarvān
jinā - nāmālayā jinālayāḥ sattvāḥ tathāgatagarbhatvāt
sarve ca te jinālayāśca sarvajinālayāḥ
tāśca visaṃvādayet sarvatathāgatān sarvasattvāṃśca sarvadharmā- visaṃvādakā māyāsvabhāvā iti vacanamuccāyato dhyāyāditi yāvat
tathā coktam
navamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā mṛṣā māyā devaputra sarvadharmāḥ
tathaivāhaṃ prakāśayāmi tenāhaṃ mṛṣāvādīti
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane cūrṇayitvā tu tāṃ samyak cittasiddhirnayet katham
atītā upadeṣṭāraḥ vartamānānuśāsakāḥ
ārādhyāgāmikāḥ buddhāḥ samyaksaṃbodhidāyakāḥ
hanyāste vai kathaṃ nātha vajreṇākṣobhyarūpataḥ
kathaṃ te pratipatsyanti vākyaṃ nānārthatattvataḥ
bhāṣasva bhagavan tattvaṃ āgāmijanitāṃ prati
yajjñātvā nirvikalpāḥ syuḥ sandhyāyārthaṃ jinaurasāḥ
atha vajradharoktaṃ tu śrutvā vāṇīṃ jagaddhitām
pratyuvāca tataḥ svāmī bhūyo vajradharaṃ vibhum
sādhu vatsa mahāsādhu sādhu samantabhadrakaḥ
idamanantaroktaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvatathāgatāyattaṃ vāgākāśamiva niḥsvabhāvatvāt vāgākāśaṃ sunirmalam
etanmantrasiddhikaraṃ proktaṃ rahasyaṃ jñānabuddhināmiti advayajñānī buddhiradhimokṣo yeṣāṃ teṣāṃ jñānabuddhīnāṃ ghaṭamānānāṃ mantrasiddhikaram
susamayena sarvasattvānāmākarṣaṇaṃ yat tat samayākarṣaṇakulama moghasiddhikulaṃ tasya tattvasamayaḥ yathābhūtasamayaḥ preraṇīyaḥ
punaḥ punarabhyasanīyaḥ yathārthataḥ yāvat
atrāha
pareṣāṃ ca sukhārthāya ācaryā sukhahetunā
ratnatrayopakārāya mṛṣāvādaṃ vadediti
athetyādi
ratnasaṃbhavasamayāvatāraḥ sa eva bhagavān trayāṇāṃ kāyādi- vajrāṇāṃ samāhāraḥ
trivajraṃ bhidyate pṛthak pṛthak kriyate iti bhedyaṃ na bhedyamabhedyam
trivajjrābhedyamapṛthagrūpaṃ yasya sa trivajrābhedyaḥ jināstyadhvavartino buddhāsteṣā- mālayaḥ utpattisthānatvāt
trivajrābhedyaśca jinālayaścāsau trivajrābhedya- jinālayaḥ
siddhiḥ vajradharatvam tadevābhedyatvād vajraṃ tatprakarṣeṇa prāpayatīti siddhivajrapraṇetāra ityārṣam
idaṃ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ vākyamudīritavān
ākāśetyādi vibhaktārthaḥ
samayamaṇḍalaṃ mahāvajradharamahāmudrā tatparā- vṛttyā ratnaketumātmānaṃ bhāvayitvā sarvavimbairidaṃ spharet iti
sarvavimbaiḥ pārṣadīyaiḥ
idaṃ kūṭāgārodaraṃ spharet pūrayet
pāruṣyavacanādyairiti
paruṣaṃ niṣṭhuraṃ paruṣameva pāruṣyaṃ pāruṣyaṃ ca tadvacanaṃ ca pāruṣyavacanam
tadādyairvacobhistadupāyena sarvasattvasaṃgrahārthamāvirbhūtaiḥ sevayan mahāvajradharamārādhayan advayajñānamāpnuyāt
ityāha bhagavānityādinā samayamupasaṃharati saṃgītikārakaḥ ityanena prakāreṇoktavān sarvatathāgatavajravyūho mahāvajradharaḥ
neyārthaḥ
`ārṣavyākhyāna- māha
atha sandhyāya dharmaikaṃ sarvajñaṃ sarvavitprabhum
vajrapāṇirvadennāthaṃ samayatattvārthabhāṣaṇam
tathataikasvabhāvā ca ye buddhāstryadhvasambhavāḥ
dharmakāyaikaniryātā kathaṃ vajreṇa cūrṇitāḥ
cūrṇayitvā tu tāṃ samyak cittasiddhirnayet katham
atītā upadeṣṭāraḥ vartamānānuśāsakāḥ
ārādhyāgāmikāḥ buddhāḥ samyaksaṃbodhidāyakāḥ
hanyāste vai kathaṃ nātha vajreṇākṣobhyarūpataḥ
adhyeśayāsamāṃ yastvaṃ tamahaṃ prativakṣyāmi sandhyāyabhāṣitāṃ prati
bhāvitārthaṃ yathāyatham
akṣobhya iti nirmāṇaṃ vajraṃ tatsyādabhedataḥ
savidyā tasya pāṇi syāt tatra vajraṃ prabhāsvaram
tasmāj jñānamayaḥ kāyaḥ pañcaraśmisamujvalaḥ
buddhāḥ skandhādayaḥ proktāstyadhvajñāste vivajratā
cūrṇanaṃ dehavajreṇa prākṛtaskandhanāśanam
etadddveṣakulaṃ ramyamakṣobhyaṃ jñānavajrajam
prāpnuvanti mahāsiddhi tatkṣetre jātiruttamā
atha mahāsukhaḥ śrīmān jñānamokṣaprasādhakaḥ
prakṛtiśuddhanirlepo bodhicaryāprasādhakaḥ |
āmantrya sarvaguhyeśaṃ vajrapāṇi mahādyutim |
bhāṣate samayaṃ tattvaṃ samyaksaṃbuddhasādhanam ||
dharmaratnapraveśena vajrasattvaṃ prabhāvayet |
vaipākikānāṃ haraṇaṃ tena kāyena sarvathā ||
sarvadravyāpaharaṇaṃ buddhabodhiprasādhakam |
cintāmaṇiḥ samastena dravyodadhiḥ prapūritaḥ ||
mohakulasya tattvo'yaṃ samayaḥ parikīrtitaḥ |
māyādeho'mitāyuḥ syāttatra skandhādiprapūraṇam ||
navamapaṭalapaṭkoṭivyākhyā prajñāpāramitāyuktān skandhādīn tatra kārayet |
catuḥśūnyasamāyogāt kuryād bodhinayottamam ||
saṃvṛttiḥ paramārthaśca dvayendriyamihendanāt |
yuganaddhasamākhyātastatprayogastadadvayam ||
tatsamāpattiyogena sarvāṃstānupabhuñjayet |
idaṃ tatsarvabuddhānāṃ mahārāgamayottamam ||
atha bhagavān viśvaḥ trivajrābhedyajinātmakaḥ |
vajrasiddhipraṇetā vacanamidamabravīt ||
pāruṣyādiyadaśrāvya skandhādyabhāvavācakaḥ |
tadarthamabhyased yogī laghu bodhimavāpnuyāt ||
iti nītārthaḥ ||
mahāvajradharaparṣanmadhyasthita mahābodhisattvā anantaroktasamādhiviśeṣa- dyotakavajrapadānāṃ niścayārthamajānānā ivānāgatapadmādimṛdvindriyāṇāṃ sattvānāṃ hitāya yathā codayanti taddarśayituṃ saṃgītikṛdathetyāha atha khalviti |
pañcamasamayaprakāśanānantaraṃ sarvatathāgatasamatāṃ yātīti sarvatathāgatasamayaḥ
ko'sau vajjraketuḥ vajracihnatvāt vajrapāṇiḥ tatpramukhāḥ tatpuraḥsarā bodhisattvā lokaviruddhavācoyuktyā samādhiviśeṣaprakāśanādāścaryamadbhutaprāptāḥ idaṃ vakṣyā- māṇarūpaṃ vajjraghoṣamapratihatarutamakārṣuḥ kṛtavantaḥ
svalakṣaṇa- kimityādinā parṣadgatatathāgatapṛcchāvyājena bhagavantameva yathā paripṛcchanti taddarśayitumāha kimiti praśne |
ayaṃ bhagavān mahāvajradharaḥ sarvatathā- tathāgatasvāmī traidhātukavyativṛttaṃ vajrapadaṃ bhāṣata iti saṃbandha:
dhāraṇād dhātuḥ
pravicayalakṣaṇā prajñā
sā tridhā mṛdumadhyādhimātrabhedāt traidhātukaṃ viśeṣeṇātivartate vyatikrāmati nānusaratīti vaidhātukavyativṛttam
ataeva sarvalokadhātuvyativṛttaṃ sarvalokadhātūnāṃ mṛdvādibhedena sthitatvāt
sarvatathāgatasarvabodhisattvaparṣanmadhye agneḥ śaityamivādbhutamapūrvam
abhūta- majātamityadbhum
taddyotikā vāk adbhutavāk
tadeva panthāḥ pravartakatvāt
vajramabhedyaṃ ādeyatvāt
tasmādeva padaṃ pratiṣṭhā sarveṣāṃ tathāgatavacanānu- sāritvāt
adbhūtavākpathaḥvajrapadam
parṣaddvayamadhye kaukṛtyajanakam
kimiti bhāṣate na vaktavyamiti yāvat
anantaroktabodhisattvabhaṇitamākarṇya bhagavatparṣatsannipatitatathāgatā bhagavantaḥ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane abhiprāyamabhilakṣya praśnaṃ yathā visarjayanti saṃgītikṛt taddarśanāya pīṭhikāṃ racayitumāha athetyādi
bhagavanta evamāhuriti saṃvandhaḥ
tān pūrvoktā- nabhilāpyetyādi vibhaktārthaḥ
mā kulaputretyādi parihāraḥ
mā pratiṣedhe kulaputra ityāmantraṇaṃ kulaṃ vajrakulādikaṃ tatra jātāḥ kulaputrāḥ
kulaputtraivamityārṣam
evāmanantaroktaṃ samayavicikitsāmukhena hīnaṃ nikṛṣṭamiti saṃjñā hīnasaṃjñā
jugupsitāṃ loka- garhitamiti saṃjñā jugupsitasaṃjñā tāṃ dvividhāmapi saṃjñāṃ notpādayata mā kuruta
hīnasaṃjñādyutpādena doṣaprakaśanāyāha tat kasmāddhetoriti kena kāraṇe- naivaṃ na kartavyamityata āha
agracaryetyādi
yaduta yā bodhisattvacaryā sā agracaryā śrāvakādicaryāpekṣayā agracaryā
kā bodhisattvacaryā ?
lokaviruddhenā- viruddhena vā yena prakāreṇa sattvārthaḥ sampadyate, tena tena prakāreṇa mantriṇāṃ yathāśaktyā pravṛttiḥ bodhisattvacaryā
yaduta yā mantracaryā sā tathāgatacaryā
guravo yebhyaḥ tattvaṃ mantraṃ kathayanti te mantrā ratnapudgalāḥ
teṣāṃ caryā niṣpanna- kramamadhigamya yadasamāhitayogena caraṇaṃ seyaṃ tathāgatacaryā
ihaiva janmani svarūpaparivartanāt
evaṃ caryādvayasya nirdeśaṃ pratipādya satyadvayavibhāgena tatsādhanamāha
tad yathetyādi
tad yathāpināmeti nipātasamudāyo yathetyasminnarthe pravartate
ākāśaṃ paramārthasatyam
sarvatrānugataṃ sarveṣu pratītyasamutpanneṣu dharmeṣvanugataṃ vyāptam
sarvadharmāṇi skandhadhātvāyatanāni ākāśānugatāni dharmadharmatayora- bhinnatvāt
na kāmadhātumāsthitānītyādināmumevārthaṃ vivṛṇoti
rūpādīni kāmayanti prārthayantīti kāmāḥ cāturmahārājakāyikādayo devāḥ
teṣāṃ vāsanād [?] vāsturbhavanaṃ tāni skandhādīni
tatrāpi na sthitāni
kāma- dhātorapi paramārthasatyagatatvāt
yasmin bhave rūparāgamātraṃ sa rūpadhātuḥ yasmin bhave rūpavairāgyeṇa vijñaptimātraṃ sa ārūpyadhātuḥ
tayorapi na sthitāni
na caturmahābhūtasthitānīti vāyumaṇḍalādikrameṇa sthitaṃ yanmaṇḍalacatuṣṭayaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamamādhārabhūtaṃ taccaturmahābhūtaṃ tatrāpi na sthitāni
sarveṣāṃ prabhāsvara- mayatvāt
evamevānenaiva prakāreṇa sarvadharmāḥ skandhādayaḥ anugantavyāḥ
imamartha- vaśamiti
imamanantaroktaṃ arthāḥ pratītyasamutpannāḥ teṣāṃ vaśaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ navamapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā pāratantryaṃ svato'pyasiddhatvaṃ vijñāyādhigamyāpi tathāgatāḥ sattvānāṃ mṛdvādibheda- sthitānāmāśayamadhimuktiṃ viditvā paricchidya dharmaṃ vāgvijñaptilakṣaṇaṃ sattvebhyo deśayanti prakāśayanti cintāmaṇibhadraghaṭṭavadanābhogena sattvānāmāśayaṃ rūpeṇa dharmadeśanāprādurbhāvo deśanā
yatheyaṃ deśanā evamevānena prakāreṇa padānāṃ niruktiḥ pratyāhāraḥ ākāśa- tulyapadaniruktirākāśapadaniruktiḥ
tathā ākāśapadaniruktyā tulyāḥ sarvatathāgatasamayāḥ anantaroktā anugantavyāḥ
evaṃ sarvadharmāṇāṃ paramārthasatyaviśuddhimupadiśyānyayānasādhāraṇavajrakāya- niṣpādakamupāyaviśeṣaṃ darśayannāha
tadyathāpītyādi
kāṇḍamaraṇiḥ agnimathane- rādhārabhūtaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ anenāgnirmathyata utpādyata iti mathaniḥ uparikāṣṭhaṃ puruṣa- hastavyāyāmaḥ mathanaṃ ghaṭanaṃ etāttritayaṃ pratītyāśritya auṣṇyānantaraṃ dhūmaḥ prādurbhavati tato dhūmānantaraṃprādurbhāvyagniḥ jvaladbhāsurākāro bhūtasaṃghātaḥ samuttiṣṭhati sameti
uṣmā dhūmaḥ pāvakaśca ete trayaḥ kāṇḍe na sthitā iti prāpte na kāṇḍasthito [na] mathanisthitaḥ na puruṣahastavyāpārasthitaḥ
evameveti yathoṣmādayaḥ kāṇḍādiṣu pṛthaṅanopalabhyante atha ca samavāyāt taireva tāratamyenotpadyante, evameva sarvatathāgatavajrasamayā anugantavyāḥ sarva- tathāgatālayatvāt
sarvatathāgatāḥ sarvaśūnyaṃ vajraṃ mahāśūnyaṃ samayo'tiśūnyaṃ anugantavyaḥ śūnyam
eteṣāṃ trayāṇāmanu paścād gamyate jñāyata iti kṛtvā
evaṃ sarvatathāgatādayo jñāyante
gamanāgamanādyairiti
gamanaṃ nāma śūnya- trayakrameṇa paramārthasatyagamanam
tasmānmahāśūnyā tiśūnya śūnyatāprāptirāgamanam
ādiśabdena gamanāgamanābhāvaparigrahaḥ, niṣpannasya praveśavyutthānābhāvāt
itiśabdaḥ parisamāptau
evaṃ paramagambhīradharmatattvamākarṇyāvadhāryārtamanaso yathodānamudānayanti, tad darśayannāha
athetyādi
āpātakṣaṇe ālokodayenāścaryamupagatāstato dharmatāmadhigamya vismayena vikasitanayanāḥ upāyajñānodayena saṃjātā idaṃ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ svapratividhyadharmato ddyotanasvarupaṃ vacanamakārṣuḥ kṛtavantaḥ
mahābhūteṣvityādi
atra mahābhūto dharmaḥ paramārthasatyam
jātyā bahu- vacanam
sarvavyāpitvāt tadevākāśasadṛśam
tārkikairvikalpayitumaśakyatvāt
nirvikalpaḥ|
saṃvṛtisatyapariśodhanāt śuddham|
tasmin paramārthasatye saṃvṛṇo- tyācchādayati cakṣuṣpathaṃ viṣayatābhāveneti|
saṃvṛtiḥ vajradharamūrtiḥ|
tuśabdaḥ punararthaḥ|
paramārthasatyābhigamanena śūnyībhūya punadṛśyatāṃ yātīti yad gīyate tadidamāścaryamiti yāvat|
paramārthaḥ prabhāsvaram|
tena śuddhaḥ paramārthaśuddhaścāsau tattvārthaśca mahāvajra- dharaḥ|
sa evālaṅghayatvāt samayaḥ|
tatpratipādako granthasandarbhaśca paramārtha- śuddhatattvārthaḥ|
sandhyābhāṣā ||
|| pradīpodyotanaṭīkāyāṃ navamapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
|| daśamaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
sāmānyottamasādhanāśritā prapañcādivividhacaryāsu jitaśramā yogino yadā siddhirna labhante tadā stradevatāhṛdayasaṃcodanā kartavyeti|
athetyādinā daśamapaṭalamupakṣipati|
athāhuriti saṃvandhaḥ|
punaḥ samājamāgamyeti|
prathama- paṭaloktakrameṇa mahāsamayavajro mahāvajradharaḥ taṃ tattvābhisaṃbodhisatyadvayādvaidhī- bhāvalakṣaṇaṃ tameva kāyavākcittarahasyatvāt kāyavākcittaguhyaṃ vineyānāṃ yathāśayabhedopagatatvāt tathāgataḥ taṃ namasyaivamāhuḥ|
tattvamavisaṃvādakaṃ mantrā mālikālijanitāḥ sarpakavandhādayasteṣāṃ sārāḥ pradhānā oṃkārādayastrayaḥ teṣāṃ samuccaya uddīpanaṃ kāyavākcittāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ guhyākhyaṃ rahasyasaṃjñakaṃ mahāsiddhinayāḥ mahāsiddhyupāyāsteṣāmuttamaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ taṃ bhāṣasva prakāśayetyadhyeṣaṇā|
adhyeṣaṇānantaraṃ vajradharaḥ upāyajñānātmakaprajñopāyopalabdhāpekṣayā virāja- mānatvāt rājā|
sarvakleśā rāgādayaḥ te ca prabhedānubhedabhinnatvāt sāgaropamā iti|
sarvakleśārṇavāḥ teṣu duḥkhadaurmanasyādiprakṛtirabhibhūya paramānandasvarūpeṇa prabhavati krīḍata iti prabhuḥ|
sarvaduḥkhendhanadahanād dīptaḥ ataeva caṇḍaḥ krodhā- tmakatvāt|
tasyodaye prākṛtānāmapi protphullanayanatvāpādanād viśālākṣaḥ idaṃ ghoṣamakārṣīt|
ghoṣapravartanasya tadāyattatvāt ||
kāyetyādi|
kāyādīnāṃ kāyavākcittabhāvanaṃ kāyavākcittasvarūpeṇa bhāvanaṃ parijñānaṃ kāyavākcittavajrāṇāṃ bhāvanaṃ tat kartavyam|
katham ?
nirvikalpaṃ nirālamvamiti kriyā viśeṣaṇam|
ayaṃ kāya iyaṃ vāk idaṃ cittamiti tattvato bhedābhāvānnirvikalpaṃ anyonyāśrayābhāvānnirālambam|
trayāṇāmekarasatā samatā tayā na kvacit sthitam|
samatayeti prāpte samate- tyārṣam|
nāsandhyam|
evaṃ bhagavadvacanena kāyādīnāṃ paramārthato'bhedaṃ pradarśya saṅgītikṛt hṛdaya- sañcodanopayoginīṃ samāsena pīṭhikāṃ racayannāha athetyādi|
anantarokta- krameṇa svabhāvaśuddhatathāgataḥ|
pāramitālocanādayo mantraḥ|
virocanādayastūpanayaḥ tayoranyonyārādhanaṃ tadeva vajramavinaśvaratvāt|
tannāmakaṃ samādhiṃ cittaikāgratāṃ samāpannaḥ prāha bhagavān mahāvajradharaḥ tānadhyeṣakān sarvatathāgatānevamāha astītyādi|
ayaṃ vakṣyamāṇo'rtho'sti|
bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatā ityāmantraṇam|
akṣobhyapramukhāḥ sarvatathāgatā ityasya pradarśana kriyābhiyogaḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ strīvimvaniṣaṇṇāḥ vidyādevyaḥ tāḥ sahacarādibhedenānekāḥ|
tāsāṃ koṭini- yutaśatasahasrairaprameyatvādupalakṣaṇabhūtaiḥ sārdhaṃ sattvāḥ viṣayopabhogena mokṣakāmāḥ teṣāmarthakriyā chandānurūpeṇa bodhiprāpaṇaṃ tasyānātmaraṅgaṃ praviṣṭasūtradhāravat pravartanaṃ nāṭakaṃ tatpradarśayanti|
kathamityāha daśadiglokadhātuparyavasāneṣu lokadhātuṣu pañcakāmaguṇaiḥ satatāhlādanakarai rūpādibhiḥ krīḍanti vikurvanti ramanti viśvaramaṇena devībhiḥ saha ramanti parivārayanti vidyāgaṇaiḥ pūjitāḥ punastāṃ pratipūjayanti iyaṃ paricaryā|
na ca te mantracaryābhiyuktamavalokayantīti|
te tathāgatāḥ krīḍādvāreṇa paramānandasukhamanubhavantaḥ mantraḥ caturvidho mantravādaḥ yaduta mantrasaṃjñā mantrārthaḥ mantravidarbhaṇaṃ vajravāk svaravarjitā ceti|
etairupāyaiḥ śāntikādiṣu pravṛttiḥ mantracaryā tattrābhiyuktaṃ tadabhiniviṣṭacittā nāvalokayanti|
na vahu manyante|
anavalokanakāraṇaṃ niṣpanno vatetyādinā darśayati|
ayamiti mantraparāyaṇaḥ tathāgatāstryadhvavartinaḥ mantrayate gurubhirupāṃśu kathyate yaḥ sa mantraḥ tadviṣayacaryā mantracaryā tāṃ nayati prāpayatīti nayaḥ saptavidhaḥ|
yaduta maṇḍalaṃ gaṇakuṇḍaṃ ca valirmelāpakakramaḥ|
tattvaṃ saptavidhākhyānaṃ sa mantranaya ucyate ||
iti|
sa eva niṣpannakramaḥ samādhisthai [ re] vāryata iti dharmaḥ|
evaṃvidhatathāgataparamparā- gatamantracaryānayadharmā[t] niṣpanno nirgato vāhyaḥ|
dharmāditi prāpte dharma ityārṣaḥ|
vata ityanādare|
itiśabdo luptanirdiṣṭaḥ|
evaṃ mantraparāyaṇeṣu tathāgatavaimukhyaṃ dṛṣṭvā teṣāṃ siddhyarthanāmanugrahāya tathāgatāvalokanopāyaṃ yathā kathayati bhagavāṃstaddarśayannāha saṅgītikṛt|
tadityādi|
yasmāt krīḍādidvāreṇa paramānandasukhamanubhavantastathāgatāḥ sādhakaṃ nādriyante, tasmāt teṣāṃ mahāsatpuruṣāṇāṃ tathāgatānāṃ vyavalokanārthaṃ sādhakābhi- mukhīkaraṇena prasādotpādanārthaṃ idaṃ vakṣyamāṇakamudājahāra|
sarvetyādi|
daśamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā sarvatathāgatānāṃ kāyavākcittarahasyabhūtaṃ sarvamantrāḥ sarpādimantradevatā teṣāṃ hṛdayaṃ jīvitaṃ saṃcodyate prabodhyate yena tat|
sarvamantrahṛdayasaṃcodanaṃ nāma paramaguhyam apātrāṇāmatyantagopyaṃ sarvatathāgatamohakulīnāḥ teṣāṃ kāyavā- cittāni samyagayanti gacchantīti samayaḥ sa mantribhirālamvyate manaskāreṇeti ālambanastaṃ samavalamvanaṃ praṇavam|
evaṃ sarvavajradharakāyādyālamvanaṃ meghanādaṃ sarvadharmaparakāyādyālamvanam āḥkāraṃ svakāyavākcittavaprebhya iti|
svo mahāvajradharastasya kāyādoṃkāraṃ vācaḥ āḥkāraṃ cittāt hū~kāraṃ yathākrama- mudājahāra niścārayāmāsa|
kathaṃ vākpathaniruktyā vākpathaṃ vacanamāspaṃ tena niruktiḥ kathanam|
tayā niruktyā manaḥ kāyābhyāmapi vaktrayā vācā udājahāra|
kimuta vācā|
strīpuṃnapuṃsakasvabhāvā varṇāste samuccīyante saṃgrahaṃ gacchanti yasmin sa mantrasamuccayaḥ|
tamakṣarattrayamudājahāra ||
athetyādi|
atha mantrasamuccayodāharaṇānantaram asminnakṣaratraye bhāṣitamātre uccāritakṣaṇe sarvabuddhā akṣobhyādaya saaurasā bodhigaṇasahitā mahāvajradharā- nugrahāyāsya sattvānāṃ avalokanena kampitā mūrcchāmāpede|
vajrasattvaṃ sarva- kulādhipamanusmaran sarvasattvānugrāhakasvabhāvaṃ smarantaḥ mūcchāṃ prītyaudvelyamāpede āpedire prāptavantaḥ|
atha smaraṇānantaraṃ vajrapāṇirmahāvajradhara idaṃ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ devatāsamayā- laṅadhyarūpamudājahāra|
ākāśetyādi|
ākāśaṃ tathatā tatra dhāryate sthāpyata iti ākāśadhātuḥ samayasattvaṃ tasya madhyaṃ hṛdayaṃ tatsthaṃ vyūhamaṇḍalaṃ jñānasattvaṃ bhāvayet dhyāyāt|
tatra tasmin jñānasattvamadhyasthaṃ hṛdayasthaṃ hūṃkāraṃ samādhi- sattvākhyam|
etadddvitayaṃ svavimbena svarūpeṇa prakalpayet niṣpādayet|
teṣu samādhisattvaṃ vajjraraśmimahādīptaṃ sphurantaṃ vibhāvayet|
vajrāḥ pañca- tathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ raśmayaḥ sitādayaḥ tairmahādīptamatyarthadīptam visphurantaṃ jvalantaṃ vicintayet|
buddhānāṃ vairocanādiniṣpannāniṣpannatathāgatānāṃ daśadiglokadhātu- vyavasthitānāṃ kāyavākcittaṃ kāyavākcittaśakti samādhisattvavinirgata sitādi- raśmyaṅgena hṛdayamākṛṣṭaṃ vicintayet dhyāyāt|
evamabhyasyan sādhakastatkṣaṇādevākṛṣya samādhisattve praveśitamātrādeva sarvamabhibhūya vajrasattvo bhavediti saṃvandhaḥ|
kiṃ svarūpaḥ ?
abhedyakāyādisva- bhāvatvāt|
kāyavākcittavajjradhṛk mahāntastathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ rañjakatvāt guhyasamājapradīpodyotane mahārājā|
sarve sthāvarajaṅgamāḥ teṣāmagrayā hariharādayaḥ|
teṣāmapi nigrahānu- grahakaraṇāt sarvāgraḥ parameśvaraḥ|
evaṃ prakāraṃ mahāvajradharamātmānaṃ bhāvayediti yāvat|
svamaṇḍalaṃ svakāyamaṇḍalaṃ tasya svavajreṇa svacittena niṣpādanavidherayaṃ idaṃ yadanantaroktaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ vyadhvavartināṃ sāraṃ sthirībhūtaṃ vajrāṇi tathāgata- cittāni teṣāṃ samuccayamākarṣaṇam|
samuccīyate ākṛṣyate aneneti kṛtvā|
evaṃ sarvatathāgatacittamayamātmānaṃ niṣpādya yathāparivāracakraṃ niṣpādayati |
taddarśayannāha |
svetyādi |
svātmānaṃ caturṣu sthāneṣu pūrvādisthāneṣu rūpataḥ yathoktavarṇabhujādisvarūpataḥ |
trimukhākārayogeneti |
ākriyante vyajyante gṛhyanta ityākārāḥ rāgādayastairyogaḥ trimukhākārayogaḥ tenopalakṣitaṃ kṛtvā trivarṇena tyakṣareṇa mantrapuruṣā vairocanādayaḥ tāṃśca tatsthāneṣu dhyātvā bhāvayedabhyaset |
anena nyāyena yathāsvaṃ tatkulapratibaddhadevatāḥ tathāgatavyūhaṃ spharet |
tathāgatacatuṣṭayasyopalakṣaṇārthatvāt |
ityevamāha avocad bhagavān khavajrasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ |
evaṃ saṃgraha- maṇḍalamabhinirmāya punaḥ kiṃ kuryādityāha |
tatvetyādi |
tatra tathāgatahṛdaya- saṃcodane idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ paramamutkṛṣṭaṃ vajrarahasyaṃ tathāgatarahasyaṃ maṇḍalānyevā- bhedānmāṇḍaleyāsteṣāṃ maṇḍalānāṃ pratyekaṃ hṛdayamadhyagataṃ hṛccandrasthaṃ sūkṣmaṃ vibhāvanaṃ kuryāt |
viśeṣeṇa vibhāvyata iti vibhāvanaṃ jñānamayatvāt sūkṣmaṃ jñānasattvaṃ bhāvayediti yāvat |
tasya jñānasattvasya madhyagataṃ hṛccandragataṃ tathaiva pratyekamakṣaramavinaśvarasvabhāvaṃ paramamutkṛṣṭamanāhatodbhavatvāt padaṃ hūṅkāraṃ yogataḥ śreyasā sādhakaṃ yojayati iti yogaḥ samādhisattvaḥ |
tasmāt pañcaśūkaṃ mahāvajraṃ vikarālaṃ bhāvayet dhyāyāt |
anena prakāreṇa trīṇi samayajñāna- samādhisattvākhyāni te abhedyatvāt vajrāṇi cintayet |
vajrāṅkuśa- prayogata iti|
vajrāstathāgatāḥ teṣāmaṅkuśamivāṅkuśam yathā gajasya |
tasya prayogo vajrāṅkuśaprayogaḥ tasmād vajrāṅkuśaprayogaṃ trīṇi vajrāṇi cintayet |
tameva prayogaṃ darśayitumāha |
hṛdayamityādi |
tena vajrattrayeṇa hṛdayaṃ sādhya- devatāhṛdayaṃ tāḍayet cālayet |
adhipatipramukhatisattvātmaka māṇḍaleyānāmekī- bhūtena raśmisamūhenāṅakuśākāreṇa sādhyadevatāhṛdayaṃ trisattvākhyaṃ kampayet yāvad devatāṃ vā pracodayediti|
vāśabdaḥ samuccaye |
vajrāṅkuśabhedanena daśamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā sādhakaḥ spharaṇotpādānantaraṃ devatāṃ purovartinaṃ niśumbharājaṃ ājñāṃ mārgayantaṃ vicintya taṃ tathāgataiḥ samayasiddhiṃ dāpayet |
codayet ājñāpayet |
idaṃ yadanantaroktaṃ vidhānaṃ tat sarvavajrāṇāṃ sarvasādhakānāṃ buddhabodhiprasādhanamiti |
buddhāstathāgatāsteṣāṃ bodhicittaprasādhanamākarṣaṇam |
vajñetyādi|
anantaroktaṃ trisattvātmakavajrāṅkuśavibhāvanaṃ vajrapadmakulā dayairiti |
tathāgataratnakarmakulaiśca sādhakairanuṣṭheyam |
idamanantaroktaṃ hṛdaye codanaṃ tathāgatahṛdayasaṃcodanaṃ tatsarvaṃ nāṭaka iva saṃbhūtatvāt nāṭakasaṃbhavam |
yathā nāṭaka eka eva sūtradhāra indrādisvarūpeṇa nepathyādibhiḥ parikalpyate, tathehāpyeka eva cittavajraḥ sādhyasādhakasādhanarūpeṇa parikalpyata iti yāvat |
asya hṛdayasaṃcodanavidhānasya kālapramāṇaṃ darśayitumāha |
saptāhamityādi |
ārambhadivasāt prabhṛti saptadināni tāvatkurvīta |
idaṃ vajranayottamamiti |
vajrāstathāgatāste nīyante ākṛṣyante yairvidhānaistāni vajranayāni teṣūttama- mūtkṛṣṭaṃ vajranayottamam |
tadvidhānamanutiṣṭhet |
sidhyata ityādinā vidhāna- syānuśaṃsanamāha |
kāyavākcittasādhyaṃ tathāgatakāyavākcittaṃ jñānavajriṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ sidhyate rahasyaṃ gurūpadeśalabhyatvāt ||
bhītā iti bhīritāḥ atikrāntāḥ bhītā evaṃbhūtā api tathāgatāḥ saṃtrasta- manasā iti |
kṛpāvaśagatayo'vighnacetasaḥ sādhakavaradā vyavalokayanti |
kāṅkṣitavarapradānenānugṛhṇanti |
anugṛhya kiṃ kurvantītyāha |
dadantītyādi |
vipulāṃ mahatīṃ manaḥ santoṣaṇena priyāmiṣṭāṃ siddhiṃ laukikalokottaralakṣaṇāṃ dadanti ḍhaukayanti |
ke dadantītyāha|
buddhāścetyādi |
buddhā akṣobhyādayaḥ bodhisattvā maitreyādayaḥ mantracaryāgradhāriṇo bodhyarthaṃ ghaṭamānasiddhapuruṣāḥ evamapratipatteḥ sādhakasya kiṃ syāt ityāha |
atikramedityādi |
yadi cenmohātmā ime tathāgatāḥ ahaṃprākṛtā yā parikalpanā sa mohaḥ|
evaṃvidho mohaḥ ātmā svabhāvo yasya sa mohātmā|
sa tasya sādhakasya jīvitaṃ tadantam |
yena karmaṇā siddhimicchati |
tenaiva karmaṇā jīvitaṃ kṣapayati na punaḥ siddhi pratilabhate hṛdayasaṃcodanavirahi- taścedityabhiprāyaḥ |
yathārutam |
evaṃ sāmānyasādhanapravṛttānāṃ hṛdayasaṃcodanaprayogaṃ nirdiśyedānīmuttamasādhana- sthānamāha |
athetyādi vibhaktārthaḥ |
idaṃ ghoṣamidaṃ vacanamakārṣīt abravīt ||
4 guhyasamājapradīpodyotane yāvantaḥ kecinmantrarahasyaṃ tatra pravṛttāḥ puruṣāḥ mantrapuruṣāḥ trivajrajñānā- stathāgatāḥ taiḥ pūritāḥ paripūrṇāḥ santaḥ teṣāṃ sarvabhāvavikalpanaṃ dvayendriya- prayogeṇa bhavati nānyathā |
sarvabhāvāḥ anantaroktāḥ vakṣyamāṇāśca sādhya- sādhanādiprayogāsteṣāṃ vikalpanaṃ niṣpādanaṃ sarvabhāvavikalpanaṃ tat |
dvayendriya- prayogeṇeti dvayendriyaṃ satyadvayaṃ tasya prayogo mīlanaṃ tena bhavati yā kācit laukikalokottarā siddhiḥ sā dvayendriyaprayogeṇa niṣpādanaṃ sarvabhāvavikalpanaṃ tadvinā na sidhyatītyabhiprāyaḥ |
tadanantaroktaprayogeṇa niṣpāditaṃ sādhanaṃ tadidaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ tathāgatasaṃvandhimantrasamayo'laṅghyo yaiste mantrasamayāḥ teṣāṃ sādhanaṃ sādhanopāyam |
vidyeśvarītyādinā dvayendriyaprayoganiṣpādanopāyamāha |
vidyeśvarī prajñāpāra- mitā |
tayā praviṣṭeṣu skandhādiṣu nirābhāsīkṛteṣu vajraṃ saṃyogaḥ ekīkaraṇaṃ tasya bhāvanā kāryā |
tataḥ siddhaḥ san kiṃ kuryādityāha |
raktāmityādi |
paramārtha- satyapraveśānantaraṃ satyadvayātmikāṃ svamahāmudrāṃ niṣpādya tāṃ vaidhātukīṃ tada- parityāgasaṃyogena raktāṃ saktām |
raktekṣaṇāṃ traidhātukasthitasattvarāśirayama- smākaṃ pitetyādibuddhimutpādya sarāgaṃ savahumānaṃ ca vīkṣed vijānīyāt |
imaṃ yadanantaroktaṃ tat samayamaṇḍalam |
samayo mahāvajradharaḥ tasya maṇḍa: sārabhūto vidhiḥ taṃ lāti gṛhṇātīti samayamaṇḍalam |
kiṃ tadvidhānam ?
evaṃ sopāyaṃ saphalaṃ ca hṛdayasaṃcodanaṃ saṃpradarśya tatsiddhasya yoginaḥ pravṛtti darśayitumāha |
athetyādi |
sarvatathāgatāḥ vairocanādisvabhāvaparikalpitāḥ rūpaskandhādayaḥ teṣāmātmā svabhāvaḥ paramārthasatyaṃ tasmājjātaḥ sarvatathā- gatātmajaḥ mahāvajradharaḥ |
sarvabuddhānāmabhiṣekabhūtatvāt sarvābhiṣekaḥ|
ataeva sarvabuddhāgraḥ |
idaṃ vakṣyamāṇavacanamabravīt |
sarveṣvanantāparyanteṣu lokadhātuṣu yāvanto hīnamadhyamapraṇītabhedabhinnā yoṣitaḥ smṛtāḥ rūpavajrādayaḥ parikalpitā mahāmudrāḥ dvividhāḥ yaduta mantramūrtirjñānamūrtiśca tasyā ādiyogaḥ tathā- gatālamvanaṃ tena tān rūpādiviṣayān upabhuñjayet |
sphared buddhapadaṃ tatra asaṃkhyakoṭivajreṇa tatra mahāmudrādiyoge buddhapadaṃ buddhanirmāṇamasaṃkhyapramāṇamaprameyaṃ vajriṇā vajrī spharetyāha bhagavān bodhisamayo mahāvajradharaḥ |
daśamapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā anena yogena bodhi prāpnuyāt |
sattvān bodhayati ātmasvarūpamiti bodhiḥ |
kīdṛśīti ?
vajrākāśasannibhā |
kāyavajrādisvabhāvā |
aspṛśya- tvādākāśasannibhā |
tāṃ bodhi prāpnoti |
ghaṭamānāvasthāyāmapyanuśaṃsāṃ darśayati |
sa bhavedityādi |
ghaṭamāno bodhisattvaiḥ vajrasattvasyeva saṃmukhārogyādi samarpitam |
dīrghamāyuryasya sa vajrasattvāyuḥ upacārājjinā guṇajinā: teṣāmudadhiḥ sthānaṃ bhaved yathā sarvaratnānāmudadhiriti |
sandhyābhāṣā |
laukikalokottarasiddhyarthaṃ tathāgatahṛdayasaṃcodanapaṭalaḥ ||
