ekādaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
evamubhayayogināṃ dviprakāreṇa hṛdayasaṃcodanamabhidhāyedānīṃ vikalpita- niṣpannayogināṃ prathamapaṭaloktadvividhadevatārūpoddyotanārthamekādaśamapaṭalamupa- kṣipannāha |
athetyādi |
sarvatathāgatavajñaṃ praṇavādyakṣarattrayaṃ tenotpadyamānā puruṣottamā devatā yasmin samādhau tannāmakaṃ samādhiṃ cittaikāgratāṃ samāpadyā- mukhīkṛtya idamudājahāra |
kiṃ tat ?
sarvatathāgatānāṃ mantraḥ |
guhyabhūtā vajravidyā sarvatathāgatamantravajravidyā oṅkārāditritayameva tairniṣpādyate puruṣadevatā yasmin tatpaṭalamuktavāniti saṅgītikāraḥ amumevārthaṃ bhagavadvacanena darśayannāha
trivajrān kāyavākcittavajrāḥ tanniṣpādakānyakṣarāṇi oṅkārādīni trivajrākṣarāṇi tāni mantrāgrāṇi sarvamantraprabhāvatvāt
tairmahāmudrāvibhāvanaṃ kartavyam
mahāmudreti bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥ prapūjyatvānmahatīṃ mudaṃ harṣaṃ dravati gacchatīti mahāmudrā
tasyā vibhāvanaṃ kartavyamanuṣṭheyam
jñānavajreṇa sādhakena sarvabodhi: pañcākārābhisaṃbodhiḥ tatsamāvaham
oṅkāramityādinā akṣaratrayasya kāritaṃ darśayati
oṅkāraṃ tathāgata- hṛdayaṃ tad yogino'bhedyakāyanivartakatvāt vajrakāyasamāvaham
bodhistathatā tato nirmito niṣpanna ātmā yasya sa bodhirnirātmā amitābhaḥ
tasya bhāvo nairātmyam
nairātmyamāḥkāraṃ tatsvabhāvaṃ yogino'bhedyavāṅanivartakatvāt vāgvajramāvaham
kāyavācorāśrayabhūtaṃ cittaṃ kāyavākcittam akṣobhyasva- bhāvam
trivajrāṇāmabhedyamabhinnatvaṃ yasmin tat trivajrābhedyaṃ cittaṃ tad- yogisantāne niṣpādayatīti trivajjābhedyasamāvahaṃ hūṅkāramityevamāha sarva- tathāgatakāyavākcittamantrapuruṣo mahāvajradharaḥ
anantaroktamahāmudrāniṣpattirānupūrvyaṃ darśayannāha, khavajñetyādi
khavajra- mākāśaṃ madhyaṃ tadgataṃ sarvavajtraṃ maṇḍalaṃ cintayet
sarvavajrāṇi locanādi- caturdevyaḥ tābhirānupūrvyeṇa nirvartitaṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhāvayet
tatra bhūbhāgopari bhrūṅkāraṃ kūṭāgāraniṣpattihetubhūtaṃ vajjrameghāstathāgatasamūhāḥ
teṣāmadhimokṣeṇa yathāsthānaṃ ekādaśapaṭalapaṭkoṭivyākhyā spharaṇaṃ tadāvahati dhārayatīti vajjrameghaspharāvaham
bhrūṅkāraṃ tattretyādinā bhrūṅkāraṃ pradarśanaṃ vajramaṇḍalaṃ sūryamaṇḍalaṃ tanmadhyasthaṃ hūṅkāraṃ prabhāvayet
hūṅkāraniṣpannaṃ sūryamaṇḍalaṃ bhāvayedityabhiprāyaḥ
tathaivoṃkārāḥkāraniṣpannaṃ candrapadmamaṇḍalaṃ bhāvayet
evaṃ maṇḍalatrayaṃ niṣpādya padmakaṇikopari tadeva vījatayaṃ vinyasedityāmnāyaḥ
bhrūṅkāra- mālayamityādinā pūrvoktamarthaṃ nigamayati
ādhāramaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvaivaṃ [ca] cihnattrayaṃ bhāvayet
nānyatheti
hṛdayamityādi
vyadhvabuddhebhyaḥ vyadhvatathāgatānāṃ vyadhvabuddhānāmiti prāptatvāt
hṛdaye candramaṇḍalaṃ cittasaṃketitatvāt
kāyavākcittaṃ tyakṣaraṃ tena yasmin rajyate sthīyate tat kāyavākcittarañjanaṃ candravimbaṃ cihnataya- mupamardyatāṃ bhāvayet
tataḥ kimityāha omityādi
tasmiścaṃdramaṇḍale buddha- kāyasaṃjñakamoṅkāraṃ tathā buddhavākpathamāḥ kāram
jñānānāṃ prakṛtīnāmoghaḥ samūho yasmin tajjñānaughaṃ cittaṃ tatsvabhāvaṃ hūṅkāram
idamakṣaratrayaṃ bodhinayottamamiti
bodhirmahāsukhapadam
tannīyate prāpyate yena tad bodhanayam
kiṃ tadakṣaratrayaparāvṛttyā saṃjātaṃ pañcākārābhi- saṃbodhisūcakaṃ vajraṃ tadevottamaṃ sarvakulaviśiṣṭatvāt
idamiti anantaroktaṃ yadvatraṃ tat sarvabuddhānāṃ mahāvajradharayogināṃ buddhabodhiprasādhakam
utpatti- krameṇa mahāmudrāniṣpādakam
nirmitamityādi
etanmahāmudrārūpaṃ jñānavajreṇa mahāvajradhareṇa nirmitaṃ pradaśitam
kimartham ? buddhā ratnapudgalāsteṣāṃ hetuḥ saṃvṛtisatyam
phalaṃ ca paramārthasatyam
hetuphalābhyāmudeti niṣpādyate iti hetuphalodayam
pariśuddha- devatārūpaṃ tadarthaṃ nirmitaṃ mahāvajradhareṇa
eta ityādi
mantrā vidyā iti ca kalpādau kīrtitāḥ niṣpādanādisamayairiti
niṣpādanaṃ pūrvamevārakṣaṇa- bhakṣaṇādisamayaiḥ sārdhaṃ kathitāḥ
buddhapūruṣā akṣobhyādaya eva nānyaiḥ
kairi- tyāha
trivajrābhedyabhāvanaistathāgataiḥ
sarvatathāgatakāyavākcitta- tyakṣaraṃ tasya samayaṃ tadudbhūtaṃ sūryādimaṇḍalavitayaṃ tasmin tattvaṃ padmopari tyakṣaraṃ jñānamiti sarvaparāvṛttiniṣpannaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ vajraṃ candramaṇḍalopari vinyastākṣaratritayanivṛttaṃ pañcasūcikamahāvajraṃ tenādhiṣṭhīyate niṣpādyata 8 guhya samājapradīpodyotane ityadhiṣṭhānaṃ mahāmudrārūpaṃ tasya hetuḥ kāraṇam
tanniṣpādikā cittaikāgratā samādhiḥ
sa tathoktaḥ
vivikteṣvityādinā sādhakasya pratipattiṃ darśayati
vivikteṣu jana- sampātarahiteṣu ramyeṣviti puṣpaphalapallavopeta vividhatarulatopagūḍhamanojñasalilā - śayavirājitamano'nukūleṣu sthāneṣu sthitvā yaḥ sādhaka: imamanantaroktaṃ yogaṃ samārabhedabhyaset
tena kāyavākcittaṃ mahāvajradharatvaṃ sidhyate sādhyate
pakṣaikeneti
pakṣaḥ gurudevatāviṣaye sampratyayaḥ
tasminnekena niścitena tatpareṇa na saṃśayaḥ
niyataṃ kāyavākcittaṃ sādhyate'nena krameṇotpattikramamabhyasan mantrī niṣpannakramasamādhimadhigamya niyatamihaiva janmani mahāvajradharatvaṃ prāpnotītya- bhiprāyaḥ
evaṃ mahāvajradharayogināmutpatti kramasādhanaṃ saṃdarśya vairocanādiyoginā- mekasmṛtisamādhiṃ darśayannāha, khavajramadhyagaṃ cintet svacchaṃ maṇḍalamuttamamiti
abhāvetyādi gāthānupūrveṇa kūṭāgārodare dvātriṃśaddevatā vinyāsaparyantamadhimokṣeṇa samāpadya naśūnyatetyādimantreṇa sarvaṃ nirābhāsīkṛtya oṅkārādyakṣarattrayeṇa candra- sūryapadmāni niṣpādya padmakaṇikopari tyakṣaraṃ vicintya sarvaparāvṛttyā svaccha- maṇḍalaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ vicintayet
niṣpādya svamantrasamayamiti
candramaṇḍalopari tyakṣaraṃ vinyasya tatparāvṛttyā cakraṃ bhāvayitvā tajjātaṃ vairocanaṃ samayasattvaṃ bhāvayet
tasya hṛdaye jñānasattvaṃ vibhāvya tasyāpi hṛdaye oṅkāraṃ samādhisattvākhyaṃ hṛdayabhūtaṃ paśñcaraśmi paśvaraśmyupetaṃ nyaset
vairocanāgrabhāvanaiḥ vairocanayogibhiḥ mahāmeghāḥ vairocanasamūhāḥ tān- oṅkāraṃ sphārayan dhyāyāt
anena samādhinā buddhasyotpattikramajñasya vajravairocanasyodadhiriva sva- kulīnānāṃ prabhavatvāt
sa yasya sādhakasya kārya kāyaḥ sidhyato pakṣamātreṇa samādhyāsaktimātreṇa kriyāprapañvanirapekṣayaivetyabhiprāyaḥ
buddhakāyaḥ sarvalakṣaṇo- petavajjrakāyaḥ
tatsamaprabhaḥ tatsamaddhiḥ sidhyati
trivajrakalpaṃ mahavajradharaprakṣo bhūtvā sevayan pañcajñānināṃ tiṣṭheyuḥ tiṣṭhet
pañcajñānāni cakṣurādīni vijñā- nāni tāni grāhakasvabhāvena yeṣāṃ te pañcavajñānino rūpādayo viṣayāḥ
tānanu- bhavannāsaṃsārā tiṣṭhedityāha bhagavān kāyavajraguhyo mahāvajradharaḥ
sarva- tathāgatacandramaṇḍalaṃ tatra vajramabhedyasvabhāvaṃ cakraṃ tasya raśmivyūho mahāmudrārūpaṃ ekādaśapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā tanniṣpādakaḥ samādhiḥ sarvatathāgatavajjraraśmivyūho nāma
khavajretyādi
tathaiva sarvaṃ saṃpādya dharmamaṇḍalaṃ padmamuttamaṃ raktamaṣṭadalaṃ dhyātvā tatparāvṛttyā svamantrapuruṣamamitābhaṃ niṣpādya tadvṛdi jñānasattvaṃ dhyātvā vākpathe jñānasattve āḥkārasamādhisattvaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ mahāvajramabhedya- svarūpaṃ lokeśvarāgrabhāvanai ramitābhagatacetovṛttibhiḥ nyaset
niṣpādya samayajñāna- vāksamayaprapañcakamiti
samayaḥ samayasattvaḥ jñānaṃ jñānasattvam
vāksamayaḥ samādhisattvaḥ
niṣpādyaitatsattvatritayamekīkṛtya bhāvayet
anenaiva nyāyena pañcakaṃ tathāgatapañcakaṃ trisattvasaṃpuṭīkaraṇena bhāvayet
dharmavākpatho'mitābhasamādhiḥ samārūḍhaḥ saṃprāptaḥ dharmaḥ paramārthasatyaṃ vajraṃ saṃvṛtisatyam
dharmasamāyuktaṃ vajraṃ dharmavajraṃ mahāvajradharapadaṃ samāvahet, prāpnuyāt
trivajrakalpamityādi vivṛtārtham
sarvatathāgato jñānasattvaḥ vāgvajrasamādhisattvasamayaḥ samayasattvaḥ sarvatathā- gatavāgvajjrasamayānāṃ saṃbhavo yasmin sa tathoktaḥ samādhiḥ
khavajñetyādi
mahāvajradharasamādhivat sarvaṃ saṃpādya svavimvapuruṣaṃ samaya- sattvaṃ niṣpādya cittaṃ jñānasattvastatsaṃsthitaṃ jñānasattva hṛdayasaṃsthitam huṅkāraṃ samādhisattvākhyaṃ cintayet
sarvavajrajino'kṣobhyaḥ tasyālayaṃ evaṃ trisattvakāyaṃ mahāsamayasattvamakṣobhyaṃ pañcavarṇaraśmiprabhāmaṇḍalopetaṃ vibhāvayet
jñānavajreṇākṣobhyayoginā
mahāmudrāsiddhi pratilabhata iti darśayitumāha
vajracittetyādi
vajraṃ paramārtha- satyaṃ cittaṃ saṃvṛtisatyaṃ tābhyāṃ ghaṭitaḥ vajracittamayaḥ devamanuṣyāṇāmanuśāsa- katvāt śāstā
jñānaguṇāḥ kāmaiśvaryādayo'ṣṭau teṣāmudadhirākaratvāt
trivajjrakalpamityādi gatārtham
ra vajraṃ samayasattvaḥ cittaṃ jñānasattvaḥ guhyaṃ samādhisattvaḥ
evaṃ trisattvā- tmakatvād vajracittaguhyo mahāvajradhara ityāha
sarvatathāgatetyādinā prakaṇārtha- mupasaṃharati
sarvatathāgato mahāvajradharaḥ kāyaṃ vākcittaṃ vairocanāditrayaṃ samayo'moghasiddhiḥ
vajro ratnasaṃbhavaḥ eṣāṃ samādhirabhedopacārāt sarva- tathāgatakāyavākcittasamayavajjo nāma
yathārutam
evaṃ kalpitayogasya phalarūpeṇa niṣpannakramaṃ sūcayitvā tacchākṣādeva guhyasamājapradīpodyotane darśayitumāha
mahāvajjradharamityādi
jñāyata iti jñānaṃ sthiravalaṃ tasya maṇḍalaṃ samūhaḥ trailokyaṃ tanmadhye mahāvajraṃ svaṃ mahāmudrārūpaṃ samādhāyādhi- patitvena sthāpayitvā sarvakāyeṣu sthāvarajaṃgameṣu khaṃkāramākāśīkaraṇa manubheda- krameṇa sarvaṃ nirābhāsīkṛtyātmanyanukrameṇa praveśaḥ
taṃkṛtvā khavajrajñānasamo bhavet
trisattvātmaka ekaḥ syāt
khamiti prabhāsvaraḥ
tasya ca buddhaiḥ vairocanādisvabhāvaiḥ rūpaskandhādibhirbodhisattvaiḥ kṣiti- garbhādisvabhāvaiḥ cakṣurādibhirmuhurmuhuriti
rūpaskandhagatetyādikrameṇa pratikṣaṇaṃ pūjyamānaḥ
jñānatrayātmake svasmin praveśyamānastridhā prajñopāyopalabdhasvabhāvena kalpyata iti trikalpaṃ jñānatrayaṃ tat samayati vilayaṃ gacchati yasmin tat trikalpasamayaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ taṃ tiṣṭhet
tiṣṭhan buddhairapi na dṛśyata iti
buddhāḥ trīṇi jñānāni
tairapi yathā na dṛśyate yathādarśanamanābhāsaḥ tathā tiṣṭhe- dityāha bhagavān khavajjrasamaya iti
khavatraṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tasmāt sameti udgacchatīti khavajjrasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ vyutthānasamādhi sūca- yannāha kāyetyādi
kāyavākcittavatraṃ mahāmudrā tasyā antarddhānaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tasmāt saṃbhavatīti saṃbhavaḥ vyūhaḥ sthiracalasarvabhāvātmakaṃ jñānakāyaṃ tasya mālā yathāśayaṃ vairocanādirūpeṇa pravṛttiḥ sā yasmin samādhau cittakṣaṇe sa tathoktaḥ
tameva sarvasamādhiṃ daśayannāha dhyātvetyādi
svamantrapuruṣo tayapariśuddha- mahāmudrārūpaṃ vajramaṇḍalamadhyataḥ prabhāsvaramadhyādutthitaṃ dhyātvā tadanantaraṃ prākṛta- sattvānāṃ viṣayībhūtanirmāṇakāyaṃ parigṛhya prāṇāyāmāṅgabhūtamanāhataṃ hūṃkāra- vajrākhyaṃ hṛdaye kṛtvā sthāpayitvā tadanantaraṃ raśmivibhāvanā kāryā
daśa raśmayaḥ prāṇāyāme sthitā
teṣāṃ vibhāvanā dakṣiṇādibhedena praveśādikrameṇa pravṛttiḥ
hūmiti anabhilāpyānabhilāpya - sūcakam
mañjuravinaśvaraḥ śrī prabhāśvaraḥ
tasmāt samudetīti mañjuśrīsamayaḥ saṃbhujyate'vaivartikaviṣayī kriyate iti sa eva saṃbhogaḥ jñānasamayaḥ kāyaḥ taṃ prāpya kāyavākcittavajrāṇi nirmitakāya- vākcittāni tāni santi yasya sa kāyavākcittavajriṇaḥ vajrīti prāpte
bodhiḥ paramārthaḥ sattvaḥ samvṛtisatyaṃ te ātmā deho yasya sa bodhisattvātmā
daśabhūmayaḥ daśaraśmayaḥ
teṣu pratiṣṭhitaḥ
yo'nantaroktakrameṇa bhāvakaḥ sa evaṃ saṃbhavet
ekādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā bodhisattvetyādinā samādhi samāpayati
satyadvayena parigṛhītaṃ rūpaṃ bodhisattvaḥ
tacca jñānaṃ sa eva samayaḥ
sa eva traidhātukācchādakatvāccandraḥ sattvārthakaraṇād vajraḥ
tanniṣpādakasamādhirapi tathocyate
sandhyābhāṣā
anantaroktakrameṇa nirmāṇakāyastha cakṣurādyāyatanaviśuddhidvāreṇa phalaṃ darśayitumāha, khadhātvityādi
ādau cakṣurgolakaśūnyīkaraṇena labdhaṃ yatsau- sīryaṃ tat khadhātuḥ
tanmadhyagataṃ thlīṃkāraṃ jvālasuprabhaṃ sūryenduraśmibhiḥ dīpyamānaṃ asyante kṣipyante yenetyastraṃ paramāḥ śakrādividhnagaṇāḥ tadarthamastraṃ paramāstram tanniṣpannena cakṣurbhramaṇena sarvavighnavidhvaṃsanāt paramāstraṃ thlīṃkāraṃ taṃ vajra- kāyena kṣitigarbhasvabhāvena dhyātvā khavajrakāyasamo bhavediti
mahāvajradhara ivānantalokadhātusthitaḥ sarvabhāvān hastāmalakavat paśyet thlīṃ iti uktārtha- sūcakaṃ bījam
khavajrasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ
tasya vyūho mahāmudrā sā ālayaḥ sthānaṃ yasya kṣitigarbhasya sa khavajrasamayavyūhālayaḥ
tatsūcakasamādhirapi tathocyate
evamanantaroktakrameṇa vakṣyamāṇena ca divyacakṣuḥ śrotrādikaṃ bhāvayati yaḥ sa yogī pañcābhijñāsamo mahāvajradharatulyo jāyate
katham ?
buddhābhi- jñāgrasamayaiḥ buddhāḥ vyadhvavartino jināḥ teṣāmabhijñāgrasamayā divyanetrādayaḥ
tairmahāvajradharasamo bhavet
idamiti yadanantaroktaṃ divyaṃ cakṣuḥ sarvasiddhinā- miti
sarve svaparayūthyāḥ teṣāṃ siddhināṃ buddhaṃ svacittaṃ tad yathābhūtamabhi- jānantīti buddhābhijñā mahāyoginaḥ
teṣāmagrasādhanam sarvasiddhīnāmapi pradhāna- sādhanam
buddhamaṇḍalaṃ sarvatathāgatasamayamuttamam
vajravadabhedyātmakaḥ sattvaḥ vajrasattvaḥ
taṃ bhāvayitvā niṣpādya khadhātumadhyagataṃ śrotrasthānabhāvitaprabhāsvara- madhyagataṃ jñānaṃ vajrapāṇiḥ tanniṣpādakamoṅkāraṃ jñānoṅkāraṃ bhāvayet
trivajrasamayo vajrapāṇiḥ
tasya sādhanena tanmayīkaraṇena trivajrākṣobhyo mahāvajradharaḥ anantalokadhātu sthita vividhaśabdānāṃ śrotravilapatitaikaśabdavat grahaṇena tatsamatvāt trivajrākṣobhyasamo bhavati yogītyāha bhagavānakṣobhyavajro mahāvajradharaḥ
akṣobhyasamakāyeneti
mahāvajradharatulyakāyaḥ tathaivāprākṛtavākcittadhāra- ṇāt vākcittāgradhṛk sadā sarvakālaṃ sarveṣu lokadhātuṣu pūjyate akṣobhyavajriṇeti śrotrasthena vajrapāṇinā pūjyate sevyate
akṣobhyasamayo'kṣobhyaḥ tasmāt saṃbhavatīti akṣobhyasamayābhisaṃbhavo vajra- pāṇiḥ
tadutpādakaḥ samādhirapi tathoktaḥ
khadhātvityādinā ākāśagarbhasamādhimāha
khadhātumadhyagataṃ prabhāsvara- madhyagaṃ buddhamaṇḍalaṃ mahāvajradhara kāya maṇḍalaṃ cintayet
ākāśavajraṃ prabhāvitveti
mahāmudrārūpasyāpi ghrāṇendriyadeśe ākāśavajraṃ prabhāsvaraṃ dhyātvā ratnaḥ ākāśagarbhaḥ tanniṣpādaka oṃkāro ratna oṃkārastaṃ vibhāvayet
trivajjrasamayadhyāneneti
trivajraḥ trividho gandhaḥ
taṃ sameti gacchatīti trivajrasamayaḥ ākāśagarbhaḥ
tasya dhyānena tatsvabhāvālamvanena
trivajra- keturmahāvajradharo'nekalokadhātusthitagandhagrahaṇena tatsamo bhavet ityāha bhagavān ratnaketuvajro mahāvajradharaḥ
kāyetyādi
evaṃ bhāvakaḥ aprākṛtakāyavākcittena ratnaketusamaprabha iti
gaganagañjasamādhinā ratnaketusamaddhiḥ sa bhavet
bodhinairātmyaṃ prabhāsvaram tatra jñānaṃ catuḥśūnyātmakaṃ mahāvajradhararūpam
tadeva śrāvakādyaviṣayatvād guhyam
tena samamālayaṃ śarīraṃ yasya sa vodhinairātmyajñānaguhyasamālayaḥ sādhakaḥ ratnasamayaḥ
triprakāro gandha[:] saṃbhujyate anubhūyate yena sa ratnasamayasaṃbhogavajraḥ ākāśagarbhaḥ
tannivartakasamādhirapi tathā
khadhātvityādi
pūrvavanmahāmudrārūpaṃ niṣpādya tasmin jihvāpradeśe lokā- nāmīśvaro bhūtakoṭistatprabhāvitvā[t] dharmo lokeśvaraḥ tannivartaka oṃkāro dharma oṃkāraḥ taṃ bhāvayet
trivatretyādi
trivajraṃ trividho rasaḥ taṃ sameti gacchatīti trivajra- samayo lokeśvaraḥ
tasya dhyānena taddvāreṇa trividharasānubhāvanāt trivajrāmito mahāvajradharaḥ daśadiglokadhātuparyāyaṃ navarasānubhavanena tatsamo bhavet ityāha bhagavānamitavajraḥ
ya evamālambayati, bhavet sa kāyādīnāmamitāyuḥ samatejāḥ
tridvīndriyādi sarvasattvānāṃ mahāyānapathaḥ buddhamārgaḥ
tadartha upāyo yasya sa mahāyānapathodayo bhavet
amitaguṇo mahāvajradharaḥ tasmin traidhātukasthitasarvarasānāṃ jihvendriyasthai- ekādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā
karasavat grahaṇena prabhā sā śobhamānā śrīryasya so'mitaguṇaprabhāśrīḥ lokeśvaraḥ |
tatsūcakasamādhirapi tadākhyaḥ |
khadhātvityādi |
pūrvavadādiyogaṃ kṛtvā samaya oṃkāraṃ prabhāvitvā vajro tpalaṃ prabhāvayedityāmnāyaḥ samayaḥ sarvanivaraṇaviṣkambhī tanniṣpādakaṃ vījaṃ samaya oṃkāraḥ sparśendristhāne prabhāsvaraśodhanapūrvakaṃ tatsamaya oṃkāraṃ prabhā- vitvā vajramevotpalaṃ mukulākāratvād vajrotpalaṃ taṃ prabhāvayet |
| trivajñaṃ trividhaṃ sparśaṃ tat sameti gacchatīti trivajrasamayaḥ |
sarvanivaraṇa- viṣkambhī |
tasya dhyānena trivajro'moghasiddhiḥ aparyantalokadhātusthitā- nekasparśānāmekamudrāpadmagatasparśavajravadanubhavanena tatsamo bhavet
| ityāha bhagavānamoghavajro mahāvajradharaḥ |
asyānuśaṃsāpradarśanaṃ kāyetyādi |
ya evaṃ bhāvayati sa kāyādinā vajrāmoghaḥ amoghasiddhiḥ tatsamā prabhā yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
jñātaṃ sarvanivaraṇaviṣkambhī tasyodadhiḥ sthānamamoghasiddhiḥ |
śrīḥ paramānandasukhaṃ tadasyāstīti śrīmān sarvasattvānāmarthaḥ sarvasattvārthaḥ |
sarvasattvārthajananalakṣaṇaḥ sa saṃbhavati yasmāditi sarvasattvasaṃbhavaḥ amoghasamayo'modhasiddhiḥ |
tasmāt saṃbhavo yasya sa sarvanivaraṇa- viṣkambhī |
tasya raśmayaḥ tadvinirgatavodhicittavindavaḥ |
teṣāṃ saṃbhavaḥ prasavo yasmin samādhau yuktaḥ sa tathoktaḥ
|| evaṃ bodhisattvānāṃ cakṣurindriyādiṣu nyāsamuktvā tathāgatanyāsenāpi niṣpā- dayediti darśayannāha, khadhātvityādi |
khadhātustathatā tanmadhyādāgataṃ khadhātu- madhyagataṃ utpattikrameṇa niṣpannaṃ suprabhaṃ buddhamaṇḍalaṃ mahāvajradhararūpaṃ cintedātmānaṃ bhāvayet
| tasya cakṣuḥsthāne vairocanavatraṃ prabhāvitvā trikāya oṃkāraṃ prabhā- prabhāvitvā trikāya oṃkāraṃ vairocanavajraṃ prabhāvayedityānupūrvyā yogaḥ |
trikāyo vairocanaḥ tanniṣpādaka oṃkāraḥ trikāya oṃkāraḥ |
taṃ cakṣurgolake bhāvayitvā tatparāvṛttyā vairocanameva cakṣurindriyasvarūpeṇa tāvat
|| trivajraṃ trividharūpaṃ cakṣurindriyasvabhāvena sametyālambayatīti trividhavajra- samayo vairocanaḥ |
tasya cakṣurbhāvena dhyānena trivajjravairocanamahāvajradharaḥ pūrvavat tatsamo bhavet
| ityāha bhagavān vairocanamahāvajraḥ |
kāyetyādinānuśaṃsāmāha |
evaṃ bhāvayati yaḥ sādhakaḥ sa kāyādivajreṇa vairocanasamaprabhaḥ vairocanasamānatejāḥ jñānasvarūpeṇa jñānamūrtitvena saṃbudhyate guhyasamājapradīpodyotane jñāyata iti jñānasaṃbodhiḥ trikāyabhedyaṃ mahāvajradharapadaṃ tat sādhayatīti trikāyā- bhedyasādhakaḥ sa sādhako bhavet
| kāyavākcittatattvenālambyate gṛhyata iti kāyavākcittālamvanaṃ mahā- vajradhararūpaṃ tasya cakṣurindriyasthāne sthitvā trividhaṃ rūpaṃ saṃbudhyate jānātīti saṃbodhiḥ |
sa evāpratihataprasaratvād vajraḥ tanniṣpādakasamādhirapi kāyavākcittā- lamvanasaṃbodhivajro nāma |
nāsandhyam |
parvateṣvityādinā sādhanasthānamāha |
parvateṣu phalapuṣpādyalaṅkṛteṣu giri- śikhareṣu vivikteṣu janasaṃpātarahiteṣu nadīprasravaṇeṣu udyānādinikaṭapradeśeṣu śmaśānādiṣviti |
ādiśabdādekavṛkṣaśūnyāgāradevatālayādyanyeṣvapi mano'nu- kūleṣu idamanantaroktaṃ dhyānasamuccayaṃ kṣitigarbhādisamādhivṛndaṃ kāryaṃ karaṇīyam
| neyārthaḥ
|| na / parvateṣu pīnastanajaghanādisamanviteṣu nārījaneṣu vivikteṣu kanyājaneṣu nadīprasravaṇeṣu sarvajanasādhāraṇeṣu veśyādistrījaneṣu śmaśānādiṣviti śmaśāna- vallokargārhateṣu caṇḍālarajakādistrījaneṣvidaṃ dhyānasamuccayaṃ kāryamiti nītārthaḥ
|| evaṃ sthānasampadamāsādya vairocananyāsena divyacakṣuḥ sādhanamabhidhāya tathaivādhipatyādinyāsena divyaśrotrādisādhanamāha akṣobhyetyādi |
akṣo- bhyaniṣpādakaṃ jñānamoṃkārabījaṃ tatparāvṛttyā saṃbhūtaṃ vajramakṣobhyaḥ akṣobhya- jñānavajrādayo'kṣobhyaratnasaṃbhavāmitābhāmoghasiddhayaḥ tān
| pūrvavat
| śrotrāya- tanādiṣu prabhāsvarālamvanapūrvakaṃ dhyātvā pañcābhijñāprayogeṇa mahāvajradharayogena sthāne kṣitigarbhādisthāne buddhāgrā vairocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ bhāvanā kāryā
| ityāha bhagavān
| mahāvajrāḥ sādhakāḥ teṣāṃ samayavajraḥ abhedyasamayaḥ tamupadeṣṭuṃ jānātīti mahāvajrasamayaḥ vajrābhijño mahāvajradharaḥ yathārutam
|| utpattikrameṇa bījavinyāsapūrvakaṃ pañcābhijñāsādhanamupadiśya niṣpanna- krameṇopakṣipannāha |
pañcaśūlamityādi |
pañcaśūlapañcendriyāṇi śūlavadviṣayeṣu nimajjanāt tadeva mahāvajramavyāhataprasaratvāt
| pañcajvālā udvāhādi - pañcaraśmayaḥ tairvibhūṣitaṃ maṇḍitaṃ spharaṇasaṃharaṇanirvartanāt
| pañcasthānaṃ rūpa- śabdādikaṃ tasmin prayogeṇa prasaraṇena vajrābhijño mahāvajradharaḥ tatsamo bhavet
| ekādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā svamantraṃ praveśādisvabhāvamakṣarattrayam anavarataṃ ghaṭīyantravat
| aharniśaṃ pravartamānatvāt
| cakraṃ sphuliṅgagahanākulaṃ pañcaraśmyātmakapañcavajrāṇi teṣu prayogeṇa preraṇe vajrābhijñāsamo bhavet
| khavajraṃ svamahāmudrārūpaṃ tanmadhyagataṃ sthitaṃ cakramindriyavṛndaṃ buddhā vairoca- nādayaḥ tadupalakṣitajvālā sitādiraśmayaḥ tatsamaprabhaṃ tatsamānavarṇaṃ dhyātvā vijñāya buddhā rūpādayo viṣayā teṣu praveśena samāyogena buddhāstvatra pañcaskandhāḥ teṣāmāśrayaḥ jñānamūrtirmahāvajradharaḥ tatsamo bhavediti
|| niṣpannatrameṇaiva samādhyantaramupakṣipannāha |
buddhamaṇḍalamityādi |
budhyata iti buddhaṃ kiṃ tat ?
jñeyamaṇḍalaṃ svakāyam
| traidhātukakāyamadhimucya vairocanaṃ nyasediti vairocanaṃ kalpayet
| oṃkāraṃ hṛdaye dhyātveti |
vaidhātukasvabhāva- kāyavajrahṛdaye oṃkārajñānasattvaṃ dhyātvā mantre jñānasattve vijñānabhāvanā vindvātmakasamādhisattvabhāvanā kartavyā
|| evamabhyasyato yoginaścittaṃ yadā nirodhamadhyagataṃ vindubhāvanāmapyapahāya yadā prabhāsvaraniṣṭhaṃ jāyate, tadā sa yogī cintāmaṇivat abhilaṣitārtha- prapūrakaḥ saṃbhārādvayopetatvāt
| śrīmān sarvabuddhāgro mahāvajradharastaṃ sādhayatīti sarvabuddhāgrasādhako bhavati
| evaṃ svacittaṃ traidhātukacittasvabhāvaṃ prakṣobhyaṃ prabhāvayet
| tatastraidhātukasamaya- sattvahṛdaye hūṃkāraṃ jñānasattvaṃ dhyātvā cittaṃ vindugataṃ nyasediti samayasattvahṛdayastha- jñānasattvākhyo vindugataṃ svacittaṃ smādhisattvarūpaṃ nyaset
| svavāgvajjaṃ tu pūrvavat traidhātukavāksvabhāvaṃ dhyātvā tadamitābhaṃ prabhāvayet
| samayasattvasya hṛdaye āḥkāraṃ jñānasattvarūpaṃ dhyātvā vajraṃ svacittaṃ samādhi- sattvagataṃ nyaset sthāpayet
| samayāgra idamityādinānantaroktaṃ samādhitrayasyānuśaṃsāmupasaṃharati mutpattikramabhāvanaṃ tasyāgraṃ trivajraṃ sarvasattvakāyavākcittaṃ tena svakāyavākcitta- vajrasyābhedyatvena bhāvanaṃ trivajrābhedyatvena bhāvanaṃ nirodhasamayo dhāraṇaṃ tajjñā- yate yena tannirodhasamayajñānaṃ buddhā rūpādayaskandhāḥ teṣāṃ siddhiḥ prākṛtadehāpa- gamena audāryagāmbhīryātmakajñānadehaparigrahaḥ
| tatsamāvahati prāpayatīti buddha- siddhisamāvaham
|| amumevārthaṃ prakārāntareṇa dyotayannāha |
khavatreṇetyādi |
khavajraṃ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane prabhāsvaraṃ tatra dhāryata iti khavajradhātuḥ traidhātukaṃ tatrasthaṃ bhāvayet
| svaccha maṇḍalaṃ kāyavajtraṃ traidhātukaṃ kāyavākcitte groṃkāra jñānasattvaṃ dhyāyāt
| taidhātukaṃ kāyavākcittopamardanena jñānakāyaṃ bhāvayediti yāvat
| evaṃ kimityāha |
kalpaṃ saṃtiṣṭhatīti sarvasattvamanorathasya kalpanaṃ saṃpādanaṃ kalpaḥ
| tasmin tiṣṭhati āsaṃsāraṃ trikāyātmako bhūtvā sakalajagadabhipretārthasaṃpādana- samarthastiṣṭhatīti yāvat
| samādhidvayamapyanenaiva vyākhyānena bodhyavyam
| dhyātvā trivajrakalpasamaya iti |
kāyādivatrairjagadarthaṃ kalpayati saṃpādayatīti trivajrakalpasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ
| yataḥ prabhṛtītyādyanuśaṃsā |
imaṃ dvividhaṃ
yogaṃ prāptābhiṣeko gurubhiranujñāto yataḥ prabhṛtyārabhya kāyavākcittavajriṇo mahāvajradharasya yaḥ paṭhe[di]ti yaḥ parebhya upadiśet, cintayediti svayama- bhyaset, sa ca vajradharo bhavet
na kevalaṃ bhāvaka ityabhiprāyaḥ
sarvatathāgatamantrā oṃkārādayaḥ
tairniṣpannasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ
tadeva mantraparāyaṇānāṃ tattvaṃ vidyā prajñāpāramitā tatsamutthitapuruṣo vidyāpuruṣaḥ
sa eva jñānasamayatvāduttamaḥ sarvatathāgatamantrasamayatattvaṃ ca vidyāpuruṣottamaśca yasmin sarvatathāgatamantrasamayatattvavidyāpuruṣottamaḥ paṭalaḥ
sandhyābhāṣā
evaṃ karmapathaviśuddha pratipādyobhayakramasya mahāmudrāpariṇāmapradarśanāya dvādaśapaṭalamupakṣipannāha
athetyādi
bhūrbhuvaḥ svaḥśāsanāt śāstā
tasyaiva janakatvāt sraṣṭā
jñānāgrā ratnapudgalāḥ tān vajropamasamādhau pratiṣṭhāpanena sādhayatīti jñānāgraṃsādhakaḥ trivajrasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ tasya tattvaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ tasmin tadvāgvajraṃ vakṣyamāṇalakṣaṇamudāharet udājahāra iti prāpte, khatulye - tyādi
khamākāśaṃ tena tulyaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ tena samamekaṃ khatulyasamayam, sarveṣāṃ paramārthasatyaniṣṭhatvāt
khatulyasamatāṃ bhūteṣu gateṣu nirvikalpasvabhāvo yeṣāṃ te nirvikalpasvabhāvinaḥ
teṣu nirvikalpasvabhāviṣu
ataeva svabhāvaśuddha- dharmeṣu ayaṃ vakṣyamāṇo nāṭaka iva prapañco vibhāvyate pradarśyate
nāsandhyam
mahāṭavītyādi
mahāṭavyāḥ pradeśeṣu prakṛṣṭeṣu deśeṣu jalāśayādimanoharatvāt
phalapuṣpādyalaṅkṛte parvate vijane anyasminnapi vivikte sarvalaukikalokottarāḥ siddhayo yasmin samuccīyante saṃgrahaṃ gacchanti tat sarvasiddhisamuccayaṃ buddhatvaṃ sādhyaṃ sādhanīyam
neyārthaḥ
.
ārṣavyākhyānamāha ākāśakalpanāyogaiḥ skandhamahāṭavī matāḥ
asthānasthitiyogena pradeśe mahatālaye
saṃbhogakalpavṛkṣe'smin vividhe siddhipuṣtite
nirmāṇaphalasaṃśobhe samyaksasaṃbodhiparvate
niḥsvabhāvavijane ramye sādhyaṃ tad vajrasaṃjñakam
iti nītārthaḥ
pa maṃ iti vakṣyamāṇasamādhisūcakam bījam
kāyetyādi
manaḥ svabhāvena yujyate sevyata iti mañjuḥ
sa evābhedyatvād vajraḥ
tasya prabhāvanā mañju- vajraprabhāvanā kāryā
kuna svakāyavākcittavatreṣu na kevalaṃ svakāyādiṣu tataḥ traidhātukakāyavākcittaspharaṇamapi tataḥ mañjuvajñasya samo bhavediti
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane yojanānāṃ śataṃ yojanaśataṃ asaṃkhyeyapramāṇavistāraṃ dīptavatraṃ svakārye yayā sā dīptavajrā tayā prabhayā anantalokadhātumavabhāsayatyālokaṃ karoti
śuddhātmā māyopamadehatvāt
dvātriṃśanmahāpuruṣalakṣaṇādyalaṅkṛtatvāt sarvā- laṅkārabhūṣitaḥ
tamevaṃvidhaṃ jñānakāyalābhinaṃ brahmādayo devāḥ kadācana kadāyadāpi na paśyanti kiṃ punaranye, avaivartakagocaratvāt
mañjuśrīvajrāgrasamayo mañjuśrī- mahāmudrārūpaṃ tasya pṛthagjanānāmadṛśyāspṛśyatvenāntardhānakārī nāma samādhirayam
imameva māyāmayadehaṃ prabhāsvaraviśodhanena sādhayitumāha
viṇmūtretyādi
viṇmūtramindriyaviṣayāḥ tairanyonyamīlanāt pañcasamayaiḥ
trivajrābhedyaṃ cittaṃ tatsaṃbhūtaiḥ indriyaviṣayaistrilohasahitāmābhāsatrayasaṃyuktāṃ mūtiṃ kṛtvā saṃpādya mukhe prabhāsvare piṇḍagrāhānubhedābhyāṃ praveśya bhāvayet
tatra prabhāsvare sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvatārkikāṇāmabhedyamaviṣayaṃ cittaṃ jñānakāryaṃ prabhāvayet
ya evaṃ bhāvayati yogī tatkṣaṇoṃdeva vyutthānalakṣaṇādeva mañju - vajrasamaprabho bhavediti
sandhyābhāṣā
evaṃ mañjuśrīsamayasamādhimupadiśya cakravidyādharādisiddhimupakṣipannāha
svamantreṇetyādi
ādau vairocanasamādhimāmukhīkṛtya svamantreṇa vairocana- niṣpādakena praṇavenaḥ cakramaṣṭāraṃ śvetavarṇaṃ sphuliṅgasuprabhaṃ sphuliṅgākṛti- raśmibhirmaṇḍitaṃ purato dhyātvā sarvabuddhānāṃ tathāgatakulīnānāṃ ālayaṃ sthāna- bhūtaṃ dhyātveti yāvat dhūmāyati ruṣmāyati prajvalati vā tāvadabhyasyate gṛhītvā tiṣṭhet yaḥ sa yogī buddhasamo mahāvajradharasadṛśo bhavet
evaṃ siddhasya vidyādharasya mahimānamāha ṣaṭtriṃśadityādi
ṣaḍadhikānāṃ triṃśatpramāṇānāṃ sumerūṇāṃ yāvat saṃkhyāḥ paramāṇavaḥ tāvatpramāṇā anucarāḥ parivārāḥ tasya bhavanti
kīdṛgvidhāḥ sarvavajradharavadavinaśvarasvabhāvāḥ
cakraṃ yena samādhinā sameti prāpnoti sa cakrasamayanāma samādhiḥ
svamantreṇetyādi vairocanayogavad draṣṭavyam
svamantro hūṃkāro maṇḍala - mākāśamaṇḍalaṃ yoṣito bhaviṣyantītyasya kāmaiśvaryaṃ darśayati mahāvajradharo - paṃmatvāt mahāvajjradharo bhavet
rudranamaskṛta ityādhakavacanena traidhātukanama- skṛtatvaṃ sūcayati
rudrasya traidhātukanamaskṛtatvāt
dvādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā vajro mahāvajradharaḥ samatīti sameṃ saha atanti gacchantīti samatā ḍākinyaḥ
vajraśca samatāśca sahacaryaḥ priyāḥ
sa ca tāśca yasmin samādhau sa tathoktaḥ
evaṃ dharmā amitābhakulīnāḥ teṣāmālaya utpattisthānaṃ vajradharmo lokeśvaraḥ tatsamo bhavet
ṣaṭtriṃśatsumerusaṃbhūtāḥ yāvatpramāṇāḥ paramāṇavaḥ tāvatsaṃkhyakāḥ śuddhātmā padmakulīnā buddhā upāyarūpāṇi pūjāgrāḥ prajñārūpāṇi
teṣāṃ maṇḍalaṃ samūhaḥ
tasmin sthāpayanti sarve mantramudrāgaṇā: svādhipatitvena sādhakamabhiṣiñcantīti yāvat
padmāḥ padmakulīnāstaiḥ samatā yasmin samādhāvabhedāt sa tathocyate
tiṣṭhedityādi vibhaktārthaḥ
triguhyaṃ paryupāsata iti
sarvabuddhasaṃvandhi- kāyavākcittaguhyaṃ paryupāsate adhigacchati tadātmako bhavatīti yāvat
svamantraṃ svamantrāditi prāpte
pañcaraśmayaḥ anyūnādhikatvena samāḥ yasyāṃ prabhāyāṃ sā pañca raśmisamā prabhā yasya taṃ khaḍgaṃ pañcaraśmisamaprabhaṃ pāṇau pāṇinā gṛhya gṛhītvā viśālākṣaḥ sādhaka: trivajrā niṣpannāḥ vidyā mahāmudrāḥ tān tadātmakatvena dhārayatīti trivajravidyādharaḥ
traidhātukeṣu ye mahānto mahadhikāḥ taiḥ pūjyāḥ daityendrāḥ asurapradhānā brahmendra [[] brahmapradhānāḥ tairapi namaskṛtaśca bhavet
trisāhasre lokadhātau ekaśūraḥ tattrādhipatitvāt guhyadharāḥ sāmānya- sādhanasthāḥ teṣāmuttamaḥ pradhānaḥ kāyavākcittavajriṇaḥ kāyavākcittavajrī sādhakaḥ yadabhilaṣati yatprārthayati tādṛśīṃ tatsvabhāvāṃ cittavajraprabhāvitāṃ manaḥ saṃkalpitāṃ siddhi lokikalokottarāṃ dadāti prayacchati
sarvatathātmakaḥ khaṅgaḥ sarvakhaṅgaḥ abhedāt samādhirapi sarvakhaḍgo nāma
evaṃ vajracakrādisiddhi nirdiśyedānīṃ vāhyadravyanirvṛttagulikāsiddhimapanīya mantreṇaiva gulikāsiddhinidarśanāyāha
oṅkāramityādi
oṅkārameva caṇa- kāsthipramāṇagulikāṃ dhyātvā madhye gulikābhyantarataḥ svadevatāvimbaṃ dhyātvā dvā mukhe śiroddhāre praveśya svarūpeṇa vicintya vibhāvayet punaḥ punarabhyasediti
ya evamabhyasyati, sa tatkṣaṇādeva tadārabhya bodhisattvasamaprabha iti
boṃdhiḥ paramārthasatyam, sattvaḥ saṃvṛtisatyaṃ tayoradvayasvarūpeṇa yaḥ sthitaḥ sa bodhisattvaḥ mahāvajradharaḥ
tasya samā prabhā ṛddhiryasya sa bodhisattvasamaprabhaḥ sādhakaḥ saṃjāyate tejasā durnirīkṣyatvāt
uditādityasaṃkāśaḥ jāmbūnada- samaprabha ityanuśaṃsā
evamākāraḥ sthityātmakaṃ raktavarṇaṃ mukhe kaṇṭhasthāne dhyāyāt
bodhijñāne mahāvajradharahaṅkāramapi gulikārūpeṇa hṛtpuṇḍarīkopari vyutthānātmakaṃ dhyāyāt
idānīṃ mahāmudrārūpeṇāpi siddhimupakṣipannāha
khadhātvityādi
khadhātu- stathatā
tatraṃ svacchaṃ candramaṇḍalaṃ vibhāvya tanmadhyasthaṃ vairocanamātmānaṃ tasya haste cakramaṣṭāraṃ dhvātvā vidyādharo bhavet
etacca kathaṃ kuryāditi cet ?
mahācakrakulamityādi
mahācakrakulaṃ vairocanakulaṃ tat pūrvavat dhvātvā jñānāgrasya vairocanasya jñānavajreṇa sādhakena kāyāgrayogataḥ vairocanayogavān
khadhātvityādi khadhātau vajraṃ sūryamaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā tanmadhyasthamadvayajñānā- tmakamakṣobhyamātmānaṃ bhāvayet
haste vajramityādi pūrvavat
khadhātau ratnaṃ vibhāvya tanmadhyasthaṃ ratnavatraṃ ratnasaṃbhavamātmānaṃ bhāvayet
haste ratnamityādi vivṛtam
khadhātau dharmāṣṭadalapadmaṃ dhātvā tatmadhyasthamamitāyuṣamātmānaṃ vibhāvya sarvaṃ pūrvavat saṃpādayet
khadhātau samayamadhyasthaṃ viśvavajjrasthamamoghāgramityādiḥ vivṛtārthaḥ
evaṃ cakrādicihnaṃ sādhanamuktvā anyadapi krodharājacihnaṃ sādhayitavyamiti triśūlādayo gṛhyante
vajraprayogata iti akṣobhyasamādhinā sādhyaḥ
tasyākṣobhyasya dhyānenaite triśūlādayaḥ sidhyanti sādhyante
kaiḥ kāyavā kcitta sādhanairiti
kāyavā- kcittāni ye prākṛtabhāvamapanīya viśiṣṭabhāvena niṣpādayituṃ śaktāste kāyavā- kcittasādhanāḥ taiḥ sādhyante netarairiti mahāsamayo mahāyānaṃ tatra siddhiranantaroktā tatprakāśako vajraḥ mahāsamayasiddhivajra ityāheti prakaraṇopasaṃhāraḥ
siddhisādhanopāyamāha catuṣpathetyādi
brāhmaṇādikulasaṃbhūtamudrācatuṣṭayī
athavā ekavṛkṣe ekākinyā vā ekaliṅge : svasamānaveśadhāraṇāt śivālaye caṇḍālādivanitāsu
eṣu yathālabdhasthāneṣu vajraṃ jñānatrayaṃ ākṛṣyate sākṣādanu- bhūyate yo na tadvajjākarṣaṇaṃ samāpattilakṣaṇaṃ tatsādhayet viśeṣato'tiśayenānya- smin viṣaye mandapracāratvāt
sandhyābhāṣā
evaṃ cādhigataviśeṣeṇa svārthasampattimupadiśya vineyajanacittāvarji dvādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā triyoga ityādināha
triyogamantrapuruṣaṃ trivajjātmakamantrapuruṣaṃ triyogavajriṇa- miti visattvātmakaṃ dhyātvātmānaṃ niṣpādya ātmanaḥ kāyavākcittamaṅakuśa- svabhāvaṃ dhyāyāt
kimarthaṃ buddhānāṃ ratnapudgalānāṃ jñānabuddhīnāmadvayajñānārthinā- mākarṣaṇārtham
tattraiva prayogaṃ darśayannāha vāyavyetyādi yaṅkārodbhavaṃ vāyumaṇḍalaṃ cañcala- rūpaṃ dhyātvā sādhyaṃ tatra saṃsthāpya tasya kāyavākcitteṣu ātmānaṃ kāyavākcitta- raśmyaṅgaṃ vyavasajya śanaiḥ śanaiḥ ātmasakāśamāgatya vāyumaṇḍalādavaruhya laṅkārodbhavaṃ māhendramaṇḍale sthitvā praṇataṃ dhyāyāt
buddhākarṣaṇamiti yadananta- roktavidhānaṃ tad bhavyajanacittākarṣaṇamuttamamanyattrāsādhāraṇaṃ vidhānam
anenaiva prayogeṇa daśadiksamayavyavasthitā yoṣito vyākṛṣyopabhuñjayet svājñāyāṃ niyojayet
khadhātusamayā devyaḥ tāsāṃ vajrākarṣaṇamabhedyākarṣaṇam
vairocanetyādi
vairocanamahācakraṃ vairocanacihnabhṛtaṃ cakraṃ vairocanakulī- nānāmutpattikāraṇatvāt
jinālayaṃ tadaṅkuśākāreṇa dhyātvā sādhyahṛdaye avasajya pūrvavadākarṣayet
anenaiva krameṇāvaśiṣṭairapi citrairākarṣaṇaṃ kuryādityāha vajñetyādi
traidhātukasamayā yakṣiṇyādayaḥ
tāsāmākarṣaṇaṃ vaidhātukasamayā- karṣaṇam
sarvākāravaropetamiti
vairocanādyākāraparighaṭitaṃ buddhavimbaṃ mahā- vajradharamātmānaṃ vibhāvayet
pāṇau tasya haste kāyavākcittaṃ sādhyaṃ tamaṅakuśādīn vibhāvayediti
aṅakuśādibhiriti grāhyam
aṅakuśenākṛṣya pāśena vadhvā vāyumaṇḍalamāropya ānīya pāṇinā gṛhītvā mudrayedityabhiprāyaḥ
anena mudraṇaprayogeṇa sādhakaḥ padaṃ vajrasattvapadaṃ tasya karma tadanuṣṭheyā kriyā tat karotīti padakarmakṛd bhavet
sarvākāretyādinā anantaroktamevārthaṃ vivṛṇoti
kāyavajraṃ svakāyaṃ yathoktalakṣaṇopetaṃ dhyāyāt
tadetad vairocanīkaraṇam
jihvaiva vajraṃ jihvāvajraṃ tasya prayogo mantrajalpanaṃ tena vāgamitābhaḥ tatsamo bhavedityamitābhīkaraṇam
svadevatāhaṅkāravān hastasthasādhyaṃ japyamantrākṣarārthaṃ ca dhyātvā vajra samo bhavediti tatsamo bhavedityakṣobhyakaraṇam
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane anena prakāreṇa triguhyaṃ kāyavākcittaṃ tatsamayaṃ trikāyādisvabhāvaṃ yāntīti triguhyasamayāvairocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ pūjāgrīṃ pūjāṃ yathāsvabhāvaparijñāna- lakṣaṇāṃ pūjāṃ pūjya kṛtvā prasādhayet
idamanantaroktaprayogaṃ saṃpādayet
yadanantaroktaṃ vidhānaṃ tat sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvatathāgatasaṃvandhisāraṃ viśiṣṭa- bhūtaṃ guhyaṃ rahasyaṃ samuccīyate ākṛṣyate yena tadvidhānaṃ guhyasamuccayaṃ mahāguhyaṃ devādyākarṣaṇaṃ samayamalaṅghayabhūtaṃ yasya sa mahāguhyasamayaḥ mahāvajradhara evamāheti saṃgītikāraḥ karmābhede māṃsāhārapradarśanāyāha mahāmāṃsetyādi
mahānto manuṣyāḥ teṣāṃ māṃsaṃ mṛtakodbhūtatvāt samayānaṃ tena vali datvā trivajra- muttamaṃ svadehaṃ sādhayet rabhasā prāpayet
niravadyalabdhanarapiśitena vighnopa- śamanārthaṃ sarvabhūtānāṃ vali dattvā khecaratvaṃ sādhayediti
samayāgreṇeti naramāṃsādikaṃ vinmūttrairyuktaṃ samayāgraṃ tena vidyādharatvaṃ sādhayitukāmo vali dadyāt
hastī eva samayo mṛtakatvāt
tasya piśitena pañcābhijñakāmo vali- dadyāt
tathaivāśvamāṃsenāntardhānasiddhi sādhayet
karmabhede noktamāṃsalābhe vidhimāha śvānetyādi
śvānamāṃsena nirdiṣṭāṃ siddhi prasādhayediti jñeyam
tathaiva niravadyagomāṃsopahāreṇa vajrā duḥsādhyāḥ rājādayaḥ
niravadyamāṃsābhāve vidhimāha uktamāṃsālābhe'pi piśitaṃ dhyātvā saṃpā- dayet
athavā piṣṭādinā narādimṛtakaṃ kṛtvā tatsvarūpeṇa niṣkrāntaṃ jīvitaṃ ca dhyātvā tadavayavena sādhayediti
anenānantaroktavajjrayogenābhe- dyopahāravidhinā sarvabuddhairadhiṣṭhyate
sādhaka: abhipretārthasiddhi prāpyate
dehapuṣṭyarthamāpyāyanavidhimāha
sarvākāretyādi
kāyavākcittavajriṇaṃ mahāvajradharamātmānaṃ dhyātvā tadanantaraṃ hṛtpadmasthitacandramaṇḍale jñānasamayaṃ jñānasattvaṃ dhyātvā tasyāpi hṛdaye samādhisattvaṃ dhyāyāt
mukuṭe uṣṇīṣa- sthāne vajrāgradhāriṇaṃ mahāvajradharaṃ candrāṃśupuñjasannibhaṃ sravatsudhaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ samayajñānasattvākhyānaṃ prīṇanaṃ dhyāyāt
idamanantaroktasarvasamayanaye uttamaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ samayāgreṇa sādhakena paraṃ rva sarvāsiddhikaraṃ dhyānaṃ kartavyaṃ karaṇīyameva
sarvasamayāḥ sādhakāḥ teṣāṃ jñānavatraṃ bodhicittamāhāraḥ prīṇanaṃ yasmin dvādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā samādhau sa tathā
kulabhedenāha jihvāsamayavajrāgra iti
samayavajrāḥ pañca- kulayoginaḥ
teṣāmagramakṣobhyakulam
tasmiṃsthitaḥ
jihvāhuṅkāravajriṇaṃ huṅkāraparāvṛttaṃ vajraṃ dhyāyāt
kiñcidannādikamabhyavaharan paśñcāmṛtaprayogeṇa pañcāmṛtīkaraṇenopayujya trivajraṃ kāyavākcittaprasrabdhimāpnuyāt
idamanantaroktamāhārakṛtyalakṣaṇaṃ vajjranayottamam
āḥkārauṅkārasamayā- dapi kartavyam
amitābha yoginā jihvā prākāraparāvṛttilakṣaṇaṃ padmaṃ dhyātvā vairocanayoginā praṇavanirvṛttaṃ cakraṃ dhyātvā kartavyamiti yāvat
anenāhāra- kṛtyayogena kāyādiprasrabdhi labdhā mahāvajradharatulyo bhavet
samayavajraṃ pañcāmṛtīkaraṇalakṣaṇo dhyānaviśeṣo'mṛtamālāmṛtapuñjasamaya- vajreṇa niṣpāditā amṛtamālā yasmin samādhau
sa tathocyate
vyāpāratāṃ yogikriyāmukhena lokikasiddhi nirdiśyedānīṃ niṣpannakramasthānāṃ mudrāsiddhayu- pāyaṃ trivajñetyādinākṣipati
trivajraṃ kāyavākcittaṃ sameti līyate yasmin sa trivajrasamayaḥ paramārthasatyaṃ tadeva siddhyagraṃ tasmin trikāyaṃ dharmasambhoga- nirmāṇalakṣaṇaṃ kāyattrayaṃ tad vajraṃ yasmāsti sa trikāyavajriṇo mahāvajradharo bhavet jāyate
vajrīti prāpte vajriṇa ityārṣam
evaṃ niṣpaṇṇasya prātihārya- sandarśanaṃ daśadigityādi daśadigvyavasthita niṣpannasarvasattvānāṃ cintāmaṇī- nāmudadhiścintāmaṇyodadhiḥ tat pra[[pto ] bhavet
vajrātmā vajrakāyaḥ avabhāsayati svadehaprabha [ yā] lokadhātuṃ samantata iti daśadiglokadhātuṃ ālokayati' tasyā vyāptikathanaṃ cakrasamayetyādi
cakrasamayo vairocanaḥ tatprativaddhā siddhiścakrasamayasiddhiḥ saivāgrayā tasmin
vuddhakāyasamo bhavet tatsadṛśo bhavet
evamakṣobhyādiprativaddhasiddhinimittam, tasyopalakṣaṇatvāt
samantataḥ siddha iti
anantaroktasarvabhāvena siddhiḥ sarvataḥ daśasu dikṣu gaṅgānadī- vālukāsama ma lokadhātūn vicaret
sarveṣvityādi
sarvasamayāḥ ḍākaḍākinyādi- samayāḥ teṣāmagrāsteṣu vidyā tatsādhanamantraḥ taṃ dhārayatīti vidyādharaḥ teṣāmapi -prabhuḥ bhavet
sarvetyādi
anantarokta ḍākādisamayasiddhayagre sati kāyavajraprabhāvanā sumahāmudrāprabhāvanā
antardhāneṣu sarveṣviti vā[k]kriyākāyāvayavāntardhāneṣu sthitvā trisāhasraikāvabhāsakaḥ
trisāhasralokadhātūnekāvabhāsenāvabhāsayannapi guhyasamājapradīpodyotane sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvasiddhīnāṃ saṃvandhinīṃ vikṛti harati gṛhṇāti
surāgrāḥ śakrādayaḥ
teṣāṃ kanyāmākṛṣyopabhuṅkte anubhavati
gaṅgetyādi
gaṅgāvālukāpramāṇān jinān trivajro mahāvajradharaḥ
tasyālayamanantāparyanto lokadhātuḥ tatrasthaṃ sthitān cakṣurvajreṇa sūkṣmaviśuddhidivya- netreṇa paśyate paśyati
hastatalagatamāmalakamiva
gaṅgāvālukasamaiḥ kṣetraiḥ kṣetreṣu ye śabdāḥ upāttānupāttobhayamahābhūtajāḥ tān sarvān śrotre vilagataṃ śabdamiva śṛṇoti
abhijñā ṛddhistatra ṛṣabhaḥ prabhuḥ pradhānaḥ abhijñārṣabhaḥ
kāyavākcittalakṣaṇaiḥ kāyādisvabhāvaiḥ gaṅgāvālukāsameṣu kāyavākcitta- lakṣaṇeṣu lokadhātuṣu sarvasattvānāṃ nāṭakavannānāprakāreṇa spandamānaṃ cittākhyaṃ ṣaṣṭyuttaraśataprakṛtīnāṃ kāraṇabhūtaṃ sa vetti
saṃsārasthitisaṃbhavamiti
śubhāśubhakarmavaśenānādisaṃsāre pañcagati- paribhūmaṇaṃ tatsaṃbhavaṃ pūrvanivāse pūrvajanmani samayaṃ kālaviśeṣaṃ dinatrayamiva atītadinatrayamiva smaret
gaṅgānadīvālukāpramāṇairātmānaḥ kāyairbuddha meghādyalaṅkṛtaiḥ vairocanādi- pañcakulīnadevatācakrādyalaṅkṛtaiḥ gaṅgāvālukopameṣu kalpeṣu sthitvā spharet nirmiṇoti
ṛddhyā agravajraṃ prabhāsvaraṃ buddhasyāsti so ṛddhāgravajriṇaḥ varjīti prāpte
samayābhijño mahāvajradharaḥ evaṃmāha
vajracakṣurityādi
vajro mahāvajradharaḥ tasyaḥ cakṣuḥ prabhāsvaraviśuddhā dṛṣṭiḥ vajracakṣuḥ
evamanyat
anena parārthaṃ prati kimityāha
buddhetyādi
buddhābhijñā divyacakṣurādayaḥ tairarthasiddhiḥ samyakparārthasampat
buddhakāyasamo bhavediti
yathā buddhakāyā anantāḥ tattvārthāḥ kriyāśca sāpyanantā
evaṃ parārthasampadyuktāṃ siddhi kathaṃ viharatītyāha
gaṅgetyādi
gaṅgāvālukāpramāṇaiḥ parivārairabhedyaceṭādibhiḥ pūjāprasarasahitaiḥ parivṛtaḥ puraskṛtaḥ samantato daśadigvyavasthiteṣu lokadhātuṣu kāyavākcitto mahāvajradharaḥ vicaret viharatīti phalaikadeśaḥ
vijahārapadādārabhy yathāsaṃbhavaṃ sāmānyottamasādhanamupadiśyedānīmutpa- ttikramasādhanāṅgaṃ punaḥ spaṣṭayannāha
sevetyādi
sevyate ālambyata iti dvādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā I sevā tathatā
saiva samayaḥ bhūbhāgādināṃ saṃyojanaṃ niṣpādanaṃ sevāsamayasaṃyogaṃ prathamamaṅgaṃ śūnyatālamvanaṃ sūryādyālambanamupasādhanam
tadeva mantravinyāsaparyantaṃ saṃbhavatītyupasādhanasaṃbhavo dvitīyaṃ sādhanārthaṃ ca samayamiti
sādhanopasthāpanāyārthaḥ adhyeṣaṇaṃ sādhanārthaḥ samayaḥ sa [ me ]ti gacchatīti samayaḥ samādhisattvo jñāna- sattvaśca sādhanārthaśca samayaśca tṛtīyam
avaśiṣṭasya maṇḍalarājāgrīkarmarā- jāgrīparyantasya mahataḥ parārthasya sādhanaṃ mahāsādhanaṃ taccaturthakam
etadaṅgacatuṣṭayaṃ vijñāya vajrabhedena kulabhedena tatastadanantaraṃ karmāṇi vakṣyamāṇānyuktamiva sādhayedityuddeśaḥ
sevāsamādhītyādinā uddiṣṭānyaṅgāni nirdiśati
bodhiliṅgālamvanaṃ sevā
saiva samādhīyate cetasi sthāpyata iti samādhiḥ saṃyojanaṃ saṃyogaḥ
kiṃ tat ? bhūbhāgādermaṇḍalacakraṃ paryantasyādhimuktyā niṣpādanaṃ sevā samādhiśca saṃyogaśca sevāsamādhisaṃyogaṃ tat kṛtvā oṃ śūnyateti mantrārthapracayodayabhūtāmuttamāṃ guhyasaṃbodhiṃ tathatālakṣaṇāṃ bhāvayediti
sūryacandrapadmāni krameṇaivoparyupari vyavasthāpya tadupari tyakṣaraṃ vinyasya sarvopamardanena śaśāṅkamaṇḍalaṃ tadupari punastyakṣaraṃ tatparāvṛttyā cihnaṃ cihnaparā- vṛttyā mahāmudrārūpaniṣpādanaṃ upasādhanaṃ upa samīpe sādhyaṃ niṣpādyate iti kṛtvā siddhirmahāmudrāsiddhiḥ tasyā agrā ādibhūtāḥ praṇavādayo mantrāḥ yasmin tadupasādhanasiddhyagraṃ vinyastasamastamantrākṣaraṃ mahāmudrārūpaṃ yasmin vajrā vairocanādisaptaparyantāḥ teṣāmāyatanāni rūpaskandhādayaḥ teṣāṃ tṛtīya- vyavasthālikrameṇa nirṇīya kāryakāraṇaparijñānavicāraṇā sādhyate adhiṣṭhānāyā- rādhyate yena tatsādhanacodanaṃ proktamiti paryāyakathanam
kiṃ tat ? buddhakāyadhara ityādigāthādvayam
mantrādhipativibhāvanamiti
mantrā oṃkārādayaḥ samādhisattvā adhipatayaḥ jñānasattvāḥ mantrādhipatīnāṃ kulabhedena yathāsaṃbhavaṃ dhyānamantrādhipativibhāvanamiti
mahāsādhanetyādi
yaduddiṣṭaṃ mahāsādhanaṃ tatsampādanakāleṣu jñānavajriṇaḥ svādhidaivatayogavān mantrī vajrapadmasaṃskārapūrvikā samāpatti kṛtvā svamantrā vajradhṛgādayaḥ tatsaṃbhūtāstathāgatāḥ svamantravajriṇaḥ teṣāṃ vimbaṃ mahāmudrākhyaṃ dhyātvā mukuṭe'dhipatiṃ dhyātveti pañcatathāgatānāṃ mahāmudrārūpasya jaṭāmukuṭamahā- vajradharamadhipatiṃ pariśiṣṭe tatkulīnānāṃ mukuṭe vairocanādīn dhyātvā sidhyate siddhimāpnoti
yathārutam
guhyasamājapradīpodyoneta evaṃ caturyogakrameṇa vajrasattvasamārādhanaṃ pratipādyedānīṃ ṣaḍaṅgakrameṇa mahāvajradharaniṣpattimāha samājottare ityādi
sāmānyottamabhedena sevā tu dvividhā bhavet
vajracatuṣkeṇa sāmānyamuttamaṃ ṣaḍabhiraṅgataḥ
sevā jñānāmṛtenaiva kartavyā sevyate mumukṣubhirabhyasyata iti sevā
kiṃ tat ? pariśuddhadevatāmūrtiḥ
sā jñānāmṛtenaiva ṣaḍaṅgayogenaiva kartavyā niṣpādya sarvataḥ sarvātmanā sadā sarvakālaṃ sarveryāpatheṣu
evakāro'vadhāraṇe
eṣo hi jñānāmṛtākhyaḥ ṣaḍaṅgayogaḥ sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ mantrāḥ sarvādayaḥ tattvaṃ devatātattvaṃ tayorarthaḥ phalaṃ yuganaddhasamādhiṃ tatsādhanānmantraṃ tattvārthasādhakaḥ
hiryasmādarthe
yasmādevaṃ ṣaḍaṅgayogaḥ tasmāttenaiva sevā kāryeti
tāni pratyāhārādīni ṣaḍaṅgāni nirdiṣṭāni samājottare sarveṣāṃ ṣaḍaṅgayogena kṛtvā sādhanamuttamam
sādhayedanyathā naiva jāyate siddhiruttamā
pratyāhārastathādhyānaṃ prāṇāyāmaśca dhāraṇā
anusmṛtiḥ samādhiśca ṣaḍaṅgo yoga ucyate
ityuddeśapadānāṃ nirdeśamāha daśānāmindriyāṇāṃ tu svavṛttisthaṃ tu sarvataḥ
pratyāhāra iti proktaḥ kāmāhāraṃ prati prati
paśca kāmāḥ samāsena pañcabuddhaprayogataḥ
kalpanaṃ dhyānamucyeta taddhyānaṃ pañcadhā bhavet
vitarkaṃ ca vicāraṃ ca prīti caiva sukhaṃ tathā
cittasyaikāgratā caiva pañcaite dhyānasaṃgrahāḥ
guhyatayaṃ vitarkaśca vicāraṃ tatprabhogatā
tṛtīyaṃ prītisaṃkāśaṃ caturthaṃ sukhasaṃgraham
svacittaṃ pañcamaṃ jñeyaṃ jñānajñeyodayakṣayaḥ
sarvabuddhamayaṃ śāntaṃ
sarvākāśapratiṣṭhitam ||
dvādaśapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā pañcajñānamayaṃ śvāsaṃ pañcabhūtasvabhāvakam |
niścārya padmanāsāgre piṇḍarūpeṇa kalpayet ||
pañcavarṇaṃ mahāratnaṃ prāṇāyāma iti smṛtam |
svamantraṃ hṛdaye dhyātvā prāṇaṃ vindugataṃ nyaset ||
niruddhe svendriye ratne dhārayed dhāraṇaṃ smṛtam |
nirodhavajragate citte nimittodgrāha jāyate ||
pañcadhātunimittaṃ tu bodhivatreṇa bhāvitam |
prathamaṃ marīcikākāraṃ dhūmrākāraṃ dvitīyakam |
tṛtīyaṃ khadyotakākāraṃ caturthaṃ dīpavajjalan ||
pañcamaṃ tu sadālokaṃ nirabhragaganopamam |
sthiraṃ vai vajramārgeṇa sphārayettaṃ svadhātuṣu ||
vibhāvya yadanusmṛttyā tadākāraṃ tu saṃsmaret |
anusmṛtiriti jñeyaṃ pratibhāsaṃ tatra jāyate ||
prajñopāyasamāpattyā sarvabhāvā: samāsataḥ |
saṃhṛtya piṇḍayogena vimvamadhye vibhāvayet ||
jhaṭiti jñānaniṣpattiḥ samādhiriti saṃjñitam |
iti ||
daśānāmityādi |
indriyāṇi indriyārthāśca indriyāṇi teṣāṃ daśānā- mindriyāṇāṃ viṣayaviṣayiṇāṃ svavṛttiḥ yathāsvaṃ grāhyagrāhakasvarūpeṇa pravṛttiḥ svavṛttiḥ |
tatra sthitaṃ svavṛttistham |
sarvato hīnamadhyottamabhedena kāmācāraṃ prati pratītiḥ |
kāmyante abhilaṣyantaḥ iti kāmā rūpādayasteṣāmindriyairyadā- haraṇaṃ grahaṇaṃ prati prati punaḥ punaḥ tatpratyāhāra ityādyaṅgasya pratinirdeśaḥ |
pratyāhāra viśodhanāya dvitīyamaṅgamāha paśñcatyādi |
pañcakāmā rūpādayaḥ indriyāṇi viṣayabhūtāḥ samāsena indriyairekībhāvena pañcabuddhāḥ cakṣurādayaḥ teṣāṃ saṃyojanaṃ yojanaṃ pañcabuddhaprayogataḥ |
tasmāt rūpādayo'pi paca buddhā ityevaṃvidhaṃ yat pariśuddhakalpanaṃ tad dhyānam |
tad vitarketyādibhedena pañcavidhaṃ bhavati |
vitarketyādi tadbhedakathanam |
guhyatayetyādi |
indriyaviṣayendri- yajñānāni guhyattrayam |
pañcendriyāṇi indriyajñānāni tadviṣayāśca pañcata- thāgatātmakā iti |
yat parikalpanaṃ sa vitarkaḥ |
tasminneva vicāraṇaṃ sthiti- vicāraḥ |
evaṃ vicārayataḥ tattvapraveśābhimukhyena yat saumanasyalakṣaṇaṃ tat guhyasamājapradīpodyotane prītīti saṃkāśaṃ tattvābhiniveśena kāyapraśrabdhyādilakṣaṇaṃ yat prāptaṃ sukhaṃ tat sukhasaṃgraham |
evamabhyasyataḥ prakarṣaparyantagamanāt jñānasya cakṣurādiṣaṭ pravṛtti- vijñānasya jñeye rūpādidharmadhātuparyante |
udayo jñānajñeyodayaḥ |
tasya kṣayaḥ |
cittasya grāhyagrāhakaśūnyatvaparijñānalakṣaṇā cittaikāgratā svacittamityuktam |
tadevaṃvidhaṃ svacittaṃ yoginaḥ |
sarvabuddhamayaṃ śāntamiti grāhyādivikalpaśamanāt |
śāntamābhāsamātraṃ tatsarvaśūnyataikaniṣṭaṃ jāyate |
pañcaprabhedaṃ dvitīyamaṅgam |
pañcetyādi |
ādarśādipañcajñānasvabhāvamadhaḥ śvāsaṃ tameva pṛthivyādyātmakaṃ svavajjravivarānniḥsāryaṃ padmanāsāgre piṇḍarūpeṇa bodhicittavindurūpeṇa dhyāyāt |
tamevoccapravṛttaśvāsaṃ pañcavarṇaṃ pañcatathāgatātmakamataeva mahāratnaṃ prāṇojjīvitam āyāmyate dīrghaṃ vistāryate yeneti sa prāṇāyāma iti |
smṛtaḥ jñātaḥ |
tameva praveśādisvabhāvenāharniśaṃ jāpyamānatvāt |
svamantraṃ hṛdaye svahṛtpuṇḍarīke dhyātvā prāṇaṃ vindugataṃ samāhataṃ nyasediti tṛtīyamaṅgam |
niruddhetyādi |
svaṃ rūpādaya indriyaṃ cakṣurādayaḥ tasmin svendriye niruddhe vilīne tato viṣayendriyādhārabhūte ratne cittaratne ca prāṇāyāmena saha niruddhe- 'staṃ gate yaddhārayet tad dhāraṇam |
kiṃ tat ?
bhūtakoṭiḥ |
etad dhāraṇākhyaṃ caturthamaṅgam |
nirodhavajjragate citte nimittodgrāho jāyate iti |
nirodhavajraṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tadbhūte tajjāte citte nimittānāmudgrahaḥ nimittapratibhāso jāyate utpadyate |
pañcadhātunimittaṃ tu bodhivatreṇa bhāvitam |
iti pṛthivyā ambhasi layanānmarīcikākāraṃ pratibhāsate prathamaṃ nimittam |
evamambhasastejasi layanāt dhūmrākāraṃ dvitīyam |
tejaso vāyau layanāt khadyotākāraṃ tṛtīyam |
sūkṣmadhātorābhāsatrayagamanād dīpavadālokapuñjasvabhāvaṃ caturtham |
prakṛtyābhāsalayanāt nirabhraṃ gaganavat satatālokaṃ prabhāsvaramātraṃ bhavati pañcamam |
etāni pañcanimittāni nirvāṇaṃ prāpayanti |
yathoktaṃ karmāntavibhāge iti |
prāṅmahī salilaṃ gacchejjalaṃ gacchati pāvakam |
pāvako vāyumanveti vāyuvijñānamāviśet ||
vijñānaṃ dhāraṇāṃ tvitthaṃ prabhāsvaramathāviśet ||
dvādaśapaṭalaṣaṭkorivyākhyā sthiramityādinā vajramārgeṇa alaṅghanīyaṃ pañcanimittānupūrveṇa prabhāsvara- praveśena sthiraṃ viśuddhaṃ svadhātuṣu lokadhātuṣu sphārayet dhyāyet dharmakāyarūpeṇa |
evamātmānaṃ prabhāsvaragataṃ vibhāvya sākṣātkṛtvā yat pūrvamanusmṛtyā marīcikādyākāreṇa bhūtakoṭi prāpitam, tadākāreṇa tenaiva krameṇa saṃspharet niṣpādayet |
etadanantaroktaṃ pañcamamaṅgamanusmṛtiriti jñeyaṃ jñātavyam |
pratibhāsaṃ saṃvit tattrānusmṛtyaṅge jāyate nānyatra |
prajñopāyetyādi |
sarvabhāvā sthāvarajaṅgamaṃ prajñopāyasamāpattyā saṃvṛtti- paramārthayogena sthitipiṇḍarūpeṇa mahāmudrārūpeṇa ekīkṛtya tasya sthāvarajaṅgamasya madhye yuganaddhātmakamahāvajradharavimbaṃ vibhāvayet jānīyāt |
anena krameṇa jhaṭiti kṣaṇena jñānaniṣpattiḥ jñānadehaniṣpattiḥ samādhiriti ṣaṣṭhamaṅgaṃ kathyate |
śrīmāyājāle'pīmameva devatāniṣpattibhedamuddyotayannāha yogastu trividho jñāyo'dhiṣṭhānaḥ parikalpitaḥ |
niṣpanno cittavimvasya yogo buddhaistu varṇitaḥ ||
adhiṣṭhāna mātrāhaṃkārayogo'dhiṣṭhāna bodhicittaviśuddhistu mantrabījodayo ucyate |
mahān ||

tat kalpiteti kathito yogaḥ kalpita ucyate ||

iti |

tatra pariśuddhamadhigatarūpaṃ sarva- nimittāpagatam |
apariśuddhaṃ sarvanimittaṃ rūpavarṇasaṃsthānaṃ ca |

sanimittena sanimittā siddhirupajāyate |
animittenānimittā siddhiriṣṭā jinavaraiḥ sadā animitte sthitvā vai sanimittaṃ prasādhyate |

saṃndhyābhāṣā ||

nītārthaḥ ||

notpattikramasādhanasthān prati |

catuḥsandhyetyādi |
pañcasthāneṣviti |
rūpādiviśiṣṭapaśvaviṣayeṣu sanni hiteṣu catuḥ sandhyāścatastro devyastābhiḥ prayoga āliṅganādiprakāreṇa tadārādhanaṃ tena catuḥsandhyaprayogeṇa buddhimān suratajñaḥ |
oṅkāraṃ kuliśāgre dhyātvā jñānavajreṇa tena oṅkārādhiṣṭhitasvakuliśena ramitvā saṃvaraṃ satsukha- mādiśet dadyāt |

adhya[dhaṃ] māsena |

tamutpādya tadbhāvenātmānaṃ niṣpādya laghu kṣipraṃ siddhirmahāmudrātmikā avāpyate |

vistareṇetyādi |
ādikarmakānāṃ mayā pūrvaṃ dvādaśaḥpaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā vistareṇa prapañcena dinabhedaprabhedanaiḥ dināni saptetyādikathanairuktam |
idānī- muttamasamādhisthasya vaśyata iti pūrvārdhābhiprāyaḥ |

pakṣaḥ saṃvṛtiḥ paramārthaśca tayorabhyantaraṃ madhyam |

sādhakasya kāyamupetya prāpya sādhakaṃ sādhayati pātrīkarotītyupasādhanaḥ kāyavajraḥ |
sa eva saṃvriyate prārthyata iti saṃvaraḥ |
upasādhanasaṃvaro vairocanaḥ |
tasya viṣayaṃ vakṣyamāṇabuddhakāyadhara ityādigāthā |
buddhāstyadhvavartinastathāgatāḥ |
teṣāṃ kāryaṃ tadātmakatayā dhārayatīti buddhakāyadharaḥ |
śrīḥ locanā sā yasya vidyate sa śrīmān |
trivajro mahāvajradharaḥ |
tenābhedyatvenaikātmyena bhāvitaḥ |
me mama kāyena tadadhiṣṭhānena padaṃ pratiṣṭhāṃ karotu kāyavajriṇaḥ vairocanaḥ |
kāyavajrīti prāpte |
evamadhipatimadhyeṣya daśadigityādinā taccakramadhyeṣate |
tattetyādinā vāgadhiṣṭhānamavatārayati |
vacasi sthitvā sādhakaṃ sādhayati niṣpādayatīti sādhanaḥ sa eva saṃvriyate prārthyata iti sādhanasaṃvaro'mitābhaḥ |
tasya viṣayaṃ vakṣyamāṇagāthā |
dharmo'mitābhaḥ vākpathaḥ vāksvabhāvatvāt |
śrīḥ pāṇḍaravāsinī |
tayā yuktaḥ śrīmān |
trivajñetyādi pūrvavat |
tatretyādinā cittādhiṣṭhānaṃ mahaccittaṃ tasmin sthitvā sādhakaṃ citta- vaśitvena yo bhūmatīti mahāsādhanaḥ sa eva saṃvaro'kṣobhyaḥ tadviṣayaṃ gāthā |
vajravaccittaṃ vajracittaṃ saṃsāranirvāṇayoralīnatvādevaṃbhūtaṃ cittaṃ dhārayatīti vajracittadharo'kṣobhyaḥ māmakī mamāyuktatvāt śrīmān |
śeṣaṃ pūrvavat |
evaṃ kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaparyantaṃ devatāyogaṃ vistareṇa pradarśya tadavaśya- kartavyatāmukhenopasaṃharannāha |
buddho vetyādi |
buddho'bhedāt vairocanayogī dharmo'mitābhayogī vajrasattvo'kṣobhyayogī yadi cet mohātmā alabdhopadeśatvāt mohākrāntaḥ atikramet kāyavākcittādhiṣṭhānaṃ pṛthaktvena kalpayet sphuṭeti niṣpannakramād dūrībhavati |
atrāsmin madvacasi saṃśayo vimatirna kāryā ityāha mahāvajradharaḥ |
sarvatathāgatāḥ kāyavajrādayaḥ teṣāmamedyasvabhāvaścaturyogo vajrayogaḥ samamīyate gamyata aneneti samayaḥ |
sa eva sādhyasvarūpatvāt sādhanaḥ niṣpanna- kramāvāhakatvādagraḥ nirdeśa [:] prakāśa: paṭalaḥ granthasandarbhaḥ pṛthagabhidheyāpekṣayā dvādaśaḥ |
yathārutam || ||
pradīpoddyotanaṭīkāyāṃ dvādaśapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā || ||
trayodaśapaṭalaḥ ||
dvividhakramābhyāsapravṛttena sādhakena vineyajanāvarjanārthaṃ karmaprasaro'pya- nuṣṭheya iti tatpradarśanārthaṃ trayodaśapaṭalamupakṣipannāha
athetyādi |
atha- āsevādividyānantaraṃ kāmaiśvaryādiyuktatvād bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatā akṣo- bhyādayaḥ jñānavajramadvayajñānaṃ tadeva pradhānatvādagraṃ taddhāraṇād jñānavajrāgra- dhāriṇaḥ |
sarvasattvānāmarthaḥ prayojanaṃ śāntikādi tadarthasaṃbhūtāḥ udyatāḥ ke te ?
bodhisattvāḥ kṣitigarbhādayaḥ dhīmantaḥ mudrāsthitāḥ evaṃvidhā buddha- bodhisattvā mahāśāstraṃ śāstāraṃ mahāvajradharaṃ sarvatathāgatamanovartitvāt, muniṃ laukikalokottarasaṃpradāyeṣvavyāhataśaktitvāt, sarvārthavajriṇaṃ satyadvayaparijñānāt samayatattvajñaṃ vāhyādhyātmikapūjābhiḥ pūjayitvā vajraghoṣamudīrayan karma- prasarādhyeṣaṇavacanaṃ vajraghoṣamudīrayan kṛtavantaḥ |
yathārutam |
aho āścarye |
buddhanayaḥ pratyāhārādiṣaḍaṅgayogaḥ vajjracatuṣkātikramaṇāt divyam ṣaḍaṅgayogaṃ prapaśya |
satyadvayamīlanenābhistuvannāha punaraho iti |
bodhiṣaḍaṅgayogaḥ tasya nayaḥ satyadvayaniyojanaṃ tadevottamaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ sarva- tathāgataavacanārthasaṃgrahaṇāt |
punarapyaho iti |
dharmaḥ satyadvayaṃ tasya nayaḥ ekīkaraṇaṃ tacchāntaṃ niṣprapañcam |
punapyaho ityādinā stutimupasaṃharati |
mantrāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ nayaṃ bodhiprāpaṇopāyaḥ |
dṛḍhamabhedyaṃ śrāvakādibhira- bhedyatvāt |
sandhyābhāṣā |
anutpanneṣvādiśānteṣu dharmeṣu skandhādiṣu bodhiprāpakatvena svabhāvā- tiśayaviśiṣṭeṣu nirvikalpeṣu manyanārahiteṣu avicāraikaramyatvenāstitvāt bhūteṣu satsu |
evaṃbhūteṣu dharmeṣu ṣaḍaṅgayogopāyena jñānolādaḥ vajrasattvotpādaḥ bhagavatā saṃpragīyate kathyate |
tadāścaryam |
nāsandhyam ||
evaṃ stutvā bhagavantamārādhyābhipretārthaṃ pratyadhyeṣayannāha |
bhāṣasveti |
sarve mantrā ālikālitanibhāste yasmin samuccīyante saṃgrahaṃ gacchanti taṃ vajrajāpasamopajāpaṃ mahājñānamanuttarajñānaprāpakatvāt, trikāyābhedyo mahā- vajradharaḥ tasya praveśasthitivyutthānasvarūpeṇa pravartanasthānaṃ maṇḍalaṃ bhāṣasva |
tameva viśinaṣṭi buddhajñānāni daśamatavaiśāradyādīni yena trivajrā - bhedyabhāvanaiḥ sādhakaiḥ prāpya sthitaṃ bhāṣasveti yogaḥ |
tasyaiva viśeṣaṇaṃ jāpavajraḥ vajrajāpaḥ tasya prayogaḥ praveśādisvarūpeṇa parikalpanaṃ tena sarvavajñaistathāgata- tairadhiṣṭhyate svīkriyate |
kulānāmityādi |
sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarpādīnāṃ kulānāṃ vṛndānāṃ kāyavākcitta- lakṣaṇaṃ kāyādisvabhāvaḥ |
mantrajāpaṃ vajrajāpaṃ adyedānīṃ praghoṣa kathaya |
kimarthaṃ jñānasāgarāḥ ratnapud galāḥ śṛṇvantu jānantu |
tyadhvasaṃbhūtāḥ praveśasthitivyutthānātmakā bhūtvā saṃbhūtā niṣpannāḥ |
ke te ?
kāyavākcittavajriṇaḥ 'sarve buddhāḥ jñānamatulamadvayajñānaṃ saṃprāptāḥ vajramantraprabhāvanairiti vajrājāpaparijñānaiḥ ||
athādhyeṣaṇānantaram |
khavatraṃ paramārthasatyaṃ jñānaṃ saṃvṛtisatyamubhābhyāṃ niṣpannatvāt khavajrajñānasaṃbhavo mahāvajradharaḥ kinte ( ? karte) tyādinā tameva viśinaṣṭi śāntyādiniṣpādakatvāt kartā |
anadhikṛtābhicārakādikarmaccheda- nāt sraṣṭā varā vṛkṣādayaḥ teṣāmagrāḥ arhatprabhṛtayaḥ teṣāmapyadhikatvāt varāgrāgro bhagavāneva svavajrajāpamudāharaduktavān
sarvamantretyādi
sarvamantrāḥ sarpādayaḥ teṣāmartho'kṣaratrayaṃ tasya jāpaḥ pradeśāditrayaḥ teṣu satsu trivajrābhedyalakṣaṇaṃ tatsvabhāvaṃ vajrajāpamudāharaditi
yogaḥ śṛṇvannāha tribhede ityādi
tribhedaḥ kāyādibhedaḥ tasya vajraparyanto vajradhāraikaniṣṭho nyāsaḥ praveśādisvarūpeṇa parijñānaṃ tat trivajramucyate jñāyate
tasya viṣayaṃ darśayannāha
trividhamityādi
kāyavākcittānāṃ sanni- dhirūpādānaṃ kiṃ tat ?
nirmāṇakāyagrahaṇaṃ tasmin sati trividhaṃ sphuraṇaṃ prave- śādilakṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ niṣpādyam
asyānuśaṃsāmāha anenānantaroktena jāpavajreṇa vajrajāpena trivajraṃ kāyādikaṃ tasya cittaṃ kāyādibhāvenāpṛthagbhūto jñānadeha- svabhāvo mahāvajradharaḥ tatsamo bhavet
anuttarapūjāṃ darśayannāha
buddhānāmityādi
kāyavākcittanirmāṇa- rūpagrahaṇaṃ dhyātvā niṣpādya buddhānāṃ daśadiglokadhātusthitānāṃ pūjāviśeṣa- kalpanaṃ kartavyam
kena ?
jñānavajreṇa niṣpannakramasamādhyārūḍhena trivajretyādyanu- śaṃsā pūrvavat
vāhyapūjābhiniveśāya nayanāyāha punarbuddhānāmityādi
pūjāgrakalpanaṃ aśeṣapūjāvyūhaṃ dhyātvā lokadhātuṣu saṃkalpya buddhānāṃ kāyavākcittapūjanaṃ kartavyam
sottarādipūjāvyūhastathāgatakāyādibhāvenāvagantavyaḥ
jñānavajreṇa idamīdṛgvidhaṃ parijñānaṃ bodhisamāvahaṃ mahāvajradharapadaniṣpādakarmetyanuśaṃsā
evamadhimātrendriyasya pūjāvidhi saṃpradarśya mṛdvindriyasyāpi darśayannāha
athavetyādi
athaveti prakārāntarapradarśako nipātaḥ
tribhedena ekaikamapi devatānirmāṇaṃ kāyabhedena prati prati pṛthak pṛthak sphuraṇaṃ svakāyamaṇḍalādutsarjanaṃ kāryaṃ karaṇīyam
nirmitamaṇḍaleṣupalambhanirāsārthamāha
kāyetyādi
kāya- vākcittanairātmyaṃ tāsāṃ devatānāṃ kāyādinaiḥ svābhāvyaṃ jñānacittena pratyavekṣaṇā- tmakena jñānaviśeṣeṇa saṃspharet prakaṭīkuryāt
saṃpuṭīkaraṇamāha
uccāryetyādi
vajraṃ nirmitadevatārvigrahaṃ [sthā]nāduccārya sthānādutthāpya saṃspharet skandhādisvarūpeṇātmanyavasthāpayet
samāptau sphuṭī- karaṇakriyāvasthāne saṃhāraṃ samādhisattvapraveśamādiśet kuryāt
idamanantaroktaṃ yat sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvapudgalānāṃ jñānonmīlitacakṣuṣāṃ vivṛtajñānanetrāṇāṃ kāyavajro vairocanaḥ so'bhisaṃbadhyate niṣpādyate yena sa kāyavajrābhisaṃbodhiḥ oṃkāraḥ tasya śrutipathagocaratvādastitvena svabhāvarahitatvanāstitvena parijñānaṃ tadbhāvābhāvavicāraṇaṃ ced buddhakāya iti proktaṃ sa kāyajāpa ucyate
vāksamayo'mitābhaḥ
tasyābhisambodhirākāraḥ sa śabdayate śrotrendriyeṇa gṛhyata iti śabdaḥ tadviparyāsādaśabdaḥ śrotreṇa gṛhīto'gṛhīta iti yāvat jāpaḥ ( ? )
yaḥ parāmarśaḥ śabdāśabdavicāraṇaṃ tad vāgvajra iti proktam
sa vāgjāpa ityucyate
cittasamayo'kṣobhyaḥ tasya bodhiḥ huṅkāraḥ tasya sthitaḥ vajrasvabhāvaḥ tasya vicāraṇaṃ yathābhūtacittācittaparāmarśaḥ
cittavajra iti proktaṃ sa cittajāpa ityucyate
evaṃ kāyavākcittadvāreṇa vajrajāpamuktvā mukhyaṃ vajrajāpaṃ darśayannāha
arthānugametyādi
oṃkārādīnāmarthaḥ praveśādi tadanugamena tadanusāreṇa yaḥ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane kṛto jāpaḥ sa arthānugamajāpaḥ tena hetunā niḥsvabhāvena nirgatasvabhāvenanāhatena cāruṇā nirantaraṃ prasphuradrūpeṇa vyadhvatvādabhedyaḥ trikālyavartī tathāgatānāṃ nivāraṇāya vijñānaṃ sarvatathāgatānāmanāhatarūpeṇādhigamaḥ sa ratnajāpa ityucyate
punarvyutthānaṃ darśayannāha
spharaṇamityādi
buddhakṣetrāt hṛtpuṇḍarīkāt samantataḥ cakṣurādyāyatayaneṣu kāyameghenānāhatena gamanāgamanavajrārthaṃ praveśa- sthityutthānātmakaṃ spharaṇaṃ yat tadamoghajāpa ucyate
evaṃ mānasajāpamuktvā vāgjāpaṃ darśayannāha
śṛṇotīti
mantrāḥ sarpādayaḥ teṣāmakṣarapadaṃ oṃkārāditaḥ svavajraṃ svajihvā tenoddhṛṣṭaṃ śabditaṃ maṇḍalaṃ śarīraṃ tat svavajroddhṛṣṭamaṇḍalaṃ śṛṇoti svayaṃ paro vā
krodhānāṃ yamāntakā- dīnāṃ mantrāḥ teṣāṃ samayaḥ uccāraṇāvidhiḥ tatparijñānena jāpaḥ krodhajāpa ucyate
kāmā rūpādayo viṣayāḥ tadarthaṃ vihvalībhūtān tadanveṣaṇaparān sarva- sattvahitakāmāḥ svārthaikaniṣṭhatvāt tān sattvān moho vairocanaḥ tasya padaṃ mohaviśuddhiḥ tasmin saṃsthāpya yā cintā sa mohajāpa iti smṛtaḥ
rāgaḥ strīpuruṣāṇāmanyonyasyāsaktiḥ saṃhatatvād vajraṃ tadudbhavān tadutthāpito vācaṃ vāṇīṃ kāyavākcittasaṃsthitāṃ kāyādisaundaryapatitāṃ vācaṃ śrutvā sarvān tān rāgiṇaḥ rāgodadhiramitābhaḥ tasya padaṃ rāgapariśuddhiḥ tasmin sthāpayitvā yā bhāvanā yoginaḥ sa rāgajāpa iti smṛtaḥ // dveṣaḥ sattvābhidrohaḥ tadeva vajraṃ tadantargataṃ cittaṃ kāyavākcittasaṃsthitaṃ kāyādiviṣayaṃ jñātvā sattvānveṣiṇaḥ dveṣaḥ akṣobhyaḥ tasyālayaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tasmin saṃsthāpya kṛtaṃ yat parijñānaṃ sa dveṣajāpaḥ trivajrasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ tasya tattvaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ samayavajriṇāṃ tathāgatānāṃ madhyaṃ hṛdayabhūtaṃ tadeva samaya- vajrāṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ napuṃsakajāpo niḥsvabhāvajāpa ityucyate
sandhyābhāṣā
vajro mahāvajradharaḥ so'dhipatiryeṣāṃ te vajrādhipatayaḥ te sarve dviprakārāḥ sādhakāḥ
na rāgo na virāgaśca madhyamā nopalabhyate iti rāgatattvam
tasyārthaḥ yathābhūtaparijñānaṃ tajjānanti rāgatattvārthasādhakāḥ
rāgajāpaṃ ca kāmaguṇopabhogajā sarvasattvahitakāriṇīṃ bodhi mahāvajradharapadaṃ kurvanti niṣpādayanti
rāgajāṃ bodhiṃ na kevalaṃ puruṣā evaṃ sādhayanti
kintu striyo'pītyāha
locanetyādi
locanādyāḥ padmamityādayaḥ mahāvidyāsu śikṣitā mudrāḥ kāma- rūpādayaḥ teṣāmarthastadviśuddhiḥ tatparāḥ tadbhāvanaikaniṣṭhāḥ yathecchataḥ yathāsukhaṃ sevyamānairabhyasyamānaiḥ kāmabhogairviṣayaiḥ sidhyanti vajradharapadamāsādayanti
yathārutam // mohasamayasaṃbhūtā ityādi
mohasamayo vairocanaḥ tatsaṃbhūtāstatprādurbhūtā vidyārājāna uṣṇīṣādayaḥ vajriṇaḥ kṣitigarbhādayaḥ napuṃsakapade siddhāḥ napuṃsakapadamoṅkāraḥ tato niṣpannaṃ uttamāṃ siddhi bodhi dadanti prayacchanti
krodhāḥ yamāntakādayaḥ dveṣālayo huṅkāraḥ tasmin jātā tasminniṣpannāḥ māraṇatatparāḥ duṣṭadamanakarmāṇaḥ māraṇārthena raudrakarmaṇā siddhayanti abhipretārthaṃ sādhayantyagrardhāmiṇa abhyuddhatasvabhāvasya sādhakasya
neyārtha
mohasamayo'vidyā tataḥ saṃbhūtāḥ gṛhītopādānaskandhāḥ vidyārājāno vajriṇaḥ strīpuruṣāḥ napuṃsakamavidyā tasyāḥ padaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tasmin tatsākṣya- karaṇena siddhā niṣpannāḥ uttamāṃ viśiṣṭāṃ siddhi mahāmudrāsiddhiṃ dadanti sādhakebhyaḥ prayacchanti
krodhā upāyaprakṛtayaḥ dveṣālayaḥ prajñā tasmin jātāḥ tadudbhavā nityaṃ sarvakālaṃ māraṇatatparāḥ viheṭhanātmikā evaṃvidhāḥ prakṛtayaḥ māraṇāryāḥ māraṇaṃ skandhādīnāṃ niḥsvabhāvīkaraṇaṃ tasyārthaḥ satyadvayādvaidhībhāvena
vajradharatvotpattiḥ tena māraṇārthena svadharmo vajropamasamādhiḥ sa yasyāsti tasyāgradharmiṇo ratna- pudgalasya siddhyante prakṛtayaḥ vaśībhavanti
mahāpuruṣasamayo mahāvajradhara ityāheti saṃgītikāravacanam
nītārthaḥ // punarapi bhāvanāntaramāha
hṛdītyādi
jñānavajriṇaḥ jñānasattvasya hṛdaye madhyagataṃ hṛdayamadhyagataṃ cakraṃ vairocanacihnam
kīdṛk ?
svacchamaṇḍalaṃ candra- maṇḍalaṃ tanmadhyasthaṃ bhāvayet
madhye cakramadhye
cakrārthabhāvaneti cakreṇārthaṃ sādhyata iti cakrārthaḥ sādhyaḥ tasya bhāvanā kartavyā
etaduktaṃ bhavati vairocanayogavān svakīyajñānasattvasya hṛdaye candramaṇḍalo- pari cakraṃ vibhāvya sādhyamākṛṣya tasmin praveśya saṃsthāpya yathāvacchāntiṃ
kuryāditi
evamakṣobhyayogavān jñānavajriṇo jñānasattvasya hṛdaye yantra- maṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ sūryamaṇḍalagataṃ vajraṃ vibhāvya tasmin vajramantrārtha bhāvanā
vajramantro huṅkāraḥ tenārthyate pīḍyata iti vajramantrārthaḥ sādhyaḥ tasya bhāvanā kartavyā
ratnasaṃbhavayogavān ratnajñāninaḥ jñānasattvasya hṛdaye ratnamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ ratnopari sthitaṃ ratnaṃ vibhāvya tasmin ratnamantrārthabhāvanā
ratnamantraḥ svākāraḥ tenārthyate puṣṇyate iti ratnamantrārthaḥ sādhyaḥ
tasya bhāvanā sā tathā
amitābhayogavān padmajñāninaḥ svahṛdgatajñānasattvasya hṛdaye dharmamaṇḍala- madhyasthaṃ padmaṃ vibhāvya tasmin padme mantrārthabhāvanā padmamantraḥ
āḥkāraḥ tenārthyate vaśīkriyata iti padmamantrārthaḥ sādhyaḥ tasya bhāvanā kartavyā
amoghasiddhiyogavān khaḍgajñāninaḥ svajñānasattvasya hṛdaye samayamaṇḍala- madhyasthaṃ viśvavajjroparisthitaṃ khaḍgaṃ vibhāvya tasmin khaḍgamantrārtha bhāvanā khaḍgamantraḥ hākāraḥ tenārthyate sarvākāraṇena sādhyate iti khaḍgamantrārthaḥ sādhyaḥ tasya bhāvanā kartavyā
atraiva vidhānaśeṣamāha
sarvamaṇḍalāni cakramaṇḍalādīni teṣāṃ pārśveṣu caturṣu pañcabuddhān vairocanādīn yathākrameṇa niveśya dhyāyāt
paśñcaraśmiprabhedena karmānurūpavarṇena spharan dhyāyāt
tataḥ kimityāha
bodhirākāṅakṣitaphala- siddhiravāpyate
idānīṃ kramabhedena spharaṇamāha
spharaṇamityādi
sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvasādha- kānāṃ spharaṇaṃ devatānirmāṇaṃ dvividhabhedena niṣpannāniṣpannabhedena kīrtitam
utpattikramasamādhi[m ] āha
trikāyetyādi
