tadeva spaṣṭayannāha
kāyasvabhāvamityādi
kāyasvabhāve kāyena virocana- svarūpeṇa cittamātmanaḥ cittasvarūpataḥ akṣobhyasvabhāvena vācamātmano vāksamūhaṃ vacaḥ svarūpeṇa tatsvabhāvena pūjyaṃ jñātvā pūjāmiṣṭārthasaṃsiddhimā- pnuyāt adhigacchet
mahāmaṇḍalāni kāyavākcittamaṇḍalāni teṣāṃ cakraṃ samudāyaḥ tena mahā- trayodaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā maṇḍalacakreṇa pañcavajro mahāvajradharaḥ tasya vibhāvanā kartavyā
madhyetyādi
anantaroktasya kāyavajriṇaḥ samayasattvasya madhye hṛdaye'dhipati jñānasattvaṃ svavimbaṃ prakṛtivimbaṃ dhyātvā jñānasattvahṛdaye svamantravajjrasya samādhisattvasya vimbaṃ mūrtiṃ dhyātvā
punarmaṇḍalānāṃ catuṣṭayaṃ vāruṇādimaṇḍalacatuṣṭayaṃ caturvarṇena sitādivarṇena saṃkalpya dhyātvā hṛdi vāruṇādimaṇḍalamadhye mantrā oṅkārādayaḥ teṣāṃ praveśādayaḥ teṣāṃ bhāvanā parijñānaṃ kartavyam
dhyānavajriṇo yoginaḥ vajracatuṣṭayaṃ vārūṇādimaṇḍalacatuṣṭayaṃ karma śāntikādi karoti niṣpādayati
eṣa hi vidhiḥ sarvayogīśvarāṇāṃ parama- śāśvataṃ sarvatathāgatāmnāyagataṃ rahasyam ādikarmakāṇāmasādhāraṇaṃ tattvam
etadeva vajracatuṣṭayaṃ karmabhedena nirdiśannāha śāntika ityādi
śānti- keṣu jvarādyapaharaṇalakṣaṇe karmaṇi locanākāraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ śuddhasvabhāvaṃ vāruṇa- maṇḍalaṃ dhyāyāt
pauṣṭike arthavṛddhyādike vairocanapadamiti yogataḥ tapta- kāśvanavat
virocate dīpyate iti vairocano ratnaketuḥ tasya padaṃ pītā- bhāsaṃ dvandvāt vinirgataṃ māhendramaṇḍalaṃ tathā vaśye padmavajriṇamiti yogaḥ
vaśye anavatīrṇānāmanuraktikaraṇalakṣaṇe padmavajriṇaṃ raktaraśmisvabhāvaṃ dakṣiṇa- dvārād vinirgatamagnimaṇḍalaṃ dhyāyāt
abhicāruke māraṇādiraudrakarmaṇi vajrakrodhaṃ kṛṣṇaraśmisvabhāvaṃ vāmadvārād vinirgataṃ maṇḍalaṃ dhyāyet
idaṃ tadityādi
yadanantaroktaṃ maṇḍalacatuṣṭayaṃ tat sarvavajrāṇāṃ sarva- sādhakānāṃ trikāyasaṃbhavaṃ mahāvajradharaprakāśitaṃ guhyaṃ rahasyaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvadevatānāṃ saṃvandhibhūtaṃ kriyānāṭakaṃ śāntikādikarmakalāpa:, tena lakṣitaṃ cihnitaṃ guhyamiti yogaḥ
caturṇāṃ karmaṇāṃ madhye abhicārasya duṣkaratvapradarśanārthaṃ tadvivakṣayā tasyaiva viṣayaṃ darśayannāha
abhaktivādina iti
abhaktirabhajanaṃ nindāratnatrayamadhi- kṛtya tadvādinaḥ sattvā anāstikā ācāryaguravaḥ vajriṇaḥ vajrācāryāḥ
teṣāṃ nindakāśca
anyeṣāmapi pañcānantaryādikāriṇāmapi dṛṣṭānāmidaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ pracodanam
amukaṃ mārayediti pracodanālakṣaṇaṃ karma kāryaṃ karaṇīyam
mahājñānacakravajraḥ kuladvayātmako mahāvajradhara ityāha evabhāṣata
vaidhātuketyādi
traidhātukasthitān sattvān kāmarūpārūpyaparyāpannān guhyasamājapradīpodyotane aṇḍajādisattvān buddhakāye buddhakāyān tathāgatagarbhān vibhāvayet adhimuñcet sādhakaḥ ripūṇāṃ sādhyaiḥ saptabhiḥ ghāṭitān sattvān kṛtvā dhyātvā tataḥ karma samārabhet iti
anantaroktakrameṇopacitakilviṣān kṛtvā saṃpuṭodghāṭana- pūrvakaṃ nigṛhṇīyāt
khadhātumadhyagatamityādi
ādau tāvat sādhaka: ātmānaṃ khadhātumadhyagataṃ prabhāsvarapraviṣṭaṃ kṛtvā tadantaramātmānaṃ sūryamaṇḍalākāreṇa pariṇataṃ dhyāyāt
tadupari dīrghahūṅkāreṇa niṣpannaṃ vajraṃ pañcaśūlaṃ karālavajraṃ dhyātvā tatparāvṛttyā caturmukhaṃ daṃṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ indranīlaprabhaṃ yadbhujaṃ sarvākāravaropeta iti sarva- krodhākāraviśeṣabhūtaṃ pratyālīḍhasthitaṃ cakramadhyasthaṃ yathopadeśaṃ vajrasattvaṃ vibhāvayet
tyadhvasamayo'nantarokto vajradharaḥ tatsaṃbhūtaṃ buddhavajjaṃ vairocanādidevatā- vyūhaṃ vibhāvayet
idamanantaroktaṃ buddhacakramahāvastraṃ vajrasattvasya dakṣiṇapāṇau dakṣiṇapārśve dhyeyamarthāyātatvāt
devīcakraṃ pañcakulātmakaṃ vāmapārśve kāryamityāmnāyaḥ
sattvāddaśadigvya- vasthitasarvatathāgatān buddhakāyaprabhedataḥ vairocanādikulabhedena saṃhṛtya piṇḍa - yogena yathāsvaṃ mahāmudrārūpeṇaikīkṛtya tāṃ devatāvyūhaṃ svakāye rūpaskandhādi- svarūpeṇa praveśayet saṃpuṭayet
punaḥ praveśyānantaraṃ jñānavajriṇāṃ jñānadehasthitānāṃ spharaṇaṃ niṣkramaṇaṃ kāryaṃ karaṇīyam
kīdṛśamityāha
kruddhānityādi kruddhādisvarūpān vicintya spharediti yogaḥ
kruddhān manasā krodhākulān kāyairata āha
vikaṭotkaṭa- bhīṣaṇāniti
vikaṭatvaṃ bhrūbhaṅgaraktanetradaṃṣṭrākarālāsyasamanvitatvāt
utkaṭatvaṃ mahākāyatvāt
bhīṣaṇatvaṃ sarpamekhalādibhūṣitatvāt
nānāpraharaṇahastāgrān paraśumudgarādivyagrahastatvāt
māraṇo mahā- vajradharaḥ tasyāmnāyaḥ ājñāviśeṣaḥ
taccittakān mahāduṣṭaratnatrayādyupakāra- bhūṣitān
ghātayantastarjayantaḥ vajrasattvamapi svayamiti
yadi vajradharo'pi evaṃ duṣṭaḥ syāt tamapi hiṃsati kiṃ punaranyamiti māyopamatvāsāmarthyaṃ darśitam
kaukṛtyanirāsārthamāha
buddha ityādi
buddho mahāvajradharaḥ dharmādikāyadharopadeśadānāt trikāyavaradaḥ
trivajrāḥ sattvāḥ teṣāmālayamaṇḍalaṃ traidhātukaṃ tadātmakatvāt
trivajrālayamaṇḍalaḥ
mohātmā trayodaśapaṭalaṣṭkoṭivyākhyā pramādaparavaśatvāt
mūḍha iti mohātmā siddhimabhipretārthaṃ sādhakasya dadāti
anyasmin siddhidāne saṃśayavirati na kriyate
idaṃ raudrakarma saptadivasāni kartavyam
anyathā saptadivasādarvāg buddhasyāpi na sidhyati
atrāyaṃ prayogaḥ
anantaroktakrameṇa mahāvajradharamātmānaṃ niṣpādya yathoktakrodhadevatāspharaṇaṃ kṛtvā tanmadhyasthitastambhamājñāpya tena pāśabaddhagalaṃ aṅakuṃśenākṛṣyamāṇaṃ vāyumaṇḍaloparisthitāgneyamaṇḍale muktakeśaṃ tailābhyaktadehaṃ udghāṭitasaṃpuṭamaśaraṇaṃ saṃjātakaukṛtyaṃ sādhyaṃ purato vicintya krodhadevatābhirnānāpraharaṇaibhidyamānaṃ niṣkrāntaprāṇaṃ buddhakṣetrotpannaṃ vicintayediti
vajrasamayāḥ krodhāḥ teṣāṃ jñānamevaṃ viheṭhayateti yojanaṃ vajrasamayajñānaṃ tadeva [cakraṃ] tadākhyaḥ samādhirayam
evamakṣobhyayoginastāvadājñācakraṃ saṃpradarśya vairocanayogino'pi darśaya- nnāha
khadhātvityādi
yathoktakrameṇātmānaṃ vairocanaṃ niṣpādya tadanantaraṃ tyadhvasamayasaṃbhūtaṃ vairocanaparāvṛtti yaṃ vajrasattvaṃ mahāyaśaṃ kruddhaṃ vibhāvya tasya haste vajramagnisphuliṅgākulaṃ karālavajraṃ dhyāyāt
sattvān daśadigvyavasthitatathāgatān vajrakāyaprabhedataḥ strīpuruṣabhedena sampannān svadehavinirgataraśmipuñjena sabhājayitvā saṃhṛtyākṛṣya svakāye tān tathāgatān praveśayet ||
sarvavajrāṇāṃ svadehasthitamāṇḍaleyānāṃ spharaṇaṃ niścāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ jñānā- gravandhunā sādhakena |
tatasteṣāmājñādānakramaṃ darśayati |
śṛṇvantvityādi |
sarvabuddhātmā sarvatathāgatasūnavaḥ kāyavākcittavajriṇaḥ trivajrātmānaḥ śṛṇvantu |
kimityāha |
ahamityādi |
[ ājñācakra ]nirvartakaḥ so'ham |
anena svadeha- sthitenādīptavapuṣā jvālāvalīḍhena vajreṇa trikāyajān kāyattrayaikasaṃbhūtān sphālayāmi cūrṇīkaromi |
ataeva laṅghayed yadi samayaṃ yaḥ kaścidatra samā- jñānaṃ kuryāt sa śīryate vinaśyati |
nātra saṃśaya iti |
svādhipatyaṃ prakāśayati |
vajrasamayo vairocanaḥ |
tasmād bhūto jñānavatraḥ vajrasattvaḥ tannāmako'yaṃ samādhiḥ |
evaṃ ratnasaṃbhavādīnāmapi parāvṛttiryojyā |
tatparivārāṇāmapyāha |
khavatretyādi |
buddhamaṇḍalavajriṇamātmānaṃ khavajra- guhyasamājapradīpodyotane madhyagataṃ prabhāsvaraniviṣṭaṃ cintayet |
tasmāt khavajrākhyaṃ svātmānaṃ yamāntakaṃ samayacakraṃ saparivāraṃ prakalpayet buddhān vairocanādīn bodhisattvān maitreyādīṃśca trikāyamaṇḍale kāya- vākcitteṣu praviṣṭān prakalpayet |
punastu punarapi buddhān saumyabhāvasthitān yamāntakākārasannibhān sphārayet |
sattvān spharitāṃstathāgatān vyadhvasaṃbhūtān trikāyātmakān punarevaṃ bhāvayet |
ripūṇāṃ ratnatrayādyapakārapravṛttānāṃ duṣṭacetasāṃ kukṛtyakaraṇopagata- cittānāṃ ghātakānāṃ bhāvayet |
kruddhaḥ idaṃ vajrāstathāgatāsteṣāmājñā duṣṭaghātanaṃ prati codanaṃ tadeva pradhānatvānmaṇḍaṃ sāraṃ lāti gṛhṇāti svīkarotīti maṇḍalam |
ki tat ?
maṇḍalādhipatireva |
sarvasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ tatsaṃbhavo yamāristena samānāṃ ekarūpā [ṇāṃ] trikāyavajrāḥ parivārakrodhā yasmin samādhau sa tathā |
kāyetyādi |
kāya- ājñācakrasya sarvadevatāsādhāraṇatvaṃ kathayannāha |
vākcittaiḥ vairocanādibhiḥ pañcabhiḥ taddevībhiśca ājñācakraṃ prayojanīyam |
svamantrāḥ svakula-prativaddhāḥ maitroyādayaḥ teṣāmartho yathāvatsampāditamahāmudrārūpaḥ tasya guṇaḥ tatparāvṛttiniṣpannakrodharūpeṇa duṣṭadamanaṃ [sa]maya uṣṇīṣa ucca- bhāgasthitakrodharājaḥ tena samaṃ samatāṃ yāntīti uṣṇīṣa samayāḥ sarvakrodhāḥ tairapyājñācakraṃ prayojayet |
ājñācakrasya prayojanānantaraṃ darśayannāha |
rakṣārthamityādi |
sarvamantrāṇāṃ indrādidikkālānāṃ vighnakāriṇāṃ rakṣārthaṃ nivāraṇārthaṃ jñānāgravajriṇā sādhakena kāryamidamājñācakram |
idaṃ yadanantaroktaṃ vighnanirasanaṃ kimarthaṃ kriyata ityāha |
tatsarvavajrāṇāṃ sarvasādhakānāṃ bodhirakṣārthaṃ cittavikṣepakāribhyaḥ tadrakṣārtha- mityājñācakrasyeva prayojanaṃ darśitam |
yathārutam ||
idānīṃ niṣpannakramasthānāmapi duṣṭagrahopāyamāha |
khavatretyādi |
duṣṭasya nirodhāt dīpyata iti vairocanaḥ sādhyaḥ |
tasya kāyāgrasaṃbhavaṃ trikāyasamayaṃ kāyavākcittātmakaṃ dharmaṃ khavantramadhyagataṃ śūnyībhūtaṃ dhyātvā āsanaṃ sādhyamādhāra- bhūtaṃ prakalpayet |
tamevākāśadhātuṃ sarvabuddheḥ pañcaraśmibhiḥ paripūrṇaṃ vyāpyaṃ vibhāvayet |
trayodaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ātmanā tu svamantrākṣaraṃ vacanaṃ padaṃ dehaṃ jñānaṃ cittākāraṃ māyopamaṃ prabhāvayet |
punastadanantaraṃ buddhānanantaroktān pañcaraśmīn cittamātre prabhāvitāṃ svamahā- mudrāṃ saṃharet praveśayet |
etat samastaṃ cittavajramiti kṛtvā vajrajñānamapi jñātvā trikāye svakāyavākcitte praveśayet |
anenābhipretārthaḥ saṃpadyate |
vajraḥ durbhedyaḥ sādhyaḥ tatprabhedako ratnamantraḥ prayogaviśeṣaḥ tasya pradyo- tanakara[:] prakāśakaro nāma samādhiḥ |
sandhyābhāṣā |
stambhanaprayogamāha |
prathamaṃ tāvadātmānaṃ māyāmayaṃ niṣpādya gaganamadhye sthāpayet |
buddhān ākāśapavanāgnijalorvīsvabhāvānakṣobhyādīni uparyupari a[T]nupūrvyā sthāpayitvā jalasyopari caṅkramet |
yathārutam |
niṣpannakramasamādhisthasya samayādakaprayoga: satyadvayaikīkaraṇaṃ tena jñānamaya- dehena mūni jalapūrṇapādaprabhāvanaṃ caṃkramaṇaṃ kartavyam |
ityāha bhagavān svabhāvaśuddhaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ |
vajrā dvividhayoginaḥ tairudadhau .samudrādau pādākrāntaṃ caṃkramaṇaṃ yasmin samādhau sa tathoktaḥ |
sandhyābhāṣā |
madhye vadanakuhare māhendraṃ maṇḍalaṃ laṅkārodbhavaṃ pītavarṇaṃ vajrapadāṅkitaṃ dhyātvā tadupari krodhaṃ dīrghahūṅkārajanitaṃ jvālāvatraṃ nyaset |
mūrdhina sādhya- śirasi karmavajraṃ viśvavajraṃ tadeva padaṃ tadākrāntaṃ tasmin sthitamamṛta- kuṇḍali prabhāvayet |
sarvatīrthyāḥ kṣapaṇakādayaḥ parapravādinaḥ pratyarthikāḥ teṣāṃ yantraḥ kāyādi- stambhanaṃ tasya saṃbhavo yasmin samādhau sa tathā |
krodhetyādi |
sādhakaḥ svayaṃ mahāvajradharaḥ pararūpaparāvṛttyā gṛhītānyatama- krodharūpaḥ trivajrāgrān svapārṣadā pītakiñjalkasannibhān padmarājapiñjarāṃ girirājamiva sumerusadṛśapramāṇaṃ caturaṅgasenopari dhyātvā cintayitvā prabhā- vayet sarvasainyaṃ pātālagataṃ paśyet |
buddhāḥ stambhanakuśalāḥ teṣāmapi sainyaṃ stambhayet |
kimanyat ?
atra sandeho na kriyate |
ityāha bhagavān sarvatathāgataḥ kāyavākcittasaṃbhavaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ |
sarvasainyaṃ buddhasainyaṃ tasya stambhanaṃ yasmin sa tathoktam |
ripusaṃtrāsanetyādi |
ripūṇāṃ saddharmapratikṣepakādīnāṃ saṃvāsanasamaye saṃtrā- sanakāle idamucyamānaṃ dhyānaṃ prakalpayet kurvīta |
buddho'pyādikarmiko'pi yadi cedimaṃ prayogamatikrāmet abhyaset sphuṭati loke prakāśate |
nātra sandehaḥ |
āheti prayogasandarśanam |
hūṅkāraparāvṛttiniṣpannaṃ karālavajraṃ pramāṇataḥ saṃsthānataḥ pañcaśūlaṃ vajrakīla- kṛtamupariṣṭāt cakṣurādyavayavopetaṃ nābheradhastādekasūcikamṛta kuṇḍalirūpaṃ dhyātvā- sādhaka: hṛdaye sādhyahṛdaye tena kīlenedaṃ khaṇḍanaṃ nikṛntanaṃ prakalpayet niṣpāda- yet |
nabuddhāstantramantrakuśalāḥ teṣāṃ sainyaṃ vṛddhaṃ kruddhaṃ pratyarthibhūtaṃ nāśayedanu- śāsayet|
nātra saṃśayaḥ |
ripuḥ śatruḥ tasya mohaḥ kalyāṇādhyāśaye'pi samatāyāṃ pravṛttiḥ |
tasyā- pakāro nirasanaṃ yasmin sa tathoktaḥ samādhiḥ |
nāryādyupadrutānāṃ śāntiprayogamāha |
nagara ityādi |
nagare rājadhānyāṃ grāme janapadaikadeśe viṣaye ca janapade prayojayet |
vakṣyamāṇakaṃ prayogaṃ kuryāt anenetyādinā prayogasāmarthyaṃ darśayati |
prathamaṃ tāvat sādhakāḥ sattveṣu mahā- karuṇāmālambya nagarādyuparyantarīkṣagataṃ tālaskandhapramāṇaṃ mahāvajraṃ pañcaraśmyu- petaṃ bhāvayet |
tannimnato raśminivahaṃ kalpoddāhamiva yugāntāgnimiva sattvānāṃ sarvakilviṣadāhakaṃ dhyātvā punaḥ paścāt saṃhāraṃ tasmin vatre sattvānāṃ prave- śamādiśet kuryāt |
spharaṇaṃ tasmād vajrāt nirgamanaṃ kāryamanuṣṭheyam |
kathaṃ ratnaṃ padmarāgādi cintāmaṇiḥ manorathamaṇiḥ prabhā sphaṭikamaṇeriva svaccho dehavarṇaḥ tai ratna- cintāmaṇiprabhaiḥ upalakṣitān dharmameghān anantaroktakrameṇa śodhitadehān sattvarāśīn prabhāvayet svasthāne niveśayet vai paścāt abhiṣekaṃ samādiśet |
anena krameṇa gaganopari vairocananavyūhaṃ svamudrāsamāpanna dhyātvā tadvi- nirgatabodhicittadhārāpravāhaiḥ sattvānabhiṣiñcedityāmnāyaḥ |
anenānantaroktena śāntyarthamuddiṣṭena dhyānavajreṇa duṣpūro'pi prapūryate pūrṇāṅgo bhavati |
sa evaṃ dhyānaphalena puṣṭakāyāścintamaṇiḥ ratnasaṃbhavaḥ tasya śrī [:] sampat sa yasyāsti sa cintāmaṇiśrīmān dānavastra [:] dānātiśayastaṃ prabhāvayati prakaṭayatīti trayodaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā
dānavajraprabhāvakaḥ
tāṃ sattvādhārabhūtāṃ pṛthivī vairocanāni tā akṣobhyavṛndaḥ svādhiṣṭhitāṃ spharet dhyāyāt
tat kimityāha
trikalpāsaṃkhyeyamadhikālaṃ tannagarādisthānaṃ sarvabuddhairadhi- ṣṭhyate paripālyate idamanantaroktaṃ sarvabuddhānāmanāvilamavisaṃvāditaṃ kāyaguhyaṃ kāyarakṣaṇam ||
sarvarogāḥ jvarādayaḥ
teṣāmapanayanaṃ praśamanaṃ tadanantaraṃ vajravat kāyasya saṃbhavo yasmin tannāmakaḥ samādhiḥ
dhyānavajreṇetyādinā ādikarmakāṇāṃ kuśalakriyāṅgabhūtaṃ rakṣāvidhāna- dhyānajena duṣṭakīlena samādānaṃ catuḥsandhyādhiṣṭhānaṃ yattra sthāne yattra tatna mano'nukūlasthāne samārabhedanena vakṣyamāṇena dhyānaṃ rakṣāvidhānaṃ tatpūrvako yogaḥ samādhiḥ tena dhyānayogena tiṣṭhan vajrasattvādiyogamabhyasyan buddheḥ svadevatābhiradhiṣṭhyate ekatāmāpadyate
taddhyānakīlaṃ darśayannāha
vajñetyādi
vajraḥ amṛtamahārājo'mṛta- kuṇḍalistaṃ vajrakīlaṃ nābherūrdhvaṃ krodharūpamadhastāt śūlarūpaṃ prabhāvayet
daśadiśaḥ pūrvādayaḥ tāsāṃ maṇḍalāgrāḥ adhipāḥ lokapālāḥ taiḥ sārdhamanyeṣāmapi vidhnānāṃ śirasi sphuliṅgaṃ jvālāsannibhaṃ kīlaṃ nikhanet āropayet
jagat sādhakādayaḥ tāṃ vikṛti cittavrkṣeipādinā nayanti prāpayantīti va jagadvinayā vighnāḥ teṣāṃ śāntinirasanaṃ tadevābhedyatvāt vajjraṃ tad yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khavajramadhyagataṃ dharmodayasthaṃ uttamaṃ śāntamaṇḍalaṃ cintet dhyāyāt
tasmin oṅkāraniṣpannaṃ vairocanavimbaṃ yathāvad dhyātvā tasya hṛdaye arthinaḥ sādhyaṃ nyaset sthāpayet
tadanantaraṃ khadhātuṃ gaganakuharaṃ locanā [nya ] grataḥ yeṣāṃ te locanāgrā vairocana- samūhāḥ tairlocanāgraimehāsukhasamāpannaiḥ paripūrṇaṃ vyāptaṃ vibhāvayet
sarvān samā- pattisamāpannān saṃhyatyaikīkṛtya tena raśmipiṇḍenāmṛtaughenāturasya śirasi pātayitvā sarvakilviṣāṇi ghātayet
tataḥ strahṛdayādavatāryāturaṃ vigatakalmaṣaṃ purataścandramaṇḍale'vasthā pya romakūpāgravivaraiḥ svakuliśavivarāt buddhameghān prāgraśmirūpeṇa svamūrdhni vivareṇa praviṣṭān spharet niścārayed vratī
katham ?
tairvairocanavyūhaḥ guhya samājapradīpodyotane punargaganakuharamāvṛṇoti chādayatīti niruktyā vrata ityuktaḥ
tadā tasmin kāle tasya puro niṣaṇṇasyābhiṣekaṃ pūrvavat svamudrāsamāpannā buddhameghā bodhi- cittadhārābhirabhiṣekaṃ dadanti
anena dhyānaviśeṣeṇa saṃ sukham ayaḥ atikrāntaḥ
samayo duḥkhasamūhaḥ abhedyo dustaraḥ samayaḥ duḥkhasamūho yasmin sa vajrasamayaḥ
āturaḥ kṣaṇādacirāt śrīmān kalyāṅgaḥ puṣṭaśarīraśca bhavati jāyate ||
buddhasamayo meghāḥ vairocanalocanāsamūhāḥ teṣāṃ vyūhaḥ samāpattiryasmin samādhau tannāmakaḥ
khavajñetyādinā samādhidvayaṃ vacanena leśato darśayati
dharmodayāntasthamāhe- ndramaṇḍalaṃ dhyāyāt
ratnasaṃbhavaniṣpattaye vimbaṃ dharmadharaṃ dhyātveti vacanāt
amitābhaniṣpattaye agnimaṇḍalaṃ dhyāyāt
hṛdaye ratnasaṃbhavāmitābhahṛdaye arthinaṃ yathāsaṃbhavaṃ sādhyadvayaṃ nyaset
khadhātubhavanaṃ ramyaṃ pāṇḍarākhyaiḥ prapūrayet
ityamitābhasamādhimapekṣya vacanam
ratnasaṃbhavasamādhau tu māmakīvyūhaiḥ pītava- rṇairityuktam
ratnaṃ suvarṇaṃ cintāmaṇiḥ padmarāgaḥ tayoḥ prabhā ratnacintāmaṇi- prabhā taṃ raśmimaṇḍalena raśmisāraprabhāvena saṃharedekīkuryāt
idaṃ saṃhṛtaṃ bodhicittadvayaṃ arthinā sādhyasya kāyavākcittanilaye dehe nipātayet
pūrvavat
romakūpāgravivarāt ratnasaṃbhavameghān spharet vratī ami- tābhayogī
· dharmameghān bhāvayet spharet
abhiṣekaṃ samādiśediti pūrvaniṣaṇṇasya ratnameghairdharmameghaiścābhiṣekaṃ kārayet
anenānantaroktena ghyānaprapañcena duṣpū- ro'pi prapūryata iti puṣṭyarthaṃ vacanam
sa hītyādirasyārthaḥ
sa hi sādhyaḥ sādhakasyābhilaṣitārthapradānāt cintāmaṇiprakhyaḥ rājaiśvaryādisamanvitatvāt
śrīmān dānavajramavicchinnadānaṃ tatprabhāsayati dyotayatīti dānavajraprabhāsakaḥ
dharmo'mitābhaḥ tasya meghaḥ samūhaḥ teṣāṃ vyūhaḥ samāpattiḥ sa yasmin samādhau sa tathā ||
ratnameghetyādyapyato niṣkṛṣya khadhātumadhyagatamiti gatārtham
vajrāṅkita- candrārdhaṃ vāyavyamaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā tasmin khavajraḥ akṣobhyaḥ dharmaḥ amitābhaḥ sa agre purastād yasya sa dharmāgro'moghasiddhiḥ vimbakhavajradharmāgramakṣobhyā- trayodaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā moghasiddhimahāmudrāṃ dhyātvā tayoramoghasiddhihṛdaye arthinaṃ sarvajanavaśyatā- kāmaṃ nyaset
buddhairvairocanādibhiḥ bodhisattvaiśca maitreyādibhiśca svakulamudrāsahitaiśca kāma- maṇḍalaṃ paripūrṇaṃ dhyāyāt
pañcatathāgatavarṇabhedena tejaḥ pañcākārabodhicittadhārāṃ tatra sādhye nipātayet
sa evaṃvidhaḥ sādhyaḥ tatkṣaṇādevācirādeva sarvatathāgatamanojño bhavati
mañjuśrītulyaḥ sadṛśaḥ samyak kāśate dīpyata iti saṃkāśaḥ padaṃ rājyādikam
tattra karmakṛt vyāpārakṛt
sa sādhyo bhavati
pūrvavat
purastāt niṣaṇṇasya prakṛṣṭātmanaḥ āttamanaskāḥ pañcakulabuddhabodhisattvā abhiṣekamahotsavaṃ prayacchanti
tataḥ kimityāha
vaśamānayatīti sarvajagadudita udaya prāpto darśanenaiva dṛṣṭimātreṇa vaśīkaroti
ratnasamayantīti ratnasamayāḥ rājāmātyāḥ teṣāṃ mevāḥ samūhāḥ taiḥ kṛto vyūhaḥ saivārthamārgasya paryupāsanaṃ yasmin tannāmakaḥ samādhiḥ
khavajradharmāgramityatra yaḥ sthāpito'kṣobhyaḥ samādhiḥ tasya karmaśeṣamāha khavajretyādi
akṣobhyayogavān khavajraṃ gaganakuharaṃ rākṣasairyakṣādibhiḥ krūraiḥ paravadhotsaivaiḥ pracaṇḍaiḥ apratihataparākramaiḥ krodhadāruṇaiḥ krodhavaśagaiḥ śṛgālaiḥ jambukādibhiḥ vividhaiḥ piśitāsanaiḥ sattvaiḥ kākaiḥ gṛdhraḥ śvānaiḥ pūrṇaṃ prabhāvayet
tataḥ śrāgneyamaṇḍalasthaṃ ripuṃ śatruṃ udghāṭitasaṃpuṭaṃ bhāvayet
sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvasattvānāṃ sopakārīti dhyātvā yogamimaṃ prayojayet ||
sādhyasyāntrādikaṃ sarvai rākṣasādibhirākṛṣṭaṃ gṛhītvā bhakṣitaṃ prabhāvayet
punaḥ nānāpraharaṇadharairākrāntaṃ khaṇḍaśacchidyamānaṃ bhāvayet
tataḥ buddho buddhayānī vajradharaḥ ādikarmikaḥ yadyanena prayogeṇa prabhāvyate cintyate pakṣābhyantaraṃ pakṣādarvāk pūrṇena pakṣeṇa vā mriyate naśyati
nāva saṃśayaḥ ||
vajramegho'kṣobhyaḥ tasya samayā rākṣasādayaḥ teṣāṃ vyūho vijṛmbhitaṃ yasmin samādhau sa tannāmakaḥ samādhiḥ
punarapyuktānāmeva devatānāṃ mukhabhujavarṇapraharaṇavyavasthāmāha
trimukhe - tyādinā vairocanabhāvamāha
kamudakaṃ tasyāṇḍamabhraṃ śaradi kāṇḍaṃ śaratkāṇḍaṃ tatsamaprabhaṃ dhyāyāt
sitaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ mahāraktaṃ mukhatrayaṃ dhārayantaṃ tatra sitaṃ guhyasamājapradīpodyota madhyamukhaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ mahāraktaṃ kusumbhapuṣpaṃ tadvarṇaṃ vāmamukhaṃ jaṭāmakuṭamaṇḍita- miti vairocanasya mukhavarṇaniyamaḥ
tadvad vajriṇaṃ akṣobhyaṃ kṛṣṇaraktasitānanaṃ dhyāyāt
lokadhātusamākulaṃ svacchamaṇḍalacakraparivṛtaṃ evaṃ rāgiṇamamitābhaṃ trimukhaṃ jaṭāmakuṭabhūṣitam
evaṃ devatāttrayaṃ bhāvayet
dhruvamuktakarmasu sidhyate
eṣāṃ vairocanādīnāṃ dakṣiṇamūlakare vajracakrapadmāni bhāvayet
vajraṃ mudrārūpaṃ ṣaḍbhujaṃ bhāvayet
nānāpraharaṇamiti sādhanoktakrameṇa yathāsvacihna- dharam
śubhamiṣṭārthasaṃpādanāt
evaṃ ratnasaṃbhavādīnāmapi mūlasvacihnaṃ bhāvyam
locanāṃ vairocanamudrāṃ vairocanavat trimukhāṃ cintet
sarvasattvahitaiṣiṇīṃ sarvasattvaśāntikāriṇīm
khavajraḥ akṣobhyaḥ taṃ nayatyātmasamīpamiti khavajranetrīṃ māmakīṃ mahā-
rājñīmagramahiṣīṃ trimukhāṃ akṣobhyavat vibhāvayet
vāgvajro'mitābhaḥ taṃ nayatīti vāgvajranetrīṃ pāṇḍaravāsinīmamitābhavat dhyāyāt
vajrāṅkitamutpalaṃ taddhārayatīti vajrotpaladharāṃ vidyāmamoghasiddhi- mudrāṃ tārāṃ mukhādinā amoghasiddhivat dhyāyāt
yamāntakamityārabhya yāvad dhyānamaṇḍalamiti sugamam
dhyānamaṇḍalamā- jñācakram
tejorāśiḥ jayoṣṇīṣādayaḥ krodhāḥ ye cānye mantracakriṇaḥ maitreyādayaḥ
ebhiḥ samaye kule saṃbhujyanta iti samayasaṃbhogāḥ vajrādicihnāni tairbhāvanīyāḥ prati prati pṛthak pṛthak
mantrāṇāṃ devatānāṃ samādhīni
amṛtānyapramāṇāni samudāhṛtāḥ kathitāḥ
ataevaikaikasya tu krodhasya yamāntakādīnāmekaikasya tu ceṭaceṭībhūtabhūktyā - diprakāreṇa samādhayaḥ
tata ūrdhvaṃ ākāśadhāturapyadhikatarā bhavatīti samādhe- rasaṃkhyeyatā kathitā
idānīṃ niṣpannadevatāniṣpatti darśayannāha khadhātvityādi
khadhātuḥ prabhāsvaraḥ tadvadbhūtaṃ svacchamaṇḍalamābhāsatrayaṃ uttamaṃ candravajñādyanapekṣatvāt
tena buddhavimbaṃ mahāvajradhararūpaṃ prabhāvitvā niṣpādya tato vairocanaṃ prabhāvayet spharet
trayodaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā katham ?
svacchaṃ candranibhaṃ nīhārādidoṣarahitatvāt, śaśivimbopamaṃ śāntaṃ saumyarasopetaṃ nānāraśmisamaprabhaṃ pañca raśmikṛtaprabhāmaṇḍalaṃ ādarśo darpaṇa: tatsaṃbhūtaṃ tadgataṃ tridhātukāsyamaṇḍanaṃ traidhātukaṃ maṇḍarnamiva mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇo- petatvāt
sarvālaṅkāraracitaṃ evamātmānaṃ dhyātvā jñātvā bodhimapratiṣṭhita nirvāṇaṃ sa paśyati labhate
anena dhyānavajraprabhāvanairiti yogaḥ
buddhamāhātmyaṃ buddhaiśvaryaṃ sarvalokavaśaṅkaraṃ śrāvakādyavanatikaraṃ ihaiva janmani asminneva dehe sthitvā prāpyate ||
vairocanaśya niṣpannakrameṇa saṃbhavo yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khadhātvityādi
vajramaṇḍalamābhāsatrayaṃ buddhavimvamityādi pūrvavat
svacchakāyaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ tattulyaḥ īṣadastrānvitatvāt krūram
śāntaṃ vajratvaṃ vṛddhatvaṃ laghu kṣipraṃ prāpnuyāt
sarvasamayā māṇḍaleyāḥ te saṃbhavanti yasmin sarvasamayasaṃbhavaḥ cāruvajro'kṣobhyaḥ yasmin samādhau sa tathoktaḥ
khavajñetyādi
dharmamaṇḍalamābhāsattrayam
vuddhavimvamityādi pūrvavat
trikāyābhedyo mahāvajradharaḥ
tatsaṃbhavaṃ tadudbhūtaṃ dharmamāhātmyaṃ vāgīśvaratvaṃ prāpyate labhate
jñānodadhivibhūpaṇaṃ niṣpanna kramasanādhisthaḥ
dharmasattvo'mitābhaḥ sa saṃbhavati yasmā[t] saddharmasattvasamudbhavo mahāvajradharaḥ
tasya samayasaṃbhavaḥ pariniṣpannakramaṃ tadeva vajraṃ yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khadhātvityādi
candropalakṣitaṃ maṇḍalamābhāsatrayam
Buddhavimvamityādi
pūrvavat
pāṇau dakṣiṇapradhānakare ratnaṃ cakraṃ ratnaṃ vaidhātukavaśaṅkaraṃ tasya svabhāva- kathanam
sarvaśuddhikaraṃ jñānamiti
cakrasya svasādhakajanasya cintāmaṇiprabhaṃ cintāmaṇiprakhyam
locanāsamayaḥ locanā mahāmudrā tasyā hastāgraṃ hastasāraṃ cakraṃ tad yasyāṃ samādhau vidyate sā tathā
khavajñetyādi
candramaṇḍalamiti śūnyatāttrayam
Buddhavimvamiti pūrvavat
khavajrāgrī māmakī
khaṃ akṣobhyaḥ tasya samayāgrī bhāryā
tasyā dakṣiṇakare raktānuviddhanīlotpalam
traidhātuketyādi tasyaiva viśeṣaṇam
siddhivajriṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ rahasyabhūtam
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane khaṃ prabhāsvaraḥ sā bhā prabhavo yasyā sākhamātā akṣobhyaḥ
ratnaraśmimeghā ratnaraśmisamūhaḥ sa vajrasvabhāvo yasya ratnaraśmirmeghavajro ratnaketuḥ tayoḥ hlādanaṃ māmakī yasmin samādhau sa tannāmakaḥ
khavajñetyādi
dharmamaṇḍalaṃ jñānatrayaṃ buddhavimvamityādi pūrvavat
māyayā lakṣyata iti māyālakṣyaḥ prabhāsvaraṃ tadeva pradhānatvādagram
tasmāt saṃbhūtāṃ māyālakṣyāgrasaṃbhūtām
nāgaraktadharo'mitābhaḥ tasya priyāmiṣṭām
tasya tattvaṃ paramārthatattvaṃ tadevābhisaṃbodhiḥ tasya darśanavatraṃ pāṇḍaravāsinī yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khavajñetyādi
siddhimaṇḍalaṃ prajñopāyopalabdhakam
Buddhavimvamityādi pūrvavat
samayo'moghasiddhiḥ
tārā tasya bhāryāgrā pradhānaguṇopetā sā yasmin samādhau sa tathā
idānīṃ krodhasamādhimāha khavajñetyādi
sūryamaṇḍalopalakṣitamālokā- bhāsādikaṃ buddhavimvamityādi pūrvavat
bhayasya maṇḍaṃ sāraḥ tān lātīti bhayamaṇḍalam dakṣiṇapradhānakareṇa gṛhītakhaḍgaṃ tasya makuṭe vairocanasthiti dhyātvā tuṣyanti nandanti vajriṇaḥ sādhakaḥ sarveṣāmanāpakāriprabhṛtayaḥ ( ? )
te krodhā yeṣāṃ sarvakrodhānāṃ jñānavajriṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ samayaḥ alakṣaṇīyo mārgaḥ
yamāntakaḥ spharaṇaḥ mahāvajradharaḥ tena viśrāmyate niṣpādyata iti yamāntakaspharaṇāvabhāso yamāntakaḥ tasya vyūhaḥ cintā yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khavajñetyādi pūrvavat
aparājitavajjravyūho niṣpattiḥ yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khavajñetyādi pūrvavat
hayaḥ hayagrīvaḥ
tasyotpattivyūha utpattiracanā yasmin samādhau sa tathā ||
khavajñetyādi pūrvavat
amṛtamayaḥ amṛtakuṇḍaliḥ vajramutpattiviśeṣo gṛhṇātīti vajrālayamaṇḍalaḥ
trividhāni vajranetrāṇi yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khavajñetyādi pūrvavat
kṛṣṇodadhiḥ krodhasamudraḥ tasya praharaṇaṃ daṇḍavajra- svabhāvo yasya taṃ daṇḍavajriṇam
vajrāṅkito daṇḍaḥ daṇḍavajraḥ samayāgraḥ alaṅghanīyatvāt
sa yasyāṃ sā vajradaṇḍasamayāgravatī tannāmakaḥ samādhiḥ
khavajjramābhāsattrayam
tasya dhātuḥ prabhāsvaraṃ tadeva padamākrāntaṃ yasmin samādhau sa tathā
khavajñetyādi
vidyā laukikasiddhayaḥ
tāsāṃ cakraṃ samūhaṃ yasya tadvidyā- cakraṃ prabhāvayet
vajrajvālāparivṛtaṃ cakraraśmyāliṅgitadeham
uṣṇīṣāḥ gaganacarāḥ teṣāṃ cakraṃ samūhaḥ
tena samāgamyate pūjyata iti uṣṇīṣacakrasamayaḥ
vidyādharasamayaḥ tena yuktaṃ calacakraṃ yasmin samādhau sa tathā ||
yathārutam ||
khadhātāvityādi
meghavarṇaṃ śarīraṃ yasya sa meghavatrī
vajrasumbhaḥ prabhāvyate niṣpādyate yasmin samādhau sa tathā
evaṃ dvividhakrameṇa samādhidvayaṃ nirdiśya tathaivopasaṃharannāha nirodha ityādi
nirodho maraṇādikaṃ krodhacakrena yamāntakādisamādhinā kriyate
kena ?
buddha- cakraniṣevinā
devatācakra ālamvakena
tadapi kathaṃ kartavyamityāha samādhī- tyādi
tathatālamvanapūrvakaṃ candramaṇḍalālamvanaṃ vajraṃ tadupari vījavinyāsapūrvaṃ vajrādyālamvanam
tatparāvṛttyā mahāvajradharādidevatālamvanamityetāni samādhi- vajrajñānāni sidhyanti phalanti
vajramaṇḍalāt ājñācakrālamvanāt
neyārthaḥ
buddhā vairocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ cakraṃ samūhaḥ
buddhacakramiti kalpanāṃ niṣevituṃ svabhāvo yeṣāṃ te buddhacakraniṣevino rūpaskandhādayaḥ
teṣāṃ nirodhaḥ paramārtha- satyānupraveśaḥ
krodhāḥ prākṛtadehāvaimukhyaṃ tadeva cakramāyudhaṃ yasya saḥ
krodha- cakraniṣpannakramasamādhisthaḥ
tena kāyavyutthānadarśanaṃ samādhivajrajñānānīti samādhyātmikāni vajrajñānāni vajramaṇḍalāt paramārthamaṇḍalāt vyutthitasya sidhyanti
nītārthaḥ
vajraḥ tathāgatāḥ samayālocanādayaḥ vyūhāḥ krodhāḥ teṣāṃ niṣpattyarthaṃ tattvārthabhāvanā paramārthasatyālamvanaṃ tatpūrvakaṃ saṃbodhirmahāmudrāniṣpattiḥ
tatpratipādako granthasamūhaḥ ||
|| pradīpoddyotanaṭīkāyāṃ trayodaśapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
|| caturdaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
idānīṃ caturvidhamantravādānusāreṇa śāntikādikarmaprasaro'nuṣṭheya iti darśanāya caturdaśapaṭala upakṣipyate |
tatra tāvat karmaprasarānupūrvī vyākhyā- tantrādavatāryate |
śāntikaṃ paiṣṭikaṃ caiva śuklapakṣe tu kārayet |
vaśyābhicārakarmādi kṛṣṇapakṣe samārabhet ||
uttarābhimukhaḥ śānti pauṣṭikaṃ prāṅmukhastathā |
yāmyāṃ tu raudrakarmāṇi vaśyākarṣaṇaṃ paścime ||
āgneyyāṃ māraṇaṃ caivamaiśānyāṃ dveṣaṇakriyām |
vāyavyāṃ sthānacalanamaiśānyāṃ stambhanaṃ bhavet ||
śāntike śuklavarṇaṃ tu pītavarṇaṃ tu pauṣṭike |
vaśyākarṣaṇayo rāktaṃ kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ tu māraṇe ||
śāntikaṃ maitracittena harṣacittena pauṣṭikam |
rāgacittena vaśyaṃ tu krodhacittena māraṇam ||
pradoṣe śāntikaṃ kuryāt pratyūṣe pauṣṭikaṃ tathā |
vaśyābhicārukaṃ karma madhyāhne cārdharātrike ||
niṣpannakramasaṃsthastu madhyāhnavelāmāśritya śāntikādi karma kuryāditi viśeṣaḥ |
yathoktaṃ bhagavatā vyākhyātantre |

athetyādi
bhagavān mahāvajradharaḥ mahāvajrasamayāḥ sādhakāḥ teṣāṃ tattvamākarṣaṇādinā sattvārthaṃ karaṇaṃ tasyotpattiḥ prakāśanaṃ tad vajramabhedyaṃ yasmin samādhau tannāmakaṃ samādhimālambya mahāsamayāḥ ghaṭamānāḥ teṣāṃ trivajrakāyavākcittaṃ tasmin guhyaṃ gopyamaprakāśyattvāt
vāksamaya pravyāhārastasya tattvamananyathātvaṃ tasya padaṃ pratiṣṭhābhūtam
ākarṣaṇādi karma svakāyavākcittavaprebhyo niścārayet
buddhavajrā dussādhyāḥ teṣāṃ trikāyeṣu kāyavākcitteṣu vajrasattvaḥ sthira- cittaḥ sādhakaḥ tena vibhāvanā dhyānaviśeṣaḥ kartavyaḥ
pāśavajrāṅkuśadharaiḥ sādhyakāyādiṣu yathākramaṃ pāśādyākāraiḥ raśmipuñjaḥ praviṣṭavibhāvanā tadeta- duttamaṃ pradhānaṃ buddhākarṣaṇaṃ piṇḍākarṣaṇam
buddha: adhyeṣakaḥ tasya vākkāyo vacanasamūhaḥ tena mahaccakraṃ maṇḍalaṃ yasya sa mahācakramaṇḍalādhipatiḥ tasya cittākṛṣṭyarthaṃ vajrāṅkuśasya prayogataḥ preraṇataḥ vajrasattvo mahārājā tasmin cittavatraḥ dhruvamekāntamākṛṣyate sadā sadya eva
trivajrābhedyabhāvanairiti cakrapadmamahāvajjairiti moharāgadveṣaracitaiḥ
vairocanādiyogibhiḥ
vajrāṅkuśaṃ vajrādyaṅakuśaṃ tatprabhedena tena sādhyasya hṛdyā- saktena sarvamantrākarṣaṇaṃ sarvasādhyākarṣaṇamuttamam
idaṃ svacihnenāpyākṛṣṭiḥ
mahāvajrasattvayogī svamantrapuruṣaṃ svadevatāṃ sarvavajramayaṃ śivaṃ śobhanaṃ dhyātvā śreṣṭhāṃ mānuṣīṃ tatsthitāṃ dhyātvā hṛdvajrāṅkuśayogataḥ hṛdyāsakta- vajrāṅkuśenākṛṣyamāṇāṃ vātamaṇḍalasaṃyogāt vātyayā preryamāṇaṃ dhyātvā dhruva- guhyasamājapradīpodyotane mākṛṣyate
tenaiva vairocanayogī vairocanamahāvidhi candramaṇḍalākārapariṇataṃ dhyātvā śakrabhavanādamṛtakuṇḍalinā śacīṃ śakrabhāryāmācchidya tatrāropyānīyamānāṃ dhyātvā [ta] damṛtakuṇḍalimantraṃ pañcāśadvārānuccārayannānayediti
vajramaṇḍalakaṃ ṭakkirājaṃ vajrāṅkuśaṃ dhyātvā tadārūḍhāṃ surakanyāṃ tenaiva hṛdyākṛṣyamāṇāṃ vātyāpreritāmākarṣayediti
krodhavajraḥ akṣobhyastasya samaya: ājñā yasmin taṃ krodhavajrasamayaṃ vajrapātālavāsinaṃ sumbharājaṃ saṃcodya śūlavajjrāṅakuśapāśaiḥ tena śūlena hṛdi vighvā vajrāṅkuśenākṛṣya pāśena vaddhvākṛṣyamāṇāṃ vātacakrārūḍhāṃ dhyātvā asurakanyāmākarṣayediti
gairikādikaṃ varṇakaṃ vajrāṅkuśaprayogataḥ vajrāṅkuśākāraṃ candragrahaṇe mukhe vajre dharmodaye vā prakṣipya yāvad grahaṇānmokṣaḥ tāvat sādhayet samārpatta kuryāt tataḥ tadapanīya saṃrakṣya brahmādīnāmanyatamasya nāma likhet sa niyata- māgacchati

bhayamutkaṇṭhāmāpede prāptā
vidyādharasya mahākanyāṃ rūpādiguṇasamanvitāṃ calatkanakakuṇḍalāṃ dolāyamāna- karṇabhūṣaṇāṃ samayāgreṇa vidhiviśeṣeṇākṛṣyānīya mantravidyayānantaroktayānayet
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane atrāyaṃ vidhiḥ strīkapāle vāyavyamaṇḍalaṃ tanmadhye vidyādhara kanyāmā likhya vajrāṅkuśādhiṣṭhitena pratyālīḍhenākramya mantramuccārayannākarṣayati nirodhetyādi sarvamantrāḥ sarpādayaḥ strīpuṃnapuṃsakabhedena yasminnirudhyante saṃgṛhyanta iti nirodhaḥ vajrarājaḥ tyakṣaramantraḥ nirodhaśca vajrarājaścāsau nirodhavajrarājaḥ tena niṣpannena praveśādirūpeṇānavarataṃ pravartamānenāgracāruṇā īpsitārthasādhanāt śobhanena trivajrajñānāḥ akṣobhyādayaḥ tebhyaḥ saṃbhūtāḥ anantaroktā locanādiyamāntakādimantrāḥ kṣaṇādacirāt kṛṣyanti ākṛṣyante siddhimupagacchanti sarvataḥ sarve mantrāḥ sandhyābhāṣā mantraparāyaṇānāṃ viśeṣavidhimāha athavetyādi sarvakrodhā yamāntakā dayaḥ teṣāṃ mantrāḥ lakṣajāpena mantriṇaḥ sādhakasya karmakarāḥ sarvārthasādhakāḥ proktāḥ vijaneṣu mahāṭavyādiṣu mahatsu parvateṣu ācāryetyādi prācāryanindanaparāḥ gurunindakāḥ vajrācāryanindakāśca mahāyānāgranindakāḥ vajrayānapratikṣepakāḥ ete cānye [ca] pāpakāriṇaḥ māraṇīyāḥ prayatnenāśithilavīryeṇa athavā teṣāṃ sthānacālanamuccāṭanaṃ kartavyam anenaiva kṛtena karmaṇā paramāṃ bodhimapratiṣṭhitalakṣaṇāṃ mantrasiddhi cā pnuyāditi ityāh ca mahāvajradharaḥ iti saṅgītikāravacanam teṣāṃ vadhyānāṃ saṃpuṭodghāṭanamāha daśadigityādi daśadikparyāpanna sattvarāśīnāṃ kāyavākcittaghātanaṃ māraṇīyaṃ bhāvanīyaṃ sādhakena bhāvyam keṣā mityāha ripūṇāṃ duṣṭacetasāmiti duṣṭacittānāmācāryanindakānāṃ bhāvanīya mityanena saṃvandhaḥ rudhiretyādi raktoṣṇodakaviṇmūtrairekīkṛtya tairvastraṃ mārjayitvā tenāvaguṇṭhitakāyo bhūtvā liṅgamākramyeti abhicārukadravyeṇa sādhyapratikṛti kṛtvā hṛdaye nāma vidarbhya viśvavajrāṅkitadakṣiṇacaraṇena prati kṛtimākramyākṣobhyayogavān krodharājaṃ yamāntakādyanyatamakrodhamantramaṣṭaśataṃ japet buddho'pi karmaprasaranipuṇo'pi śīryate mriyate salilārdragatamiti vastraṃ kevalamuṣṇodakenāpi māpitvā pūrvoktavidhimā caret viṇmūtreṇāpi temayitvā prayogamārabhet tadvat bhasmodakena vastraṃ rañjayitvā māraṇamārabhet duḥsādhyo'pi mriyate caturdaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā nagna ityādi yamāntakādikrodha[[]haṅkāramālambya daṃṣṭrākarālatvā dvikaṭaḥ atyuccadehatvāt utkaṭo vināśodyatatvāt saṃbhrāntaḥ sādhakaḥ liṅgaṃ pūrvavanniṣpannāṃ pratikṛti pādena viśvavajrāṅkitadakṣiṇapādatalenākramya khadhātuma pyamūrtamākāśamapi kiṃ punarmūrtamityatiśayoktyā dhyānamāhātmyamāviṣkriyate mātṛgṛha ityādinā prastutasya karmaṇaḥ sthānamāha brahmāṇī vaiṣṇavī rudrāṇī indrāṇī kauverī vārāhī cāmuṇḍā ceti saptalokamātaraḥ tāsāṃ gṛhe śmaśānaśūnyāgāracatuṣpathaikaliṅgaikavṛkṣeṣu mānuṣāsthimayamityādi jaṅghāpradeśe asthi aṣṭāṅgulapramāṇaṃ saṃgṛhya śmaśānakarpaṭe abhicārukadravyeṇa viśvavajramālikhya tadvaraṭake sādhyanāmālikhya taccakramasthisuśire prakṣipya sādhyapādapāṃśunā pidhāya aṣṭaśatavārakrodhamantra japtamaridvāre nikhanet buddhāḥ prājñāḥ sattvāḥ trikāyāḥ hīnamadhyotkṛṣṭaśabdarāśayaḥ teṣāmīpsitārtha pradānād varadaḥ jñānaṃ pratyupakāracintā ajñānaṃ nirapekṣatā tābhyāṃ varjitaḥ evaṃvidho'pi sannācāryanindakatvena dūṣitaḥ syāt pakṣābhyantareṇa pūrṇena pakṣeṇa vā bhrasyate sthānāt mriyate vā kapālaparipūrṇamavraṇamādāya vijño vidyān tatra kapāle mantrapadaṃ yathābhipretaṃ kopamantraṃ sādhyanāmasahitaṃ likhet japan vā vajrabhāṣayeti vajrabhāṣā krodhavāk tayā japan vāgjāpaṃ kuryāt tatkapālamaridvāre grāme vā nikhanet proccāṭayed dhruvamiti tālapatre'nyatva karpaṭādau vā abhicārukadravyeṇa nāmasahitaṃ krodhamantra mālikhya arigṛhanikaṭe dvāre vā gopya nikhanya tameva mantraṃ catuḥ sandhyaṃ krodha vācā paṭhet evaṃ kṛte śuṣyati pūrvakrameṇa paścānmriyate mahāsamayo māraṇādikaṃ tasya heturvajraḥ saṃgītikāraḥ mahāvajradharaḥ evamāheti athetyādi bhagavān mahāvajradharaḥ yathā yathā duṣṭabhāvaṃ gatāḥ tathā tathā niṣedhyatvaṃ gatāḥ sādhyāḥ teṣāṃ kāyavākcittanivandhanaṃ niścalīkaraṇaṃ vajrama bhedyasvarūpaṃ yasmin tannāmakaṃ samādhimāmukhīkṛtya vaidhātukāḥ hīnamadhyotkṛṣṭāḥ trividhasādhyāḥ teṣāṃ kāyādipīḍanārthaṃ vajramabhedyakīlanaṃ mantraṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayāmāsa guhyasamājapradīpodyotane omityādiko mantraḥ nirantaḥ gha gha gha gha ghātaketyāmantraṇam ghātaya sarvaduṣṭāniti preraṇam phaṭ phaḍiti tadīyakaṃ bījam kīlaya kīlaya niścalī kuru sarvapāpān pāpādhyāśayān phaṭ bhedanam hū~ hū~ tattvacodanam vajra kīleti kṛtyenāmantraṇam vajradhara ājñāpayatīti vajradhara evātmānaṃ nirdi śati kāyavākcittavajramiti duṣṭānāmityabhiprāyaḥ hū~ phaṭ phaṭ punarapi codanā athetyādi asmin kīla[na] mantre niścārite duḥsādhyatvāt vajrāḥ mahānubhāvatvānmahadhikāḥ śakrādayaḥ mantrasparśāt pūrvaṃ mūrcchitā bhayamāpannāḥ tadanantaraṃ labdhasaṃjñakāḥ bhītāḥ khavajracitto mahāvajradharaśaraṇaṃ gatāḥ mānuṣāsthītyādinātra vidhimāha abhicārukadravyaiḥ sādhyapratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā karpaṭe viṣādinā nāmālikhya puttalikāhṛdaye nidhāya mānuṣāsthikhadirasārā dīnāmanyatamakīlamūrdhvataḥ krodharūpamayaḥ śūlākṛtiṃ nirmāya mastakādārabhya yāvatpādāntaṃ mantramāvartayan vajjramudgareṇākoṭṭayet anena kāyavākcitta duṣṭānāṃ vināśanaṃ bhavati dhyānena kīlanavidhimāha vajrasattvetyādi sādhanoktakrameṇa bodhicitta vajrayogavān akṣobhyotsargānupraveśena sphuliṅgākulasuprabhamiti īṣaddaṃṣṭrā karālavadanaṃ raktaprabhāmaṇḍalamadhyagamātmānaṃ dhyātvā daśakrodhānutsārya digvidikṣu sthāpayitvā trivajjrakāyaparyantaṃ vyātmakaṃ vimbaṃ sumbharūpaṃ dhyātvā prayojayet anantaroktaṃ kīlanavidhi dhyānena kuryāt na kevalamakṣobhyarūpeṇetyāha vairocanetyādi vairocanādyālamvanenāpīdaṃ kīlanaṃ kāryamiti bhūtārthaḥ caturdaśapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā anena dhyānaprayogeṇa buddho'pi kīlavidhijño'pi sādhakaḥ kīlyate niścalī- kriyate

bhagavān mahāvajradharaḥ
mahāvairocanasvabhāvaḥ kāyaḥ

asmin vijṛ- mbhitaṃ kīlasthavilasanaṃ yasmin samādhau taṃ samāpadya [sva]kāyasamayaḥ kāyavalaṃ tasyākṣepakaṃ nikṛntanaṃ vajrakīlamantraṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayāmāsa
omityādiko niranto mantraḥ

anyonyetyādinā kīlana prayogamāha

hrīriti sādhyavāgākarṣaṇaṃ vījaṃ bhūrbhuva ityāmantraṇe
vikasita ityādi
vikasitaṃ prasāritaṃ jñānatrayodayakaraṃ padmaṃ jñānapadmaṃ tena vikasitajñānapadmena yoṣidbhagena bhage vajramevāṅguliḥ vajrāṅguliḥ tasya viśeṣaṇaṃ rāgavajrapadākrāntamiti rāgavajraḥ sādhyavacanapravandhaḥ tasya padena kuliśāgreṇākrāntamākramaṇaṃ yathā bhavati tathā
idaṃ vajrakīlanipātanam
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane hatamāttre yonyantarbhāvitasādhyajihvāgre spṛṣṭamāte mahārājaḥ amitābhaḥ svabhāvaḥ sādhyavākprapañcaḥ trikāyālayasaṃbhavaḥ
ākāśodbhūta uttiṣṭhate

athetyādinā cittakīlanavidhimāha

cittotsāhaḥ tasyākṣepaḥ cittotsāhāya nayanaṃ pratikīlanamantramuccārayāmāsa

svamudrādharmodayāntarbhūtāgneyamaṇḍalasthasādhyahṛtpadme bhāsvareṇa svakuliśena hatamātre ākoṭitamāne mahāvastraṃ sādhyacittaṃ tatsvabhāvaṃ cittaṃ vajrasamayāgreṇa prayogaviśeṣeṇa uttiṣṭhate hṛdayānnirgacchati

athetyādi
te buddhā niṣpannakramasthā bodhisattvā mahāsattvāḥ kīdṛgvidhāḥ ?
buddhayānīyā anarghayānasaṃprasthitāḥ ādikarmakāḥ trikāyāḥ teṣāmagrāḥ sattvadhātu- hitaiṣiṇaḥ sattvārthakaraṇasamarthāḥ te tuṣṭāḥ prathamaṃ śrutimātreṇa tataḥ prāmodya- matiśayaprītiṃ saṃprāptāḥ svadhigame idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ svānubhavaprakāśakaṃ ghoṣaṃ akārṣuḥ kṛtavantaḥ
aho ityāścarye guhyapadaṃ mantrapadaṃ tat śreṣṭham
'sārasamuccayaḥ mantrāṇāṃ- prayogaviśeṣaḥ dharmapadaṃ śāntīmati tasyaiva padasya dhyānena saṃpādanam
vajra- vidāraṇaṃ duṣṭīvagrahaḥ
l sarvabuddhānāṃ karmaprasarasamarthānāṃ kīlanaṃ tadāpyāścaryam
bodhisattvā anye sāmānyajanāḥ mahāyaśāḥ dikpālāḥ teṣāmapi kīlanaṃ yat tadapyāścaryam
pṛthak kāyādīnāṃ yad dhyānena kīlanamuktaṃ tadāpyāścaryamityāhū ratnapudgalāḥ

mantratattvasamuccayamiti

caturdaśapaṭala ṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittānyevādbhūtāni
eko'pi kāyavākcittaḥ svaṃ sva- paravibhāgamālambya nikṛntatīti tairmantrāṇāmālikālijanitānāṃ triliṅgaṃ svabhāveṣu tyakṣareṣu praveśanaṃ tadākarṣaṇaṃ tena vijṛmbhitaṃ vilasitaṃ śāntikādi karmaprasarasādhanaṃ tenaiva rājate dīpyata ityarthaḥ

nāsandhyam ||
niṣprapañcacaryāṃ darśayannāha dvādaśadikāṃ dvādaśavarṣapramāṇa [i] caṇḍālasya kanyāṃ duhitaraṃ śraddhādiguṇānvitatvāt mahātmā sādhayediti tantramantra suśikṣitāṃ kārayet nityaṃ sarvakālaṃ suśikṣayet

katham ?
vajramaṇḍalabhāvanaiḥ vajra- maṇḍalamakṣobhyamaṇḍalaṃ tadbhāvayanti prakaṭayantīti vajramaṇḍalabhāvanāni māṇḍale - yadevatārūpāṇi tairmaṇḍitaṃ maṇḍalaṃ kārayedityabhiprāyaḥ ||
sarvalakṣaṇaṃ pādādikeśāntaṃ yacchubhalakṣaṇaṃ tena saṃśuddhāmujvalāṃ cāruvaktrāṃ sādhakonmukhāṃ suśobhanāṃ ananyaraktāṃ sarvālaṅkārāḥ ratikauśalaṃ tena saṃpūrṇā- evaṃ sādhakaḥ kāyavākcittavajriṇamātmānaṃ vairocanamahāvimbaṃ maṇḍala - madhyasthaṃ dhyātvā kathaṃ dhyāyamānā mantrā akṣobhyādayaḥ teṣāṃ prayogaḥ yathā- vasthopamaṃ tena buddhasamaprabhaḥ vairocanībhūya sādhayediti

eṣo hītyādi
sarvamantrāṇāṃ ratnasaṃbhavāmitā- ddhī bhāmoghasiddhināṃ samayaḥ sādhanavidhiḥ duratikramo'laṅghanīyaḥ
sa sādhakaḥ tatkṣaṇādeva bhāvanānantarameva vajrasattvasamaprabhaḥ ratnaketutulyo bhavati
tadyogayuktaḥ sarvadharmadharo rājā amitābhatulyo bhavati
tadyogayuktaḥ kāmamokṣaprasiddhako'moghasiddhistattulyo bhavati karmaku[śa ] layogī
cāruvaktrāmityādinā amitābhayogino viśeṣavidhiṃ darśayati
cāruvaktrāṃ sādhakonmukhīṃ viśālākṣīṃ prajñāvatīṃ suśobhanāṃ raktagaurāṃ sādhayet sādhako nityaṃ vajradharmavibhāvanairamitābhaprayogaiḥ
evaṃ pravṛttaḥ sādhakaḥ vajradharmātmā amitābhasvabhāvaḥ daśabhūmīśvaro bhavati
vāksamayaḥ vāgaiśvaryaṃ taddhārakaḥ sarvāgraḥ sarvasattvaviśiṣṭaḥ parameśvaro rājā cakravartī bhavati
kalpitayogittrāt
evaṃ kalpitayogināṃ mudrāvibhāgaṃ pradarśya niṣpannakramasthānāṃ mudrāmāha

tadātmā tadadhigatavān idaṃ guhyasamāvaham

astaṃgata ityādi vajrārke sūrye astaṃ gate sandhyākālamatikramya vinaṣṭe yathālabdhopakaraṇena gurumārādhayet
tato guruprasādādardharātre prāptābhiṣekaḥ aruṇodgamavelāyāṃ rātribhāgāpagame sūryodayāt prāk bhāvanottamairabhisaṃbodhikramopadeśaiḥ sidhyate viśuddho bhavatītyayaṃ vāhyābhisaṃvodhikramaḥ
adhyātmamāha
vajretyādi
vajrārke upāyajñāne samāpattilakṣaṇe samu[d]- bhūya bhūyo'staṃ gate prajñābhāvamāpadya niruddhe sādhanaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ pūrva[i]gaṃ matvā prabhāsvarasādhakaṃ ālokopalabdhakaṃ samārabhet prādurbhavati
aruṇa: avidyā guhyasamājapradīpodyotane tasyodgamavelāyāṃ atikramaṇakāle sādhako bhāvanottamaiḥ śūnyātiśūnyamahāśūnyaiḥ hetupratyayaiḥ sidhyate prabhāsvarasākṣātkaraṇena mahāmudrāsiddhirbhavati
sā kīdṛśītyāha
sarvetyādi
sarvālaṅkārasampūrṇāṃ sarvalakṣaṇamaṇḍitāṃ gandhapuṣpavibhūṣitāṃ daśavalavaiśāradyopetāṃ vajrasattvāḥ sādhakāḥ teṣāmagrāṃ siddhi mahāmudrānipatta laghu kṣipramavāpnuyāt
evaṃ siddha: trikāyavaradaḥ trailokyepsitavarapradaḥ buddhalakṣaṇāni prasiddha- lakṣaṇāni tairlakṣitaḥ tairupetaḥ yojanaśatavistārasyopalakṣaṇārthatvāt visāhasrādau lokadhātau sa prabhayāvabhāsaṃ karoti
dvayendriyaprayogaḥ
satyadvayādvaidhīkaraṇena mahāvajradharaniṣpādanaṃ dvayendriya- prayogaḥ
tena sarvayogaśāntyādikaṃ samārabhet
eṣaḥ anantaroktaniṣpannasamādhiḥ sarvasiddhīnāṃ laukikalokottarāṇāṃ duratikramo durlaṅadhyaḥ samayo niṣpādakaḥ
viṇmūtrasamayaṃ bhakṣediti
viḍa rūpādayo viṣayā mūtraṃ cakṣurādīndriyāṇi tayoḥ samayo viṣayaviṣayībhāvena mīlanaṃ taṃ bhakṣet pratyāhārakrameṇānubhūyāt vajriṇaḥ vajrī sādhakaḥ siddhi mahāmudrāsiddhi yadi prārthayet
eṣo hi samayaḥ sarvasiddhīnāṃ duratikramo mārgaḥ
viṇmūtrasamaya ityādi
anantaroktaviṣayendriyapuraḥ sareṇa sādhakena dvayendriya- prayogataḥ satyadvayādvaidhīkaraṇāt anuttaraṃ tattvaṃ buddhabodhipadamapratiṣṭhitanirvāṇaṃ śivaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tattvamavisaṃvādi
anuttaraṃ sarvasiddhiviśiṣṭaṃ sidhyate labhyate || ityāhetyādi saṃgītikāravacanam
kāmaḥ saṃsāraḥ mokṣo nirvāṇaṃ tau samamekīkṛtya eti jānātīti kāmamokṣasamayavajro mahāvajradharaḥ
sandhyābhāṣā
evaṃ mahāvajradharasādhanamuktvā punarapi laukikasiddhimupakṣipannāha
athe- tyādi
bhagavān mahāvajradharaḥ mahāsamayākhyo vajraḥ krodho yasmin samaye tannāmakaṃ samādhiṃ samāpadyemaṃ sarvatathāgatāḥ sarve sādhyāḥ teṣāṃ kāyavākcitta- vajrāṇāṃ santrāsanakaraṃ krodhaṃ mantrātmakaṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayāmāsa
omityādiko mantraḥ sarpaḥ omiti mantradevatāmahāmudrāpradarśanaṃ hrīrityā- hvānaṃ ṣṭrīriti vikṛtavatsvarūpakathanam
vikṛtānana vībhatsamukha sarvaśatrūn pratyathikān nāśaya stambhaya palāyato'pyānīya vilayaṃ prāpayaṃ hū~ hū~ ityetadartha- dyotakaṃ bījam
phaḍita māraṇadayotakaṃ bījam
svāheti mantradevatāsamāśvā- sanam
asya vidarbhaṇaṃ dṛḍhavaditi
pañcadaśapaṭalapaṭko ṭivyākhyā viṣamityādi
karmayantrādi viṣam
kṛtrimaṃ rudhiram
yathālabdhaṃ lavaṇaṃ tailaṃ rājikā prasiddhā
samastamekīkṛtya kaṇṭakāgnau madanādikaṇṭakendhana- prajvālite kruddho yamāntakādyanyatamakrodhayogavān kanyānāmapadaiḥ saheti abhi- pretanāmākṣaraividabhitaṃ mantraṃ paṭhan tilatailādibhirāmodayitvā vakṣyamāṇakrameṇa juhuyāt
madhyāhne'rdharātre vā idaṃ stambhanakarma sarvathā sainyādīnāmapi gamyate
trikoṇe tyasre kuṇḍe mahāsamayavajjrakrodhaṃ vajramukhavivṛtaṃ daṃṣṭrākarālaṃ lalajjihvaṃ vibhāvyāṣṭasahasraṃ juhuyāt vidhānataḥ sainyastambhanādīnāṃ svasvavidhānataḥ
idaṃ vidhānaṃ divasattrayaṃ prayoktavyam
kanyānāṃ saṃvandhi mahābhogāptihetoḥ stambhanaṃ kanyāyāḥ sādhakādanyasmādanantena prayogeṇa bhavati
kiyatkālamityāha
trikalpo vālyayauvanamadhyāni tānyatikramya yāvadasaṃkhyamagaṇyatvaṃ jarājarjaritatvaṃ tāvat sthāsyati
sadeti antarāle'pi
puṃso yogābhāvaṃ darśayati
buddho vairocanayogī
vajradharmaḥ amitābha- yogī
vajrasattvo'kṣobhyayogī
ratnayogī karmayogī
anyatamakrodhayogī vā yaḥ kaścidatikramediti idaṃ stambhanaṃ saṃchettumicchet, tasya sādhakasya jīvitaṃ tadantamiti tatkarmārambhāt prabhṛti antamavasānaṃ bhavati
asyaiva mantrasya prayogāntaramāha
caturdaśyāmityādi
kṛṣṇapakṣasyā- ṣṭamīcaturdaśyoḥ śastrahatapuruṣacityaṅgāraṃ gṛhītvā aṣṭottaraśatasahasraṃ kapāla- saṃpuṭe saṃsthāpya japtvā tena vīrakarpaṭake mahāsamayakrodhavajrarūpaṃ yamāntakavadā- likhyāvāhyaṃ hṛdi pāṇinā spṛṣṭvā anantaroktamantramāvartyaṃ phaṭkārānantara- mamuṣmai idaṃ dāpaya hrīḥ svāheti mantraḥ arddharāve madhyāhne vā aṣṭottarasahasraṃ japet
sa sādhakaḥ sattvānāmabhimataphaladāyako bhavati sadā
anenaiva mantreṇa prayogāntaramāha
rekhāmityādi
pūrvavat japtvā aṅgāreṇa śmaśānakarpaṭe yasya kasyacit śatroḥ pratikṛtimālikhya hṛdaye nāma vidarbhya kṛṣṇadine abhicārukavelāyāṃ yatheṣṭakrodhasamādhistho yogī oṃ hrīḥ ṣṭrī: vikṛtānana amukasya hṛdayaṃ pāṭaya sādhetyuktvā śeṣaṃ paṭhan tadaṅgāraṃ śastrākāraṃ dhyātvā mastakādārabhya dvidhākaraṇāśayena madhye rekhāṃ dadyāt
rekhākṣaṇādeva bhinnahṛdayo mriyate
mudgaramityādi
mṛdā śatroḥ pratimāṃ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane yamāntakayogavān sādhyapādapāṃśusahitanadyubhayataṭa- vijñānamantarbhāvyam
kruddhaḥ san kṛtvāvā hya phaṭkāramuccārayan mastake ghātayet
evaṃ kṛte patati
dhruvamiti sādhya- śirasi pratyakṣeṇaiva mudgaraṃ patati
athavā dhyānenāpadaṃ sarvaṃ saṃpādayediti
hū~kāramityādi
nisumbhayogavān anantaroktavidhinā sādhyapratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā svahastasthadīptavajraṃ vikarālaṃ hū~kārajvālāsaṃyuktaṃ dīrghahū~kārodbhavakṛṣṇa- jvālābhiravalīḍhaṃ dhyātvā sādhyaśirasi prabhāvayet
patitaṃ dhyāyet || evaṃ kṛte sādhakaḥ sarvaduṣṭānāṃ nāśakaḥ pātayitā bhavati
kasmin yoge sthitvā idaṃ karma kuryādityāha
vajrapāṇikule smṛta iti
vajrapāṇi- kulaṃ vajrakulaṃ tasmin kule krodho nisumbharājaḥ nisumbhayogavān asmin karmaṇi vajrapāṇinā smṛtaḥ anujñāta iti yāvat
etat sarvamadhyātmaparāyaṇo dhyānena saṃpādayediti
khaṭiketyādi
yena kenacid varṇakena puruṣaṃ vā striyaṃ vā śmaśānakarpaṭādāvālikhya yamārisamādhisthaḥ svapāṇau dīrghahūṃkāraparāvṛttijaṃ kuṭhāraṃ bhāvayitvā phaṭkāramuccārayan tena grīvāṃ chinnāṃ vibhāvayet
buddha- sādhyatvena jñātaḥ trikāyaratnāgraḥ viśiṣṭa kāyavākcittātmakaḥ sarvasattvā hīnotkṛṣṭamadhyāḥ teṣāmasākalyenāpi hitaṃ saṃpādayatīti sarvasattvahitaiṣī rājā atiprasādavihārī
anena prayogeṇa hanyate prahato bhavet
yadi vā mriyate pañcatvaṃ yāti nāva saṃśayaḥ
karmavajjramityādi
amṛtakuṇḍalisamādhistho yogī pītaṃ viśvavajraṃ mahādīptamabhimatadeśasthagaṃ ( ? ) dhyātvā tanmadhye sphuliṅgagahanākulaṃ kalpāgni- nibhaṃ karālavajramūrdhvaśikhaṃ dhyātvā tadraśminivahaiḥ varṣadvārivṛndaṃ pluṣṭaṃ dagdhaṃ dhyāyāt
vāristambhanaṃ varṣastambhanamuttamamutkṛṣṭam
maṇḍala ityādi
vajradhātumahāmaṇḍalādau ācāryeṇa likhyamāne vātyādyaṃ vātyā vātasamūhaḥ
ādiśabdenāśanipātapāṣāṇavṛṣṭimaṇḍalādhastādudaka
sarpodgamādayo'pi gṛhyante
anena vātyādyaṃ vighnaṃ paraprayuktaṃ cautpātikaṃ vā yadi pramādāt jāyate tataḥ daṃṣṭrāmudrāmiti daṃṣṭrā vāsukiprabhṛtayo nāgāḥ utpīḍanārthaṃ mudrā daṃṣṭrāmudrā taṃ vaddhvā svīkṛtya duṣṭā vighnāḥ teṣāṃ śatruḥ haya- grīvaḥ tamanusmaran manasā dhyāyan svahastasthapraharaṇaiḥ vighnān yathāvat niṣedhayet
buddhāḥ svayūthyā mantriṇaḥ bodhisattvāḥ parayūthyāḥ tairapi nirmitaṃ prayuktaṃ pañcadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā vighnajātaṃ yadbhavet dṛṣṭamātreṇa mudrādarśanamātreṇa śīryate antardhatte
na cet yadyevaṃ prabhṛti kriyate, nāśamavānuyāt prastutakaraṇīyaṃ na sidhyati
// buddhaścetyādi
ye svaparayūthyā mantriṇaḥ ye cānye duṣṭajantavaḥ prākṛta- sattvāḥ trāsitāstarjitāḥ
anena mantravijṛmbhaṇena mriyante nātra saṃśayaḥ
tatra raudrakarmaṇi idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ sarvamantrāḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ rahasyam
amukasya hṛdayamiti
krūramantrāṇāṃ jīvitam
kiṃ tat ?
phaṭ māraṇe
// idānīṃ pañcaguhyamahātattvasvarūpaṃ darśayannāha
jñānasattvetyādi
jñāna- krameṇa niṣpannasattvaḥ jñānasattvaḥ tasya prayogaḥ jhaṭityālamvanam
tenopāyena madhye āsanacatuṣṭaye madhyāsane vimbaṃ mahāvajradharavimbaṃ prabhāvayet
catu:- sthāneṣu locanādisthāneṣu mantrajño devatātattvajño yoṣito mantratantreṣvaśikṣitāḥ locanādibhāvena parikalpitāḥ catasro yoṣitaḥ sadā aharniśaṃ saṃsthāpayet
kīdṛgvidhāḥ ?
sarvālaṅkārasaṃpūrṇāḥ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitāḥ yathoktastrīlakṣaṇo- petāḥ tataḥ padmaṃ prasāritaṃ kṛtveti
svahṛtpadmaṃ mukhakamalaṃ ca prasāritaṃ vika- sitaṃ kṛtvā idaṃ pañcātmakaṃ cakraṃ mantraṃ hū~kāraṃ vibhāvayet jānīyāt citta- mātramadhyavasyediti yāvat
pañcaraśmītyādi
yogavajriṇaṃ viṣayākāreṇa sthitaṃ mahāvajradharaṃ pañcaraśmayaḥ cakṣurādayaḥ teṣāṃ prabhā trividhaviṣayagrahaṇaṃ tena dīptaṃ prasphuṭaṃ bhāvayet itya- bhyaset
evamabhyasya svakāyādiṣu viṣayasvarūpaṃ vajriṇaṃ pātayan mīlayan bodhiṃ paramānandasukhamavāpnuyāt adhigacchet
evaṃ caran sa yogī tatkṣaṇādevācireṇaiva vairocanasamaprabhaḥ kāyavajratulyaḥ vajrasattvo'kṣobhyaḥ mahārāgo ratnasaṃbhavaḥ buddho'mitābhaḥ trikāyavajradhṛk amoghasiddhiḥ tairapi tulyaḥ syāt
sarvasattvāḥ vairocanādayaḥ tadbhāvotpādakaṃ vajrajñānaṃ yasmin samādhau sarva- sattvotpādanavajro nāma
// idānīmekamudrayāpi sahacaryāmāha
yoṣitamityādi
yoṣitaṃ brāhmaṇādyanya- tamakulodbhūtakanyakāṃ prāpya labdhvā vidhinā atroktena vidhinā
kīdṛśīṃ cāruvaktrāṃ prasannavadanāṃ hitaiṣiṇīṃ sādhakahitām
pracchanne vijane mano'nukūle pūjāṃ samāpattiṃ prārabhet
guhyaṃ rāgāditrayaṃ gṛhya niścitya vibhakṣayet prabhāsvareṇa viśodhayet nirābhāsīkuryāt
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane sa evamabhyasan yogī tatkṣaṇādacireṇaiva mañjuśrīrmahāvajradharaḥ tulyatejasā tattulyaprabhāveṇa yuktaḥ antardhānaṃ prākṛtātmabhāvaparityāgaḥ tataḥ patiḥ prabhuḥ mudrayā saha siddhatvāt, śrīmān jāmbūnadasamaprabhaḥ ratnaketusadṛśo bhavati
gaganagañjasamādhinā sattvārthasaṃpādanāt
idānīṃ sādhakasyāhārakṛtyaṃ darśayannāha
bhakṣyaṃ lokasādhāraṇamannādikaṃ viṣṭhaṃ gomayādikaṃ māṃsaṃ yathālabdhamanyad vā kiṃcit abhimantraya sādhanā- dikrameṇa saṃskṛtya hṛtkaṇṭhe ca saṃśodhya praveśayet
asya vidhānataḥ vidhinā budhaiḥ paṇḍitaṃmanyaiḥ durgatairna dṛśyate na jñāyate ayaṃ kramaḥ
ityāha ca mahāvajradhara iti vakṣyamāṇāpekṣayā
// viṣṭhaṃ tadahatirbhakasya carcaḥ (?)
vidhāneneti amṛṣṭabhūmikaṃ gṛhya śarāvasaṃpuṭe kapālasaṃpuṭe nyaset sthāpayet
tataḥ śatāṣṭavārān aṣṭoraśataṃ saṃcodya tyakṣareṇābhimantraya gulikāṃ kṛtvā trilohenāveṣṭayitvā mukhe prakṣipan buddhasūryaiḥ siddhavidyādharairapi na dṛśyate iti prayogānuśaṃsaḥ
śvānetyādi
śvahayamanuṣyāṇāṃ piśitaṃ gatāyuṣā vidhānato'nantarokta- vidhinā gṛhya kapālasaṃpuṭayogena tathaivābhimantrya gulikāṃ kṛtvā bhakṣayan mukhe nikṣipan taiḥ siddhādibhiḥ na dṛśyate
viṣṭena saha saṃyuktāmamṛtacatuṣṭayaghaṭitāṃ gulikāṃ trilohaveṣṭitāṃ sutaruveṣṭitāṃ dvayendriyaprayogeṇeti rāhugrahaṇe sparśādārabhya yonau prakṣipya yāvanmokṣastāvat samāpattiṃ kṛtvā tato'panīya nimittamavalokya dhūmāyate tadā siddhiriti jñātvā mukhe prakṣipet
tadā sarvabuddhairna dṛśyate
tathaiva mahāmāṃsena pañcāmṛtena sahitāṃ gulikāṃ trilohaveṣṭitāmanantarokta- dveyendriyaprayogeṇa sādhayitvā mukhagatāṃ kṛtvā sarvaburddharna dṛśyate
gomāṃsena śvānamāṃsenetyādyanantaroktopadeśena neyaṃ prāṇakairviṣṭena saha vahirgataiḥ sattvaiḥ saha gulikāṃ parāmṛtena kārayed vratī cīrṇavrataḥ dvayendriyaprayogeṇa pūrvavat sādhayet karpūracandanaiḥ raktacandanaiḥ saha pañcāmṛtena gulikāṃ kṛtvā pūrvavat sādhayet
rocanākṛṣṇāgurubhyāṃ saha gulikāṃ kṛtvā pūrvavat sādhayitvā mukhe prakṣipya paryaṭan mahāvalo'pratihatavīryo bhavati
karpūrakuṅkumairyuktāṃ pañcāmṛtena tathaiva sādhayet
ityāha ca
vakṣyamāṇakamidānīṃ lokettarasiddhipradarśanārthamāha
adhiṣṭhāyetyādi
māṇḍaleyadevatānāṃ madhye yamāntakāderyasyāpi mahāmudrāṃ raudrāṃ śāntāṃ vā adhiṣṭhāya pañcaṃdaśapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā niṣpādya vineyajanāvarjanārthaṃ śāntyādikarma sādhayitvā tādṛgbhavatītyetadeva na
kevalaṃ tu sa yogī yādṛśo mahāvajradharaḥ tādṛśaḥ saṃjāyate aṣṭaguṇaiśvarya- yogāt
śrīmān trailokyaśāsakatvāt
mahāvalaparākramaḥ
ekakṣaṇena saṃpūrṇa- yojanakoṭipramāṇaṃ bhūmerupariṣṭāt gatirbhavet
bhūmeradhastādapi tāvatpramāṇameva vajra[sya][śaneriva gatiḥ
tathaiva pūrvāsu digvidikṣu kṣaṇena visāhasraloka- dhātāvapratihatagatirbhavati, aneka mudrāgaṇaparivṛtattrāt
śrīmān daśavala- vaiśāradyādiguṇasamanvito buddhaḥ
ayamapi tathāvidhatvāt tatsadṛśa ityabhi- prāyaḥ
evamanāvaraṇagatiḥ yathārucigatitvāt
kāmadhātvityādi
cāturmahārājakāyikāḥ trayastriṃśat yāmatuṣita- nirmāṇaratiparanirmitavaśavartīti ṣaḍvidhaḥ kāmadhātuḥ
tatra sthitāṃ kanyāṃ surabhogāṃ surāṇāmiva bhogo yasyāstāṃ surabhogāṃ cakravartyādīnāṃ kanyāṃ kilā- nīya kulāni nāgāsurakulāni teṣu prabhāṃ tatprativaddhāṃ kanyām, na kevalaṃ kāmadhātusthitāṃ kāmayati, yadi santi rūpadhātau striyastāśca kāmayati ityatiśayokti darśayati
samayasattvākhyaḥ prākṛtakāyaḥ so'ntardhīyate yena mahāvajrākhyena sattvena sa samayāntardhānamahāvajro vajradharaḥ evamāheti saṃgītikṛt
athetyādi
buddhāḥ ratnapudgalāḥ pūrvaṃ śravaṇamātreṇa bhrāntacittāḥ artha - syātyadbhutatvāt paścā[c] cittā bhāvanāmayenādhigatatvāt
manīṣiṇaḥ uditajñānāḥ tadanantaraṃ prahṛṣṭātmāḥ deśakaprasādena tenaiva vismayena vika- sitanayanāḥ idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ praharṣasūcakaṃ vacanamudīrayan kṛtavantaḥ
aho ityādi
ekenaiva mantreṇānekakarmaprasādhanaṃ yat tadidaṃ aho suvi- smayamityāścaryam
strībhiḥ saha krīḍanaṃ laukikalokottarasiddhi sādhayediti
yad guhyapadākṣaraṃ tadapyāścaryam
tatpañcāmṛtapradīpādigulikābhiḥ yataḥ siddhi- sampat taddharmāṇāṃ svabhāvaśuddhatvam
etadapyāścaryam
yāṃ kāṃcana mahāmudrā- mālambya karma saṃsādhyate mahāvajradharapadaṃ yat prāpyate tattrāliptatā sunirmalatvaṃ tadapyāścaryam
sandhyābhāṣā
idānīṃ kulabhedena svādhidevatābhiṣekamākṣipannāha
athetyādi
vajradharaḥ śāstā sarvaduṣṭanigrahāt śāstā laukikādisiddhisaṃpādanāt kartā vairāvanādīnā- guhyasamājapradīpodyotane mutpādakatvāt sraṣṭā, atha ca ṣaṣṭhasvabhāvatvāt mahākṣaraḥ buddho vairocanaḥ vajro'kṣobhyaḥ mahādharmo'mitābhaḥ trivajrātmakaḥ vajraghoṣamakārṣīt kṛtavān
-sattvavajjo'kṣobhyaḥ tasya prayogataḥ mūrdhni dhāraṇaṃ tena sattvavajramakṣobhyaṃ kulīnānāṃ toṣamabhiṣekaḥ buddhabodhiḥ vairocanaḥ tasya prabhedaḥ
sukulīnānāṃ
mūrddhasu pṛthak sthitvā toṣaṇamuddīpanaṃ teṣāṃ buddhavajriṇāṃ vairocanakulīnānām
vajralocaneti
vajralocanādīnāmiti grāhyam
vimvādyaivirocanādi- rūpaiḥ uṣṇīṣārādhanaṃ śekharīkaraṇaṃ smṛtaṃ jñātavyam
krodhānāmapi tatkulaprati- vaddhānāṃ buddhavajrā vairocanādayaḥ taiḥ prabhāvanaṃ termakuṭe sthitvojjvalīkaraṇaṃ śreṣṭhamiṣṭataram
vidyā māmakī tasyā rājāgra[:] ratnasaṃbhavaḥ so'gradharmā prabhuryeṣāṃ te vidyārājāgradharmāṇaḥ teṣāṃ ratnakulīnānāṃ ratnaketuvibhāvanā makuṭe ratnaketva- bhiṣecanaṃ kartavyam
vidyārājñī pāṇḍaravāsinī tayā prayogaḥ saṃvandhasaṃbandhāt yeṣāṃ te vidyārājñīprayogāḥ teṣāṃ padmakulīnānāmamitāyurvibhāvanā makuṭeṇtra- mitābhasthāpanā kartavyā
sarvakamikamantrāḥ karmakulīnāḥ teṣāmamoghā jñānavibhāvanā makuṭeṣu sarveṣāmeva mantrāṇāṃ kulādhipānāṃ vairocanādīnāṃ vajrasattvavibhāvanā makuṭe- 'kṣobhyavibhāvanā kartavyā
yakṣiṇyo vajjravetāliprabhṛtayaḥ teṣāṃ mantrā[ṇā] mutpaṃttibījādayaḥ te tantryante sādhyante yairmudrārūpaisteṣāṃ yakṣiṇīmantratantrāṇāmabhiṣekasthāne yamāntakasyaiva kalpanaṃ sthāpanaṃ kartavyam
sarveṣāmanantaroktānāmabhiṣeke yujyanta iti yogāḥ teṣāṃ yogamantrāṇām ūrdhvasthitānāṃ pracodanaṃ svasvamudrābhiḥ saha mahāsukhānubhavanaṃ śastaṃ samāpyāyanam
oṃ ityāha bhagavān mahāsamayavajro vajradharaḥ || -
anenānantaroktābhiṣekadhyānavistareṇa mantrā devatāḥ teṣāmārādhanamaṇḍalam ārādhane maṇḍāḥ sārāḥ tān lāti gṛhṇātīti mantrārādhanamaṇḍalaṃ mahāsaṃmaya- sādhanam
sādhakānāṃ ghaṭamānānāṃ hitaṃ hitāvāhakaṃ proktaṃ mahāsamayo mahāmudrā tatsādhanaṃ niṣpādakamiti

vajretyādi
vajrasattvādayaḥ akṣobhyapramukhāḥ mantradhyānapracoditāḥ svamantreṇa svamudrālamvanena ca pracoditāḥ preritāḥ citra- karmāṇi vividhakarmapathāni kurvanti niṣpādayanti
vācaḥ karma kriyā yasya yatprativaddhā sa vākkarmāmitābhaḥ tasya vaco yathāvad dvayaṃ nānāvidhaṃ karma karoti
evaṃ kṛtapuraścaraṇaḥ sādhakaḥ svayamakṣobhyayogavān pātraṃ tathatācandrabījacihna- krameṇa vajradharmamahāvimvaṃ padmarāgaprabhaṃ dhyātvā śiraḥ kaṇṭhahṛddeśeṣu tyakṣaraṃ vinya[sya ] tasmin kāyavākcittavajraṃ tviti vyūhatrayamantarbhāvayet

skandhetyādi
skandhavajreṇa pañca- skandhātmakatvena asmin tadhātukottare yāvantaḥ sattvāḥ aṇḍajādayaḥ tiṣṭhanti tān sarvān ghātanena māraṇenātmagatān svātmagatān vajrasattvatvamāpannān cintet jānīyāt
