mahākrūrasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ tasmāt nirgatyaikībhāvamāpanno dveṣavajraḥ vajrakrodhaḥ evamāheti saṃgītikārakaḥ
athetyādi
vajradharo dveṣavajraḥ rājā dīpyamānaḥ sarvākāśaḥ sarvaśūnyātmakaḥ ataeva mahāmuniḥ sarveṣāmabhiṣekaḥ kṣara[ṇaṃ] taṃ saṃbuddhaḥ jñātavān jñānavajraṃ cintāmaṇiprakhyamīpsitaphalapradam avinaśvaraprabhāvamudīrayan vakṣamāṇodāna- gāthayā prakaṭayāmāsa
aho ityāścarye
svabhāvasaṃśuddhaṃ sarvavikalpāpagatatvāt svabhāvarahitaṃ vajrayānaṃ prabhāsvaramanuttaraṃ yasya prabhāvāt anutpanneṣu kāraṇarahitatvāt ajāteṣu dharmeṣu upāttānupāttabhūteṣu śāntyādikarmaprasaraṇānutpatiḥ saṃbhavaḥ taistraikālya- vartibhistathāgataiḥ kathitāḥ
pañcadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā tattvetyādi
dharmanairātmye sthitasyedaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ kṣudrairalpakairupakaraṇaiḥ sādhyata iti kṣudrasādhanakarma
tathāpi sārabhūtatvāt vajraṃ tasmādeva gopya - tvāt rahasyam
dahantamagnivat duḥsahaṃ bhāvayet
cakārāt vāsukipramukhānāmanyatamasya vijñāna- hrīḥkārāṅakuśenākṛṣya praveśya sarpa sphurantaṃ cintayet
calate dhruvam
tatra tasmin sarpe sarvasthāvarajaṅgamebhyo viṣākṛṣṭo hṛdayaṃ kiṃ tat ?
hrīḥ
traidhātukasthitaṃ hīnamadhyotkṛṣṭabhājanagataṃ vividhasaṃbhavaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamātmakaṃ sarvaṃ niḥśeṣam tena hrī: kāreṇa hṛtamākṛṣṭaṃ bhāvayet
tataḥ sarpavigrahe patamānaṃ praviśan cintayet smaryaḥ
tatkṣaṇādeva dhyānamātre viṣasamudravat sudāruṇo bhavati
spṛṣṭamāte anena mantrapreritena daṣṭamātre spṛṣṭamātreṇa vā jagat sarvaṃ viṣodgamanena mṛtaṃ bhavati
nātra saṃśayaḥ
maṇḍūketyādi
anantaroktenaiva vidhinā kacchapādayaḥ sattvaviśeṣāḥ kartavyāḥ kaiḥ ?
yogotpattikalakṣaṇaiḥ
yogo viṣam
citritamaṇḍūkādau karaṇaṃ tasmin lakṣaṇaiḥ kuśalaiḥ tatra ityādinā
viṣasya sthānāntarāt sthānāntarasaṃkrāntimāha dṛṣṭivajñetyādi
dṛṣṭiviṣaṃ daṃṣṭrāviṣādīnāṃ viṣaṃ ye cānye sattvā viṣadāruṇāḥ teṣāṃ ca viṣaṃ pātraśarīramata ūrdhvasthitasitapraṇavaraśmipreritaṃ pādatalasthaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ visphuradrūpaṃ dhyātvā khavajra- maṇḍalasya pātraṃ hṛdgataṃ khaṃkāropari jñānavajreṇa hrīḥkārāṅakuśenākṛṣya praveśayanti mantriṇā ityāha bhagavān mahāviṣamayavajro mahāvajradharaḥ
tatredamityādinā viṣāpaharaṇamārabhate viṣacikitsāhṛdayam
kiṃ tat ?
hūṃ
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane hṛdaye pādatalahṛdaye idaṃ hūṅkāraṃ śuklavarṇaṃ mahāvajraṃ raśmipuñjātmakaṃ candrāṃśuvad dedīpyamānaṃ vibhāvayet
catuḥsthānaprayogeṇeti
pādatalasuśireṇodgatya viṣaṃ svaraśminivahena ūrdhva kṣiptvā gudapadmamāgamya tasmin chitvā tato vinirgatya tathaiva nābhipadma- gataṃ dhyātvā tasmādapi hṛtpadmagataṃ vicintya tato'pi kaṇṭhapadmagataṃ dhyāyāt
evaṃ saṃharaṇatyāgena uttiṣṭhatyūrdhvaṃ gacchati viṣam
evaṃ dvitrirvārān dhyātvā viṣaṃ kṛṣṇarūpaṃ chandayantamāturaṃ dhyāyāt
anenaiva krameṇa mastakādārabhya yāvatpādatalaṃ gatvā tadvivarānnirgataṃ viṣaṃ dhyāyāditi kecit
khadhātumākāśakuharaṃ viṣeṇa bharitamapi anena prayogeṇa sadyo nirviṣaṃ kurute
ityāha ceti prastūyamāṇāpekṣaḥ
tatra viṣāpaharaṇe upaviṣāṇi dhattūra- lāṅgaliprabhṛtīni kṛtrimaviṣāṇi ca teṣāṃ sarvavipaḥ [ṇa] mapakarṣaṇahṛdayam
kiṃ tat ?
āḥ
na kevalamupaviṣasyaiva pratīkāraḥ gaṇḍādīnāmapīti darśayati
gaṇḍetyādi
gaṇḍāḥ vyālagardabhādayaḥ
piṭakā masūrikādayaḥ
lūtā vaṇīpadyādayaḥ
teṣāṃ yad vīryaṃ yecānye vyādhayo jvaravaisarpādayaḥ dhyānamātreṇa vakṣyamāṇena dhyānena bhraśyantyāturadehāt dūrī bhavanti
vajrapāṇivacanametad darśayati
yatheti
aṣṭetyādi
aṣṭadalaṃ mahāpadmaṃ śvetaṃ vāmakaratale dhyātvā tatra kaṇikāyāṃ pañcaraśmiprapūritam āḥkāraṃ daleṣu ca hroḥkāraṃ dhyātvā hrīṅkāramuccārayan upamardya (?) viṣādikamākṛṣya āḥkāreṇa praveśya muṣṭisaṃhāreṇa bhūmigataṃ kuryāt
tadevāḥkārabījaṃ dhyānaviśeṣeṇa viśeṣayannāha saṃharet sitasannibhamiti yojyam
viṣāpaharaṇe sitaṃ dhyātavyam
codane viṣotthāpane kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ dhyeyam
idamuktaprakāraṃ dhyānapadabījaṃ guhyaṃ durbhagebhyo gopanīyam
tasmād rahasyam buddharahasyaṃ jñānanirmalaṃ prabhāsvarapariśodhitatvāt
pūrvamupaviṣaprasaṅgenoktvā viśeṣeṇa vyādhicikitsāmākṣipannāha
tatretyādi
tatra viśeṣavidhāne śastrādyāgantukanimittā abhicāranimittāśca vāhyāḥ doṣadhātumalāhāravaiṣamyanimittāḥ ādhyātmikāsteṣāṃ vāhyāghyātmikavyādhīnāṃ cikitsā pratīkāraḥ tadarthaṃ vajravadabhedya hṛdayabhūtamantrākṣarapadāni jinajigi- pañcadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā yadityādi
eṣu yanmantrapadamiṣṭamabhipretaṃ bhaktirabhyāsaḥ tayā guṇāvahaḥ phalavāhakaṃ bhavati, tādṛśaṃ tadanurūpaṃ vimbaṃ devatārūpaṃ bhāvayet
vyādhivajraṃ duḥsādhyo vyādhiḥ tatpracodanaiḥ tanniścārakairadhyāśayaiḥ dhyāyāt
katham ?
vānarākārasamayaṃ vānarākārarogaṃ śvānasaṃbhavaṃ kukkurākāraṃ vā
īdṛgrūpaṃ vyādhiṃ kāyavākcittapadaiḥ kāyavākcittāvaṣṭambhanaiḥ sthitaṃ niścarantaṃ sādhyadeśāt niṣkrāntaṃ vikalpayet dhyāyāt
tat- cakraṃ vā vikarālavajraṃ vā vajrapade dakṣiṇakare sthitaṃ dhyātvā tena muktena kāyavākcittasamayakāyādisādhakaṃ rogaṃ cūrṇitaṃ bhasmībhūtaṃ bhāvayet
tataḥ prabhṛti tatkālamārabhya saṃbuddhā akṣobhyādayaḥ bodhisattvāḥ maitreyādayaḥ mahāyaśāḥ svadehasthāḥ tasyāntarasyādhiṣṭhānapadaṃ roganirjaye pratiṣṭhāṃ dadanti prayacchanti hṛṣṭacakṣuṣaḥ projvalanayanāḥ ityāha ceti vakṣyamāṇāpekṣayā saṃgītikārakaḥ
ityāha katham ?
āturasya kāyavākcittavatreṣu buddhameghān locanāsamāpannān vairocanavyūhān tathaiva vajrarājamahāmeghān yathāsaṃbhavamakṣobhyameghān amitābha- meghāṃśca sthāpayet
evaṃ kṛte vyādhimokṣaṇaṃ bhavati
niṣpannakramasthasya vyādhinigrahamāha
daśadigityādi
sarbabuddhānāṃ rūpa- skandhādisvabhāvānāṃ bodhisattvāśca bodhisattvānāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ dhīmatāṃśca vijñānasaṃprayuktānāṃ kruddhān doṣakṣobheṇa kruddhānāṃ tasyāturasya eteṣāṃ pāramārthikaṃ māraṇaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ praveśalakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam
] anena dhyānayogena prabhāsvarālamvanena yad vyādhijātaṃ karmajaṃ vāpi karmajamapi yat smṛtaṃ jñātaṃ tat saptadivasaivinaśyati
athavā yena vajrajāpe- nānusmaraṇena vinaśyati taduktam
śatāṣṭajāpayogeneti
śatayuktvānā- maṣṭaśatānāṃ caturmaṇḍaleṣu navaśatānāṃ japaḥ śatāṣṭajapaḥ tatprayogeṇa vā naśyati vyādhiḥ
athavā svamantrarājena svādhiṣṭhānadehena vajradhyānaṃ vyādhividhvaṃsanaṃ dhyānaṃ tasya vidhiḥ vidhānaṃ smṛtaṃ smartavyam
eṣo hi uktaḥ sarvavidhiḥ sarva- vyādhīnāṃ vāhyādhyātmikānāṃ duratikramaḥ alaṅadhyanīyaḥ samayo vajrasattvaḥ prajñaptiriti
guhya samājapradīpodyotane evaṃ karmaprasaraṃ nirdiśyedānīṃ māyopamasamādhisamāpannānāṃ mahāmudrāsādhana- pravṛttānāmiṣṭasvapnasaṃdarśanāyāha athetyādi
jñānāṅkuśā vairocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ madhye mahādyutiḥ prabhuḥ kāmena mokṣapradatvāt kāmamokṣaḥ mahāvajro mahā-
parṣatkaḥ idaṃ vacanamabravīt
svapnopameṣu svapnasaṃdarśaneṣu dharmeṣu bhāveṣu anutpannasvabhāviṣūtpādakāraṇā- bhāvāt svabhāvaḥ prabhāsvaraḥ tena śuddhaṃ vyapagatakaluṣaṃ tattvaṃ devatārūpaṃ yeṣu te svabhāvaśuddhatattvāt teṣu bhrāntivatraṃ pragīyate
bhrāntiḥ vibhramaḥ tasya vajramupagamanam
svapnadṛṣṭāntena sarvadharmāṇāmapi svapnopamatvaparijñānāt śāśvatocchedabhrāntiḥ pragīyate prahīyata ityabhiprāyaḥ
paśyantītyādi
sādhakāḥ dvividhāḥ nityaṃ satatamabhyasyantaḥ japadhyānārtha- tatparāḥ buddhāḥ adhyātmaparāyaṇāḥ bodhisattvāḥ mantraparāyaṇā dvividhā bhedena sanimittānimittabhedena darśanaṃ svapnaṃ paśyanti
tatra dvividhasādhakeṣu idamucyamānaṃ mahatāṃ phalānāṃ sūcakaṃ svapnaṃ mahāsvapnaṃ tadevāvisaṃvādakatvāt samayaṃ sādhakam
bodhijñānāgraṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tatpraviṣṭamātmānaṃ paśyati sa buddhasuprabhaṃ jñānamūrti paśyati kadācit sa yogī ātmānaṃ buddhānāṃ saṃbhogakāyaṃ prāptaṃ laghu paśyati acirāt sākṣāt karoti
vaidhātukamahāsattvaiḥ śakrabrahmādibhirmahānubhāvaiḥ pūjyamānamātmānaṃ sa sādhakaḥ paśyati
svavimvamātmadehaṃ mahājñānasamaprabhaṃ mahāvajradharatulyakānti viśiṣṭaiḥ pūjyamānamātmānaṃ sa sādhakaḥ paśyati
svavimvamātmadehaṃ mahājñānasamaprabhaṃ mahāvajradharatulyakāntiṃ viśiṣṭaiḥ rūpādiviṣayaiḥ buddhabodhisattvaiḥ pūjyamānaṃ dhruvamekāntena paśyati
acireṇaiva mahāmudrāsiddhi sākṣātkarotīti yāvat
sādhakaḥ svavimvamātmānaṃ vajrasattvarūpamakṣobhyarūpaṃ mahāvimbaṃ vairocanarūpaṃ vajradharmamamitābharūpaṃ vā mahāyaśaṃ ratnaketuṃ vā
guhyavajramamoghasiddhirūpaṃ vā paśyati
taṃ sādhakaṃ mahābuddhāḥ śrāvakapratyekabuddhāḥ bodhisattvāḥ samānajñānarūpāḥ vajriṇo ghaṭamānayoginaḥ
tathaiva sādhakasya īdṛgvidhaṃ vajrasattvādivimvān pareṣāṃ kāyavākcittasiddhidān mahāmudrāsiddhidān acireṇa drakṣyati svapna- phalam
pañcadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā sarvālaṅkārasampūrṇāṃ surakanyāṃ urvaśīprabhṛtidivyanārīḥ paśyati atha vā manuṣyakanyāṃ vā dārakaṃ sarvalakṣaṇopetaṃ tadaiva dārikāṃ vālāṃ yaḥ paśyati sa siddhi laukikīmapi gacchati prāpnoti
daśadigvyavasthitān sarvatathāgatān svabuddhakṣetravyavasthitān buddhān dhruvaṃ niścayena yaḥ sādhakaḥ paśyati yathā yasmai hṛṣṭacittāḥ tathāgatā manoramaṃ prītikaraṃ dharmaṃga saddharmarājaṃ pustakagataṃ dadati
yaḥ dharmacakragataṃ dharmāsanārūḍhaṃ kāyamātmadehaṃ sarvabuddhairbodhisattvaiḥ parivṛtaṃ yogasamayaḥ sādhakaḥ dhyāna pratiṣṭhitaḥ niṣpannakramasthaḥ paśyati, sa lokottarasiddhi sādhayatīti darśanaphalam
ārāmaṃ tapovanamudyānaṃ rājakrīḍāvanaṃ evamādi vividhān manoramapradeśān devanāgakanyādibhiralaṅkṛtān dhyānasamayaḥ parikalpitayogastho yaḥ paśyati sa sarvabuddhairadhiṣṭhitaiḥ sarvatathāgataniṣṭhāṃ labhate
tasmādevepsitalaukikaṃ sukhaṃ labhata iti svapnapākaḥ
buddhabodhisattvairabhiṣicyamānaṃ ātmānaṃ yaḥ paśyati svapne sa vidyādhara- mahārājaiḥ vidyādharanāyakaiḥ pūjyamānamātmānaṃ paśyati labhate svapnapākaḥ
ityāha ceti
iṣṭasvapnopasaṃhārārthamāha vividhānuktānevaṃbhūtānanuktāṃśca vajrasaṃbhūtān tathāgatadarśitān nirmalān śubhān svapnān paśyati yat tenānuttaraṃ tattvaṃ kāyavākcittavajrajaṃ prākṛtakāyavākcittāt jātaṃ niṣpannamanuttaraṃ tattvaṃ pariśuddhadevatārūpaṃ sidhyate prāpyate
caṇḍāletyādi
samayabhraṣṭasyāśusvapnaṃ darśayati
caṇḍālaśvānādibhiryogaḥ
ādiśabdena vānarapretādayo virūpā gṛhyante
vajradhṛk sādhakaḥ yadi cet paśyati tasya dhīmataḥ dṛṣṭasattvasyāpi vajrasattvasya dṛḍhavīryasya cittanilayaṃ śarīraṃ sidhyate pāpamupagacchati
samayollaṅghanaṃ kṛtvā samayaṃ paripūjya laukika- lokottarasādhaneṣu pravṛttaḥ san viṣamāparihāreṇa kālaṃ karotīti yāvat
tattvasādhakenedaṃ prastūyamānaṃ svapna vicāraṇasamayaḥ tatpratyavekṣaṇākālaḥ tasmin hṛdayaṃ sandehacchedakaṃ sāravacanam
cittanidhyaptau cittavicāraṇe cittasvabhāvaparijñāne sarvadharmāḥ svapnadarśana- kālacittāt vāhyabhāvena pratiṣṭhitāḥ pratibhāsamāpannāḥ ye te sarve svacittameva guhyasamājapradīpodyotane nānyat kiñcidasti
taccittaṃ kutra sthitamityāha khavajracittastha iti
khavajraṃ prabhāsvaraṃ tasmin sthitaṃ cittaṃ khavajracittaṃ tasmin sthitā ete bhāvāḥ suptāvasthāyāṃ jāgradavasthāyāṃ tu cittāt pṛthagbhāvāḥ santīti cedāha na dharmā iti
bhāvānāmabhāvaḥ dharmadhātuḥ kadācid vidyate iti cedāha
na ca dharmateti
pravāha tathaiva dhātvāyatanendriyādau jñānadvaye tava susaṃhate'smin
svapnaṃ prapaśyet khalu vātasaṃśrayāt
śūnye mahattve sati yaḥ prasuptaḥ supte prabuddhe ca na ca nānyabhedaḥ nāma saṃkalpayet svapnaphalābhilāṣī ka svapnopamāste khalu sarvadharmā mṛṣāmṛṣāścāpi tayorabhāva:
athetyādi
sarvatathāgatā akṣobhyādayaḥ āścaryaprāptāḥ svapnadarśane svapnaphalāvāptāvadbhutaprāptaḥ sarvatathāgatasamayaḥ vajrasattvasamādhiḥ
tasmin saṃśayacchettāraṃ vajrasattvaṃ pṛṣṭavantaḥ kimidamiti praśnaḥ
etad duṣkaramitya- bhiprāyaḥ
niḥsvabhāveṣu vidyamāneṣu phalasūcakeṣu dharmeṣu dharmatattvaṃ prāpya phalatattva- mudāhṛtam
tasmādākāśe ādhārabhūte ākāśabhāvanamādheyaṃ kṛtamiti
yāvadāścaryasaṃbhūtam
āścaryaṃ prakāśitam
- tathāgatāṃnā mabhiprāyamevāviṣkurvannāha
athetyādi
mahāvajradharaḥ tathā- gatānevamāha
bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatā ityāmantraṇam
ākāśaṃ kenacid dharmeṇa ghaṭapaṭādinā na saṃyuktam, pṛthaktvādarśanāt
nāpyasaṃyuktaṃ bhāvasya sarvasya tadātmakatvāt
sarvagatvaṃ satsvapi bhāveṣu vyāpittraṃ teṣu bhāveṣu sannidhāna- manurdāśatvam
etad dvayaṃ samastītyabhimānaścāsya nāsti
etadāha
na caivākāśasyaivaṃ bhavati, sarvagato'ham, sarvatrānudarśī ceti
evaṃ dṛṣṭāntamuktvā tena saṃyojayannāha
evameveti
yathākāśaṃ na kenacid dharmeṇa saṃyuktaṃ nāpyasaṃ- yuktam, tathā svapnaphalayoḥ saṃyogaviyogī draṣṭavyau
yathā nākāśasya sarva- gatatvasarvatrānudaśitvamanyanā nāsti tathā svapnatatphalayorapīti
tadāha svapnāḥ svapnasaṃbhūtāścānugantavyā iti
tasyaiva viśuddha darśayannāha
tadyathetyādi
yathākāśamanirūpyamevamevaṃsvabhāvamiti
anidarśanaṃ cakṣurādi- bhiryanna viṣayīkriyate
apratighaṃ kuḍyādibhirna vāryate
evameva tadvat sarvadharmāḥ skandhādayaḥ parijñeyāḥ
kimuta svapnāḥ svapnaphalāni cetyabhiprāyaḥ
punarapyāha |
tadyathetyādi |
sarvadharmāḥ bhājanalokāḥ kāyavākcittaṃ sattvalokaḥ vajrapadasamayaśeṣadhātmāyaṃ sarvatrānugatamanantāparyanta lokadhātugatameka- svabhāvamekalakṣaṇaṃ parijñeyam |
yaduta kathaṃ cittasvabhāvaṃ cittamayaṃ yaśca kāyavā- kcittadhāturiti sarvadharmāḥ ākāśadhātuścaitat advayamabhinnamadvaidhīkāramaminna- lakṣaṇam ||
tadyathetyādinā amumarthameva vivṛṇoti |
sarvasattvā aṇḍajādayaḥ ākāśa- dhātusthitāḥ |
sa cākāśadhātuḥ na kāmadhātvādiṣu sthitaḥ |
evameva yo dharmaḥ vaidhātuke na sthitaḥ tasyotpādo nāsti |
yasyotpādo nāsti sau dharmaḥ kenacid dharmeṇa yena kenacit svabhāvena na saṃbhāvyate, na vyavasthāpyate |
sa ca dharmaśca naiḥsvābhāvyānnātivartate tasmāt tarhi itthaṃ kṛtvā sarvadharmāḥ niḥ- svabhāvāḥ prabhāsvarasvabhāvāḥ |
|
tadyathetyādi |
sarvatathāgatāḥ tyadhvavartinaḥ teṣāṃ jñānaṃ jñānadehaḥ tasyo- tpādane vajrapadākāraṃ vajrapadasvabhāvaṃ bodhicittameva |
tacca bodhicittaṃ na kāyasthitaṃ na kāmadhātau sthitaṃ na vāksthituṃ na rūpadhātau sthitaṃ na cittasthitaṃ arūpadhātāvapi na sthitam |
evaṃ yo dharmaḥ traidhātuke na sthitaḥ tasyotpādo nāsti |
yat traidhātukalakṣaṇaparijñānaṃ tat sarvatathāgatajñānotpādane vajrapadamabhedyamavi- saṃvādakaṃ kāraṇama evaṃ svapnanaiḥ svābhāvyena vastunaiḥ svābhāvyaṃ nirūpya punarapi svapnanidhya- ptimevākṣipannāha |
na cetyādi |
ahaṃ |
taidhātuke trividhasattvānāṃ svapnapadaṃ svapnena phalaṃ darśayāmīti |
evaṃ saṃkalpanā na bhavati svapnasya |
svapnastu mayā draṣṭavya iti prārthanā na bhavati puruṣasya |
atha ca vaidhātuka- kriyā vividhasattvarāśīnāṃ jāgradavasthāyāṃ śubhāśubhātmikā kriyā svaptopamāḥ svapnavaddhi tathā ca svapnasadṛśī svapnavat vicitrā sā svapnasaṃbhūtāḥ svapna- sūcitā bhavati yathā, evamevānena prakāreṇa daśadiglokadhātuṣu buddhāḥ niṣpannāḥ bodhisattvāḥ pramuditādibhūmiḥ prāptāḥ yāvantaḥ sarvasattvāścāṇḍaja- jarāyujasaṃsvedajāḥ upapādukāḥ sarve te svapnanairātmyapadenānugantavyāḥ svapneṣviva māyopamasamādhisthena boddhavyāḥ |
evaṃ manomayadevatāmūrtervyāpti pradarśya tatsvarūpaṃ darśayannāha |
tadyathetyādi |
cintāmaṇiratnaṃ sarvalaukikaratnānāṃ viśiṣṭataramīpsitapradānāt divyamānuṣyaṃ mānuṣyakairguṇairupetaṃ sattvaiḥ prārthitam |
suvarṇādikaṃ cintitamātreṇābhilaṣita- mātreṇa yat saṃpādayati tadratnādikaṃ sattvānāṃ na cittādvinirgataṃ na cintāmaṇi- ratnādāgataṃ yathā, evameva sarvadharmā māyālakṣaṇāḥ buddhāḥ niṣpannā niṣpanna- sattvarāśayaḥ teṣāṃ dharmāḥ kāraṇaṃ ṣaṣṭhaḥ tasmāt sarvasiddhayaḥ anujñātavyāḥ |
athetyādi |
bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ akṣobhyādayaḥ praharṣotphullalocanāḥ pañcaprītisamanvitatvād sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittavajñaṃ visphāritanayanāḥ mahāvajradharamevamāhuḥ |
āścaryaṃ bhagavannetadadbhutaṃ ākāśasamavasaraṇeṣu ākāśa- vyāpteṣu sarvadharmeṣu anavasthiteṣu buddhadharmāḥ jñānadehaḥ samavasaraṇaṃ saṃgrahaṃ gacchanti yānti iti yatra hi nāma yat tadadbhutam |
evaṃ protsāhya tataḥ kiṃ kṛtavanta ityāha |
athetyādi |
sarvabuddhāḥ bodhi- sattvāḥ vajrapāṇermahāvajradharasya pādayornipatya praṇamya evamāhuḥ |
bhagavatā tvayā mantravajrasiddhisamuccayamiti |
mantramālā mantrādayaḥ |
vajrāṇi dhyānāni |
taiḥ kṛtāḥ siddhayo laukikāḥ lokottarāśca tāsāṃ samuccayaṃ vṛndaṃ yadbhāṣitaṃ bhāṣitāni sarvamantrasamuccayasiddhīni kutra sthitānīti praśnaḥ |
athetyādi prativacanam |
vajrapāṇirmahāvajrapāṇirmahāvajradharaḥ |
teṣāṃ sādhukāraṃ datvā tānevamāha |
bhagavanta ityādi |
sarvamantravajjrasamuccayasiddhīni sarvamantrāḥ sādhakāḥ teṣāṃ kāyavākcittasthitāni na bhavanti tāvat |
tasmāt na kvacit sthitāni |
tat kasya hetoḥ kasmāt kāraṇāt ?
paramārthataḥ kāyādīnāṃ mantrādisiddhīnāṃ ca paramārthato'bhāvāditi |
sarvamantrasiddhisādhanāni tahi sarvathā naśyantītyāha |
kiṃ tvityādi |
saṃvṛtisattvena sarvasiddhīni sarva- buddhadharmāṇi daśavalavaiśāradyādayaḥ svakāyavākcittavajrasvabhāvāḥ santi |
tarhi kāyādīnāmastitvena tadādhāra [[]ṇāṃ siddhīnāmupalambhaḥ |
tannirākurvannāha |
taccetyādi |
tat kāyādikaṃ na kāmadhātvādisthitam |
traidhātukasthitatva- muktvā anyonyādhārādheyabhāvamapi nirākurvannāha |
na cittaṃ kāyasthitamityādi |
cittaṃ tāvat na kāye sthitam |
kāyamapi na citte sthitam, dvayorādhārādheya- bhāvavirodhāt |
tathā vāgapi na citte sthitam |
cittamapi na vāci sthitam |
tathā vāgapi na kāye sthitā, kāyo'pi na vāci sthita iti draṣṭavyam |
tat kasya hetoḥ ākāśavat svabhāvaśuddhatvāt na trayāṇāmapyanyonyādhārā- dheyabhāvaḥ ||
athetyādi |
te vajradharamāhuḥ |
sarvatathāgatā akṣobhyādayaḥ |
tān vārayatīti sarvaṃtathāgatadharmāḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ |
sa kutra sthitaḥ kuto vā saṃbhūta iti praśnaḥ |
āha vajradharaḥ svakāyavākcittasthitaḥ svakāya vākcittasaṃbhūtaḥ |
āhuḥ tathāgatāḥ praśnayanti |
cittaṃ kutra sthitam ?
cittavatraḥ kutra sthitaḥ ?
āha vajradharo visarjayati |
ākāśasthitam |
prabhāsvarasthitam |
āhustathāgatāḥ ākāśaṃ prabhāsvaraṃ kutra sthitamiti ?
āha bhagavān na kvacit sthitam |
tat prabhāsvaraṃ na kvacit sthitam, ātmanyeva sthitamiti yāvat |
athetyādi saṃgītikṛdvākyam |
te buddhabodhisattvā āścaryaprāptāḥ hetoḥ adbhutaprāptāḥ phalaiḥ svacittadharmatāvihāraṃ svacittavilasitaṃ dhyāyantaḥ ālamvayantaḥ vigatasandehatvāt tūṣṇīṃ sthitāḥ maunamāpannāḥ abhūvan vabhūvuḥ |
sthiracalātmakaṃ cittaṃ sarvacittaṃ tadātmaka: samayaḥ sarvacittasamayo mahāvajradharaḥ tasya sāraḥ laukikalokottarāḥ siddhayaḥ tā eva vāvandhyatvād vajrāṇi teṣāṃ saṃbhūtiḥ prakāśanaṃ yena granthasamūhena, sa tannāmakaḥ |
sandhyābhāṣā ||
||
pradīpodyotanaṭīkāyāṃ pañcadaśapaṭale ṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā ||
||
ṣoḍaśaḥ paṭalaḥ ||
uktalakṣaṇāḥ sarvasiddhayaḥ maṇḍalābhiṣiktasyaiva niṣpadyanta iti śiṣya- pātrīkaraṇārthaṃ maṇḍalavidhānopayogikaṃ ṣoḍaśapaṭalamupakṣipannāha
athetyādi |
punaḥ punarapyekābhiprāyo bhūtvā mahāvajradharaṃ sarvatathāgata- kāyavākcittavajrapadaiḥ anantāparyantalokadhātusthitatathāgatakāyavākcittavajra- pratiṣṭhitaiḥ stottraiḥ sarvatathāgatasāntamityevaṃ prakāraiḥ adhyeṣyābhimukhīkṛtya sarvatathāgataratnavajrapūjāvyūhaiḥ divyaiḥ viṣayameghaiḥ pūjayāmāsuḥ
maṇḍalavidhi- śravaṇābhiprāyeṇeti śeṣaḥ |
sarvavajrāḥ tathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ maṇḍalaṃ kāyamaṇḍalaṃ tadeva siṃhaḥ kleśamṛgavidhvaṃsanāt samayarājaḥ tasmin kṛto'dhyavasā viśeṣaḥ tenāviṣkṛto vyūho maṇḍalaparikaro yasmin samādhau taṃ samāpadya vajrakāyo vairocanaḥ tasya maṇḍalaṃ tadādhipateyaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvasādhakānāṃ prayojanabhūtaṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayāmāsaḥ
ataheti vākyopādāne |
ataḥ śabdo hetau |
maṇḍalaśravaṇakutūhalāḥ yataḥ tataḥ samyak pravitatya vakṣyāmi |
kāyamaṇḍalaṃ vairocanamaṇḍalamuttamam sarva- siddhidam |
tat kathamityāha cittetyādi |
cittavajrapratīkāśamakṣobhyamaṇḍala- sadṛśaṃ caturasrādiguṇena yat sarvamaṇḍalaṃ taduttamam |
ṣoḍaśahastamiti |
catuḥpāśrvapekṣayā ekaikapārśvena caturhastaṃ jñeyaṃ taccatu- rasramavartulam |
taccaturasramityupalakṣaṇatvāt caturdvārādīni gṛhyante |
suśobhanaṃ raṅganairmyalyāt |
kīdṛgvidham ?
maṇḍalaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvatathāgataiḥ praśastaṃ kāyavajjro vairocanaḥ tena pratiṣṭhitamadhipatitvena śobhitam |
tasya caturasramaṇḍalasyābhyantarataḥ cakraṃ vartulaṃ cakramālālaṅkṛtam |
katha- mityāha vidhivayeti |
vidhistattvasaṃgrahādyuktaṃ maṇḍalasūtraṃ vidhānaṃ tadeva vajraṃ suniścitaṃ yasyāṃ buddhau tayālikhet |
evaṃ maṇḍalamālikhya mantrāṇāṃ devatānāṃ madhye vairocanapadaṃ mudrāvajrapadaṃ svamudrāsaṃyuktadevatāvimbaṃ kuryāt
akṣobhyādīnāṃśca pūrvādisthitān likhet |
ṣoḍaśapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā
kevalaṃ sthāpayet |
kāyavākcittavajrāgrīn locanādyān sarvakoṇeṣu āgneyyādi- koṇeṣu yathāsaṃbhavaṃ likhet niveśayet |
krodho yamārirādiryeṣāmaparājitahayagrīvāmṛta kuṇḍalīnāṃ tāṃścaturṣu dvāreṣu niveśayet mahāvalaparākramān acalādīnāṃ koṇe niveśayet rūpādīn maitreyā- dīṃśca yathāsthānaṃ niveśayet mantrajñaḥ ācāryaḥ guhyavajraprabhāvitā padmavajra- saṃyogakṛtāṃ pūjāṃ maṇḍalamadhye svamudrāsahito vidadhyāt |
eṣa vidhiḥ sarvamantrāṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ samayaḥ yogatantravyavasthā duratikramaḥ |
alaṅghanīyaḥ |
viṇmūtrādyaṃ pañcāmṛtam viśeṣataḥ asmin kāyamaṇḍale avaśya- meva ekāntena dātavyaṃ prāsitavyam |
eṣa pañcāmṛtaprāśaḥ kāvavajriṇāṃ kāyamaṇḍalābhiyuktānāṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvasādhakānāṃ samayaḥ alaṅghanīyaḥ sarvatathāgatābhimatametat kāyamaṇḍalam |
athetyādinā vāṅmaṇḍalavidhānamahāvajradharaḥ sarvavāgvajrasamayāḥ ami- tābhamāṇḍaleyāḥ |
teṣāṃ meghaḥ samūhaḥ tasya vyūho yathāsthānasanniveśo yasmin tannāmakaṃ samādhiṃ samāpadya imaṃ vāgvajramaṇḍalaṃ tadarthaṃ vacanasamūhaḥ svayaṃ kāyā- dibhyo niścārayet |
taddarśayati |
athāta ityādi vibhaktārthaḥ |
viṃśatihastaṃ prakurvīta ekaikapārśvataḥ |
pañcahastaṃ sūtrayet vajrabhāvanaiḥ pañcatathāgatavarṇaiḥ sūttraiḥ caturasramaṇḍalasya madhye mahaccakraṃ pradhānabhūtaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ padmālaṅkṛtamālikhet |
tasmin maṇḍale sarvamudrāparipūrṇām ālikhed vidhi- tatparaḥ vidhikuśalaḥ |
katham ?
amitāyumahāmudrāṃ svamudrāsaṃyuktāṃ tasya madhye niveśayet |
yat kāyacittamaṇḍalayorvajrapadaṃ tathāgatavinyasanaṃ sarveṣāmapi kalpayet kuryāt |
vidhānena tantroktavidhinā maṇḍalaṃ parisphuṭaṃ samyak kṛtvā guhyapūjāṃ dvayendriyasamāpattilakṣaṇaṃ tathāgatapūjāṃ niṣpādayet |
vajriṇo daśadigvyavasthitāḥ buddhāḥ evaṃ kṛte tuṣyanti sādhakasya prasīdanti |
viṇmūtraśukramayaiḥ pañcāmṛtaiḥ pūjyaśiṣyānātmānaṃ ca pūjayitvā abhipretārtha- siddhiravāpyate |
eṣo hīti vivṛtārthaḥ |
sarvatathāgatapraśastamamitābhamaṇḍalam |
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane evaṃ kāyavākcittavajrāṇāṃ maṇḍalatritayamuktvā avaśiṣṭatathāgatadvayasya anyeṣāṃ māṇḍaleyānāmapi madhye yasya prādhānyaṃ vivakṣyate tasya naṃ darśayannāha athetyādi |
samantato nānāmukhāḥ meghavat sarvārthasiddhiṃ kurvantīti samanta- meghāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ vyūhaṃ vṛndaṃ tasya vajraṃ pṛthaṅamaṇḍalaṃ yasmin samādhau nirdiśyate taṃ samantameghavyūhavajrākhyaṃ samādhiṃ cittaikāgratānāmukhīkṛtya idaṃ buddhisthaṃ paramaguhyamatyantagopanīyaṃ maṇḍala rahasyamaprakaṭaṃ svakāyādibhyo niścāra- yāmāsa |
yasyetyādi |
vajradharāgrasya vajradharaprabhṛtīnāṃ māṇḍaleyānāṃ madhye yasya vimbaṃ maṇḍalaṃ madhye sthāpyate tasya devatāviśeṣasya maṇḍalacakraṃ bhavet |
kāya- vākcittaguhyajaṃ triguhyātmakaṃ ratnakarmakulayostadantarbhāvāt |
sarvatathāgatā akṣobhyādayaḥ teṣāṃ kāyavākcittavajrapadaṃ kāyādimaṇḍalaṃ tasmin jñānaṃ sūtrapātanādividhānaṃ tasya rahasyaṃ maṇḍalaparāvṛttilakṣaṇaṃ yasmin paramaguhye paramopadeśe sa tathā |
athetyādi |
sarveṣāmakṣobhyādīnāṃ maṇḍalacakramādhāramaṇḍalaṃ tatsaṃbhavati yasmiṃstannāmakaṃ samādhi cittaikāgratāṃ samāpadya teṣāmeva sarvamaṇḍalānāṃ kāya- vākcittavajrasvabhāvaṃ guhyaṃ svakāyādivajñebhyaḥ udājahāra āviścakāra |
tatretyādi |
tatra kāyādiguhye imāni prastūyamānāni vajramaṇḍalāni tathāgatacakrāṇi teṣāmanastrāṇi cittārādhanasvabhāvāni, tānyeva daivatatvāt hṛdayāni maṇḍalasūtraṇo yogitvāt sūtrāṇi avinaśvaratvādakṣarāṇi |
sarva- mantrāṇāṃ pratiṣṭhābhūtatvāt padāni |
kānītyāha |
oṃ āḥ hū~ iti ||
pātanamityādi |
vajrā akṣobhyādayaḥ tadvarṇaiḥ raktaṃ sūtraṃ vajrasūtram |
tasya tena pātanaṃ maṇḍalasūtraṇaṃ rajaso'pi paśñcaraṅgikasya pātanaṃ vikīraṇaṃ omi- tyādibhirmantraiḥ kāryamiti śeṣaḥ |
na kāryamityādi |
mantrasattve parāyaṇena dvayajñānayuktena uktaprakāramaṇḍalalekhanaṃ na kāryam |
yadi kuryāt tasya bodhiḥ vajradharatvaṃ durlabhā dūre bhavati |
yadi tattvasaṃgrahādyuktavidhānena kuryāditi bhāvaḥ |
.. tasmādityādi |
niṣpannakramasthātavyaḥ utpattikramasthaḥ tīrṇavidyāvrataḥ yadi maṇḍalālekhanaṃ kuryāt tasya vidhānaṃ samayāḥ kulabhedenoktamudrāḥ tāsāṃ ṣoḍaśapaṭalaṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā vidhānaṃ samājoktavidhānena viracanaṃ tajjño mantrī |
avatāryeti |
mantraḥ sādhakaḥ tasya devatā svamudrā tāmavatārya purato'vasthāpya adhiṣṭhānaṃ pañca- kulīkaraṇaṃ tasya padaṃ sthānaṃ dhyātvā niṣpādya maṇḍalānāṃ kāyādimaṇḍalānāṃ sūtrapātādikrameṇa viracanaṃ kuryāditi |
evaṃ sāmānyavidhānamuktvā kulabhedena vidhimāha |
tatra kāyamaṇḍalaṃ likhitukāmo mantrī svayaṃ vairocanayogavān svamudrāṃ locanāṃ sākṣāt loca- nāmiva niṣpādya tayā saha maṇḍalasūtraṇādikaṃ kuryāt |
asya karaṇasyānuśaṃsāṃ kāyo vairocanaḥ tasya maṇḍalapadaṃ tadadhīnaṃ cakraṃ ramyamīṣatphala- pradānānmanoharaṃ kāyavajro vajrakāyaḥ tasya guṇaḥ anantāparyantalokadhātuṣu sattvānāṃ nānārūpeṇa dharmadeśanā pravartanaṃ tadāvāhakaṃ bhavati sādhakānām |
vajradharmetyādi |
mantrī vajradharma mahārājamātmānaṃ dhyātvā svadharmaṃ ca svamudrāṃ pāṇḍaravāsinīṃ dhyātvā avatārayet |
vāgmaṇḍalaṃ likhet |
eṣa kramaḥ |
sarvamantrāṇāṃ padmakulīnānāṃ paramaśāśvataṃ anādikālikatathāgatānāṃ guhyaṃ rahasyam |
vajrasattvetyādi |
ātmanāmakṣobhyarūpaṃ dhyātvā svamudrāṃ māmakīmadhiṣṭhā- yāvatārayet cittamaṇḍalaṃ likhet |
idaṃ tat sarvamantrāṇāṃ vajrakulīnānāṃ sādhakānāṃ paramādbhutamuttarottaraṃ rahasyam |
evaṃ kṛtena maṇḍalavidhānena prasannā manīṣiṇaḥ buddhabodhisattvāḥ sānnidhya- māgamya sannipātaṃ kṛtvā harṣānvitāḥ atituṣṭāḥ paramaguhyamatirahasyabhūtaṃ maṇḍalaṃ likhanti niṣpādayanti |
mudrāsahitasya sādhakasya kāyavākcittamadhi- ṣṭhāya maṇḍalaṃ tābhyāṃ saṃpādayantīti yāvat ||
ityāha ceti saṃgītikṛdvacanam ||
kartavyamityādi |
mantrasiddhena anantaroktavidhinā samāpitamaṇḍalena mantriṇā vajjraguhyaṃ tathāgatarahasyamatyadbhutamatiśayopetaṃ kāryam |
tat kimityāha |
ākṛṣyetyādi |
krodharājena nisumbhena sarvabuddhān niṣpannān samājenākāśa- maṇḍalasthitān ākṛṣya yathāsvaṃ saṃpuṭayitvā pūjayet |
triṣkālaṃ sandhyāttraye samayā rūpavadrājādaya: tābhiḥ kṛtā pūjā samayapūjā trivajrāmalavajriṇo māṇḍaleyaṃ kartavyam |
katham ?
trivajrayogena trivajrāḥ tyadhvavartino buddhāḥ tairyogena tyadhvavartitathāgatairekīkaraṇenāpūjayediti yāvat |
guhya samājapradīpodyotane etad vidhānaṃ kiṃ karotītyāha |
mantrasiddhipravartanamiti |
mantrāstathāgatāḥ tebhyaḥ siddhiḥ laukikalokottaralakṣaṇa tasyāḥ pravartanaṃ niṣpādanamityāha |
sarveṣāmityādinā sannipatati mantrāṇāṃ valiṃ santarpaṇaṃ dadyāt prayacchet |
mahādbhutamatyudāram |
ayamevādhyātmiko valiḥ |
taddarśayannāha viḍādayaḥ pañca viṣayāḥ niruktenābhipretāḥ |
atha pañca viṣayāḥ na saṃbhavanti tadā kimityāha śukreṇeti |
śucaṃ śokaṃ krakacavat pāṭayati nāśayatīti śukraṃ madyaṃ tena sarvamantrāṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ prīṇanaṃ tarpaṇaṃ samudāhṛtaṃ kathitam |
eṣa hi vidhiḥ samayaḥ alaṅghanīyaḥ |
śreṣṭhaḥ pradhānabhūtaḥ |
buddhā mantriṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ bodhiḥ cittaṃ tatprapūraka: āpyāyakaḥ |
mantritarpaṇameva tathāgatasantarpaṇamityabhiprāyaḥ |
evaṃ suśikṣitamudrāsahitasya maṇḍalālekhanavidhimuklā tadvirahitasya vidhi-
māha sūtrasyetyādi
idaṃ maṇḍalasūtrapātanaṃ svayameva prajñāvirahitatvāt ekākī yadācaret tadā kāyamaṇḍale vairocanamātmānaṃ prabhāvitvā śiṣyavajrasattvaṃ prabhāvayet
athavā śiṣyaṃ vajramahādyutiṃ amṛtakuṇḍali vā kalpayet tadanantaroktaṃ karmapadaṃ maṇḍalakaraṇīyaṃ sarvabuddhaniṣevitaṃ praśastam
evaṃ cittamaṇḍale akṣobhyamātmānaṃ dhyātvā śiṣyaṃ vairocanamamṛtakuṇḍali vā prakalpayet
evaṃ vāṅmaṇḍale'pi yathāsaṃbhavaṃ jñeyam
pañcabuddhamahārājān akṣobhyādīn sūtravajragatān kṛṣṇādisūtnagatān huṅkārādirūpeṇa praviṣṭān nyaset dhyāyāt
eṣa sūtrapātanavidhiḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ paramādbhutaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ rahasyam
parśvāviṃśatītyādi
ekaikasyāpi rajasaḥ pañcātmakatvāt pañcaviśatibhedena bhinnasya pañcarajaḥskandhasya nipātanaṃ kāryam
ekaikasmin rajasi pradhānagauṇa- rūpeṇa tathāgatapañcakaṃ bhāvayedityetad darśitam
sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ rahasyamuttamaṃ bodhiviśiṣṭaṃ sampradāyam
maṇḍalalikhanakāle vighnopaśamanavidhimāha
sarvamantrā māṇḍaleyāḥ teṣāṃ bāhyopadravebhyo rakṣaṇārthaṃ vajrahuṅkāro'ta yamāriḥ tasya bhāvanā kāryā
kathamityāha kāyetyādi
divyamasādhāraṇaṃ kāyavāksamayaṃ kāyavākcitta- vikurvitaṃ pṛthivyādikṛtopadraveṣu yamāriyogavān bhāvayet darśayet ||
ṣoḍaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā tatvālīḍhādinā vighnacūrṇanaṃ kāyavikurvaṇam

vātyākṛto vāyavyaḥ

eteṣu pañcasthāneṣu vajrahuṅkārabhāvanā kāryā

nyāsaṃ kalaśavajrāṇāmiti ||
prokṣaṇavijaya kalaśādīnāṃ nyāsaṃ kiṃ- viśiṣṭaṃ mantrasaṃgrāhakaḥ tantro mantratantraḥ tamabhiyanti yathoktavidhinā pālayantīti mantratantrādhipāḥ tattvasaṃgrahādyabhiyuktāḥ taiḥ smṛtam
sthānauṣadhivījaliṅgādi- nyāsaiḥ kalpitaṃ sajjīkṛtaṃ kalaśaṃ vajrasattvasamādhistho maṇḍalācāryaḥ dṛḍhabuddhimān avikṣiptasmṛtiḥ kalpayet yathāsthānaṃ sthāpayet
evaṃ maṇḍalaṃ parisamāpya tataḥ homaṃ havanaṃ kurvīta mantrajñaḥ sādhakaḥ sarva- siddhiphalārthikaḥ laukikalokottarasiddhikāmaḥ ubhayatantroktavidhānairiti śeṣaḥ
viṇmūtretyādinā ādhyātmikahomamāha

pūrṇetyādi

katham ?
madhye kuṇḍamadhye svadevatāvimvamiti śāntyādiprakārabhinnadevatāvimbaṃ dhyātvā cakre tasya mukhe pūrṇāhuti dadyāt
evaṃ gaṇacakrasthena homaṃ niṣpādya punaḥ dvayendriyaprayogeṇa daivatīsamāpattiḥ sthitenāpyaṣṭottaraśatavāram ucchiṣṭānnādikaṃ karmabhedena juhuyāt homayediti ||
eṣo hītyādi sugamā narutavyākhyā
vairocanetyādinā āveśapūrvakaṃ praveśavidhi darśayati
vividhaprakāreṇa dīpyata iti vairocano mahāvajradharaḥ taṃ prayogaḥ tatparāvṛttyā dveṣavatrīkaraṇaṃ guhyasamāzapradīpodyotane tena trivajraṃ praṇavāditrayaṃ tasmāt saṃbhavo yasya taṃ trivajrasaṃbhavaṃ śiṣyaṃ kṛtvā raktavarṇamāḥkāramaraśmikaṃ kāyavākcittasarvāṅge dhyātvā āveśavidhāne kṛte vajreṇāveśavajreṇa gṛhyate svīkriyate

taddyotakā - kṣarātmako mantravajro'yam
tatra maṇḍalavidhau mahāvajraḥ abhiṣekārthaṃ śiṣyaḥ tasyābhiṣekaḥ mahāvajrābhiṣekaḥ tadeva gopyatvāt guhyaṃ jñānarahasyam ||
khadhātuṃ ākāśakuharaṃ sarvabuddhaiḥ vairocanādibhiḥ paripūrṇa vibhāvayet dhyāyāt
vādyagandhairmahāmeghairiti diśecchādibhiḥ pañca kāmaguṇaiḥ tatra sthitān pūjayitvā vajradhīḥ ācāryaḥ bhāvayet
svayaṃlikhitavajjo maṇḍale bhāvayet yathākrameṇa saṃpuṭīkuryāt ||

trivatretyādi
trivajrasamayavajñaiḥ ātmasvamudrākāyavākcittaikīkaraṇa- saṃbhūtaiḥ sarṣapairbodhicittavindubhiḥ tathāgatān vratī ācāryaḥ bhāvayet
śiṣyābhi- ṣekadānārthaṃ tāḍayet codayet
tadā tasmin kāle coditāḥ tathāgatāḥ svayamevopetyābhiṣekamadhiṣṭhānaṃ dadanti
athavā buddhān vairocanādīn vajrasattvasamādhinā bhāvayet dhyāyāt
evaṃ dhyātvā tān kalaśān sulocanādibhirdhāritān bhāvayet
budha ācāryaḥ
tataḥ kimityāha
dṛḍhamatiḥ ekāgrabuddhiḥ
śiṣyaṃ vajravairocanaṃ cintet
tadanantaraṃ mantrajñaḥ devatāniṣpattibījajñaṃ kāyavākcittavajriṇaḥ śiṣyasya kāyamaṇḍale yathāvat nyāsaṃ kurvīta
evaṃ vinyāse kṛte idaṃ vakṣyamāṇakaṃ sarvasmin maṇḍale abhiṣekaḥ sarvā- bhiṣekaḥ tatra rahasyaṃ gopyabhūtaṃ sarvācāryāḥ cittamaṇḍalādāvabhiṣekadātāraḥ .... teṣāṃ śiṣyaṃ prati vāgvajrodīraṇaṃ vāgvajrāviṣkaraṇam ||
d ṣoḍaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā
kiṃ tadityāha
abhiṣekamityādi
abhiṣekaṃ vaidhātukarājyapratiṣṭhāpanaṃ mahāvajramaparihāryaṃ traidhātukanamaskṛtaṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ purataḥ triguhya- vajro mahāvajradharaḥ tatsaṃbhavaṃ tadanvayāgatam ahamadya dadāmi tvayātra kiṃ pratipattavyamiti śiṣyasamāśvāsanam
tattraivamuktavati vajragurau idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ sarvaśiṣyāḥ kulabhedena vyavasthitāḥ taiḥ mahāvajro vajraguruḥ tasya prārthanā adhyeṣaṇā tatra vidhiḥ vidhānaṃ tadeva rahasyaṃ bhavati
kiṃ tat ?
bodhivatreṇetyādi
vodhivajro mahāvajradharaḥ buddhānāṃ akṣo- bhyādīnāṃ mahamaho mahābhiṣekaḥ yathā yena prakāreṇa dattaḥ tāraṇārthāya māṃ saṃsārasāgarottāraṇāya khavajramahāvajradharatulya adya idānīṃ me tvatśaraṇaṃ gaṃtāya dadāhi prayaccheti prārthanā
tadā tasmin kāle prahṛṣṭacetasaḥ adhyeṣaṇābhiprasannamanāḥ guruḥ abhiṣekaṃ dadyāt
devatāvimbaṃ vāhyamaṇḍalaṃ tena yogaḥ śiṣyakāyamaṇḍale yāvadvinyāsaḥ tena hetunā hṛdaye tasyaiva hṛtpadme adhipati maṇḍalādhipatiṃ nyaset sthāpayet
evaṃ kṛtātmakasya śiṣyasya dhīmataḥ prajñāsaṃprayuktasya maṇḍalaṃ yathākrameṇa tattvato darśayet
samayamityādi
sarvabuddhaiḥ niṣpannatathāgataiḥ udāhṛtaṃ kathitaṃ samaya- malaṅghanīyaṃ karaṇīyaṃ śrāvayet vajraśiṣyāya prakāśayet
prāṇinaśca tvayā ghātyā ityādi samayacatuṣṭayaṃ pañcamapaṭaloktasandhyābhāṣāvyākhyānena jñeyam
anenānantaroktena ca vajramārgeṇābhedyasamādhinā sarvasattvān vajraśiṣyān pracodayet anuśiṣyāt
eṣa mārgaḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ duratikramaḥ samayasaṃvaraḥ

// vajrasattvopadeśabhūtaṃ rahasyaṃ karma prastūyate vajrasattvetyādi|
vajrasattvo- 'kṣobhyaḥ tasya mahājñānaṃ cisvarūpaṃ huṅkāraṃ vāgvajro'mitābhaḥ taṃ dhārayati niṣpādayatīti vāgvajradharaḥ|
kāyavajjo vairocanaḥ tasya mahānyāsaniṣpādaka- moṅkāraḥ etairakṣaraiḥ kiṅkaraṃ codayet adhitiṣṭhet sadā niṣpādanāt prabhṛti sarvakāleṣu|
ṣoḍaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā tatra kiṅkarasādhanaviṣaye idaṃ prastūyamānaṃ vajrajñānāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ cakraṃ vṛndaṃ tena kṛtaṃ mantrā vacanāni teṣāṃ samayapadaṃ alaṅghanīyā vyavasthā|
tacca samayacodanādibhedena caturvidham|
samayo mahāvajradharājñātena codanaṃ vijñāpanaṃ samayapreraṇaṃ tenaiva samayena kāryaṃ prayojanaṃ samayaḥ sādhyo'rthaḥ tasmin āmantraṇaṃ saṃjñāniyojanaṃ samayaḥ kṛtyaṃ tasmin vandhaḥ haṭhānniyojanam|
evaṃ siddhāḥ kiṃ kurvantītyāha khadhātvityādi
ākāśadhātuṃ vipula- manantaṃ śuddhamanāvaraṇaṃ sarvadharmavivarjitaṃ darśanādyatītam
evaṃvidhamākāśadhātuṃ piṇḍarūpeṇa vajravat kaṭhinarūpaṃ sādhakacoditāḥ kiṅkarāḥ kurvanti
kiṃ punastairasādhyamityatiśayoktiḥ
ityāha bhagavān sarvabuddhaikaputraḥ sarvabuddhā ekaputrā yasya sa mahāvajradhara evamāha
idānīṃ rājādīnāṃ vaśīkaraṇamāha buddhaṃ vetyādi
buddhaprājñaṃ śāstrakulaṃ vajrasattvo dhīraḥ tamapi vaśamānetuṃ svābhiprāye sthāpayituṃ yadīcchet idaṃ prastūya- māṇaṃ mahāguhyaṃ rahasyabhūtaṃ trivajrāgradharaṃ kāyādisvabhāvaṃ mahāmañjuśrīrūpaṃ cintet dhyāyāt
kathamityāha khavajñetyādi
jhaṭityātmānaṃ mañjuvajraṃ mahāvalaṃ duḥsādhya- sādhanaṃ khavajramadhyagataṃ prabhāsvaraniṣpannaṃ bhāvayet

pañcavāṇāḥ madanamohananirmohanamurcchananiśceṣṭīkaraṇamiti pañcaśarāḥ teṣāṃ prayogeṇa preraṇena makuṭāgre sādhyavare saṃspharet dhyāyāt cet
kathaṃ tat
mūrddhanābhiguhyacaraṇeṣu pañcasthāneṣu mantrajñaḥ bījajño mantrī krūravajreṇa dṛḍhādhyāśayena pātayet
pañcāvāṇa[[n] nikṣipet vālaḥ mañjughoṣaḥ tasmin buddhiścintā yasya sa vālabuddhiḥ sādhakaḥ mahāyaśairmahāśaktibhiḥ madanādivāṇaiḥ sādhyaṃ mūrchitaṃ mūḍhaṃ tastamaśaraṇaṃ bhāvayet dhyāyāt
idaṃ vaśīkaraṇadhyānam
guhyacodanena adhyātmaparāyaṇaiḥ ekaṃ pakṣamardhamāsaṃ dhyātavyam
sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ rahasyaṃ vajrārthabuddhinā mahāvajradhareṇa gītaṃ kathitam
vinayārthaprayogamāha khavañetyādi
ākāśakuharagataṃ hūṅkāraṃ dhyātvā tatparāvṛttyā buddhamaṇḍalaṃ sarvatathāgatavyūhamuttamaṃ cintayet
vajramantra oṅkāraḥ
tadādyaiḥ pañcabhirbījaiḥ trivajrādīnasādhyabhūtān duṣṭān prabhāvayet
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane teṣāṃ cakṣurgataṃ cakṣugolakagataṃ oṅkāraṃ prasphuradrūpaṃ dhyātvā gaganakuhara- vatiṃbuddhamaṇḍalamete paśyantītyadhiṣṭhānavidhānādarśayet
evaṃ kṛte sarvamantrāṇāṃ sarvatathāgatānāṃ vimbaṃ mahāmudrārūpaṃ paśyate sādhyāḥ paśyanti
kṣuttṛṣādyairityādi

mahāvajradharavacanamevaṃ darśayannāha
vairocanetyādi
vairocanarūpaṃ sarvārtha- saṃpadaṃ dhyātvā vaṃkāraṃ pūrṇacandramaṇḍalākāram amṛtavinduvarṣiṇaṃ vaktragataṃ tāluma- dhyagataṃ oṅkāraṃ sitavarṇaṃ vaṅkārasravadamṛtavindumantaḥ praveśayantaṃ nyaset
maṇivibhūṣitaṃ yauvanārogyasatsukhapradatvāt cintāmaṇivat pūjyaṃ sarvaduḥkhaharaṃ viṣagarabhūtādisarvopadravanivāraṇaṃ śāntaṃ sarvakleśaśamanaṃ ca jñānavajrapracoditaṃ bhāvanaṃ mahāvajradharatvāpādānam
ityāha bhagavān cintāmaṇivajraḥ ratnaketusvabhāvo mahāvajradharaḥ athetyādi
mahāvajjradharaḥ mahāvīravajraḥ tathāgata iti kaścit samādhiviśeṣaḥ
vajrabhāvanā tanniṣpādikā cittaikāgra tayā āviṣkṛtaṃ padāgraṃ bījaviśeṣaḥ taṃ vāgvajñebhyo niścārayet
vīḥ
tadvījaṃ khavajramadhyagataṃ buddhamaṇḍalasuprabhamakṣobhyā dibuddhacakraparivṛtaṃ trivajjrakāyayogena kāyavākcittaikīkaraṇena vajraniṣpādya idaṃ vicintayet
sarvālaṅkārajaṃ puṣṭaṃ mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇopetatvāt
pītavajjraḥ ratnasaṃbhavaḥ tasyaiva vijṛmmitaṃ vilasitaṃ yasya sa tathā jaṭāmakuṭadharaṃ śāntaṃ dhyātvā sarvaṃ śāntyādikaṃ samārabhet kuryāt
vīravajraḥ mahāvajradharaḥ

ja~
khavajramadhyagataṃ buddho jambhalaḥ sa eva sāratvānmaṇḍaḥ taṃ lāti niṣpāda- yatīti buddhamaṇḍalabījam

nānārūpagrahaṇena kāyasaṃsiddhayā nānārūtena vāksiddhayā yathābhilaṣitārthaprāptyā cittasaṃsiddhayā proktāḥ vajraḍākinyaḥ
tāsā rūpadha rāḥ tatsadṛśāḥ prakarṣeṇa bhāntīti prabhā devyaḥ jāmbūnadaprabhākārāḥ upalakṣaṇāśrayatvāt kanakādiprabhāḥ hīnasiddhi svarūpaparivartanādilakṣaṇe samāśritāḥ
ṣoḍaśapaṭala ṣaṭko ṭivyākhyā vajraḍākinīnāṃ laukikaḍākinīnāṃ ca samāpattiṃ phalabhedaṃ darśayannāha antardhānetyādi
antardhānaṃ niḥsvabhāvīkaraṇaṃ tadādiḥ uccāṭanādikaṃ yāsāṃ tāḥ antardhānādayaḥ vajraḍākinyaḥ tāsāṃ saṃsiddhau samāpattau sādhakaḥ bhaved vajradharaḥ prabhuḥ
yakṣarājye laukikaḍākinyaḥ tābhiḥ samāpattau mantraparāyaṇo vidyādharaḥ prabhurbhavatīti viśeṣaḥ

akṣobhyetyādi

kimiti sandehe
ayaṃ mahāvajrātmā deśakaḥ sarvadharmāḥ sthiracalasvabhāvā ye teṣāṃ vajraṃ tattvaṃ niṣṭhārūpaṃ tadanabhijñāya anadhigamya guhyākṣaraṃ sarvasattvā- sādhāraṇaṃ rahasyaṃ nirdiśati, uta vijñāyeti yeṣāṃ sandeho'bhūt teṣāṃ sandeha- nirasanārthamātmanaḥ satyadvayādhigamapradarśanāya prabhāsvarapraveśavyutthānaṃ darśita- vānityabhisandhiḥ

kāyavākcittaguhyoddyotakamakṣaraṃ vajrapadaṃ prāpya mahāvajradharasakāśāt sākṣāt śrutvāpi kāyavākcittabodhi- miti
kāyavākcitto mahāvajradharaḥ tasya bodhi phalādhigamaṃ vayaṃ tāvat na sākṣātkurmaḥ
kiṃ punarbrahmādaya ityakṣobhyādaya ūcuḥ tat kasmādityādinā svānadhigamakāraṇaṃ darśayati sarvakalpanāsvabhāvaniḥsṛtatvāt niḥsvabhāvāḥ ataeva akṣaraṃ paramārthasatyaṃ tasmāt saṃbhūtaḥ prasūtaḥ anutpādavajro mahāvajradharaḥ tasyā- pi saṃbodhiḥ hetuphalādvaidhībhāvaḥ
evaṃ vajradharābhisaṃbodhidurlabhatāṃ pradarśya punaḥ sakalasattvasādhāraṇatvena sulabhatāṃ pradarśayanta āhuḥ yāvanta ityādi
kulaputrā iti bodhisatvānāhvayanti sattvasaṃgraheṇa saṃgṛhītāḥ aṇḍajādayaḥ sarve ye sattvāḥ te bodhipratiṣṭhitāḥ bodhistattvādhigamaḥ tasmin pratiṣṭhitāḥ śrutacintā- bhāvanākrameṇa pratipannāścet saṃbuddhavajrāḥ tathāgatā eva
tat kasmādityādināta hetumāha
vata niścaye
ime sattvāḥ traidhātukaparyāpannāḥ
kāyavākcittavajro mahāvajradharaḥ tasya jñānamadvayajñānaṃ tatprāptāḥ prāptabhājanasthitāḥ bhūtavadupacārāt
trikāyavajraḥ dharmādikāyattrayaṃ tad dhārayatīti trikāyavajradharaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ tasya bhāvaḥ trikāyavajradharatā tāmupādāya kāraṇaṃ kṛtvā sarvasattvā buddhavajrā iti yojyam
evaṃ bodhisattvatathāgatasaṃspandanāya prasanno bhagavān tamevārthaṃ svavacanena dyotayatīti darśayannāha athetyādi
svabhāvaśuddhāḥ sthiracalā bhāvāḥ teṣāṃ nairātmyaṃ prabhāsvaratāṃ dharmadhātuḥ bodhicittavajraḥ tanniriti
niḥśeṣeṇālaye dīni saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭ koṭivyākhyā utpattisthāne sati vajjrasaṃbhūtāḥ kāyavākcittavajrasaṃbhūtāḥ
kalpanā buddhiviku- rvaṇādikriyā gīyate indrāyudhavat dṛśyamānatvāt
na ca gīyate, vicārato'labhya- mānatvāt
evaṃ mahāvajradharasamādhi niścitya ghaṭamānayogināṃ buddhatvasiddhihetuṃ codyaparihāramukhena darśayitumāha athetyādi
sarvatathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ kāyādi- siddhayaḥ kutaḥ samavasaranti utpadyante kva vā sthitāḥ kva līnā iti
tathāgatānāṃ codyavajjradharaḥ parihāramāha svakāyetyādi
svakāyavākcittānāṃ santānaḥ kṣaṇaparamparayotpattiḥ
tadeva vajramaprabhāsvarapraveśāt
sthiteḥ tatra pratiṣṭhitāḥ sarvalaukikalokottarāḥ siddhayaḥ tata evotpadyanta iti grāhyam
sarvasiddhīnāmutpattisthitipralayahetuḥ kāyattrayamiti yāvat
sarvetyādi
na kevalaṃ laukikāḥ siddhayaḥ kāyāditrayodbhūtāḥ kiṃ tu daśavalavaiśāradyādisarvaguṇāḥ traidhātukaṃ ca kāyavākcittodbhūtaṃ kāyavākcitta- pratiṣṭhitameveti
parihāraḥ sarvatathāgatā ityādi
sarvatathāgatakā yavākcitta- vajrasiddhīriti laukikalokottarasiddhinimittāni kāyavākcittāni tadhātu- kaśca pṛthivyādinī ca
kutra sthita iti tathāgatānāṃ praśnaḥ
ākāśadhātuḥ bhūta- koṭiḥ tatra pratiṣṭhitamiti parihāraḥ
bhūtakoṭiḥ kutra sthita iti punaśco - dyam
vajradharaḥ prāha na kvaciditi
sarvatathāgatānāmāścaryaprakāramāha athetyādi
āścaryaprāptāḥ viśuddhau adbhutaprāptā vyutthāne
aho vajra aho vajreti prasāde āmreḍitam
aho vajrasya deśaneti deśanādurlabhatve āścaryam
vajrasya saṃvṛttiḥ paramārtha- satyasya deśanā yatra yasmin paramārthasatyena kāyavākcittaṃ tatra tasmin deva- tārūpaṃ sarvākāravaropetātmakaṃ vibhāvyate niṣpādyata ityāścaryakāraṇam
evamanimittasamādhi niścitya tadupāyadevatāmūrtiryathā niṣpādyate taṃ darśayannāha athetyādi
trivajrāḥ sarvasattvāḥ teṣāmagrayāḥ hariharahiraṇyagarbhāḥ teṣāmagrayāḥ śrāvakapratyekajinā teṣāmagrayo mahāvajradharaḥ trivajraparameśvara iti trivajñeṣu kāyavākcittavajñeṣu paramamatyarthamaiśvaryeṇa pravartata iti trivajrapara- meśvaraḥ
tathā coktam, kāyaiśvaryaṃ vāgaiśvaryaṃ cittaiśvaryaṃ tathaiva ca | ṛddhiḥ sarvagataiśvarya icchā kartā guṇāṣṭamaḥ || iti
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane sarvasiddhīnāṃ laukikalokottarāṇāṃ sādhanabhūto vidyāmayaḥ puruṣo vidyā- puruṣaḥ tasya bhāvanāṃ niṣpādanakramaṃ bhāṣate kathayati
khavatretyādi
buddha- maṇḍalaniṣpannāniṣpannaṃ sattvarāśi khadhātuvajramadhyasthaṃ prabhāsvaragataṃ dhyāyāt
tataḥ sakalasattvarāśiṃ kāyavajrahetubhūtaṃ candravimbaṃ dhyātvā vajraṃ mūrdhni
prabhāvayediti
mūrdhni candramaṇḍalopari vajraṃ pañcaśūkaṃ prabhāvayet
tatparā- vṛttyā akṣobhyaṃ trimukhaṃ trikāyasaṃbhūtaṃ trikāyātmakaṃ visphurantaṃ pañca raśmi- spharaṇasaṃharaṇopetaṃ cintayet
cakraṃ vajraṃ māṇḍaleyadevatāvṛndaṃ tadātmanaḥ parivārarūpeṇa dhārayati sphara- tīti cakravajradharaḥ
evaṃvidhaṃ devatārūpaṃ dhyātvā yogī bodhimakṣobhyatā- mavāpnuyāt
anenaiva krameṇa vairocanādiyogī cakrādiparāvṛttyā sarveṣāṃ vairocanādīnā - midaṃ guhyaṃ kulabhedena devatārūpagrahaṇarahasyaṃ vicintayet
teṣāṃ vairocanā- dīnāmanyathā prakārāntareṇa bhāvanaṃ siddhiḥ siddhikāraṇaṃ na bhavatītyāha bhagavān
evamātmānaṃ niṣpādya tataḥ kiṃ kuryādityāha vidhātubhūtāmityādi
tridhātuḥ śarīraṃ tatra bhūtāṃ sthitāṃ mahārājñīṃ devīṃ vajradhātvīśvarīṃ prīṇayantīṃ ānandaṃ sukhaṃ janayantīṃ vicintayet paśyet
vṛṣabhāḥ vajropamasamādhisthā yoginaḥ
kathaṃbhūtā ityāha vajrakāyatrilakṣitā iti
vajrakāyābhyāṃ puṃstrī- ndriyābhyāṃ lakṣitāḥ anubhūtāḥ
trayaḥ rāgavirāgamadhyarāgāḥ tena vajrakāya- trilakṣitāḥ
anuśaṃsāmāha yaścedamityādi
yaḥ kaścid dṛṣṭasatyo vodhiḥ jñānamudrā tasyāṃ sattvaṃ cittaṃ yasya sa bodhisattvaḥ utpattikramānupūrveṇārūḍhaniṣpannakramatvāt mahāyaśāḥ
trikāyo mahāvajradharaḥ tadīyāṃ siddhimavāpnoti
mahādyutiḥ uttaptavīryaḥ saptāhenācireṇaiva
jñānamudrāsamāpattyā ca dṛḍhacittānāṃ vāhyāṅganāsamāpattyā paramānanda- sukhānubhavaḥ kartavya iti darśayannāha athetyādi
kāyavākcittavajrāṇāṃ rāgā- naladrutānāṃ vindurūpeṇa samudayenaikībhāvo yasmin sa kāyavākcittavajrasamuccayaḥ
kiṃ tat ?
guhyaṃ dharmodayaṃ tasya sādhanāṅgatvena vidhānaṃ rahasyaṃ tat svakāyā- dibhyo niścārayet
saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā kāyetyādi
kāyavākcittaiḥ sādhanaṃ sametyanuyātīti kāyavākcittasamayā mahāmudrā
sādhakasyārtha ānandaḥ
tamāliṅganādibhiḥ kalpayati saṃpādaya- tīti mahāmudrārthaṃkalpanā
kāyavākcittasamayā ca mahāmudrārthakalpanā ca tāṃ sahadharmacāriṇīṃ sarvarāgavirāgamadhyarāgāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaiḥ lakṣaṇaparijñānahetoḥ vidhivat mudrāsaṃskārādividhipūrvakaṃ bhāvayedupacaret yaḥ sarvabuddhatvamāpnuyāt
athetyādinā samāhitayogīnāṃ caryāprakāramāha athetyādi
sarvasādhakāḥ kalpitayoginaḥ teṣāṃ kāyavākcittānāmaviṣayatvād rahasyaṃ vacanaṃ tat svakāyā- dibhyo niścārayet
hastamudrāmityādi
uttamāṃ bodhi vajradharatvaṃ tādātmyabhāvenecchet bhāvayet hastamudrāṃ vāhyadevatābhiniveśakarīṃ na vadhnīyāt
vajrasattvaḥ svayaṃ sākṣāt kimanyat tu gaveṣate |
iti vacanāt
sarvetyādi
sarvārthasādhakaṃ mantrarahasyaṃ yeṣāṃ te sarve mantrāḥ teṣāṃ samājayogināmayaṃ samayaḥ jinairapi tathāgatairapi samājābhiyuktena hastamudrā vaddhavyeti yo'yaṃ samayaḥ sa nātikramyo dhārayituṃ na śakyate iti
sa[T]mājikeṣvapi kalpiyogināṃ samayamāha athetyādi
sarvabuddhāḥ kāyavajjrakulīnāḥ teṣāṃ samayamanuṣṭheyaṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayāmāsa
viḍityādi viḍādīnāṃ jugupsāṃ pratighaṃ bhāvaṃ naiva kārayet mantrī
tasmād vidhinā āliṅganacumvanādiprayogena [sa] mutpāditaṃ yadamṛtaṃ tat nityaṃ bhakṣayet upabhuñjet
kasmāt ityāha idaṃ guhyaṃ trivajrajamiti
kāyavākcitta- sausthityakaraṃ rasāyanamiti yāvat
● tadarthamevārabhya vāgvajjrasamayamāha vaidhātuketyādi
yāvantya uttamādhamamadhyama- yoṣitaḥ tāsāṃ traidhātukapathe ramye traidhātukāḥ sattvāḥ teṣāṃ panthā mārgaḥ vaidhātuka- pathaḥ apatyamārgaḥ padmaṃ tasmin ramye sarvajanāhlādakare vidhivat anurāgaṇādi- vidhisahitaḥ sarvāḥ madanātapattrapūrṇaśaśāṅkakūrmakādikā nāḍī: vāgvajñaiḥ jihvā- vyāpāraiścālayet kāmayet harṣayet āsṛṣṭi na jugupsayet vyāpārotpannaṃ yat tanna vā parityajet āsvādayediti yāvat
athetyādinā cittavajrasamayamāha yāvanta ityādi
sarvasamayāḥ padminyādi- striyaḥ trivajrakāyasaṃsthitāḥ vairocanādisvabhāvāstāḥ
trivajrasamayairātmānaṃ kāyavākcittavyāpāraiḥ prīṇayet harṣayet
cittavatraṃ na jugupsayediti
tāsāṃ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane cittaṃ na virāgayet cittamūlatvād dehasya dehamūlatvād bodhicittasyeti bhāvaḥ
mahānta asādhāraṇā trivajrasamayā: kāyavākcittasamayena deśitāḥ sa mahāvajradhara evamāheti saṃgītikāravacanam
trividhacaryārūḍhayogināṃ vāhyabuddhabodhisattvābhiniveśāpanayanārthamātmanaścā- dhidevatvakhyāpanārthamāha athetyādi
sarvatathāgatā: traiyadhvikā jinā: tairdeśitaṃ guhyavajraṃ hīnādhimuktikebhyo gopyatvāt taddarśitavān samāsena kulaśataṃ saṃkṣipya vairocanādayaḥ pañca buddhā prakīrtitāḥ
te pañca skandhāḥ
te rūpaskandhādayo nānye tadvyatiriktā iti grāhyāḥ
vāhyāḥ kṣitigarbhā - dayo bodhisattvāḥ santīti cet ?
taddṛṣṭyapakarṣaṇāyāha vajretyādi
viṣaya- grahaṇavibhutvāt vajramindriyaṃ tasyāyatanāni sthānāni cakṣurādīni yoniḥ
bodhisattvāgrayamaṇḍalaṃ kṣitigarbhādijinaurasacakraṃ yattadindriyāṇyeva nānyat bodhisattvavṛndadarmati
yo|locanādividyāsvadevatābhiniveśaḥ tadapanayanopāyamākṣipannāha athetyādi
traidhātukaṃ sattvabhājanākhyaṃ bhavatrayaṃ samuccīyate niṣpadyate yena sa traidhātuka- samuccayaḥ pṛthivyādiḥ sa āprabhāsvarapraveśāt sthitervajñaṃ tanniścāritavān
pṛthivyādi
yā locanādayo devyaḥ parikalpitāḥ upāttānupāttāḥ pṛthivyādayo catvāro dhātavo nānyā locanādayaḥ
khavajñetyādi
khavajjradhātuḥ sa ākāśadhātuḥ sarvaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ sametya - nugacchatīti samaya: sa eva vajradhara ityāha
sarvatathāgatāḥ pañcaskandhasvabhāvāḥ vairocanādayaḥ bhuvanaṃ locanādisvabhāvāḥ pṛthivyādayaḥ pañcadhātavaḥ teṣāmīśvaro mahāvajrasattva ityāh
evaṃ vairocanādīnāṃ pañcaskandhādisvabhāvaṃ nirdiśya parṣadi praśnāvasara- dānāya sthitavāni darśayannāha athetyādi
sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittavajraḥ tathāgato mahāvajjradharaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ samatāvihāramiti
sarvatathā- gatā vairocanādayaḥ kṣitigarbhādayo locanādayaśca teṣāṃ samatā pañcaskandhā- dibhiradvayatā tasya vihāro vyākaraṇaṃ tannāmadheyaḥ samādhiḥ ekāgreṇa cetasā- viṣkaraṇaṃ tāṃ samāpannaḥ kṛtavān
samāpadya ca evamuktvā saṃhṛtya sarvatathāgata- parṣanmaṇḍalaṃ pañcaskandhādisvabhāvān vairocanādīn yathāsthānasthitānavalokya tūṣṇīmabhūt
praśnāvakāśaṃ kṛtvā paramārthasatyaṃ praviṣṭavāniti yāvat
saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā bhagavān vairocanena vairocanādīnāṃ svabhāvaṃ parijñāya deśakaviṣaye svabhāva- parijñānaṃ prati jātasandehaḥ
adṛśyabhūte bhagavati praśnāsaṃbhavāt
pārṣadyān tathāgatān yathā pṛcchati taddarśayannāha athetyādi
sarvatathāgatāḥ niṣpannā- niṣpannāḥ teṣāṃ kāyavākcittavajraguhyaṃ tattvaṃ tatprakāśako granthasandarbhaḥ samājaḥ
tattrābhiṣiktaḥ sarvatathāgatairanujñātaḥ adhikṛtaḥ bhagavanta ityāmantraṇam
laukikalokottarācāraṃ darśayatītyācāryaḥ adhipatiḥ
sarvatathāgataiḥ yuṣmābhiḥ sarvabodhisattvairasmābhiśca kathaṃ kena svarūpeṇa draṣṭavyaḥ jñātavya iti maitreyeṇo- ditāstathāgatāḥ prāhu: vodhicitta ityādi
yo'yaṃ deśakaḥ sa bodhicittavajra eva mahāvajradharo draṣṭavyaḥ jñātavyaḥ
tat kasmāt hetoḥ ?
bodhicittaśca mahāvajradharaścācāryaścākṣobhyaśca, etadadvayamadvaidhīkāram hetuphalayoḥ pṛthakphala- hetusvabhāvenābhinnatvāt
yāvadityādi
kulaputreti maitreyāmantraṇam
yāvat saṃkṣepeṇeti atyantasaṃkṣepeṇetyarthaḥ
daśadiglokadhātuṣu pūrvādidiṅaniviṣṭeṣu saṃkhyeyeṣu lokadhātuṣu tathaivāsaṃkhyā buddhabodhisattvā ye tiṣṭhanti dharmakāyenā- calatvāt ghriyante saṃbhogakāyenākaniṣṭhabhavane deśanābhirdhāryamāṇatvāt
yāpayanti nirmāṇakāyena sattvān svargāpavargaprāpaṇāt |
te triṣkālaṃ trisandhya- māgamya tamācāryaṃ guhyasamājadeśakaṃ sarvatathāgatapūjābhiḥ kāyavākcittapūjābhi: saṃpūjya svabuddhakṣetraṃ gacchanti |
evaṃ pitāsmākamityādivāksamūhaṃ niścārayanti udgīranti |
yad yathāpi nāmetyādi sugamam |
bhītaḥ saṃtrāsasya ca kāraṇaṃ ācāryaḥ sarvatathāgataiḥ kathaṃ draṣṭavya iti vitarkavedanam pūjyasthāne āśaṅkāyā mahā- doṣatvāt |
ataeva tūṣṇīṃbhāvaḥ |
I maitreyasya tuṣṇīṃbhāvena prativacanamalabdhvā punastadvācanārthameva tadanyān bodhisattvānāmantya punarapi mahāvajradharasvarūpaṃ yathā prakāśayanti tad darśayannāha athetyādi |
sarvavajjradharasiddhisamayāvalambanamiti vajramaṇirdeśyaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ tattvam |
tadutsargapraveśabhāvena dhāryata iti vajradharaḥ maṇḍalādhipatiḥ tasya siddhi- samayāḥ nirmāṇakāyāḥ |
sarve ca te vajradharasiddhisamayāśca sarvavajradharasiddhi- samayāḥ teṣāmālamvanamāmukhīkaraṇaṃ yasmin tannāmakamabhedyaṃ samādhi samāpadya tān sarvabodhisattvā akṣobhyādayaḥ śṛṇvantvityādinā āmantrayate sma |
tathāgatavacanamākarṇya punarvodhisattvā yat praśnayanti tad darśayannāha guhyasamājapradīpodyotane athetyādi |
sarvatathāgatāḥ kāyavākcittasiddhiriti |
sarvatathāgatātmaka- nirmāṇarūpāṇi kutra sthitānīti bodhisattvānāṃ praśnaḥ |
trikāyaguhyaḥ kāya- vākcittavajrācāryakāyavākcittasthitānīti akṣobhyakāyavākcittasthitānīti parihāraḥ |
kāyavākcittavastraṃ kutra sthitamiti praśnaḥ |
na kutraciditi parihāraḥ |
bodhicittavatre sthitānīti kāyavākcittavastre sthitānīti vaktavye na kutraciditi vacanamidaṃ taditi vaktumaśakyaṃ gurūpadeśagamyatvāt |
evaṃ bodhisattvā vigatasandehāḥ yathā vabhūvuḥ taddarśayannāha athetyādi |
āścarya- prāptā deśake adbhutaprāptāḥ taddhetau |
atha khalu maitreya ityārabhya na kvacit sthita ityetatparyantena granthasamūhena yathāniṣpannairbuddhabodhisattvairmaṃṇḍalādhipati- rakṣobhyaḥ samājadeśakatvena satkartavyo gurūkartavyaḥ pitāsmākamityādibhirmadhura- vacanairabhiṣṭutya vāhyādhyātmikapūjābhiryathā pūjitaḥ tathāgramadvayaṃ samāśritya sapta- vidhālaṅkārākhyāyikānyāyena yaḥ śrīsamājaṃ vivṛṇoti so'pyana gatairvajra- pāṇibhiḥ sākṣānmahāvajradhara ityādaramutpādya pūjya ityayamartho darśitaḥ |
amumevārthaṃ dyotayannāha śrīmāyājālamahātantra rāje ācāryaḥ kathaṃ draṣṭavyaḥ sarvabuddhasamo guruḥ |
sarvavajradharoṃ rājā ratnaketustathāgataḥ ||
ācāryasya guṇā grāhyā doṣāśca na kadācana |
guṇagrahaṇaṃ siddhiḥ syād doṣāṇāṃ siddhihānita: ||
tasmāt sarvaprayatnena buddhatvaphalakāṃkṣibhiḥ |
pūjyo'sau bhaktimatā sarvairacintyaphaladāyakaḥ ||
iti |
tathāha mahāmahāyānaratnarājasūtre bhagavān vajrapāṇiḥ 'oḍḍiyānaparvate niṣaṇṇaḥ sarvāṃśca vajrayāna śikṣitānā- mantrayāmāsa |
śṛṇuta nikhilavajrayāna śikṣitāḥ tathāgataṃ parinirvṛttaṃ na paśyata śāstāram api tu vajrācāryo vajraguruḥ |
so'yaṃ śāstā bhavatīti ||
ata āha lākṣībhaṭṭārikāyāṃ kalyāṇamitramupanisṛtya buddhabodhimiti ||
athetyādi |
bodhisattvasaṃspandanakāle prabhāsvarātmako bhūtvā tadanantaraṃ vajrapāṇiḥ tathāgato'kṣobhyaḥ guhyavajra stathāgatāḥ te samādhīyante vilīyante saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭ koṭivyākhyā yasmin guhyavajrasamādhiḥ bhūtakoṭiḥ tasmād vyutthāya punaḥ pūrvavat dveṣa- vajro bhūtvā tathāgatādīn śṛṇvantvityādinā āmantrya samādhimaṇḍalaṃ deśita - vān |
sarvatathāgato niṣpannakramārūḍho mahāyogī |
tasya bodhiścittam |
sa saṃbhava utpattisthānaṃ yasya maṇḍalasya tannāmakamavinaśvaraṃ samādhimaṇḍalaṃ mānasa- maṇḍalaṃ śrotumāmantritavān ||
bhagavadvacanena santuṣṭāḥ buddhabodhisattvāḥ yathā bhagavantaṃ vacanenādhyepita- vantaḥ taddarśayannāha atha khatvityādi |
tat śrutvā mahāvajradharaḥ prāha khadhātvityādi |
khadhāturākāśadhātuḥ madhyagataṃ yasmin maṇḍale caturasramiti catasro'srāḥ pṛthivyādisvabhāvā locanādayo yasmin taṃ caturasraṃ kāyamaṇḍalaṃ suśobhanamaprākṛtaṃ cintet dhyāyāt |
buddhamaṇḍalayogeneti buddhamaṇḍalaṃ paśñcaguhyatattvātmakametat śarīrameva |
tadātmako yogaḥ samādhiḥ dhyānavajramiti dhyāyate cintyate yogibhiriti dhyānamakṣobhyaḥ ṣaṣṭhena yojyamānatvāt vajraḥ taṃ pracodayet |
utsṛjyānupraveśyātmanā kṣīro- dakavadekīkuryāditi yāvat ||
vajramaṇḍalamakṣobhyamaṇḍalaṃ tasmin devatāvinyāsakramo dhyānaṃ tena vajra- maṇḍaladhyānena sarvacakriṇāṃ māṇḍaleyānāmāsanasthāpanaṃ kāryam
evaṃ sādhāraṇa- kāyamaṇḍalaṃ vibhāvya karaṇīyamāha pūjāmityādi |
tenaiva vidhinā citta-maṇḍalavidhinaiva matimān svamudrāsahitāḥ pūjāṃ samāpattilakṣaṇāṃ sadā kurvīta anutiṣṭhet |
samārabdhapūjā evābhiṣekaṃ śiṣyasya kuryādityāha ācāryamityādi |
svacaryācāriṇaḥ svapadaṃ pātayati prāpayatīti ācāryo mahāvajradharaḥ taṃ hṛdaye hṛtpuṇḍarīke yathāvat dhyātvābhiṣekaṃ mānasaṃ samācaret kuryāt |
khadhātumityādi |
khadhātuṃ dharmodayaṃ sarvabuddhaiścittamaṇḍalavat saṃsthitaiḥ paripūrṇaṃ vibhāvya tanmadhye'bhi- ṣicyamānaṃ śiṣyaṃ vibhāvayet dhyāyāt ||
tadanantaraṃ sarvān vairocanādīn buddhavodhisattvān tribhirabhiṣekapadaiḥ omityādyakṣaratritayaiḥ prāṇāyāmasthitaiḥ pātayet śiṣyaśirasi bodhicitta- vajratvamāpannān pātayet patitān dhyāyāt |
abhiṣekānuśaṃsāmāha anenetyādi |
anenābhiṣekakaraṇenābhiṣiktaḥ śiṣyaḥ sarvasattvānugrahayogyān bodhi pratiṣṭhita- nirvāṇameti parārthe saṃpat tasyaiva dṛṣṭadharme sarvasiddhimahādbhutamiti |
sarva- guhyasamājapradīpodyotana siddhayo daśabalādayo buddhaguṇāḥ tairmahādbhutamatiśayaprāptaṃ kāyavākcittaṃ sidhyate siddhimupagacchatīti svārthasaṃpat |
sarvabuddhabodhisattvāḥ vairocanādayaḥ kṣitigarbhādayaśca yathāvat samaya- mekībhāvaṃ gacchanti yasmin dhyānenaiva yanniṣpādyate prabuddhajanābhiṣekārthaṃ tadabhedyaṃ dhyānamaṇḍalamiti ||
punarapi duṣṭanigrahapāpaprakāramākṣipannāha athetyādi |
vajrapāṇirdeśaka: punarapīti trayodaśacaturdaśapaṭaloktakrūrakarmābhiratasya saṃbandhaṃ darśayati sarva- tathāgatayogavajjramiti sarve sādhujanāḥ tathatāṃ pañcatāṃ yairgacchanti sarvatathāgatāḥ krūrāśayāḥ teṣāṃ yogavajraṃ nigrahopāyo yasmin tat kāyavākcittaṃ guhyaṃ kāyādiniṣpādakamakṣaratritayaṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayet ||
|| hūṃ hrīḥ khaṃ ||
khadhātvityādi |
tāvat sādhakaḥ svakāyamasthimāṃsasnāyumayaṃ dṛṣṭvā khadhātumadhyagataṃ prabhāsvarapraviṣṭaṃ cintet |
tatastrikāyavākcittahṛdayamiti trividhamanantaroktaṃ kāyavākcittavījātmakamakṣaratritayaṃ vajrasattvasya mahāvala- parākramasya vajraḍākasya niṣpattyarthaṃ bhāvayet |
tataḥ krūraṃ duṣṭāsṛgbhakṣaṇatatparaṃ vikṛtaṃ saṃkruddha nīlotpalasamaprabhaṃ caturbhuja- vidhāneneti vakṣyamāṇe [na] prakaraṇavidhinā bhāvayet |
evaṃ vibhāvitaḍākarūpo mantrī tataḥ [kiṃ ] kuryādityāha paścaraśmītyādi |
pañcaraśmayaḥ yaralavahā iti vāyvanalādipañcavījāni teṣāṃ prabhāḥ kṛṣṇādi- raśmayaḥ tairupadyotitāṃ dedīpyamānāṃ svajihvāṃ pañcasūcikavajrākārāṃ vratī vibhāva- yet |
dhyānavajraprayogeṇeti |
anantaroktakrameṇa vibhāvitajihvāvajraṃ taddhyāna- vajram ||
tasya prayogaḥ ||
bījapañcakamuccārya pañcaraśmīn sādhyahṛdayaṃ praveśya ūlūkavat sarvāṅgagatarudhiramākṛṣya taṃ tvagasthiśeṣaṃ dṛṣṭvā raśmīnupahṛtya
svadehe praveśyātmani niṣiktaṃ dhyāyāditi rudhirākarṣaṇamuttamamiti
anenaiva vajrayogena hastasthapraharaṇakathanadvāreṇa prayogāntaramākṣipyāha triśūlamityādi
dakṣiṇe prathame triśūlaṃ dvitīye dāruṇottamamiti dāruṇaṃ kīlakamekaśūkavatraṃ prathame kapālamuktameva vajrayogena vajraḍākayogenaiva sādhaka: saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā buddhakāyamapi tathāgataśarīramapi ebhiḥ praharaṇaividhvā pīḍayet
kiṃ punaḥ prākṛtaśarīramiti prayogātiśayaḥ
athetyādi
punarapīti pūrvoktabhakṣaṇasamayasya pūrvoktakān smārayati
vajrāḥ sādhakāḥ teṣāmāhārasamayo bhojana kāyaḥ tasmin kṛtyamanu [ti]ṣṭhet
yat tat svakāyādibhyo niścārayāmāsa
annaṃ śālyodanādikaṃ pānaṃ kṣīrādikaṃ vratī trividho'pi sādhakaḥ bhakṣayet pived vā tat sarvaṃ viṇmūmāṃsayogena vidhivat āhāraṃ hṛtkaṇṭhaśodhanapūrvaka- mupayuktasyānnapānasya rasamāṃsādisvabhāvena pariṇataṃ dhyāyāt
avaśiṣṭa viṇmūtrabhāvena parikalpayet
prayamādhyātmiko homaḥ
athetyādi
sarvatathāgatā vairocanādisvabhāvāḥ sarvasattvāḥ pañcabuddhātma- kuṃ sarvajayamiti vacanāt
teṣāṃ kāyavākcittapūjāgrayaṃ pūjāviśeṣaṃ svakāyādibhyo niścārayet
pañcopahārāḥ pañca viṣayāḥ teṣāṃ pūjāgraiḥ manojñaviṣayaiḥ pūjyamātmānaṃ caitāṃśca parān prapūjayet
eṣa ayaṃ vidhiḥ sarvavajrāṇāṃ sarvayogibhiḥ dura- tikramo'laṅghanīyaḥ samayaḥ
evaṃ sādhāraṇapūjāmuktvā viśeṣapūjā mākṣipyāha athetyādi
sarvatathāgatā anantaroktāḥ eṣāṃ kāyādipūjārahasyaṃ guhyaṃ niścārayet
dvayendriyaprayogeṇa vajrapadmasamāyogena svaśukrādiparigrahaiḥ svacetomahārajasā kalalādibhāvena santatya putrapautraprabhṛtīn vidhivat rakṣaṇapoṣaṇādividhibhiḥ pūjayet saṃgṛhṇīyāt
evaṃ kṛtvā buddhavodhimavāpnuyāditi
evaṃ svaparapūjāmuktvā niravaśeṣasya sattvadhātoḥ pūjāmā kṣipyāha athetyādi
yathā janmakāle āgatāḥ vināśe tathaiva gatā iti tathāgatāḥ sarvasattvāḥ
kāyavākcittasaṃvaramiti
teṣāṃ yogināṃ saṃvaraṇamanugrahaḥ
taṃ bhagavān svakāyā- dibhirniścāritavān
anantasya sattvadhātoḥ aṇḍajādeḥ sattvarāśeḥ mātāṃ locanām
kāsau ?
pṛthivī
kīdṛśīmityāha sattvānāmutpādasthitibhaṅgalakṣaṇāstisro'vasthāḥ samayaḥ tāṃ dhārayatīti samayadhāriṇī tāṃ samayadhāriṇīm aśeṣasattvadhātusahitāṃ pṛthivīṃ kāmayet
trivajrasamayairiti
ātmanaḥ śubhaiḥ kāyavākcittavyāpāraiḥ hitasukhopasaṃhitaiḥ kāmayet ramayet anugṛhṇīyāt
ayaṃ samayaḥ sāmājikānāṃ mahādbhutaḥ mahataḥ sattvarāśeḥ parigrahāt viśiṣṭataraḥ
evaṃ pūjāprakāramuktvā tadviśuddhi darśayannāha athetyādi
bodhyupakaraṇatvāda- nantaroktāḥ pūjāvidhayaḥ sarvasādhanāni teṣāṃ samādānaṃ saṃvaraḥ tasya sāraḥ vajraḥ taṃ niścārayet
pūjāvidhiṣu yaḥ kāyādivyāpāraḥ kāyavākcittasaṃbhogaḥ taṃ triguhyālaya- vajrajaṃ prabhāsvarasamutthitamadhimuñcediti śeṣaḥ
evamadhimucya kiṃ kuryādityāha sādhayāmyahamiti
bhadraṃ buddhatvaṃ vajradharatvaṃ sādhayāmi prāpnomīti dāḍharca- mutpādayet
yadyevaṃ na kuryāt tataḥ kimityāha nāśayedityādi sarvān bodhi- saṃbhārān niṣphalīkuryāt sopalambhatvāt
evaṃ yoginaḥ sattvaviṣaye pravṛtti pradarśya punarapyātmanaḥ śarīrāpyāyanaṃ darśayannāha athetyādi
sarvabuddhatvādikaṃ sādhayatyaneneti sarvasādhakasvaśarīraṃ tasya vajrasattvasaṃvaraṃ vajjrasattvasaṃpādanopāyo vajrasattvasaṃvaraḥ taṃ niścārayet
vitastimātramityādi
svaśirasyuparigagane vakāraparāvṛttisaṃjñājātasya candramaṇḍalasya kalpanāṃ bhāvanāṃ tāvat kuryāt
tataḥ oṅkāraṃ praveśātmakaṃ candramaṇḍalamadhyagataṃ dhyātvā pañcāmṛtanipātanaṃ kāryam
na mriyanta ityamṛtāḥ pañcāmṛtāstathāgatāḥ teṣāṃ nipātanaṃ pañcavaraśmisvabhāvena mūrdhna āpādatalāda- bhiṣecanaṃ kāryam
anenoktalakṣaṇena vajjrayogena tathāgatālamvanena tejasvī vapuṣmān jāyate kṣaṇādacirāt
kāyādisīsthityaṃ cāpnotītyanuśaṃsā
niṣpannakramādhigatasya mahāmudrāsādhanapraviṣṭasya pravṛttimāha athetyādi
sarve mantrā rahasyā yasmin sa sarvamantraḥ samājaḥ taṃ dhārayatīti sarvamantradharaḥ sāmājiko mahāyogī tasya kāyavākcittaguhyaṃ kāyādipravṛttirahasyaṃ svakāyā- dibhyo niścārayet
caityakarma mṛdvālukādistūpapūjākriyāṃ na kurvīta ātmana eva sarvatathāgatā- tmakasya caityapūjābhiniveśāyogāt [i] na ca pustakavācanamiti anābhogenaiva vajrajāpaṃ satatameva pravartayataḥ anyasmin svādhyāyādau pravṛtterna yuktatvāt
evaṃ vāhyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tanna kurvīta
svakāyamaṇḍalamuktvā vahirmaṇḍalasya tattvato- saptaṃ daśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā 'bhāvāt
trivajrāgrāḥ śrāvakapratyekabuddhasamyaksaṃbuddhāḥ teṣāṃ kāyādinā praśamanaṃ vandanaṃ tanna kuryāt
ātmanaḥ sarvatathāgatabhāvasya virodhāt
etat sarvaṃ lokaprajñaptimāgamya kartavyam, na virudhyata evānabhiniveśata iti pañcadaśapaṭaloktaṃ kṣudrakarmaśeṣamākṣipannāha athetyādi
sarvaviṣaṃ sthāvara- jaṅgamaṃ laghuprakāraṃ tasyāpaharaṇādikaṃ kāyādibhyo niścārayet
tadyathā cakra- madhyagatamityādi viṣābhibhūtaṃ cakramadhyagataṃ sitapraṇavodbhavāṣṭāracakranābhisthaṃ śītāṃśuraśmigahanaṃ pītavarṇairviṣatejobhistāvad vyāptadehaṃ sitacakreṇaiva śirasi sthagitaṃ tadraśminivahaiḥ śanaiḥ śanairapanīyamānapītāvabhāsaṃ vicintya nirviṣaṃ kuryāditi viṣāpaharaṇam
pītasannibhamiti
etatsarvaṃ stambhanābhiprāyeṇa pītavarṇaṃ dhyāyāditi viṣastambhanam
etat sarvaṃ dhūmravarṇaṃ dhyātvā yacchati tattra saṃkrāmayediti viṣāpakarṣaṇamuktaṃ bhavati
trivajjraraśmisamayaiḥ sitapītadhūmraraśmiprayogaiḥ vījo'yaṃ oṅkāraḥ guhyasaṃbhavaḥ viṣāpaharaṇāditattvasthito dhyeyaḥ
evaṃ viṣāpaharaṇādiprayuktamantriṇo rakṣārthamāha athetyādi
kāyavā- kcittarakṣārthaṃ cakraṃ tasya mantravajraṃ mantrasāraṃ kāyādibhyo niścārayet
oṅkārādiphaṭkārāvasāniko nirantākhyo mantraḥ bhūrjapatrādipu cakramaṣṭāraṃ karmavajrapratiṣṭhitaṃ viśvavajravaraṭakasthitamālikhya phaṭakāramadhyagataṃ kṛtveti cakranābhigataṃ phaṭkāramālikhya tanmadhye rakṣyanāma samālikhet
mantrākṣarapadaiḥ omityādikaiḥ samyagānupūrvyā maṇḍitamārasandhiṣu śobhitaṃ sadā sarvakālaṃ karaṇḍakādau saṃsthāpya pūjayet
yoṃ prathamāre

amṛte saptame
[hūṃ] phaṭ aṣṭame're iti mantrākṣaravinyāsaḥ
evaṃ rakṣācakravinyāsaḥ
sarvamantrāṇāṃ sādhakānāṃ viguhyaṃ kāyavākcittaṃ tasyālayaḥ samuccayaḥ rakṣāsamūha iti
idānīṃ karmāvasāne vajrajñānaprayogaṃ darśayannāha athetyādi
vajrāñjanaṃ vaśyādiṣu visaṃvādakatvāt tadeva sarvasaṃpadāmāspadatvāt padaṃ pratiṣṭhā, taṃ niścārayet
sādhakasthānaṃ catuṣpathādikaṃ prasiddhaṃ vajrāñjanapadamityādikasyāyamupadeśaḥ pañcāmṛtabhāvitārkaṃpadmasūtreṇa kṛtvā kapālacullīmadhyasthavasāpūrṇa- vartitaṃ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane kapālamadhyagataṃ saṃsthāpya tadupari surāparipūrṇakapālaṃ saṃsthāpya anantarokta- cakramantramaṣṭottaraśataṃ japet
bhūtadine'rdharātrau kapālāsanasthaḥ sādhakaḥ ājñā- cakroktavidhinā ca rakṣāṃ kṛtvā kajjalaṃ pātayediti vajrāñjanapadavidhiḥ
tataḥ kajjalamādāya karaṇḍake saṃsthāpya tameva mantramaṣṭottaraśatavārānāvartya japet
trividhā siddhiruttamamadhyamādhamāḥ
tattrāntardhānamuttamā
bhūgatanidhāna- darśanaṃ madhyamā
vaśīkaraṇaṃ [adhamā] siddhirityāha bhagavān samantabhadraḥ
atha saṃgītikārastantramāhātmyamudbhāvayannāha
atha bhagavantaḥ sarva- tathāgatāḥ vajrapāṇi sarvatathāgatādhipatimevamāhuḥ
katibhirbhagavan guhyākṣaraiḥ samanvāgatāḥ te bodhisattvā mahāsattvā [ya] idaṃ sarvatathāgataguhyasamayaṃ śraddhāsyanti bhāvayiṣyanti ceti bhagavantaṃ pṛṣṭavantaḥ
atha vajrapāṇiḥ tān sarvatathāgatānevamāha triguhyākṣaraiḥ bhagavantaḥ sarva tathāgatāḥ te bodhisattvā ya idaṃ sarvatathāgatavodhicaryāvajraṃ śraddhāsyanti bhāvayiṣyanti ceti parihāraḥ
sarvatathāgatāḥ prāhuḥ katamaistribhiriti punaścodyam
vajradharaḥ prāha sarvatathāgatakāyavajreṇa sarvatathāgatavāgvajreṇa sarvatathāgata- cittavajreṇa ebhistribhiriti parihāraḥ
atha te sarvatathāgatā bhagavato vajrapāṇeḥ pādayoḥ praṇipatya tūṣṇīṃ sthitā abhūvanniti
evaṃ vigatasandehānāṃ tathāgatānāṃ bhūyo'pi tantrasya duṣprāpyatvakhyāpanena prasādābhivṛddhyarthaṃ tathāgatā anyathā mantrayanti taddarśayannāha atha vajrapāṇiḥ sarvatathāgatādhipatiḥ tān sarvatathāgatān bodhisattvāṃścāmantrayate sma
bhūtapūrvaṃ bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ anabhilāpyānabhilāpyabuddhakṣetraparamāṇurajaḥ samāḥ kalpāḥ kṣīṇā yāvat bhagavato dīpaṅkarasya tathāgatasyārhataḥ samyaksaṃbuddhasyāpyati- krāntasya kāśyapasyāpi mahāmunerabhisaṃbuddhasya na bhāṣitam
tat kasmāt hetoḥ ?
abhavyā bhagavantaḥ sattvāḥ asya mahāguhyapadārthasya tena kālena tena samayena na bhāṣitamiti bhūtapūrvamityādinā atītavṛttakamāhṛtya kāśyapatathāgate parinirvṛterapravṛtti pradarśitavān
tat kasmāt hetorityādinā
kimarthaṃ na bhāṣitam ?
atra kāraṇamāha tāvati kāle sattvāḥ hīnādhi- muktikāḥ dānaśīlādivirāgadharmavineyā gambhīradeśanāyāmuttrastasaṃjñinaḥ ityato bhavyāḥ
tasmin kāle mahāguhyapadārthaṃ na prakāśitavān
asmin kāle rāgacaritānāṃ pañcakaṣāyoddhutānāṃ sattvamadhimuktimupalakṣyāgatya tathāgatena kevalaṃ deśitam
api tu buddhatvamanena prāpyata iti kepāṃcidāśaṅkā syāt
ataḥ tannirāsāyāha api tu bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ asmin guhyasamāje buddha- bodhi kṣaṇalavamuhūrteṇaiva niṣpādayanti
yadanekaiḥ gaṅgānadīvālukāsamaiḥ kalpaiḥ ghaṭayanto vyāyacchanto bodhisattvāḥ bodhi na prāpnuvanti, tadihaiva janmani guhya- samājābhirato bodhisattvaḥ sarvatathāgatānāṃ buddha iti saṃkhyāṃ gacchati
buddha- bodhiḥ kṣaṇalavāḥ muhūrteṇaiva niṣpādayatītyayamarthaḥ
adhyātmābhisaṃvodhikrameṇa paramārthasatyaṃ praviśya sakalakalimalaṃ prakṣālya kṣaṇalavamuhūrttanimeṣamātreṇa svādhiṣṭhānakrameṇa vyutthāya sarvasaṃpatsaṃvandhāgato bhavati
asmin śrīguhyasamāje adhigatatattvānāṃ dṛṣṭadharma eveha janmani buddhatvaprāpti śrutvā tantraṃ granthataḥ sākalyena śrutvāpi ṣaṭkoṭipadavibhāgena tantrārthe asaṃtyaktamohāścandanādayaḥ śrotāro na āttamanaso vabhūvuḥ tad darśayannāha atha te bodhisattvā idaṃ vāgvajrākṣarapadaṃ śrutvā prarodayāmāsuriti
evaṃ duḥkhitamanaskān mṛdvindriyān praruditān dṛṣṭvā ratnapudgalā ubhayatra viśāradā yathāśvāsayāmāsuḥ tad darśayannāha atha te sarvatathāgatāḥ tān vodhi- sattvānevamāhuḥ
mā bhagavanto bodhisattvāḥ prarodata mā ca triduḥkhaṃ bhāvayata
evaṃ ratnapudgalaiḥ samāśvastāścandanādaya ātmano duḥkhakāraṇaṃ yathā nivedayanti, tad darśayannāha atha te mahābodhisattvāḥ tān sarvatathāgatānevamāhuḥ
kathaṃ bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ na prarodāmahe ?
kathaṃ triduḥkhaṃ na prabhāvayāmahe ?
tat kasya hetoḥ ?
suabhavyā bhagavantaḥ triguhyākṣare'ntagaḥ taddarśanamaśravaṇenāpīti triduḥkhamiti
duḥkhaduḥkhaṃ vipariṇāmaduḥkhaṃ saṃskṛtaduḥkhaṃ ca
atha vā kāyavāṅa- mānasaṃ duḥkhaṃ triguhyākṣara iti kāyavākcittaṃ triguhyaṃ akṣarārthaṃ tyakṣaraṃ tiguhyaṃ samastāṅgaṃ tvaritavivandhavāspakaṃ triguhyakam
garbhī
prajñopāyopalabdhi triguhyam kolikaṃ
etāni viguhyākṣarāṇi
evaṃ viśuddhi dyotayannāha sarvatathāgatāḥ prāhuḥ sāmānyākṣaraṃ kulaputtrāḥ yathā bhavadbhirna jñātaṃ na śrutaṃ tathāsmābhirapi sarvatathāgataiḥ sarvabodhisattvaiśca kulaputrāḥ yatte guhyākṣaraṃ na prāptāḥ nābhisaṃbuddhāḥ, tat kasya hetoḥ ?
triguhyā- kṣaraviśuddhatvāt
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane atha te sarvabodhisattvāḥ tūṣṇīmabhūvanniti bhavadbhiryathā na jñātaṃ na śrutamiti yat tadasmākaṃ sarvatathāgatabodhisattvānāṃ ca sādhāraṇametat
kimarthaṃ tadguhyā- kṣaraṃ na prāptāḥ grāhyābhāvena nābhisaṃbuddhāḥ grāhakābhāvena
tat kasmāt hetoḥ ?
guhyākṣaraviśuddhatvāt
guhyākṣaraṃ prabhāsvaraḥ tenaḥ viśodhitatvāt tathāgataiḥ tattvamuddīpitam
evamudghaṭitajñaiḥ ratnapudgalaiḥ apanītasandehāḥ stimitaprajñāścandanādayaḥ tattvamadhigamya prasrabdhāḥ yamavasthāviśeṣaṃ prāptāḥ tad darśayannāha atha te bodhi- sattvāḥ tūṣṇīmabhūvanniti
nāsandhyam
evaṃ tathāgatavacanena vigatasandehānāṃ bodhisattvānāṃ tatphalapradarśanāya pañcatathāgatāḥ paṣṭhātmakāḥ yathā vabhūvuḥ taddarśayannāha atha bhagavantaḥ sarva- tathāgatāḥ sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittavajrayoṣidbhageṣu vijahāreti vibhaktārthaḥ
evaṃ prabhāsvarātmakaṃ mahāvajradharaṃ catasro dayitāḥ tadvirahotkaṇṭhitamanasaḥ darśanakāṃkṣiṇyaḥ sakaruṇaṃ yathā pralapanti tad darśayannāha atha sarvatathāgatakāya- vākcittadayitā bhagavantaṃ sarvatathāgatādhipati mahāvajradharaṃ prītyā stunuyāmā- seti
sarvatathāgato vairocanaḥ
tasya kāyavākcittadayitā iṣṭā locanā prītyā saṃgama bhilāṣeṇa stunuyāmāsa āṣāḍhavādyaiḥ kākalilalitamadhurasvaraiḥ stavapūrvakaṃ codayāmāsa tvamiti
purorvārtanamivotprekṣyābhilapati vajracitteti cittavaśitāprāpte dvaidhātukadharmarājyaprāptatvāt bhuvaneśvaraḥ sattvadhātuḥsarvabhūtātmakatvāt
ratiḥ krīḍā
tasmāt manojñaḥ āhlādajananāt ratimanojñaḥ mahārthaḥ paramānandaḥ taṃ kāmayanti niṣpādayantīti mahārthakāmāḥ īdvagvidhaiḥ ratimanojñamahārthakāmaiḥ āliṅganādi- bhirmāṃ trāyāhi ramasva
sarvotpattihetubhūtatvāt janakaḥ sattvānāṃ hitasukhakaraṇāt sattvamahāgravandhuḥ mahyaṃ mama jīvitaṃ nātha svāmin, kāmāhi ramasva
punarmāmakīprabodhaṃ darśayitumāha
athetyādi
sarvatathāgatacittamakṣobhyaḥ tasya dayitā āryamāmakī
vajrakāmaratipūjāgraṃ aprākṛtaviśiṣṭasuratapravṛttiḥ
tatsūcakairakṣarapadaiḥ stunuyāmāsa
avinaśvarakāyatvāt vajrakāyaḥ vahusattvapriyājñacakra iti tvaṃ vahusattva- hitānukārī ājñācakrapravartakatvāt
buddhārtha bodhiparamārtha hitānudarśīti
buddhāḥ pañcaskandhāḥ teṣāmarthaḥ saṃvṛtisatyaṃ vodhiḥ paramārthaḥ tasya satyantayoḥ saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā saṃyogajamahāsukhamūrtiḥ tat sattvānāṃ hitāśayena prakāśanāt hitānudarśī
rāgeṇa rāgaviśodhanākāreṇa rāgasamayāṃ rāgaracitasvabhāvāṃ māṃ kāmayasva ramayasva athetyādi
lokeśvaro'mitābhaḥ tasya kāyavākcittanetrī pāṇḍaravāsinī mahāvajradharaṃ codayati tvaṃ vajravāceti
vāgaiśvaryaprāptatvāt sakalasya sattva- dhātoḥ hitānukampītyatiśayakṛpātmakaḥ ataeva caturbrahmavihāreṇa sattvānāmartha- karaṇāya sadā sarvakālaṃ samyakpravṛttaḥ
kāmāhi māṃ bhaja suratacaryayā
samantasya sattvadhātoḥ bhadraṃ kalyāṇamāvahatīti samantabhadraḥ
anyad vibhaktam
sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittavajjo'moghasiddhiḥ
tasya dayitā bhāryā tārā mahāvajradharaṃ ṣaṣṭyuttaraśataprakṛtipracāraṃ niruddhya jñānanidrayāṃ sarvaśūnyagataṃ dṛṣṭvā prabodhayati tvaṃ vajrakāmeti
amoghasiddhidvāreṇa mantrayati vajrapadma- samāyogāt
utsṛṣṭamāṇḍaleyadevatābhiḥ dveṣādicaritasattvadhātuviśodhanāt
vajrakāmaḥ karmakulādhipatitvāt
samayāgraḥ rāgacaritasattvānāṃ rāgaviśuddhayapāyena mokṣapradānāt mahāhitārthaḥ
śākyavaṃśotpannānāṃ tilakabhūtatvāt saṃbuddhavaṃśa- tilakaḥ
athavā kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānasvabhāvena sakalajagadarthakaraṇatvāt saṃbuddha- vaṃśatilakaḥ
svaparavibhāgopalambharahitatve'pi sattveṣu anukampakatvāt samatā-
nukampī |
evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭo guṇanidhiṃ vahuratnabhūtāṃ māṃ kāmāhi bhajasva |
yasmāt rāgavirāgamadhyarāgaprabhāsvarajñānotpādikeyamiti ātmānaṃ praśaṃsayati yadīcchase jīvitaṃ mañjunātheti |
rāgadveṣamohaviśuddhayapāyena vajrasattvāyurjī- vitadānaiḥ vijayasvetyabhiprāyaḥ |
evaṃ caturbrahmavihārasvabhāvairdevībhiḥ saṃcodyamānaḥ pūrvapraṇidhānāpravandha- sāmarthyāt śūnyatāyogāt vyutthāya śrīsuratasukharāgacaritapradarśanāya āliṅganacumvanabhūṣaṇapulakatā ḍanadaṃśananakhadānamardanadolāsūcīgrāmyādikaraṇamo- hanatayā pracalitamuktāhāravalayakaṭakakeyūranūpuraravaviṣayendriyavajrapadmasamāyoga- prajñopāyasamāpattyālikālimadanāt yatra kūrmakaṇṭhapūrṇaśaśāṅkanāḍītrayatyaṅgulica- lanāpracodanena ratimāyādhvaninā paramānandasukhamāsvādya skandhadhātvāyatanasvabhā- vān sarvatathāgatān mastakādārabhya nirjharadhārākāreṇa drāvayitvā sravati bodhi- cittasvabhāvena dharmodayamadhye saṃsthāpya tena dveṣādicaritasattvaṃ viśodhanāya maṇḍala- cakramutsṛjya punarapi prabhāsvaraṃ praviśya nirvikalpatāṃ gataḥ |
guhyasamājapradīpodyotane imamarthameva dyotayannāha atha bhagavān vajrapāṇistathāgataḥ sarvakāmopa- bhogaśriyaṃ nāma samādhi samāpannaḥ tāṃ sarvatathāgatadayitāṃ samayacakreṇa kāmayan tūṣṇīmabhūditi |
sarvakāmā rūpādayo viṣayāḥ teṣāmupabhogaḥ svabhāvaparijñānena cakṣurādibhiḥ saṃpuṭīkaraṇaṃ tasya śrīsamatānandasukhenāvasthānaṃ yasmin tannāmakaṃ samādhiṃ samāpannaḥ prāptaḥ san tathāgatādhipatiḥ tāṃ sarvatathāgatadayitāṃ locanā- disvarūpāṃ samayacakreṇa virocanādisvabhāvena kāmayan ramayan tūṣṇīmabhūt deśanādivyāpārarahitaḥ jñānamudrayā saha samāpattyā sthitavāniti yāvat |
atha bhagavantaḥ jñānamudrāsamāpattyanantaram ayaṃ sarvākāśadhātuḥ sarvatathāgata- kāyavākcittasamayacakreṇa paripūrṇavajrodakakumbha iva sthito'bhūditi |
ayaṃ gaganakuharaḥ vajradhārātmakaḥ trivajjrasaṃyuktavairocanāditathāgatasamūhena paripūrṇaḥ tilaśimvikāvannirantara san vajrodakakumbha iva paramānandakalaśavat sthitaḥ niścalo'bhūdityarthaḥ |
evaṃ bhagavati samāpanne sattvārthakṛtyarthaṃ darśayannāha athāsmin vajrākāśa- dhātau ye sattvāḥ trikāyasaṃbhūtāḥ te vajraśriyeṇa spṛṣṭāḥ sarve te tathāgatāṃ arhantaḥ samyaksaṃbuddhāḥ trivajrajñānino'bhūvanniti |
asyāyamarthaḥ |
vajrākāśa- dhātau tribhuvane ye sattvāḥ trikāyasaṃbhūtāḥ trivajjrasvabhāvā vajraśriyeṇa mahāvajra dharasaukhyena spṛṣṭā viṣayīkṛtāḥ sarve tathāgatatvaṃ prāptāḥ trivajro mahāvajradharaḥ tasya jñānāni daśavalavaiśāradyādīni anubhavanti sma |
tataḥ prabhṛtīti |
vajraṃ raśmyabhiṣekādārabhya sarvatathāgatakāyavākcittaira- bhiṣiktāḥ santaḥ samantabhadrāḥ samanta[to] bhadrā ityabhūtārthanāmakā abhūvan |
evaṃ sarvasattvān jñānābhiṣekeṇābhiṣicya sattvasamatāpradarśanena yathābhilaṣati tad darśayannāha atha vajrapāṇistathāgata: tān sarvatathāgatānevamāha dṛṣṭā bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ sarvadharmasamatāḥ atha te sarvatathāgatā vajrapāṇi sarvatathāgatādhipatimevamāhuḥ dṛṣṭā bhagavan dṛṣṭā sugata sarvatathāgatavajjrajñāna- caryeti |
ana sarvatathāgato mahāvajradharaḥ tasya vajrajñānacaryā svasamāpattisukhānu- bhavanenaiva sarvasattvānāṃ samāpattisukhāṅgīkaraṇamiti |
evaṃ bhagavato vajrajñānacaryā anubhūya punaḥ samāpattervyutthāya yathā prahṛṣṭa- manaso bhagavantamabhilapanti taddarśayannāha bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatāḥ yoṣidbhageṣva- saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā bhiniṣkramya bhagavantaṃ mahāvajrapāṇi sarvatathāgatādhipatimevamāhuḥ āścaryaṃ bhagavan, āścaryaṃ sugata yatra hi nāma rāgākṣarapadairbuddhabodhiranugantavyeti |
yatra hi nāma yasmāt kāraṇāt rāgākṣarapadaiḥ rāgacaritaiḥ sarvairbuddhavodhiḥ mahāvajradharatānugantavyā prāptavyeti |
• .. yadā tadāścaryamiti nāmāścaryavacanaṃ śrutvā teṣāṃ rāgādīnāṃ viśuddhipradarśanena saṃdehaṃ karoti, tatpradarśanāyāha atha vajrapāṇiḥ sarvatathāgatādhipatiḥ tān sarvatathāgatānevamāha [ mā] bhagavantaḥ sarvatathāgatā evaṃ vadata |
tat kasmāt hetoḥ ?
khavajrasamaya tulyatvāt sarvadharmāṇāṃ na rūpaskandho na vedanāskandho na dhāturnāyatanaṃ na rāgo na dveṣo na moho na dharmo nādharma iti |
khavajrasamayaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ tathāgataḥ tattulyatvāt sarvadharmāṇām |
mā evaṃ vadatha iti niḥsaṃdehakaraṇam |
na rūpaskandha ityādināmumevārthaṃ prapa- cayati |
atha te sarvatathāgatāḥ tūṣṇīmabhūvanniti |
tathāgatoktaṃ sarvadharma- prabhāsvaratvaṃ vacanasamanantarameva sākṣāt kṛtvā tāmevānabhilāpyadharmatāṃ dhyāyantaḥ sthitā abhūvanniti vajrapadasyārthaḥ |
evaṃ guhyadeśanāṃ parisamāpya tadvineyasattvān tathāgateṣu yathā parīndayati taddarśayannāha atha bhagavān vajrapāṇiḥ tān sarvatathāgatān bodhisattvāṃścā- mantrayate sma |
ārocayantu bhagavantaḥ sarvalokadhātuṣvidaṃ sarvatathāgatakāyavā- kcittaguhyam |
tat kasmāt hetoḥ ?
bhavyā ime daśadiksaṃsthitā bodhisattvā asya dharmaparyāyasyeti |
ārocayantu prakāśayantu sarvatathāgatakāyavākcitta- vajraguhyāḥ |
dvividhamahāvajradharasamādhiḥ tatprakāśakaḥ tantrarājaśca asya dharmaparyāyasyedānīṃ bhavyatvaṃ rāgajavodhipadaprāpterbhājanatvāt |
evaṃ viṣayāsaktānāṃ sattvānāmasya tantrasya pātratvaṃ pradarśya anāgata- sattvānāṃ saṃprakāśanārthaṃ vajradharme dharmatantraṃ samarpayannāha atha vajrapāṇiḥ sarva- tathāgatādhipatiḥ vajradharmamāmantrayate sma |
udgṛhāṇa kulaputra imaṃ sarvatathāgata- samayatattvam |
tvaṃ hi sarvatathāgataiḥ sarvadharmeśvara ityabhiṣiktaḥ |
atha vajradharmo bodhisattvo mahāsattva evamastviti kṛtvā tūṣṇīmabhūditi |
asyāyamarthaḥ |
imaṃ samājanayaṃ sarvatathāgatasamayatatvaṃ sarvatathāgataparamparā- yātaṃ samayamalaṅghanīyaṃ tattvaṃ kāyavākcittarahasyam idānīṃ mayā prakāśitaṃ tvamudgṛhāṇa manasi sthāpaya, yataḥ sarvatathāgataiḥ vairocanādibhiḥ sarvadharmagrantharūpā- pannāḥ deśanāḥ teṣāṃ dharmāṇāmanantapratibhānatvādīśvaraḥ prabhuḥ abhiṣiktaḥ anujñātaḥ |
etacca deśanāyāṃ vāgvajrasyādhikārāt |
yathā bhagavadvacanamaṅgīkṛtavān, mahāsattvastūṣṇīmabhūt |
tantrasamarpaṇānantaraṃ sarvatathāgatairanabhilāpyabuddhakṣetraparamāṇurajaḥsamaiḥ kiṃ kṛtamityāha atha te sarvatathāgatāḥ trivajrasattvākṣareṣu kāyavākcittaṃ praveśayā- māsuḥ |
etaduktaṃ bhavati |
sarve pañcakulīnāḥ nairmāṇikarūpāḥ anantāparyanta- buddhabodhisattvakrodharājādayaḥ vairocanāmitābhākṣobhyatvaṃ prāptā iti yāvat |
tattra vairocanastathāgataḥ traidhātukakāyavajrasvabhāvo yathā taddarśayannāha atha bhagavān vairocanastathāgata ityādi |
asmin tribhavālaye aṇḍajādīnā- muttamamadhyamādhamasattvarāśīnāṃ yaḥ kāyaḥ sa vairocana ityayamarthaḥ sarvannaidhātuka- kāyavajreṣu vijahāretyanena darśitaḥ |
kāyavairocanayorādhārādheyatvenābhāvāt |
tathaiva niṣpannāniṣpannakāyayorabhinnatāṃ prakāśayannāha sarvatathāgatakāyasamatāṃ māyopamatāmālambya tūṣṇīmabhūditi |
evaṃ taidhātuke sattvānāṃ yā vāk so'mi- tābha ityayamarthaḥ |
atha vāgvajrastathāgataḥ sarvatraidhātukavāgvatreṣu vijahāretyanena darśitaḥ |
evameva sarvasattvānāṃ yaccittaṃ tadakṣobhya ityayamarthaḥ |
atha vajrapāṇistathāgataḥ sarvatraidhātukacittavajñeṣu vijahāretyanena vajrapadena darśitaḥ |
sarvatathāgatānāṃ yaccittaṃ sarvasattvānāṃ ca yaccittamekameveti darśayannāha sarvatathāgatacittasamatāṃ satyadvayaikatāmālambya tūṣṇīmabhūditi paryantamāha guru- pādaprasādāt |
tantre arthanayadharmatāyāmapagatasaṃśaya[:] anyatamaḥ kaścit saṃśayo me |
ṣaṭ- koṭivyākhyānavahirmukhā mantriṇo yathārutadeśanāpāṭhamāśritya svakīyācāryāmnāyena pañcākārābhisaṃbodhikrameṇa candravajrakrameṇa bhavotpattikrameṇa vā parikalpita- devatāyogamabhyasyanti |
pariśuddhadevatāyogastu sarvakalpanātītaḥ pratyātmavedyaḥ buddhagocaraḥ skandharatnakaraṇḍāntargataḥ sadasatpakṣarahitaḥ vyañjanānusāriṇāṃ
tārkikāṇāṃ cāviṣayaḥ kevalagurūpadeśagamyaḥ
īdṛgvidhadevatātattvaṃ tathāgate parinirvṛte vajrapāṇyā[di]saṃgītikāra[1]ṇāmapyabhāve paścime kāle buddhasaṃpradāyakaṃ ko'sau pravartayatyavicchedena vineyajanasantāne kathaṃ pravartate ?
saptadaśapaṭalaṣaṭkoṭivyākhyā āha imamanāgatārthaṃ mañjuśrībhaṭṭārako'pi 'laṅkāvatārasūtre tathāgataṃ pari- pṛṣṭavān
tadihāvatāryate
pratyātmavedyaṃ jñānaṃ te tārkikāṇāmagocaram
iti
paścātkāle gate nātha brūhi ko dhārayiṣyati
||
nirvṛte sugate paścāt kālātīto bhaviṣyati
|

||
dakṣiṇāpathavaidalyāṃ bhikṣuḥ śrīmān mahāyaśāḥ
|
[śrī]nāgārjunanāmā ca sadasatpakṣadālakaḥ
||
prakāśya loke mahad yānaṃ mahāyānamanuttaram
|
āsādya bhūmi muditāṃ yāsyate'sau sukhāvatīm

tadanenānukrameṇa tathāgate parinivṛte'pi yāvalloke saddharmo sthātā tāvad vajrayāne mahāyogatantrāgamamāśritya utpattikramānupūrveṇa niṣpannakramādhigama :
`śrīnāgārjunabhaṭṭārakapādādārabhya nidhānamiva guruparvakrameṇa guruvaktrāt sāmājikā- nāmasmin janmani buddhatvabhājanānāṃ santāne saṃkrāmati
evaṃ śrīnāgārjuna- pādabhaṭṭārakānuprāptasvakārthaḥ
pratyātmavedyaṃ mahāvajradharasamādhiṃ loke pratipādya devamanuṣyasukhamatikramya tīrthikaśrāvakapratyekavuddhadhyānasamādhisamāpatti sukha- matikramya utpādabhaṅgarahitaṃ sarvākā ravaropetamāsecanakavigrahaṃ daśavalavaiśāradyādi- buddhālaṅkṛtaṃ tathāgatakāyaṃ pratilabhya sukhāvatīṃ gatvāṣṭaguṇaiśvaryānvito viharati
evaṃ vyākṛtāvyakṛtavikalpamapanīya svabhāvajñasyeha paratna paryavasānaṃ niści- tyedānīṃ tantramupasaṃharati evamityādinā
sakalatantraṃ saptavidhālaṅkārā- khyāyikānyāyena śrutvā arthanayadharmatāmadhigamya kramadvayopadeśādānapūrvakaṃ śrotṛbhyaḥ saṃprakāśyārakṣārthaṃ parisamāpayannānmanaḥ pratijñāśayaṃ saṃprakāśayan saṃgītikṛdāha idamavocaditi
idaṃ guhyasamājatantranayamavocat prakāśita- vān mahāvajradharo mahāsukhaḥ
sarvatathāgatānāṃ kāyavākcittarahasyamuddiśya pravṛtto `guhyānāṃ samājaḥ samūho yasmiṃstasmin guhyasamāje tantrarāje sarva- tathāgatasamayasaṃvaravajrādhiṣṭhānarājo nāma paṭala iti
sarvatathāgatāḥ vai- rocanādayaḥ teṣāṃ samayo'nuṣṭheyārthaḥ rakṣaṇādipañcasaṃvaraḥ pratipattiḥ tathāgata- kulādipañcakaṃ tadadvayamalaṅghanīyatvād vajraṃ tasyādhiṣṭhānaḥ rājo mahāvajradharaḥ guhyasamājapradīpodyotane sarvakuśalātmakaḥ anādinidhanaḥ ādibuddhaḥ mahāvidyāpuruṣamūrtiḥ jñānamūrtiḥ prakṛtiprabhāsvarātmakaḥ trilokātmakaḥ triyānātmakaḥ trivajrātmakaḥ satya- dvayātmakaḥ ityādi paṣṭhatathāgatasyānanto nāmaparyāyaḥ
tatpratipādako śeṣa- granthasandarbhaḥ pūrvavat
paṭalamiva paṭalaḥ
paṭkoṭiyuktirahitaḥ khalu yogatantre vedyo'tivakti sugatasya vaco'tisūkṣmam
tattvaṃ na paśyati hi so'kṣaramātradarśī candraṃ didṛkṣuriva cāṅgulimīkṣamāṇaḥ
||
bhūyobhirjanapuṅgavairviracitaṃ ṣaṭkoṭimāgaṃ cirāt yenāttrākhilatantranītivivṛtirvyākhyāya tantrantvimam
yatprāptaṃ kuśalaṃ mayā ciramiyaṃ ṭīkā pradīpojjvalā stheyā tena ca satsukhaṃ padavaraṃ bhūyājjagaccandradhīḥ
||
||
iti pradīpoyotanaṭīkāyāṃ saptadaśapaṭale paṭkoṭivyākhyā
||
samāptā ceyaṃ pradīpoddyotanaṭīkā
||
ye dharmā hetuprabhavā hetuṃ teṣāṃ tathāgato hyavadat
teṣāṃ ca yo nirodha evaṃvādī mahāśramaṇaḥ
||
deyadharmo'yaṃ pravaramahāyānayāyinaḥ śrāmaṇerakaguṇākarapālasya
yadatra puṇyaṃ tad bhavatvācāryopādhyāya mātāpitṛpūrvaṅgamaṃ kṛtvā sakala sattvarāśeranuttarajñānaphalāvāptaya iti
||
śubhamastu
||
parameśvaraparamasaugatamahārāja
||
śubhamastu sarvasattvānām
||
yānyasyā 'vṛtavyā' 'tantrāntimam
